FILE: scripts\finalTwoMessageGateV22.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from 'fs';
import path from 'path';
import crypto from 'crypto';

import { Address, Cell } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import {
    JettonMaster,
    JettonWallet,
} from '@ton/ton';
import { DEX, pTON } from '@ston-fi/sdk';
import {
    TonConnect,
    CHAIN,
} from '@tonconnect/sdk';

const USER = Address.parse(
    'EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix'
);

const ROUTER_ADDRESS = Address.parse(
    'EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3'
);

const ROOSTER_MASTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx'
);

const PTON_MASTER = Address.parse(
    'EQBnGWMCf3-FZZq1W4IWcWiGAc3PHuZ0_H-7sad2oY00o83S'
);

const EXPECTED_POOL = Address.parse(
    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf'
);

const EXPECTED_USER_ROOSTER_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQBzQOOJu9qQVli2R0o6p3pXvi_wj9O2ncMhv3UyAjvMiMOh'
);

const EXPECTED_POOL_ROOSTER_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQCePy3CW0qsWj03tvCwBxpavtYcu27LURfEy7H6tRhTbwsq'
);

const EXPECTED_POOL_PTON_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQCSIMGBps_qzRG3uPYhON8bucyCtu0mYdL1-u4gSz77IBa3'
);

const ROOSTER_AMOUNT = 100000000000n;
const PTON_AMOUNT = 249908n;

const MIN_LP_OUT = 150109265n;

const EXPECTED_ROOSTER_TX_VALUE = 300000000n;
const EXPECTED_PTON_TX_VALUE = 310249908n;

const EXPECTED_TOTAL_TX_VALUE =
    EXPECTED_ROOSTER_TX_VALUE +
    EXPECTED_PTON_TX_VALUE;

const EXTRA_TON_RESERVE = 100000000n;

const JETTON_TRANSFER_OPCODE = 0x0f8a7ea5;
const TON_TRANSFER_OPCODE = 0x01f3835d;
const PROVIDE_LP_OPCODE = 0x37c096df;

const STORAGE_PATH = path.resolve(
    process.cwd(),
    'temp',
    'testnet',
    'tonconnect.json'
);

const MANIFEST_URL =
    'https://raw.githubusercontent.com/ton-org/blueprint/main/tonconnect/manifest.json';

function section(title: string) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('='.repeat(64));
    console.log(title);
    console.log('='.repeat(64));
}

function pass(label: string) {
    console.log(`${label}: PASS`);
}

function fail(label: string, reason: string): never {
    console.log(`${label}: FAIL`);
    throw new Error(`FINAL GATE STOP: ${reason}`);
}

function sameAddress(
    a: Address,
    b: Address
): boolean {
    return a.toRawString() === b.toRawString();
}

function fmtTon(value: bigint): string {
    const whole = value / 1000000000n;
    const fraction = (value % 1000000000n)
        .toString()
        .padStart(9, '0');

    return `${whole}.${fraction} TON`;
}

function sha256(buffer: Buffer): string {
    return crypto
        .createHash('sha256')
        .update(buffer)
        .digest('hex');
}

function createStorage() {
    return {
        async setItem(
            key: string,
            value: string
        ): Promise<void> {
            const dir = path.dirname(STORAGE_PATH);

            if (!fs.existsSync(dir)) {
                fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
            }

            let data: Record<string, string> = {};

            if (fs.existsSync(STORAGE_PATH)) {
                try {
                    data = JSON.parse(
                        fs.readFileSync(
                            STORAGE_PATH,
                            'utf8'
                        )
                    );
                } catch {
                    data = {};
                }
            }

            data[key] = value;

            fs.writeFileSync(
                STORAGE_PATH,
                JSON.stringify(data, null, 2)
            );
        },

        async getItem(
            key: string
        ): Promise<string | null> {
            if (!fs.existsSync(STORAGE_PATH)) {
                return null;
            }

            try {
                const data: Record<string, string> =
                    JSON.parse(
                        fs.readFileSync(
                            STORAGE_PATH,
                            'utf8'
                        )
                    );

                return data[key] ?? null;
            } catch {
                return null;
            }
        },

        async removeItem(
            _key: string
        ): Promise<void> {
            /*
             * This gate never intentionally modifies
             * the TonConnect connection.
             */
        },
    };
}

function decodeJettonTransfer(body: Cell) {
    const s = body.beginParse();

    const opcode = s.loadUint(32);
    const queryId = s.loadUintBig(64);
    const amount = s.loadCoins();
    const destination = s.loadAddress();
    const responseDestination = s.loadAddress();

    const hasCustomPayload = s.loadBit();

    if (hasCustomPayload) {
        s.loadRef();
    }

    const forwardTonAmount = s.loadCoins();
    const hasForwardPayload = s.loadBit();

    let forwardPayload: Cell | null = null;

    if (hasForwardPayload) {
        forwardPayload = s.loadRef();
    }

    return {
        opcode,
        queryId,
        amount,
        destination,
        responseDestination,
        hasCustomPayload,
        forwardTonAmount,
        hasForwardPayload,
        forwardPayload,
    };
}

function decodeTonTransfer(body: Cell) {
    const s = body.beginParse();

    const opcode = s.loadUint(32);
    const queryId = s.loadUintBig(64);
    const amount = s.loadCoins();
    const refundAddress = s.loadAddress();

    const hasForwardPayload = s.loadBit();

    let forwardPayload: Cell | null = null;

    if (hasForwardPayload) {
        forwardPayload = s.loadRef();
    }

    return {
        opcode,
        queryId,
        amount,
        refundAddress,
        hasForwardPayload,
        forwardPayload,
    };
}
function decodeProvideLp(payload: Cell) {
    const s = payload.beginParse();

    const opcode = s.loadUint(32);
    const routerWallet = s.loadAddress();
    const refundAddress = s.loadAddress();
    const excessesAddress = s.loadAddress();
    const deadline = s.loadUintBig(64);

    const nested = s.loadRef().beginParse();

    const minLpOut = nested.loadCoins();
    const receiverAddress = nested.loadAddress();
    const bothPositive = nested.loadBit();

    const customGas = nested.loadCoins();

    const hasCustomPayload = nested.loadBit();

    if (hasCustomPayload) {
        nested.loadRef();
    }

    return {
        opcode,
        routerWallet,
        refundAddress,
        excessesAddress,
        deadline,
        minLpOut,
        receiverAddress,
        bothPositive,
        customGas,
        hasCustomPayload,
    };
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '================================================================'
    );
    console.log(
        'STON.fi v2.2 FINAL TWO-MESSAGE READ-ONLY GATE'
    );
    console.log(
        '================================================================'
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('MODE: FINAL READ ONLY');
    console.log('NETWORK: TESTNET');
    console.log('SEND: NO');
    console.log('APPROVAL: NO');
    console.log('BROADCAST: NO');
    console.log('STATE CHANGE: NO');

    section('1. TONCONNECT WALLET CAPABILITY');

    if (!fs.existsSync(STORAGE_PATH)) {
        fail(
            'TonConnect storage',
            'Storage file not found.'
        );
    }

    const connector = new TonConnect({
        storage: createStorage(),
        manifestUrl: MANIFEST_URL,
    });

    await connector.restoreConnection();

    if (!connector.wallet) {
        fail(
            'TonConnect connection',
            'No restored wallet.'
        );
    }

    const wallet = connector.wallet as any;

    const connectedAddress =
        wallet.account?.address;

    if (!connectedAddress) {
        fail(
            'Wallet address',
            'Wallet did not report an account address.'
        );
    }

    const connectedUser =
        Address.parse(connectedAddress);

    console.log(
        `Connected wallet: ${connectedUser.toString({
            bounceable: true,
            testOnly: true,
        })}`
    );

    console.log(
        `Expected wallet: ${USER.toString({
            bounceable: true,
            testOnly: true,
        })}`
    );

    if (!sameAddress(connectedUser, USER)) {
        fail(
            'Wallet identity',
            'Connected wallet does not match USER.'
        );
    }

    pass('Wallet identity');

    if (
        wallet.account?.chain !==
        CHAIN.TESTNET
    ) {
        fail(
            'TESTNET',
            `Wallet chain is ${wallet.account?.chain}, expected ${CHAIN.TESTNET}.`
        );
    }

    pass('TESTNET');

    const features =
        wallet.device?.features;

    if (!Array.isArray(features)) {
        fail(
            'Wallet features',
            'device.features is unavailable.'
        );
    }

    const sendFeature =
        features.find(
            (feature: any) =>
                feature?.name ===
                'SendTransaction'
        );

    if (!sendFeature) {
        fail(
            'SendTransaction capability',
            'Wallet does not advertise SendTransaction.'
        );
    }

    const maxMessages =
        Number(sendFeature.maxMessages);

    console.log(
        `Wallet maxMessages: ${maxMessages}`
    );

    if (
        !Number.isFinite(maxMessages) ||
        maxMessages < 2
    ) {
        fail(
            'Two-message capability',
            'Wallet does not advertise support for 2 messages.'
        );
    }

    pass('Two-message capability');

    section('2. STON.fi v2.2 ROUTER');

    const router =
        provider.open(
            DEX.v2_2.Router.CPI.create(
                ROUTER_ADDRESS
            )
        );

    const routerData =
        await router.getRouterData();

    const routerVersion =
        await router.getRouterVersion();

    console.log(
        `Router: ${ROUTER_ADDRESS.toString()}`
    );

    console.log(
        `Router ID: ${routerData.routerId}`
    );

    console.log(
        `Version: ${JSON.stringify(routerVersion)}`
    );

    console.log(
        `Locked: ${routerData.isLocked}`
    );

    if (
        routerVersion.major !== 2 ||
        routerVersion.minor !== 2
    ) {
        fail(
            'Router version',
            'Router is not v2.2.'
        );
    }

    pass('Router v2.2');

    if (routerData.isLocked) {
        fail(
            'Router unlocked',
            'Router is locked.'
        );
    }

    pass('Router unlocked');

    section('3. LIVE POOL');

    const pool =
        await router.getPool({
            token0: ROOSTER_MASTER,
            token1: PTON_MASTER,
        });

    console.log(
        `Resolved pool: ${pool.address.toString()}`
    );

    console.log(
        `Expected pool: ${EXPECTED_POOL.toString()}`
    );

    if (
        !sameAddress(
            pool.address,
            EXPECTED_POOL
        )
    ) {
        fail(
            'Pool identity',
            'Resolved pool does not match expected pool.'
        );
    }

    pass('Pool identity');

    /*
     * IMPORTANT:
     * router.getPool() returns a CPIPoolV2_2 contract object,
     * not a bare Address.
     */
    const poolContract =
        provider.open(pool);

    const poolData =
        await poolContract.getPoolData();

    console.log(
        `Pool router: ${poolData.routerAddress.toString()}`
    );

    if (
        !sameAddress(
            poolData.routerAddress,
            ROUTER_ADDRESS
        )
    ) {
        fail(
            'Pool router',
            'Pool router does not match v2.2 router.'
        );
    }

    pass('Pool router');

    console.log(
        `Pool locked: ${poolData.isLocked}`
    );

    if (poolData.isLocked) {
        fail(
            'Pool unlocked',
            'Pool is locked.'
        );
    }

    pass('Pool unlocked');

    section('4. POOL TOKEN WALLET CONSISTENCY');

    const poolToken0 =
        poolData.token0WalletAddress;

    const poolToken1 =
        poolData.token1WalletAddress;

    console.log(
        `Pool token0 wallet: ${poolToken0.toString()}`
    );

    console.log(
        `Expected ROOSTER pool wallet: ${EXPECTED_POOL_ROOSTER_WALLET.toString()}`
    );

    if (
        !sameAddress(
            poolToken0,
            EXPECTED_POOL_ROOSTER_WALLET
        )
    ) {
        fail(
            'ROOSTER pool wallet',
            'Pool token0 wallet mismatch.'
        );
    }

    pass('ROOSTER pool wallet');

    console.log(
        `Pool token1 wallet: ${poolToken1.toString()}`
    );

    console.log(
        `Expected pTON pool wallet: ${EXPECTED_POOL_PTON_WALLET.toString()}`
    );

    if (
        !sameAddress(
            poolToken1,
            EXPECTED_POOL_PTON_WALLET
        )
    ) {
        fail(
            'pTON pool wallet',
            'Pool token1 wallet mismatch.'
        );
    }

    pass('pTON pool wallet');

    section('5. LIVE RESERVES AND LP CALCULATION');

    console.log(
        `ROOSTER reserve: ${poolData.reserve0}`
    );

    console.log(
        `pTON reserve: ${poolData.reserve1}`
    );

    console.log(
        `LP total supply: ${poolData.totalSupplyLP}`
    );

    const currentLpFromRooster =
        (
            ROOSTER_AMOUNT *
            poolData.totalSupplyLP
        ) /
        poolData.reserve0;

    const currentLpFromPton =
        (
            PTON_AMOUNT *
            poolData.totalSupplyLP
        ) /
        poolData.reserve1;

    const expectedLp =
        currentLpFromRooster <
        currentLpFromPton
            ? currentLpFromRooster
            : currentLpFromPton;

    console.log(
        `ROOSTER-side expected LP: ${currentLpFromRooster}`
    );

    console.log(
        `pTON-side expected LP: ${currentLpFromPton}`
    );

    console.log(
        `Limiting expected LP: ${expectedLp}`
    );

    console.log(
        `Configured minLpOut: ${MIN_LP_OUT}`
    );

    if (
        MIN_LP_OUT > expectedLp
    ) {
        fail(
            'minLpOut safety',
            'Configured minLpOut exceeds current expected LP.'
        );
    }

    pass('minLpOut safety');

    section('6. LIVE USER BALANCES');

    const roosterMaster =
        provider.open(
            JettonMaster.create(
                ROOSTER_MASTER
            )
        );

    const userRoosterWallet =
        await roosterMaster.getWalletAddress(
            USER
        );

    console.log(
        `Resolved user ROOSTER wallet: ${userRoosterWallet.toString()}`
    );

    console.log(
        `Expected user ROOSTER wallet: ${EXPECTED_USER_ROOSTER_WALLET.toString()}`
    );

    if (
        !sameAddress(
            userRoosterWallet,
            EXPECTED_USER_ROOSTER_WALLET
        )
    ) {
        fail(
            'User ROOSTER wallet',
            'Resolved wallet mismatch.'
        );
    }

    pass('User ROOSTER wallet');

    /*
     * IMPORTANT:
     * userRoosterWallet is an Address.
     * provider.open() requires a Contract.
     */
    const userRoosterContract =
    provider.open(
        JettonWallet.create(
            userRoosterWallet
        )
    );

const roosterBalance =
    await userRoosterContract.getBalance();

console.log(
    `User ROOSTER balance: ${roosterBalance}`
);

if (
    roosterBalance <
    ROOSTER_AMOUNT
) {
    fail(
        'ROOSTER balance',
        'Insufficient ROOSTER.'
    );
}

pass('ROOSTER balance');

    const userState =
        await provider.provider(
            USER
        ).getState();

    const tonBalance =
        userState.balance;

    console.log(
        `User TON balance: ${fmtTon(tonBalance)}`
    );

    console.log(
        `Required TX total: ${fmtTon(EXPECTED_TOTAL_TX_VALUE)}`
    );

    console.log(
        `Extra reserve: ${fmtTon(EXTRA_TON_RESERVE)}`
    );

    const minimumRequired =
        EXPECTED_TOTAL_TX_VALUE +
        EXTRA_TON_RESERVE;

    console.log(
        `Minimum recommended balance: ${fmtTon(minimumRequired)}`
    );

    if (
        tonBalance <
        minimumRequired
    ) {
        fail(
            'TON balance',
            'TON balance does not cover both transactions plus reserve.'
        );
    }

    pass('TON balance');

    section('7. FRESH DEADLINE');

    const now =
        Math.floor(
            Date.now() / 1000
        );

    const deadline =
        now + 600;

    console.log(
        `Current Unix: ${now}`
    );

    console.log(
        `Fresh deadline: ${deadline}`
    );

    console.log(
        `Validity: ${deadline - now} seconds`
    );

    if (
        deadline <= now
    ) {
        fail(
            'Deadline',
            'Deadline is not in the future.'
        );
    }

    pass('Deadline');

    section('8. BUILD FRESH ROOSTER LEG');

    const roosterTx =
        await router.getProvideLiquidityJettonTxParams({
            userWalletAddress: USER,
            sendTokenAddress: ROOSTER_MASTER,
            otherTokenAddress: PTON_MASTER,
            sendAmount: ROOSTER_AMOUNT,
            minLpOut: MIN_LP_OUT,
            receiverAddress: USER,
            refundAddress: USER,
            excessesAddress: USER,
            deadline,
        });

    console.log(
        `ROOSTER TO: ${roosterTx.to.toString()}`
    );

    console.log(
        `ROOSTER VALUE: ${fmtTon(roosterTx.value)}`
    );

    if (
        !sameAddress(
            roosterTx.to,
            EXPECTED_USER_ROOSTER_WALLET
        )
    ) {
        fail(
            'ROOSTER destination',
            'ROOSTER outer destination mismatch.'
        );
    }

    pass('ROOSTER destination');

    if (
        BigInt(roosterTx.value) !==
        EXPECTED_ROOSTER_TX_VALUE
    ) {
        fail(
            'ROOSTER value',
            'ROOSTER transaction value mismatch.'
        );
    }

    pass('ROOSTER value');

    section('9. BUILD FRESH pTON LEG');

    const proxyTon =
        pTON.v2_1.create(
            PTON_MASTER
        );

    const ptonTx =
        await router.getProvideLiquidityTonTxParams({
            userWalletAddress: USER,
            otherTokenAddress: ROOSTER_MASTER,
            sendAmount: PTON_AMOUNT,
            minLpOut: MIN_LP_OUT,
            receiverAddress: USER,
            refundAddress: USER,
            excessesAddress: USER,
            deadline,
            proxyTon,
        });

    console.log(
        `pTON TO: ${ptonTx.to.toString()}`
    );

    console.log(
        `pTON VALUE: ${fmtTon(ptonTx.value)}`
    );

    if (
        !sameAddress(
            ptonTx.to,
            EXPECTED_POOL_PTON_WALLET
        )
    ) {
        fail(
            'pTON destination',
            'pTON outer destination mismatch.'
        );
    }

    pass('pTON destination');

    if (
        BigInt(ptonTx.value) !==
        EXPECTED_PTON_TX_VALUE
    ) {
        fail(
            'pTON value',
            'pTON transaction value mismatch.'
        );
    }

    pass('pTON value');

    section('10. SERIALIZE FRESH BOCS');

    if (!roosterTx.body) {
        fail(
            'ROOSTER body',
            'SDK returned no ROOSTER payload body.'
        );
    }

    if (!ptonTx.body) {
        fail(
            'pTON body',
            'SDK returned no pTON payload body.'
        );
    }

    const roosterBody =
        roosterTx.body;

    const ptonBody =
        ptonTx.body;

    const roosterBoc =
        roosterBody.toBoc();

    const ptonBoc =
        ptonBody.toBoc();

    console.log(
        `ROOSTER BOC bytes: ${roosterBoc.length}`
    );

    console.log(
        `ROOSTER SHA256: ${sha256(roosterBoc)}`
    );

    console.log(
        `pTON BOC bytes: ${ptonBoc.length}`
    );

    console.log(
        `pTON SHA256: ${sha256(ptonBoc)}`
    );

    if (
        roosterBoc.length !== 264
    ) {
        fail(
            'ROOSTER BOC size',
            'Unexpected ROOSTER BOC size.'
        );
    }

    pass('ROOSTER BOC size');

    if (
        ptonBoc.length !== 224
    ) {
        fail(
            'pTON BOC size',
            'Unexpected pTON BOC size.'
        );
    }

    pass('pTON BOC size');

    section('11. DECODE ROOSTER BOC');

    const roosterTransfer =
        decodeJettonTransfer(
            roosterBody
        );

    console.log(
        `Opcode: 0x${roosterTransfer.opcode.toString(16)}`
    );

    if (
        roosterTransfer.opcode !==
        JETTON_TRANSFER_OPCODE
    ) {
        fail(
            'ROOSTER transfer opcode',
            'Unexpected opcode.'
        );
    }

    pass('ROOSTER transfer opcode');

    if (
        roosterTransfer.amount !==
        ROOSTER_AMOUNT
    ) {
        fail(
            'ROOSTER amount',
            'Unexpected ROOSTER amount.'
        );
    }

    pass('ROOSTER amount');

    if (
        !sameAddress(
            roosterTransfer.destination,
            ROUTER_ADDRESS
        )
    ) {
        fail(
            'ROOSTER nested destination',
            'ROOSTER transfer does not point to router.'
        );
    }

    pass('ROOSTER nested destination');

    if (
        !sameAddress(
            roosterTransfer.responseDestination,
            USER
        )
    ) {
        fail(
            'ROOSTER response address',
            'ROOSTER response destination mismatch.'
        );
    }

    pass('ROOSTER response address');

    if (
        roosterTransfer.hasCustomPayload
    ) {
        fail(
            'ROOSTER custom payload',
            'Unexpected custom payload.'
        );
    }

    pass('ROOSTER custom payload');

    if (
        roosterTransfer.forwardTonAmount !==
        235000000n
    ) {
        fail(
            'ROOSTER forward TON',
            'Unexpected forward TON amount.'
        );
    }

    pass('ROOSTER forward TON');

    if (
        !roosterTransfer.hasForwardPayload ||
        !roosterTransfer.forwardPayload
    ) {
        fail(
            'ROOSTER forward payload',
            'Forward payload is missing.'
        );
    }

    pass('ROOSTER forward payload');

    const roosterProvide =
        decodeProvideLp(
            roosterTransfer.forwardPayload
        );

    if (
        roosterProvide.opcode !==
        PROVIDE_LP_OPCODE
    ) {
        fail(
            'ROOSTER PROVIDE_LP opcode',
            'Unexpected PROVIDE_LP opcode.'
        );
    }

    pass('ROOSTER PROVIDE_LP opcode');

    if (
        !sameAddress(
            roosterProvide.routerWallet,
            EXPECTED_POOL_PTON_WALLET
        )
    ) {
        fail(
            'ROOSTER router wallet',
            'ROOSTER leg does not point to pTON pool wallet.'
        );
    }

    pass('ROOSTER router wallet');

    if (
        !sameAddress(
            roosterProvide.refundAddress,
            USER
        )
    ) {
        fail(
            'ROOSTER refund',
            'Refund address mismatch.'
        );
    }

    pass('ROOSTER refund');

    if (
        !sameAddress(
            roosterProvide.excessesAddress,
            USER
        )
    ) {
        fail(
            'ROOSTER excesses',
            'Excesses address mismatch.'
        );
    }

    pass('ROOSTER excesses');

    if (
        roosterProvide.deadline !==
        BigInt(deadline)
    ) {
        fail(
            'ROOSTER deadline',
            'Deadline mismatch.'
        );
    }

    pass('ROOSTER deadline');

    if (
        roosterProvide.minLpOut !==
        MIN_LP_OUT
    ) {
        fail(
            'ROOSTER minLpOut',
            'minLpOut mismatch.'
        );
    }

    pass('ROOSTER minLpOut');

    if (
        !sameAddress(
            roosterProvide.receiverAddress,
            USER
        )
    ) {
        fail(
            'ROOSTER receiver',
            'Receiver mismatch.'
        );
    }

    pass('ROOSTER receiver');

    if (
        !roosterProvide.bothPositive
    ) {
        fail(
            'ROOSTER bothPositive',
            'bothPositive is not true.'
        );
    }

    pass('ROOSTER bothPositive');

    if (
        roosterProvide.customGas !==
        0n
    ) {
        fail(
            'ROOSTER custom gas',
            'Unexpected custom gas.'
        );
    }

    pass('ROOSTER custom gas');

    section('12. DECODE pTON BOC');

    const ptonTransfer =
        decodeTonTransfer(
            ptonBody
        );

    console.log(
        `Opcode: 0x${ptonTransfer.opcode.toString(16)}`
    );

    if (
        ptonTransfer.opcode !==
        TON_TRANSFER_OPCODE
    ) {
        fail(
            'pTON TON_TRANSFER opcode',
            'Unexpected opcode.'
        );
    }

    pass('pTON TON_TRANSFER opcode');

    if (
        ptonTransfer.queryId !==
        0n
    ) {
        fail(
            'pTON queryId',
            'Expected queryId 0.'
        );
    }

    pass('pTON queryId');

    if (
        ptonTransfer.amount !==
        PTON_AMOUNT
    ) {
        fail(
            'pTON amount',
            'Unexpected pTON amount.'
        );
    }

      pass('pTON amount');

    if (
        !sameAddress(
            ptonTransfer.refundAddress,
            USER
        )
    ) {
        fail(
            'pTON refund address',
            'pTON refund address mismatch.'
        );
    }

    pass('pTON refund address');

if (
    !ptonTransfer.hasForwardPayload ||
    !ptonTransfer.forwardPayload
) {
    fail(
        'pTON forward payload',
        'Forward payload is missing.'
    );
}
    

    pass('pTON forward payload');

    const ptonProvide =
        decodeProvideLp(
            ptonTransfer.forwardPayload
        );

    if (
        ptonProvide.opcode !==
        PROVIDE_LP_OPCODE
    ) {
        fail(
            'pTON PROVIDE_LP opcode',
            'Unexpected PROVIDE_LP opcode.'
        );
    }

    pass('pTON PROVIDE_LP opcode');

    if (
        !sameAddress(
            ptonProvide.routerWallet,
            EXPECTED_POOL_ROOSTER_WALLET
        )
    ) {
        fail(
            'pTON router wallet',
            'pTON leg does not point to ROOSTER pool wallet.'
        );
    }

    pass('pTON router wallet');

    if (
        !sameAddress(
            ptonProvide.refundAddress,
            USER
        )
    ) {
        fail(
            'pTON refund',
            'Refund address mismatch.'
        );
    }

    pass('pTON refund');

    if (
        !sameAddress(
            ptonProvide.excessesAddress,
            USER
        )
    ) {
        fail(
            'pTON excesses',
            'Excesses address mismatch.'
        );
    }

    pass('pTON excesses');

    if (
        ptonProvide.deadline !==
        BigInt(deadline)
    ) {
        fail(
            'pTON deadline',
            'Deadline mismatch.'
        );
    }

    pass('pTON deadline');

    if (
        ptonProvide.minLpOut !==
        MIN_LP_OUT
    ) {
        fail(
            'pTON minLpOut',
            'minLpOut mismatch.'
        );
    }

    pass('pTON minLpOut');

    if (
        !sameAddress(
            ptonProvide.receiverAddress,
            USER
        )
    ) {
        fail(
            'pTON receiver',
            'Receiver mismatch.'
        );
    }

    pass('pTON receiver');

    if (
        !ptonProvide.bothPositive
    ) {
        fail(
            'pTON bothPositive',
            'bothPositive is not true.'
        );
    }

    pass('pTON bothPositive');

    if (
        ptonProvide.customGas !==
        0n
    ) {
        fail(
            'pTON custom gas',
            'Unexpected custom gas.'
        );
    }

    pass('pTON custom gas');

    section('13. CROSS-LEG CONSISTENCY');

    if (
        !sameAddress(
            roosterProvide.refundAddress,
            ptonProvide.refundAddress
        )
    ) {
        fail(
            'Same refund address',
            'Refund addresses differ.'
        );
    }

    pass('Same refund address');

    if (
        !sameAddress(
            roosterProvide.excessesAddress,
            ptonProvide.excessesAddress
        )
    ) {
        fail(
            'Same excesses address',
            'Excesses addresses differ.'
        );
    }

    pass('Same excesses address');

    if (
        !sameAddress(
            roosterProvide.receiverAddress,
            ptonProvide.receiverAddress
        )
    ) {
        fail(
            'Same receiver',
            'Receiver addresses differ.'
        );
    }

    pass('Same receiver');

    if (
        roosterProvide.minLpOut !==
        ptonProvide.minLpOut
    ) {
        fail(
            'Same minLpOut',
            'minLpOut differs between legs.'
        );
    }

    pass('Same minLpOut');

    if (
        roosterProvide.deadline !==
        ptonProvide.deadline
    ) {
        fail(
            'Same deadline',
            'Deadlines differ between legs.'
        );
    }

    pass('Same deadline');

    if (
        !sameAddress(
            roosterProvide.routerWallet,
            EXPECTED_POOL_PTON_WALLET
        )
    ) {
        fail(
            'ROOSTER -> pTON pool',
            'Opposite wallet relationship invalid.'
        );
    }

    pass('ROOSTER -> pTON pool');

    if (
        !sameAddress(
            ptonProvide.routerWallet,
            EXPECTED_POOL_ROOSTER_WALLET
        )
    ) {
        fail(
            'pTON -> ROOSTER pool',
            'Opposite wallet relationship invalid.'
        );
    }

    pass('pTON -> ROOSTER pool');

    section('14. OUTER MESSAGE STRUCTURE');

    const message1 = {
        address: roosterTx.to.toString({
            bounceable: true,
            testOnly: true,
        }),
        amount: BigInt(
            roosterTx.value
        ).toString(),
        payload: roosterBoc.toString(
            'base64'
        ),
    };

    const message2 = {
        address: ptonTx.to.toString({
            bounceable: true,
            testOnly: true,
        }),
        amount: BigInt(
            ptonTx.value
        ).toString(),
        payload: ptonBoc.toString(
            'base64'
        ),
    };

    console.log(
        `Message 1 TO: ${message1.address}`
    );

    console.log(
        `Message 1 VALUE: ${fmtTon(
            BigInt(message1.amount)
        )}`
    );

    console.log(
        'Message 1 payload: PRESENT'
    );

    console.log(
        `Message 2 TO: ${message2.address}`
    );

    console.log(
        `Message 2 VALUE: ${fmtTon(
            BigInt(message2.amount)
        )}`
    );

    console.log(
        'Message 2 payload: PRESENT'
    );

    if (
        !sameAddress(
            Address.parse(message1.address),
            EXPECTED_USER_ROOSTER_WALLET
        )
    ) {
        fail(
            'Message 1 destination',
            'Outer destination mismatch.'
        );
    }

    pass('Message 1 destination');

    if (
        BigInt(message1.amount) !==
        EXPECTED_ROOSTER_TX_VALUE
    ) {
        fail(
            'Message 1 amount',
            'Outer amount mismatch.'
        );
    }

    pass('Message 1 amount');

    if (
        !sameAddress(
            Address.parse(message2.address),
            EXPECTED_POOL_PTON_WALLET
        )
    ) {
        fail(
            'Message 2 destination',
            'Outer destination mismatch.'
        );
    }

    pass('Message 2 destination');

    if (
        BigInt(message2.amount) !==
        EXPECTED_PTON_TX_VALUE
    ) {
        fail(
            'Message 2 amount',
            'Outer amount mismatch.'
        );
    }

    pass('Message 2 amount');

    section('15. TWO-MESSAGE REQUEST');

    const nowFinal =
        Math.floor(
            Date.now() / 1000
        );

    const validUntil =
        deadline;

    const request = {
        validUntil,
        network: CHAIN.TESTNET,
        messages: [
            message1,
            message2,
        ],
    };

    console.log(
        `Network: ${request.network}`
    );

    console.log(
        `validUntil: ${request.validUntil}`
    );

    console.log(
        `Message count: ${request.messages.length}`
    );

    if (
        request.messages.length !== 2
    ) {
        fail(
            'Message count',
            'Request does not contain exactly two messages.'
        );
    }

    pass('Message count = 2');

    if (
        request.network !==
        CHAIN.TESTNET
    ) {
        fail(
            'Request network',
            'Request is not TESTNET.'
        );
    }

    pass('Request network TESTNET');

    if (
        request.validUntil <=
        nowFinal
    ) {
        fail(
            'Request validUntil',
            'validUntil is not in the future.'
        );
    }

    pass('Request validUntil');

    if (
        maxMessages <
        request.messages.length
    ) {
        fail(
            'Wallet message capacity',
            'Wallet capability is below request message count.'
        );
    }

    pass('Wallet capacity >= 2');

    section('16. VALUE ARITHMETIC');

    const message1Value =
        BigInt(message1.amount);

    const message2Value =
        BigInt(message2.amount);

    const total =
        message1Value +
        message2Value;

    console.log(
        `Message 1: ${fmtTon(message1Value)}`
    );

    console.log(
        `Message 2: ${fmtTon(message2Value)}`
    );

    console.log(
        `Total: ${fmtTon(total)}`
    );

    console.log(
        `Expected: ${fmtTon(EXPECTED_TOTAL_TX_VALUE)}`
    );

    if (
        total !==
        EXPECTED_TOTAL_TX_VALUE
    ) {
        fail(
            'Total value',
            'Two message values do not match expected total.'
        );
    }

    pass('Total value');

    section('17. FINAL PAYLOAD FINGERPRINTS');

    console.log(
        `ROOSTER BOC SHA256: ${sha256(roosterBoc)}`
    );

    console.log(
        `pTON BOC SHA256: ${sha256(ptonBoc)}`
    );

    console.log(
        `ROOSTER BOC bytes: ${roosterBoc.length}`
    );

    console.log(
        `pTON BOC bytes: ${ptonBoc.length}`
    );

    section('18. FINAL GATE RESULT');

    console.log('Wallet identity: PASS');
    console.log('TESTNET: PASS');
    console.log('Wallet SendTransaction: PASS');
    console.log(
        `Wallet maxMessages: ${maxMessages}`
    );
    console.log('Router v2.2: PASS');
    console.log('Router unlocked: PASS');
    console.log('Pool identity: PASS');
    console.log('Pool router: PASS');
    console.log('Pool unlocked: PASS');
    console.log('Pool token wallets: PASS');
    console.log('Live reserves: PASS');
    console.log('Live user balances: PASS');
    console.log('Fresh deadline: PASS');
    console.log('ROOSTER BOC: PASS');
    console.log('pTON BOC: PASS');
    console.log('Cross-leg consistency: PASS');
    console.log('Two-message structure: PASS');
    console.log('Value arithmetic: PASS');

    console.log('');

    console.log(
        'STATUS: FINAL TWO-MESSAGE GATE PASSED'
    );

    section('19. ABSOLUTE SAFETY');

    console.log(
        'connector.sendTransaction(): NOT CALLED'
    );

    console.log(
        'sender.send(): NOT CALLED'
    );

    console.log(
        'wallet.send(): NOT CALLED'
    );

    console.log(
        'Tonkeeper approval: NOT REQUESTED'
    );

    console.log(
        'Transaction broadcast: NO'
    );

    console.log(
        'Wallet state change: NO'
    );

    console.log(
        'Pool state change: NO'
    );

    console.log('');

    console.log(
        'FINAL GATE IS READ-ONLY.'
    );

    console.log(
        'No transaction was submitted.'
    );
}

import { Address, Cell } from '@ton/core';
import fs from 'fs';
import path from 'path';

const ROOT = path.resolve('.');

const ROOSTER_BOC = path.join(ROOT, 'rooster_v22_generated.boc');
const PTON_BOC = path.join(ROOT, 'pton_v22_generated.boc');

const ROUTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3',
);

const POOL = Address.parse(
    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf',
);

const USER = Address.parse(
    'EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix',
);

const ROOSTER_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQCePy3CW0qsWj03tvCwBxpavtYcu27LURfEy7H6tRhTbwsq',
);

const PTON_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQCSIMGBps_qzRG3uPYhON8bucyCtu0mYdL1-u4gSz77IBa3',
);

const ROOSTER_AMOUNT = 100000000000n;
const PTON_AMOUNT = 249908n;
const MIN_LP = 150109265n;
const DEADLINE = 1790697420n;

const JETTON_TRANSFER = 0x0f8a7ea5;
const TON_TRANSFER = 0x01f3835d;
const PROVIDE_LP = 0x37c096df;

function addressEqual(a: Address | null, b: Address): boolean {
    return a !== null && a.equals(b);
}

function pass(label: string, ok: boolean, detail = '') {
    console.log(
        `${ok ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL'} | ${label}${detail ? ` | ${detail}` : ''}`,
    );
}

function showAddress(label: string, actual: Address | null) {
    console.log(`${label}: ${actual?.toString() ?? 'null'}`);
}

function loadBoc(file: string): Cell {
    if (!fs.existsSync(file)) {
        throw new Error(`Missing BOC: ${file}`);
    }

    const data = fs.readFileSync(file);

    const magic = data.readUInt32BE(0);

    pass(
        `${path.basename(file)} BOC magic`,
        magic === 0xb5ee9c72,
    );

    const cells = Cell.fromBoc(data);

    if (cells.length !== 1) {
        throw new Error(
            `${path.basename(file)}: expected one root cell, got ${cells.length}`,
        );
    }

    return cells[0];
}

console.log(' STON.fi v2.2 FINAL READ-ONLY LIQUIDITY VALIDATOR');
console.log('============================================================');
console.log('READ ONLY');
console.log('NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT.');
console.log('NO WALLET SIGNATURE WILL BE REQUESTED.');
console.log('');

console.log(`Router:         ${ROUTER}`);
console.log(`Pool:           ${POOL}`);

console.log(`pTON wallet:    ${PTON_WALLET}`);

console.log('');
console.log('===== EXPECTED LIQUIDITY =====');

console.log(`ROOSTER amount: ${ROOSTER_AMOUNT}`);
console.log(`ROOSTER display: ${Number(ROOSTER_AMOUNT) / 1e9}`);

console.log(`pTON amount:    ${PTON_AMOUNT}`);
console.log(`pTON display:   ${Number(PTON_AMOUNT) / 1e9}`);

console.log(`minLpOut:       ${MIN_LP}`);
console.log(`deadline:       ${DEADLINE}`);

console.log(' 1) ROOSTER JETTON LEG');
console.log('============================================================');

const roosterRoot = loadBoc(ROOSTER_BOC);

console.log(`Root bits: ${roosterRoot.bits.length}`);
console.log(`Root refs: ${roosterRoot.refs.length}`);

const rr = roosterRoot.beginParse();

const roosterOpcode = rr.loadUint(32);

pass(
    'ROOSTER transfer opcode',
    roosterOpcode === JETTON_TRANSFER,
);

const roosterQuery = rr.loadUintBig(64);

pass(
    'ROOSTER query_id',
    roosterQuery === 0n,
);

const roosterAmount = rr.loadCoins();

pass(
    'ROOSTER amount',
    roosterAmount === ROOSTER_AMOUNT,
    `actual=${roosterAmount} expected=${ROOSTER_AMOUNT}`,
);

const roosterDestination = rr.loadAddress();

showAddress('ROOSTER destination', roosterDestination);

pass(
    'ROOSTER destination = Router',
    addressEqual(roosterDestination, ROUTER),
);

const roosterResponse = rr.loadAddress();

pass(
    'ROOSTER response = User',
    addressEqual(roosterResponse, USER),
);

const roosterCustom = rr.loadBit();

pass(
    'ROOSTER custom payload absent',
    roosterCustom === false,
);

const roosterForwardTon = rr.loadCoins();

pass(
    'ROOSTER forward TON = 0.235',
    roosterForwardTon === 235000000n,
    `actual=${roosterForwardTon}`,
);

const roosterForwardPresent = rr.loadBit();

pass(
    'ROOSTER forward payload present',
    roosterForwardPresent === true,
);

const roosterForward = rr.loadRef();

pass(
    'ROOSTER root fully consumed',
    rr.remainingBits === 0 && rr.remainingRefs === 0,
);

const rp = roosterForward.beginParse();

const roosterProvideOpcode = rp.loadUint(32);

pass(
    'ROOSTER PROVIDE_LP opcode',
    roosterProvideOpcode === PROVIDE_LP,
);

const roosterTargetWallet = rp.loadAddress();

pass(
    'ROOSTER PROVIDE_LP target wallet = pTON wallet',
    addressEqual(roosterTargetWallet, PTON_WALLET),
);

const roosterRefund = rp.loadAddress();

pass(
    'ROOSTER refund = User',
    addressEqual(roosterRefund, USER),
);

const roosterExcess = rp.loadAddress();

pass(
    'ROOSTER excesses = User',
    addressEqual(roosterExcess, USER),
);

const roosterDeadline = rp.loadUintBig(64);

pass(
    'ROOSTER deadline',
    roosterDeadline === DEADLINE,
    `actual=${roosterDeadline}`,
);

const roosterInner = rp.loadRef();

const ri = roosterInner.beginParse();

const roosterMinLp = ri.loadCoins();

pass(
    'ROOSTER minLpOut',
    roosterMinLp === MIN_LP,
    `actual=${roosterMinLp}`,
);

const roosterReceiver = ri.loadAddress();

pass(
    'ROOSTER receiver = User',
    addressEqual(roosterReceiver, USER),
);

const roosterBothPositive = ri.loadBit();

pass(
    'ROOSTER bothPositive',
    roosterBothPositive === true,
);

const roosterDexGas = ri.loadCoins();

pass(
    'ROOSTER DEX custom gas = 0',
    roosterDexGas === 0n,
);

const roosterDexPayload = ri.loadBit();

pass(
    'ROOSTER DEX custom payload absent',
    roosterDexPayload === false,
);

pass(
    'ROOSTER inner fully consumed',
    ri.remainingBits === 0 && ri.remainingRefs === 0,
);

pass(
    'ROOSTER PROVIDE_LP fully consumed',
    rp.remainingBits === 0 && rp.remainingRefs === 0,
);

console.log('');

const pr = ptonRoot.beginParse();

const ptonOpcode = pr.loadUint(32);

pass(
    'pTON TON_TRANSFER opcode',
    ptonOpcode === TON_TRANSFER,

const ptonQuery = pr.loadUintBig(64);

pass(

const ptonAmount = pr.loadCoins();

pass(

    ptonAmount === PTON_AMOUNT,

const ptonRefund = pr.loadAddress();

pass(
    'pTON refund = User',
    addressEqual(ptonRefund, USER),
);

const ptonForwardPresent = pr.loadBit();

pass(
    'pTON forward payload present',
    ptonForwardPresent === true,
);

const ptonForward = pr.loadRef();

pass(
    'pTON root fully consumed',
    pr.remainingBits === 0 && pr.remainingRefs === 0,
);

const pp = ptonForward.beginParse();

const ptonProvideOpcode = pp.loadUint(32);

pass(
    'pTON PROVIDE_LP opcode',
    ptonProvideOpcode === PROVIDE_LP,

const ptonTargetWallet = pp.loadAddress();

pass(
    'pTON PROVIDE_LP target wallet = ROOSTER wallet',
    addressEqual(ptonTargetWallet, ROOSTER_WALLET),

const ptonProvideRefund = pp.loadAddress();

pass(

const ptonExcess = pp.loadAddress();

pass(
    'pTON PROVIDE_LP excesses = User',
    addressEqual(ptonExcess, USER),

const ptonDeadline = pp.loadUintBig(64);

pass(

    ptonDeadline === DEADLINE,

const ptonInner = pp.loadRef();

const pi = ptonInner.beginParse();

const ptonMinLp = pi.loadCoins();

pass(

    roosterAmount === ROOSTER_AMOUNT &&

import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET = 0x093e6b;

const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function readU32(pos: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = buf[pos++];

    value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
      return { value, next: pos };

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function readS32(pos: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = buf[pos++];

    value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40))
        value -= 2 ** (shift + 7);

      return { value, next: pos };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function hex(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(6, "0");
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FIND FUNCTION CONTAINING 0x093E6B");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TARGET OFFSET : ${hex(TARGET)}`);
console.log(`WASM SIZE     : ${buf.length}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let p = 8;
let found = false;

while (p < buf.length) {

  const sectionId = buf[p++];

  const sizeInfo = readU32(p);
  const sectionSize = sizeInfo.value;
  p = sizeInfo.next;

  const sectionEnd = p + sectionSize;

  if (sectionId !== 10) {
    p = sectionEnd;
    continue;
  }

  const countInfo = readU32(p);
  const bodyCount = countInfo.value;
  p = countInfo.next;

  console.log(`DEFINED BODIES : ${bodyCount}`);

  for (let body = 0; body < bodyCount; body++) {

    const bodySizeInfo = readU32(p);
    const bodySize = bodySizeInfo.value;
    p = bodySizeInfo.next;

    const bodyStart = p;
    const bodyEnd = p + bodySize;

    const localInfo = readU32(p);
    const localCount = localInfo.value;
    p = localInfo.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < localCount; i++) {
      p = readU32(p).next;
      p++;
    }

    const codeStart = p;

    /*
     * We only need to know whether TARGET is inside
     * this body's code range.
     */

    if (TARGET >= codeStart && TARGET < bodyEnd) {

      found = true;

      console.log("");
      console.log("############################################################");
      console.log("TARGET FUNCTION FOUND");
      console.log("############################################################");
      console.log(`REAL FUNC   : ${body + 83}`);
      console.log(`BODY INDEX  : ${body}`);
      console.log(`BODY START  : ${hex(bodyStart)}`);
      console.log(`CODE START  : ${hex(codeStart)}`);
      console.log(`BODY END    : ${hex(bodyEnd)}`);
      console.log(`BODY SIZE   : ${bodySize}`);
      console.log("############################################################");
      console.log("");

      /*
       * Dump the complete body around the target.
       */
      const from = Math.max(codeStart, TARGET - 160);
      const to = Math.min(bodyEnd, TARGET + 220);

      console.log("RAW BODY CONTEXT");
      console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

      for (let q = from; q < to; q += 16) {

        const e = Math.min(to, q + 16);

        const bytes: string[] = [];

        for (let k = q; k < e; k++) {
          bytes.push(
            buf[k].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")
          );
        }

        console.log(
          `${hex(q)}  ${bytes.join(" ")}`
        );
      }

      console.log("");
      console.log("SELECTOR PATTERN");
      console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

      /*
       * Search this function for:
       *
       * i32.const 0x19534c
       * i32.load
       * i32.const 4788
       * i32.ne
       *
       * and report exact locations.
       */

      for (let q = codeStart; q < bodyEnd - 8; q++) {

        if (buf[q] !== 0x41)
          continue;

        const x = readS32(q + 1);

        if ((x.value >>> 0) !== 0x19534c)
          continue;

        console.log(`CONST 0x19534C @ ${hex(q)}`);

        const e = Math.min(bodyEnd, q + 30);

        const bytes: string[] = [];

        for (let k = q; k < e; k++) {
          bytes.push(
            buf[k].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")
          );
        }

        console.log(bytes.join(" "));
      }

      console.log("");
      console.log("CALL TARGETS NEAR SELECTOR");
      console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

      for (let q = codeStart; q < bodyEnd - 2; q++) {

        if (
          buf[q] === 0x10 &&
          buf[q + 1] === 0x7a
        ) {
          console.log(
            `CALL FUNC_122 @ ${hex(q)}`
          );
        }
      }

      /*
       * Stop after finding the body.
       */
      p = bodyEnd;
      break;
    }

    p = bodyEnd;
  }
}

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function u(p) {
  let v=0,s=0;
  while(true){
    const x=b[p++];
    v+=(x&127)*2**s;
    if(!(x&128)) return [v>>>0,p];
    s+=7;
  }
}

let p=8, code=0;

while(p<b.length){
  const id=b[p++];
  const [n,q]=u(p); p=q;
  if(id===10){ code=p; break; }
  p+=n;
}

p=code;
const [count,q0]=u(p); p=q0;

const targets = new Map([
  [122, 0x7a],
  [2057, 0x80f]
]);

for(let i=0;i<count;i++){
  const [size,q]=u(p); p=q;

  const func=83+i;
  const start=p, end=p+size;

  for(let x=start;x<end;x++){

    if(b[x]!==0x10) continue;

    const [target,qx]=u(x+1);

    if(!targets.has(target)) continue;

    console.log(
      `\nCALL FUNC ${target} FROM FUNC ${func} @ 0x${x.toString(16)}`
    );

    console.log(
      [...b.subarray(
        Math.max(start,x-60),
        Math.min(end,x+30)
      )]
      .map(v=>v.toString(16).padStart(2,"0"))
      .join(" ")
    );

    console.log("--------------------------------------------");
  }

  p=end;
}

FILE: scripts\find122ConstPairs.ts

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGET = 122;

function uleb(pos: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[pos++];

    value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if (!(b & 0x80))
      return { value, next: pos };

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function findBodies() {
  let p = 8;
  let imported = 0;
  const bodies: { start: number; end: number }[] = [];

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const section = wasm[p++];
    const s = uleb(p);
    const start = s.next;
    const end = start + s.value;

    if (section === 2) {
      let q = start;
      const n = uleb(q);
      q = n.next;

      for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
        const ml = uleb(q);
        q = ml.next + ml.value;

        const nl = uleb(q);
        q = nl.next + nl.value;

        const kind = wasm[q++];

        if (kind === 0) {
          imported++;
          const t = uleb(q);
          q = t.next;
        } else {
          break;
        }
      }
    }

    if (section === 10) {
      let q = start;
      const n = uleb(q);
      q = n.next;

      for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
        const bs = uleb(q);
        const bodyStart = bs.next;
        const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bs.value;

        bodies.push({
          start: bodyStart,
          end: bodyEnd,
        });

        q = bodyEnd;
      }
    }

    p = end;
  }

  return { imported, bodies };
}

const { imported, bodies } = findBodies();

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 122 — CONSTANT ARGUMENT PAIRS");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

let hits = 0;

for (let bi = 0; bi < bodies.length; bi++) {

  const { start, end } = bodies[bi];
  const func = imported + bi;

  for (let p = start; p < end - 1; p++) {

    if (wasm[p] !== 0x41)
      continue;

    const a = uleb(p + 1);

    if (wasm[a.next] !== 0x41)
      continue;

    const b = uleb(a.next + 1);

    if (wasm[b.next] !== 0x10)
      continue;

    const c = uleb(b.next + 1);

    if (c.value !== TARGET)
      continue;

    hits++;

    console.log(
      `FUNC ${func}  @ 0x${p.toString(16)}  ` +
      `CONST1=${a.value}  CONST2=${b.value}  CALL=122`
    );
  }
}

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log(`TOTAL : ${hits}`);
console.log("============================================================");

const fs = require("fs");

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const buf = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const BASE = 0x1901dc;
const TARGET = 0x1901ec;
const END = 0x1901fc;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FIND REFERENCES TO DATA TABLE 4271..4279");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("WASM SIZE :", buf.length);
console.log("TABLE     :", "0x" + BASE.toString(16), "..", "0x" + END.toString(16));
console.log("TARGET    :", "0x" + TARGET.toString(16));
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

function u32LE(pos) {
  if (pos + 4 > buf.length) return null;

  return (
    (buf[pos]) |
    (buf[pos + 1] << 8) |
    (buf[pos + 2] << 16) |
    (buf[pos + 3] << 24)
  ) >>> 0;
}

function hex(v) {
  return "0x" + v.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

/*
 * Search raw little-endian addresses in the entire WASM.
 *
 * This catches:
 *
 *   1C 01 19 00  -> 0x19011C
 *   EC 01 19 00  -> 0x1901EC
 *
 * for direct embedded addresses.
 */

console.log("");
console.log("RAW ADDRESS REFERENCES");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let rawCount = 0;

for (let addr = BASE; addr <= END; addr += 4) {

  const pattern = Buffer.alloc(4);

  pattern.writeUInt32LE(addr, 0);

  for (let p = 0; p <= buf.length - 4; p++) {

    if (
      buf[p] === pattern[0] &&
      buf[p + 1] === pattern[1] &&
      buf[p + 2] === pattern[2] &&
      buf[p + 3] === pattern[3]
    ) {

      rawCount++;

      console.log(
        "ADDRESS",
        hex(addr),
        "FOUND AT FILE",
        hex(p),
        addr === TARGET ? " <-- TARGET 4275" : ""
      );
    }
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("TOTAL RAW ADDRESS REFERENCES :", rawCount);

/*
 * Search WASM code for i32.const values corresponding
 * to the table memory addresses.
 *
 * We scan for:
 *
 *   41 <signed LEB32>
 *
 * This avoids assuming a fixed byte representation.
 */

console.log("");
console.log("I32.CONST ADDRESS REFERENCES");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

function readSLEB(pos) {

  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (pos < buf.length) {

    const b = buf[pos++];

    result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {

      if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
        result |= (~0 << (shift + 7));
      }

      return {
        value: result | 0,
        next: pos
      };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }

  return null;
}

let constCount = 0;

for (let p = 0; p < buf.length - 1; p++) {

  if (buf[p] !== 0x41) continue;

  const r = readSLEB(p + 1);

  if (!r) continue;

  const value = r.value >>> 0;

  if (value >= BASE && value <= END) {

    constCount++;

    console.log(
      "I32.CONST",
      hex(value),
      "AT FILE",
      hex(p),
      value === TARGET ? " <-- 0x1901EC / 4275" : ""
    );
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("TOTAL I32.CONST ADDRESS REFERENCES :", constCount);

/*
 * Search for the actual DATA addresses as 16-bit pieces too.
 *
 * Useful if an address was constructed from:
 *
 *   i32.const 0x190000
 *   i32.const 0x1EC
 *   i32.add
 *
 * We therefore look for common high/low components.
 */

console.log("");
console.log("REFERENCE COMPONENTS");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

const high = BASE & 0xffff0000;
const lowBase = BASE & 0xffff;

console.log("HIGH COMPONENT :", hex(high));
console.log("LOW BASE       :", hex(lowBase));
console.log("TARGET LOW     :", hex(TARGET & 0xffff));

/*
 * Finally, print all raw occurrences of the exact
 * little-endian address of TARGET with context.
 */

console.log("");
console.log("TARGET 0x1901EC RAW CONTEXT");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

const targetBytes = Buffer.alloc(4);
targetBytes.writeUInt32LE(TARGET, 0);

let targetRawCount = 0;

for (let p = 0; p <= buf.length - 4; p++) {

  if (
    buf[p] === targetBytes[0] &&
    buf[p + 1] === targetBytes[1] &&
    buf[p + 2] === targetBytes[2] &&
    buf[p + 3] === targetBytes[3]
  ) {

    targetRawCount++;

    const from = Math.max(0, p - 24);
    const to = Math.min(buf.length, p + 28);

    console.log("");
    console.log(
      "MATCH",
      targetRawCount,
      "AT FILE",
      hex(p)
    );

    console.log(
      buf.subarray(from, to)
        .toString("hex")
        .match(/.{1,2}/g)
        .join(" ")
    );
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("TOTAL EXACT TARGET RAW REFERENCES :", targetRawCount);

FILE: scripts\find195348FamilyWriters.mjs

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const BASE = 0x195348;
const END  = 0x19535c;

function uleb(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, n = 0;

  while (p < b.length) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;
    n++;

    if (!(x & 0x80))
      return { value: v >>> 0, len: n };

    s += 7;
  }

  return null;
}

function sleb(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, n = 0, x;

  do {
    x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;
    s += 7;
    n++;
  } while (x & 0x80);

  if (s < 32 && (x & 0x40))
    v |= (~0 << s);

  return { value: v | 0, len: n };
}

function hx(x) {
  return "0x" + (x >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function targetName(v) {
  const x = v >>> 0;

  if (x === 0x195348) return "BASE";
  if (x === 0x19534c) return "TARGET_19534C";
  if (x === 0x195350) return "195350";
  if (x === 0x195354) return "195354";
  if (x === 0x195358) return "195358";

  return null;
}

let hits = 0;

console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log("195348 FAMILY WRITER SCAN");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("=".repeat(72));

console.log(
  `RANGE : ${hx(BASE)} .. ${hx(END - 4)}`
);
console.log("");

/*
 * Conservative scan:
 * find exact i32.const of any family member,
 * then inspect nearby store instructions.
 */

for (let p = 0; p < b.length - 6; p++) {

  if (b[p] !== 0x41)
    continue;

  const r = sleb(p + 1);

  if (!r)
    continue;

  const name = targetName(r.value);

  if (!name)
    continue;

  const target = r.value >>> 0;

  /*
   * Look forward only 96 bytes.
   * We want stores whose address expression can still
   * plausibly originate from this constant.
   */
  const limit = Math.min(b.length, p + 96);

  for (let q = p + 1; q < limit; q++) {

    const op = b[q];

    if (op !== 0x36 &&
        op !== 0x3a &&
        op !== 0x3b)
      continue;

    const align = b[q + 1];
    const off = uleb(q + 2);

    if (!off)
      continue;

    hits++;

    console.log("=".repeat(72));
    console.log(`HIT ${hits}`);
    console.log("=".repeat(72));

    console.log(`CONST : ${hx(p)}  ${name}`);
    console.log(`VALUE : ${hx(target)}`);

    console.log(
      `STORE : ${hx(q)}  ${
        op === 0x36 ? "i32.store" :
        op === 0x3a ? "i32.store8" :
        "i32.store16"
      }`
    );

    console.log(`ALIGN : ${align}`);
    console.log(`OFFSET: ${off.value}`);

    console.log("");
    console.log("RAW WINDOW:");

    const lo = Math.max(0, p - 12);
    const hi = Math.min(b.length, q + off.len + 4);

    for (let x = lo; x < hi; x++) {

      console.log(
        `  ${hx(x)}  ${b[x].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
      );
    }

    console.log("");
  }
}

console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log(`TOTAL CANDIDATES : ${hits}`);
console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log("DONE");
console.log("=".repeat(72));

FILE: scripts\find19534CIndirectWriters.mjs

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGET = 0x0019534c;

// Known target-derived offsets.
// We are interested in patterns such as:
//   i32.const TARGET
//   ... local.set/tee
//   local.get
//   i32.const offset
//   i32.add
//   i32.store
//
// and:
//   local.get X
//   i32.const delta
//   i32.add
//   i32.store

const MAX_BACK = 96;

function readULEB(p) {
  let v = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let n = 0;

  while (p + n < b.length) {
    const x = b[p + n++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if (!(x & 0x80)) return { value: v >>> 0, len: n };

    shift += 7;
    if (shift > 35) return null;
  }

  return null;
}

function hex(v) {
  return "0x" + v.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log("INDIRECT WRITERS FOR 0x19534C");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log(`TARGET : ${hex(TARGET)}`);

let candidates = 0;

for (let p = 0; p < b.length; p++) {

  // Look for i32.store / store16 / store8.
  // 0x36 = i32.store
  // 0x3b = i32.store8/16 family
  if (b[p] !== 0x36 && b[p] !== 0x3a && b[p] !== 0x3b)
    continue;

  const op = b[p];

  const align = b[p + 1];
  const r = readULEB(p + 2);
  if (!r) continue;

  const offset = r.value;

  // Inspect preceding MAX_BACK bytes for TARGET constant.
  const start = Math.max(0, p - MAX_BACK);

  let targetPos = -1;

  for (let q = start; q < p; q++) {
    if (b[q] !== 0x41) continue;

    const x = readULEB(q + 1);

    if (x && x.value === TARGET) {
      targetPos = q;
    }
  }

  if (targetPos < 0) continue;

  // Confirm that target constant is reasonably close.
  candidates++;

  console.log(
    `\nCANDIDATE ${candidates}`
  );
  console.log(
    `STORE @ ${hex(p)} opcode=0x${op.toString(16)} ` +
    `align=${align} offset=${offset}`
  );
  console.log(
    `TARGET CONST @ ${hex(targetPos)}`
  );

  const s = Math.max(0, targetPos - 8);
  const e = Math.min(b.length, p + 12);

  console.log(
    Array.from(b.slice(s, e))
      .map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
      .join(" ")
  );
}

console.log("\n" + "=".repeat(72));
console.log(`CANDIDATES : ${candidates}`);
console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log("DONE");
console.log("=".repeat(72));

FILE: scripts\find19534CStoreFlow.mjs
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGET = 0x19534c;

function uleb(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, n = 0;
  while (p < b.length) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;
    n++;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) return { value: v >>> 0, len: n };
    s += 7;
  }
  return null;
}

function sleb(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, n = 0, x;
  do {
    x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;
    s += 7;
    n++;
  } while (x & 0x80);

  if (s < 32 && (x & 0x40))
    v |= (~0 << s);

  return { value: v | 0, len: n };
}

function hx(x) {
  return "0x" + (x >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function readName(op) {
  if (op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) {
    const r = uleb(pos);
    if (!r) return null;
    pos += r.len;
    return r.value;
  }
  return null;
}

/*
 * This scanner deliberately starts from the two already-proven
 * references to TARGET:
 *
 * FUNC 122  @ 0x0000eece
 * FUNC 2057 @ 0x00093e6b
 *
 * Then follows the local stack backwards toward the store.
 */

const refs = [
  { func: 122, ref: 0x0000eece, end: 0x0000ef40 },
  { func: 2057, ref: 0x00093e6b, end: 0x00093ec0 }
];

console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log("0x19534C STORE DATA-FLOW");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log(`TARGET : ${hx(TARGET)}`);
console.log("");

for (const R of refs) {

  console.log("-".repeat(72));
  console.log(`FUNC ${R.func}`);
  console.log(`TARGET CONST : ${hx(R.ref)}`);
  console.log(`SCAN         : ${hx(R.ref)} .. ${hx(R.end)}`);
  console.log("-".repeat(72));

  let p = R.ref;

  while (p < R.end) {

    const at = p;
    const op = b[p++];

    let text = `${hx(at)}  ${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;

    if (op === 0x41) {
      const r = sleb(p);
      if (!r) break;
      p += r.len;

      text += `  i32.const ${r.value} (${hx(r.value)})`;

      if ((r.value >>> 0) === TARGET)
        text += "  <<< TARGET";

    } else if (op === 0x20) {
      const r = uleb(p);
      if (!r) break;
      p += r.len;
      text += `  local.get ${r.value}`;

    } else if (op === 0x21) {
      const r = uleb(p);
      if (!r) break;
      p += r.len;
      text += `  local.set ${r.value}`;

    } else if (op === 0x22) {
      const r = uleb(p);
      if (!r) break;
      p += r.len;
      text += `  local.tee ${r.value}`;

    } else if (op === 0x28) {
      const a = b[p++];
      const r = uleb(p);
      if (!r) break;
      p += r.len;
      text += `  i32.load align=${a} offset=${r.value}`;

    } else if (op === 0x36) {
      const a = b[p++];
      const r = uleb(p);
      if (!r) break;
      p += r.len;

      text += `  i32.store align=${a} offset=${r.value}`;

      console.log(text);

      /*
       * Print a larger backward window around every store.
       * This lets us see whether TARGET is actually the address
       * consumed by this store.
       */
      const lo = Math.max(R.ref, at - 28);

      console.log("  BACKTRACE WINDOW:");

      let q = lo;

      while (q < at) {

        const qa = q;
        const qo = b[q++];

        let qs =
          `    ${hx(qa)}  ${qo.toString(16).padStart(2,"0")}`;

        if (qo === 0x41) {
          const z = sleb(q);
          if (!z) break;
          q += z.len;
          qs += `  i32.const ${z.value} (${hx(z.value)})`;

          if ((z.value >>> 0) === TARGET)
            qs += "  <<< TARGET";

        } else if (qo === 0x20) {
          const z = uleb(q);
          if (!z) break;
          q += z.len;
          qs += `  local.get ${z.value}`;

        } else if (qo === 0x21) {
          const z = uleb(q);
          if (!z) break;
          q += z.len;
          qs += `  local.set ${z.value}`;

        } else if (qo === 0x22) {
          const z = uleb(q);
          if (!z) break;
          q += z.len;
          qs += `  local.tee ${z.value}`;

        } else if (qo === 0x28 || qo === 0x36) {
          const al = b[q++];
          const z = uleb(q);
          if (!z) break;
          q += z.len;

          qs +=
            `  ${qo === 0x28 ? "i32.load" : "i32.store"}` +
            ` align=${al} offset=${z.value}`;

        } else if (qo === 0x6a) {
          qs += "  i32.add";

        } else if (qo === 0x6b) {
          qs += "  i32.sub";

        } else {
          qs += "  opcode";
        }

        console.log(qs);
      }

      console.log("");
      continue;

    } else if (op === 0x6a) {
      text += "  i32.add";

    } else if (op === 0x6b) {
      text += "  i32.sub";

    } else if (op === 0x10) {
      const r = uleb(p);
      if (!r) break;
      p += r.len;
      text += `  call ${r.value}`;

    } else if (op === 0x0d) {
      const r = uleb(p);
      if (!r) break;
      p += r.len;
      text += `  br_if ${r.value}`;

    } else if (op === 0x0c) {
      const r = uleb(p);
      if (!r) break;
      p += r.len;
      text += `  br ${r.value}`;

    } else if (op === 0x0b) {
      text += "  end";
    }

    console.log(text);
  }

  console.log("");
}

console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log("IMPORTANT");
console.log("A store is a TRUE DIRECT TARGET WRITER only when");
console.log("the address operand reaching i32.store is proven to be");
console.log("0x19534C (or TARGET + known constant).");
console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log("DONE");
console.log("=".repeat(72));

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function u(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 127) * 2 ** s;
    if (!(x & 128)) return [v >>> 0, p];
    s += 7;
  }
}

let p = 8, code = null;

while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p++];
  const [n, q] = u(p);
  p = q;
  if (id === 10) code = [p, p + n];
  p += n;
}

p = code[0];
const [count, q0] = u(p);
p = q0;

const TARGET = 0x19534c;

for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
  const [size, q] = u(p);
  p = q;

  const func = 83 + i;
  const start = p;
  const end = p + size;

  for (let x = start; x < end; x++) {
    if (b[x] !== 0x36) continue; // i32.store

    // Search backward only for an exact i32.const TARGET
    let found = false;

    for (let z = x - 1; z >= Math.max(start, x - 50); z--) {
      if (b[z] !== 0x41) continue;

      const [v, qv] = u(z + 1);

      if (v === TARGET) {
        console.log(`FUNC ${func} STORE @ 0x${x.toString(16)}`);
        console.log(`TARGET CONST @ 0x${z.toString(16)}`);

        console.log(
          [...b.subarray(Math.max(start, z - 20), Math.min(end, x + 12))]
            .map(v => v.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
            .join(" ")
        );

        console.log("--------------------------------------------");
        found = true;
        break;
      }
    }
  }

  p = end;
}

    s += 7;
  }
}

let p = 8, code = null;

while (p < b.length) {

            .map(v => v.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
            .join(" ")
        );
        console.log("--------------------------------------------");

        found = true;
        break;
      }
    }
  }

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function uleb(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 127) * 2 ** s;
    if (!(x & 128)) return [v >>> 0, p];
    s += 7;
  }
}

function sections() {
  let p = 8, code = null;
  while (p < b.length) {
    const id = b[p++];
    const [n, q] = uleb(p);
    p = q;
    if (id === 10) code = [p, p + n];
    p += n;
  }
  return code;
}

const code = sections();
let p = code[0];
const [count, q0] = uleb(p);
p = q0;

const TARGET = 0x195354;
let total = 0;

for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
  const [size, q] = uleb(p);
  p = q;

  const func = 83 + i;
  const start = p;
  const end = p + size;

  for (let x = start; x < end; x++) {
    if (b[x] !== 0x41) continue;

    const [value, qv] = uleb(x + 1);

    if (value !== TARGET) continue;

    for (let y = qv; y < Math.min(end, qv + 40); y++) {
      if (b[y] === 0x36 || b[y] === 0x37 || b[y] === 0x38 || b[y] === 0x39) {
        console.log(
          `FUNC ${func} CONST @ 0x${x.toString(16)} ` +
          `STORE @ 0x${y.toString(16)} ` +
          `OP=0x${b[y].toString(16)}`
        );

        const from = Math.max(start, x - 8);
        const to = Math.min(end, y + 8);

        console.log(
          [...b.subarray(from, to)]
            .map(v => v.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
            .join(" ")
        );
        console.log("--------------------------------------------");

        total++;
        break;
      }
    }
  }

  p = end;
}

console.log("TOTAL :", total);

FILE: scripts\find1953Writes.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGETS = [0x195350, 0x195354];

function readULEB(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[p++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
        shift += 7;
    }

    return { value: value >>> 0, next: p };
}

function readSLEB(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;
    let b = 0;

    while (true) {
        b = wasm[p++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;
        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
        value |= (~0 << shift);
    }

    return { value: value | 0, next: p };
}

function findSection(id: number) {
    let p = 8;

    while (p < wasm.length) {
        const sid = wasm[p++];
        const s = readULEB(p);
        p = s.next;

        if (sid === id) {
            return { start: p, end: p + s.value };
        }

        p += s.value;
    }

    throw new Error(`SECTION ${id} NOT FOUND`);
}

function parseCode() {
    const sec = findSection(10);
    let p = sec.start;

    const c = readULEB(p);
    p = c.next;

    const bodies: any[] = [];

    for (let ord = 0; ord < c.value; ord++) {
        const size = readULEB(p);
        p = size.next;

        const bodyStart = p;
        const bodyEnd = p + size.value;

        const locals = readULEB(p);
        p = locals.next;

        for (let i = 0; i < locals.value; i++) {
            const n = readULEB(p);
            p = n.next;
            p++;
        }

        const codeStart = p;

        bodies.push({
            ord,
            globalFunc: ord + 83,
            bodyStart,
            codeStart,
            bodyEnd
        });

        p = bodyEnd;
    }

    return bodies;
}

const bodies = parseCode();

console.log("FIND WRITES TO 0x195350 / 0x195354");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const target of TARGETS) {
    console.log("");
    console.log(`TARGET = 0x${target.toString(16)} (${target})`);
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

    let found = 0;

    for (const body of bodies) {
        let p = body.codeStart;

        while (p < body.bodyEnd) {
            const at = p;
            const op = wasm[p++];

            /*
             * i32.const target
             */
            if (op === 0x41) {
                const x = readSLEB(p);
                p = x.next;

                if ((x.value >>> 0) === target) {
                    const from = Math.max(body.codeStart, at - 8);
                    const to = Math.min(body.bodyEnd, p + 16);

                    console.log(
                        `CONST TARGET in FUNC ${body.globalFunc}` +
                        ` @ 0x${at.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
                    );

                    console.log(
                        `  context 0x${from.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}` +
                        ` .. 0x${to.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
                    );

                    found++;
                }

                continue;
            }

            /*
             * Skip immediates
             */
            switch (op) {
                case 0x10: { // call
                    const x = readULEB(p);
                    p = x.next;
                    break;
                }

                case 0x11: { // call_indirect
                    const t = readULEB(p);
                    p = t.next;

                    const table = readULEB(p);
                    p = table.next;
                    break;
                }

                case 0x20:
                case 0x21:
                case 0x22: {
                    const x = readULEB(p);
                    p = x.next;
                    break;
                }

                case 0x28:
                case 0x29:
                case 0x2a:
                case 0x2b:
                case 0x2c:
                case 0x2d:
                case 0x2e:
                case 0x2f:
                case 0x36:
                case 0x37:
                case 0x38:
                case 0x39: {
                    const a = readULEB(p);
                    p = a.next;

                    const o = readULEB(p);
                    p = o.next;
                    break;
                }

                case 0x41: {
                    const x = readSLEB(p);
                    p = x.next;
                    break;
                }

                case 0x42: {
                    const x = readSLEB(p);
                    p = x.next;
                    break;
                }

                case 0x43:
                    p += 4;
                    break;

                case 0x44:
                    p += 8;
                    break;

                case 0x02:
                case 0x03:
                case 0x04: {
                    p++;
                    break;
                }

                case 0x0c:
                case 0x0d: {
                    const x = readULEB(p);
                    p = x.next;
                    break;
                }

                case 0x0e: {
                    const n = readULEB(p);
                    p = n.next;

                    for (let i = 0; i < n.value + 1; i++) {
                        const x = readULEB(p);
                        p = x.next;
                    }
                    break;
                }

                case 0x3f:
                case 0x40:
                    p++;
                    break;

                default:
                    break;
            }
        }
    }

    console.log(`TOTAL DIRECT CONST REFERENCES = ${found}`);
}

console.log("");
console.log("IMPORTANT");
console.log("This scan identifies constant references.");
console.log("Next step is to identify STORE instructions whose effective address");
console.log("is 0x195350 or 0x195354.");
console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("READ ONLY - NO EXECUTION - NO TRANSACTION");

FILE: scripts\find25100CWriter.ts
