import fs from "fs";

const file = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const TARGET = 0x25100c;

const b = fs.readFileSync(file);

function u32(p: number) {
  return (
    (b[p] |
      (b[p + 1] << 8) |
      (b[p + 2] << 16) |
      (b[p + 3] << 24)) >>>
    0
  );
}

function readULEB(p: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, n = 0;
  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;
    n++;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) break;
    s += 7;
  }
  return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };
}

// Find raw little-endian i32.const TARGET:
// 41 <ULEB 25100c>
function isTargetConst(p: number) {
  if (b[p] !== 0x41) return false;
  const r = readULEB(p + 1);
  return r.value === TARGET;
}

// Find exact byte pattern for i32.store with offset=0,
// where the address is expected to be TARGET.
// This is a raw scan; symbolic address resolution comes next.
console.log("FUNC / BODY RAW SCAN");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TARGET : 0x25100C");
console.log("============================================================");

let hits: number[] = [];

for (let p = 0; p < b.length; p++) {
  if (isTargetConst(p)) hits.push(p);
}

console.log(`CONST HITS : ${hits.length}`);

for (const p of hits) {
  const end = Math.min(b.length, p + 24);
  const bytes = Array.from(b.subarray(p, end))
    .map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
    .join(" ");

  console.log(`\n0x${p.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ${bytes}`);
}

// Also search for direct raw 32-bit occurrences of TARGET.
console.log("\n============================================================");
console.log("RAW 32-BIT TARGET OCCURRENCES");
console.log("============================================================");

let raw = 0;

for (let p = 0; p + 4 <= b.length; p++) {
  if (u32(p) === TARGET) {
    raw++;
    console.log(`0x${p.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  0c 10 25 00`);
  }
}

console.log(`\nRAW OCCURRENCES : ${raw}`);
console.log("\nDONE");

const fs = require("fs");

const WASM =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

const TARGET = 3317;
const IMPORTS = 83;

function u8(p) {
  return buf[p];
}

function readULEB(pos) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (true) {
    const b = u8(p++);
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
    shift += 7;
  }

  return { value: value >>> 0, next: p };
}

function hex(n) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function dump(pos, radius = 20) {
  const s = Math.max(0, pos - radius);
  const e = Math.min(buf.length, pos + radius + 16);

  let a = [];

  for (let i = s; i < e; i++) {
    a.push(buf[i].toString(16).padStart(2, "0"));
  }

  return a.join(" ");
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Locate CODE section
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let p = 8;
let codeStart = 0;

while (p < buf.length) {
  const id = u8(p++);
  const sz = readULEB(p);

  p = sz.next;

  const start = p;
  const end = start + sz.value;

  if (id === 10) {
    codeStart = start;
    break;
  }

  p = end;
}

if (!codeStart) {
  throw new Error("CODE SECTION NOT FOUND");
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Parse bodies
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let q = codeStart;

const count = readULEB(q);
q = count.next;

const bodies = [];

for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
  const sz = readULEB(q);
  q = sz.next;

  const start = q;
  const end = q + sz.value;

  bodies.push({
    func: IMPORTS + i,
    start,
    end
  });

  q = end;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FIND ALL REFERENCES TO FUNC 3317");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TARGET :", TARGET);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// 1. Direct call 3317
//
// opcode 10 + function index ULEB
// ------------------------------------------------------------

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DIRECT CALLS TO 3317");
console.log("============================================================");

let direct = 0;

for (const fn of bodies) {

  for (let x = fn.start; x < fn.end; x++) {

    if (u8(x) !== 0x10) continue;

    const r = readULEB(x + 1);

    if (r.value === TARGET) {

      direct++;

      console.log("");
      console.log(
        "CALL 3317 FROM FUNC",
        fn.func,
        "@",
        hex(x)
      );

      console.log(dump(x));
    }
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("TOTAL DIRECT CALLS :", direct);

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// 2. ref.func
//
// 0xd2 + function index ULEB
// ------------------------------------------------------------

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("REF.FUNC 3317");
console.log("============================================================");

let refs = 0;

for (const fn of bodies) {

  for (let x = fn.start; x < fn.end; x++) {

    if (u8(x) !== 0xd2) continue;

    const r = readULEB(x + 1);

    if (r.value === TARGET) {

      refs++;

      console.log("");
      console.log(
        "REF.FUNC 3317 FROM FUNC",
        fn.func,
        "@",
        hex(x)
      );

      console.log(dump(x));
    }
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("TOTAL REF.FUNC :", refs);

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// 3. Search for exact function index bytes where useful.
// This is NOT treated as proof, only diagnostic.
// ------------------------------------------------------------

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("============================================================");

FILE: scripts\find3336Callers.mjs

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function u(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 127) * 2 ** s;
    if (!(x & 128)) return [v >>> 0, p];
    s += 7;
  }
}

let p = 8, code = 0;

while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p++];
  const [n, q] = u(p);
  p = q;
  if (id === 10) {
    code = p;
    break;
  }
  p += n;
}

p = code;
const [count, q0] = u(p);
p = q0;

for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
  const [size, q] = u(p);
  p = q;

  const func = 83 + i;
  const start = p;
  const end = p + size;

  for (let x = start; x < end; x++) {
    if (b[x] !== 0x10) continue;

    const [callee] = u(x + 1);

    if (callee !== 3336) continue;

    console.log(
      `FUNC ${func} CALL3336 @ 0x${x.toString(16)}`
    );

    console.log(
      [...b.subarray(Math.max(start, x - 80), Math.min(end, x + 8))]
        .map(v => v.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
        .join(" ")
    );

    console.log("--------------------------------------------");
  }

  p = end;
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\find4275BaseCandidates.js
====================================================================================================
const fs = require("fs");

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const buf = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const TABLE_BASE = 0x1901dc;
const TABLE_END  = 0x1901fc;

const LOW_START = 0x1dc;
const LOW_END   = 0x1fc;

const HIGH_START = 0x18f000;
const HIGH_END   = 0x191000;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FIND 4275 BASE CANDIDATES");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TABLE BASE :", "0x" + TABLE_BASE.toString(16));
console.log("TABLE END  :", "0x" + TABLE_END.toString(16));
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

function readULEB(pos) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (pos < buf.length) {
    const b = buf[pos++];

    result += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return { value: result >>> 0, next: pos };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }

  return null;
}

function readSLEB(pos) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (pos < buf.length) {
    const b = buf[pos++];

    result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {

      if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
        result |= (~0 << (shift + 7));
      }

      return {
        value: result | 0,
        next: pos
      };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }

  return null;
}

function hex(v) {
  return "0x" + (v >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

/*
 * Locate CODE section.
 */
let p = 8;
let codeStart = null;
let codeEnd = null;

while (p < buf.length) {

  const id = buf[p++];

  const sizeInfo = readULEB(p);
  if (!sizeInfo) break;

  p = sizeInfo.next;

  const payloadStart = p;
  const payloadEnd = payloadStart + sizeInfo.value;

  if (id === 10) {
    codeStart = payloadStart;
    codeEnd = payloadEnd;
    break;
  }

  p = payloadEnd;
}

if (codeStart === null) {
  throw new Error("CODE section not found");
}

let q = codeStart;

const countInfo = readULEB(q);
q = countInfo.next;

const functionCount = countInfo.value;

let candidateCount = 0;

for (let func = 0; func < functionCount; func++) {

  const bodySizeInfo = readULEB(q);
  q = bodySizeInfo.next;

  const bodyStart = q;
  const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bodySizeInfo.value;

  /*
   * Decode locals.
   */
  let x = bodyStart;

  const localGroupsInfo = readULEB(x);
  if (!localGroupsInfo) break;

  x = localGroupsInfo.next;

  for (let g = 0; g < localGroupsInfo.value; g++) {

    const n = readULEB(x);
    if (!n) break;

    x = n.next;

    // valtype
    x++;
  }

  const constants = [];
  const offsets = [];

  /*
   * First pass: collect i32.const values.
   */
  while (x < bodyEnd) {

    const opPos = x;
    const opcode = buf[x++];

    if (opcode === 0x41) {

      const r = readSLEB(x);
      if (!r) break;

      const value = r.value >>> 0;
      x = r.next;

      if (
        (value >= LOW_START && value <= LOW_END) ||
        (value >= HIGH_START && value <= HIGH_END)
      ) {

        constants.push({
          pos: opPos,
          value
        });

        if (
          value >= LOW_START &&
          value <= LOW_END
        ) {
          offsets.push({
            pos: opPos,
            value
          });
        }
      }

      continue;
    }

    /*
     * local get/set/tee
     */
    if (
      opcode === 0x20 ||
      opcode === 0x21 ||
      opcode === 0x22
    ) {
      const r = readULEB(x);
      if (!r) break;
      x = r.next;
      continue;
    }

    /*
     * call
     */
    if (opcode === 0x10) {
      const r = readULEB(x);
      if (!r) break;
      x = r.next;
      continue;
    }

    /*
     * call_indirect
     */
    if (opcode === 0x11) {

      const a = readULEB(x);
      if (!a) break;

      x = a.next;

      const b = readULEB(x);
      if (!b) break;

      x = b.next;
      continue;
    }

    /*
     * branches
     */
    if (
      opcode === 0x0c ||
      opcode === 0x0d
    ) {

      const r = readULEB(x);
      if (!r) break;

      x = r.next;
      continue;
    }

    if (opcode === 0x0e) {

      const n = readULEB(x);
      if (!n) break;

      x = n.next;

      for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
        const r = readULEB(x);
        if (!r) break;
        x = r.next;
      }

      const r = readULEB(x);
      if (!r) break;

      x = r.next;
      continue;
    }

    /*
     * block / loop / if
     */
    if (
      opcode === 0x02 ||
      opcode === 0x03 ||
      opcode === 0x04
    ) {
      x++;
      continue;
    }

    /*
     * memory instructions.
     */
    if (
      opcode >= 0x28 &&
      opcode <= 0x3e
    ) {

      const a = readULEB(x);
      if (!a) break;

      x = a.next;

      const b = readULEB(x);
      if (!b) break;

      x = b.next;
      continue;
    }

    /*
     * memory.size/grow
     */
    if (
      opcode === 0x3f ||
      opcode === 0x40
    ) {

      const r = readULEB(x);
      if (!r) break;

      x = r.next;
      continue;
    }

    /*
     * globals
     */
    if (
      opcode === 0x23 ||
      opcode === 0x24
    ) {

      const r = readULEB(x);
      if (!r) break;

      x = r.next;
      continue;
    }
  }

  /*
   * Look for functions that contain BOTH:
   *
   *   table offset
   *   AND
   *   address near 0x190000
   */
  const highConstants =
    constants.filter(c =>
      c.value >= HIGH_START &&
      c.value <= HIGH_END
    );

  if (
    offsets.length > 0 &&
    highConstants.length > 0
  ) {

    candidateCount++;

    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log("CANDIDATE FUNCTION :", func);
    console.log("============================================================");

    console.log("TABLE-LIKE OFFSETS:");

    for (const o of offsets) {
      console.log(
        "  CODE",
        hex(o.pos),
        "VALUE",
        hex(o.value)
      );
    }

    console.log("NEAR-BASE CONSTANTS:");

    for (const h of highConstants) {
      console.log(
        "  CODE",
        hex(h.pos),
        "VALUE",
        hex(h.value)
      );
    }

    /*
     * Print compact byte context around every offset.
     */
    console.log("CONTEXT:");

    for (const o of offsets) {

      const from = Math.max(bodyStart, o.pos - 16);
      const to = Math.min(bodyEnd, o.pos + 48);

      console.log(
        "  OFFSET",
        hex(o.value),
        "AT",
        hex(o.pos)
      );

      for (let z = from; z < to; z += 16) {

        const rowEnd = Math.min(to, z + 16);
        const bytes = [];

        for (let k = z; k < rowEnd; k++) {
          bytes.push(
            buf[k].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")
          );
        }

        console.log(
          "    " +
          hex(z) +
          " : " +
          bytes.join(" ")
        );
      }
    }
  }

  q = bodyEnd;
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SUMMARY");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("BASE + OFFSET CANDIDATES :", candidateCount);
console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\find4275Stores.js

const fs = require("fs");

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const TARGET = 4275;
const TARGET_HEX = 0x10b3;

const IMPORT_COUNT = 83;

function readULEB(pos) {

  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (true) {

    const b = wasm[p++];

    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if (!(b & 0x80))
      break;

    shift += 7;
  }

  return {
    value: value >>> 0,
    next: p
  };
}

function readSLEB(pos) {

  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;
  let b;

  do {

    b = wasm[p++];

    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    shift += 7;

  } while (b & 0x80);

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40))
    value |= (~0 << shift);

  return {
    value: value | 0,
    next: p
  };
}

function hex(n) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

/*
 * ============================================================
 * LOCATE CODE SECTION
 * ============================================================
 */

let p = 8;

let codeStart = 0;
let codeEnd = 0;

while (p < wasm.length) {

  const id = wasm[p++];

  const size = readULEB(p);

  const start = size.next;
  const end = start + size.value;

  if (id === 10) {

    codeStart = start;
    codeEnd = end;

    break;
  }

  p = end;
}

if (!codeStart)
  throw new Error("CODE SECTION NOT FOUND");

/*
 * ============================================================
 * BODY TABLE
 * ============================================================
 */

p = codeStart;

const bodyCount = readULEB(p);

p = bodyCount.next;

const bodies = [];

for (
  let bodyIndex = 0;
  bodyIndex < bodyCount.value;
  bodyIndex++
) {

  const size = readULEB(p);

  const start = size.next;
  const end = start + size.value;

  bodies.push({
    bodyIndex,
    funcIndex: IMPORT_COUNT + bodyIndex,
    start,
    end
  });

  p = end;
}

/*
 * ============================================================
 * SCAN
 * ============================================================
 */

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FIND STORES OF TABLE INDEX 4275");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log("WASM SIZE :", wasm.length);
console.log("TARGET VALUE :", TARGET);
console.log("TARGET HEX :", hex(TARGET_HEX));
console.log("TARGET STORE OFFSET : 4");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let candidates = 0;

for (const body of bodies) {

  let q = body.start;

  /*
   * Skip local declarations
   */

  const localGroups = readULEB(q);

  q = localGroups.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < localGroups.value; i++) {

    const n = readULEB(q);

    q = n.next + 1;
  }

  /*
   * Rolling instruction history
   */

  const history = [];

  while (q < body.end) {

    const off = q;
    const op = wasm[q++];

    let text = "";
    let immediate = null;

    try {

      switch (op) {

        case 0x20: {

          const x = readULEB(q);
          q = x.next;

          text = `local.get ${x.value}`;

          break;
        }

        case 0x21: {

          const x = readULEB(q);
          q = x.next;

          text = `local.set ${x.value}`;

          break;
        }

        case 0x22: {

          const x = readULEB(q);
          q = x.next;

          text = `local.tee ${x.value}`;

          break;
        }

        case 0x41: {

          const x = readSLEB(q);
          q = x.next;

          immediate = x.value;

          text = `i32.const ${x.value}`;

          break;
        }

        case 0x36: {

          const align = readULEB(q);
          q = align.next;

          const offset = readULEB(q);
          q = offset.next;

          text =
            `i32.store align=${align.value} offset=${offset.value}`;

          /*
           * i32.store offset=4
           */

          if (offset.value === 4) {

            /*
             * Search recent history for TARGET constant.
             */

            const found = history.some(
              x =>
                x.opcode === 0x41 &&
                x.immediate === TARGET
            );

            if (found) {

              candidates++;

              console.log("");
              console.log(
                "============================================================"
              );

              console.log(
                "CANDIDATE",
                candidates
              );

              console.log(
                "FUNC :",
                body.funcIndex
              );

              console.log(
                "STORE :",
                hex(off)
              );

              console.log(
                "STORE OFFSET : 4"
              );

              console.log(
                "------------------------------------------------------------"
              );

              for (const h of history.slice(-12)) {

                console.log(
                  hex(h.off),
                  h.text
                );
              }

              console.log(
                hex(off),
                text
              );

              console.log(
                "TARGET 4275 SEEN IN RECENT DATAFLOW"
              );
            }
          }

          break;
        }

        case 0x10: {

          const x = readULEB(q);
          q = x.next;

          text = `call ${x.value}`;

          break;
        }

        case 0x11: {

          const type = readULEB(q);
          q = type.next;

          const table = readULEB(q);
          q = table.next;

          text =
            `call_indirect type=${type.value} table=${table.value}`;

          break;
        }

        case 0x28: {

          const align = readULEB(q);
          q = align.next;

          const offset = readULEB(q);
          q = offset.next;

          text =
            `i32.load align=${align.value} offset=${offset.value}`;

          break;
        }

        case 0x2d: {

          const align = readULEB(q);
          q = align.next;

          const offset = readULEB(q);
          q = offset.next;

          text =
            `i32.load8_u align=${align.value} offset=${offset.value}`;

          break;
        }

        case 0x6a:
          text = "i32.add";
          break;

        case 0x6b:
          text = "i32.sub";
          break;

        case 0x6c:
          text = "i32.mul";
          break;

        case 0x45:
          text = "i32.eqz";
          break;

        case 0x46:
          text = "i32.eq";
          break;

        case 0x47:
          text = "i32.ne";
          break;

        default:

          /*
           * Unknown opcode.
           *
           * Do not attempt dangerous decoding.
           * Reset history because instruction boundaries
           * are no longer guaranteed.
           */

          history.length = 0;

          text = `UNKNOWN ${hex(op)}`;

          break;
      }

    } catch {

      break;
    }

    history.push({
      off,
      opcode: op,
      immediate,
      text
    });

    if (history.length > 20)
      history.shift();

    if (op === 0x0b)
      break;
  }
}

/*
 * ============================================================
 * RESULT
 * ============================================================
 */

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("RESULT");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log(
  "4275 STORE CANDIDATES :",
  candidates
);

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("READ ONLY : YES");
console.log("NETWORK : NO");
console.log("TRANSACTIONS : NO");
console.log("============================================================");

FILE: scripts\find446And248SameFunction.js

const fs = require("fs");

const path =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(path);

const IMPORTED = 83;

function u32(p) {
    let v = 0, s = 0;

    while (true) {
        const x = b[p++];

        v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;

        if (!(x & 0x80))
            return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };

        s += 7;
    }
}

function s32(p) {
    let v = 0, s = 0;

    while (true) {
        const x = b[p++];

        v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;

        if (!(x & 0x80)) {
            if (s < 32 && (x & 0x40))
                v |= (~0 << (s + 7));

            return { value: v | 0, next: p };
        }

        s += 7;
    }
}

function skipBlockType(p) {
    const x = b[p];

    if (
        x === 0x40 ||
        x === 0x7f ||
        x === 0x7e ||
        x === 0x7d ||
        x === 0x7c
    )
        return p + 1;

    return s32(p).next;
}

function skip(p) {

    const op = b[p++];

    if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04)
        return skipBlockType(p);

    if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d)
        return u32(p).next;

    if (op === 0x0e) {
        const n = u32(p);
        p = n.next;

        for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++)
            p = u32(p).next;

        return u32(p).next;
    }

    if (op === 0x10)
        return u32(p).next;

    if (op === 0x11) {
        const a = u32(p);
        const c = u32(a.next);
        return c.next;
    }

    if (op >= 0x20 && op <= 0x24)
        return u32(p).next;

    if (op >= 0x28 && op <= 0x3e) {
        const a = u32(p);
        const c = u32(a.next);
        return c.next;
    }

    if (op === 0x3f || op === 0x40)
        return p + 1;

    if (op === 0x41)
        return s32(p).next;

    if (op === 0x42) {
        while (true) {
            const x = b[p++];

            if (!(x & 0x80))
                return p;
        }
    }

    if (op === 0x43)
        return p + 4;

    if (op === 0x44)
        return p + 8;

    return p;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Parse code section
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let p = 8;
const bodies = [];

while (p < b.length) {

    const section = b[p++];

    const z = u32(p);
    p = z.next;

    const end = p + z.value;

    if (section === 10) {

        const n = u32(p);
        p = n.next;

        for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {

            const bodyStart = p;

            const sz = u32(p);
            p = sz.next;

            const bodyEnd = p + sz.value;

            const localCount = u32(p);
            p = localCount.next;

            for (let j = 0; j < localCount.value; j++) {
                const cnt = u32(p);
                p = cnt.next + 1;
            }

            bodies.push({
                func: IMPORTED + i,
                instr: p,
                end: bodyEnd,
                bodyStart
            });

            p = bodyEnd;
        }
    }

    p = end;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Find functions containing BOTH CALL 446 and CALL 248
// ------------------------------------------------------------

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 446 + FUNC 248 SAME-FUNCTION SCAN");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

const candidates = [];

for (const f of bodies) {

    let q = f.instr;

    const calls446 = [];
    const calls248 = [];

    while (q < f.end) {

        const op = b[q];

        if (op === 0x10) {

            const r = u32(q + 1);

            if (r.value === 446)
                calls446.push(q);

            if (r.value === 248)
                calls248.push(q);

            q = r.next;
            continue;
        }

        q = skip(q);
    }

    if (calls446.length && calls248.length) {

        candidates.push({
            func: f.func,
            calls446,
            calls248
        });
    }
}

console.log(
    `FUNCTIONS CALLING BOTH : ${candidates.length}`
);

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (const c of candidates) {

    console.log(`FUNC ${c.func}`);

    console.log(
        "  CALL 446 : " +
        c.calls446
            .map(x => "0x" + x.toString(16))
            .join(", ")
    );

    console.log(
        "  CALL 248 : " +
        c.calls248
            .map(x => "0x" + x.toString(16))
            .join(", ")
    );
}

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Also explicitly inspect known 446 callers.
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const known = [5460, 6618, 6619];

console.log("KNOWN 446 CALLERS");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (const fn of known) {

    const f = bodies.find(x => x.func === fn);

    if (!f) {
        console.log(`FUNC ${fn} : NOT FOUND`);
        continue;
    }

    let q = f.instr;
    let c446 = [];
    let c248 = [];

    while (q < f.end) {

        const op = b[q];

        if (op === 0x10) {

            const r = u32(q + 1);

            if (r.value === 446)
                c446.push(q);

            if (r.value === 248)
                c248.push(q);

            q = r.next;
            continue;
        }

        q = skip(q);
    }

    console.log(
        `FUNC ${fn}: CALL446=${c446.length} CALL248=${c248.length}`
    );

    if (c446.length)
        console.log(
            "  446: " +
            c446.map(x => "0x" + x.toString(16)).join(", ")
        );

    if (c248.length)
        console.log(
            "  248: " +
            c248.map(x => "0x" + x.toString(16)).join(", ")
        );
}

console.log("============================================================");

FILE: scripts\find446Field24Producers.js

const fs = require("fs");

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm"
);

function u32(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[p++];
    v |= (b & 0x7f) << s;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };
    s += 7;
  }
}

function hx(x) {
  return "0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Find CODE section
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let p = 8;
let codeStart = 0;
let codeEnd = 0;

while (p < wasm.length) {
  const id = wasm[p++];

  const sz = u32(p);
  p = sz.next;

  const start = p;
  const end = p + sz.value;

  if (id === 10) {
    codeStart = start;
    codeEnd = end;
    break;
  }

  p = end;
}

const c = u32(codeStart);
p = c.next;

const functions = [];

for (let i = 0; i < c.value; i++) {

  const bs = u32(p);
  p = bs.next;

  const start = p;
  const end = p + bs.value;

  functions.push({
    fn: 83 + i,
    start,
    end
  });

  p = end;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Scan all code for i32.store offset=24
//
// We only report stores where the immediate preceding bytes
// contain a local.get / load pattern suggesting:
//
//     object + 24
//
// The full local dataflow is intentionally NOT assumed.
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const hits = [];

for (const f of functions) {

  for (let q = f.start; q < f.end - 5; q++) {

    if (wasm[q] !== 0x36)
      continue;

    const a = u32(q + 1);
    const o = u32(a.next);

    if (o.value !== 24)
      continue;

    hits.push({
      fn: f.fn,
      off: q
    });
  }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FIELD +24 PRODUCER SCAN");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TYPE : i32.store`);
console.log(`OFFSET : 24`);
console.log(`HITS : ${hits.length}`);

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Print only compact context around each hit
// ------------------------------------------------------------

for (const h of hits) {

  const f = functions.find(x => x.fn === h.fn);

  console.log("");
  console.log(`FUNC ${h.fn} @ ${hx(h.off)}`);
  console.log("--------------------------------------------");

  const start = Math.max(f.start, h.off - 24);
  const end = Math.min(f.end, h.off + 12);

  for (let q = start; q < end; q++) {

    const op = wasm[q];

    if (op === 0x20) {
      const x = u32(q + 1);
      console.log(
        `${hx(q)} local.get ${x.value}`
      );
    }

    else if (op === 0x21) {
      const x = u32(q + 1);
      console.log(
        `${hx(q)} local.set ${x.value}`
      );
    }

    else if (op === 0x22) {
      const x = u32(q + 1);
      console.log(
        `${hx(q)} local.tee ${x.value}`
      );
    }

    else if (op === 0x28) {
      const a = u32(q + 1);
      const o = u32(a.next);

      console.log(
        `${hx(q)} i32.load offset=${o.value}`
      );
    }

    else if (op === 0x41) {
      console.log(
        `${hx(q)} i32.const`
      );
    }

    else if (op === 0x36) {
      const a = u32(q + 1);
      const o = u32(a.next);

      console.log(
        `${hx(q)} i32.store offset=${o.value}` +
        (o.value === 24 ? "  <=== TARGET" : "")
      );
    }

    else if (op === 0x10) {
      const x = u32(q + 1);

      console.log(
        `${hx(q)} CALL ${x.value}`
      );
    }
  }
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\find446Offset4.js

const fs = require("fs");

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const buf = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

function uleb(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;

  while (p < buf.length) {
    const b = buf[p++];
    v += (b & 127) * Math.pow(2, s);

    if (!(b & 128))
      return { value: v, next: p };

    s += 7;
  }

  return null;
}

function sleb(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;

  while (p < buf.length) {
    const b = buf[p++];

    v |= (b & 127) << s;
    s += 7;

    if (!(b & 128)) {
      if (s < 32 && (b & 64))
        v |= (~0 << s);

      return {
        value: v | 0,
        next: p
      };
    }
  }

  return null;
}

function hex(x) {
  return "0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function decode(p, end) {

  if (p >= end)
    return null;

  const start = p;
  const op = buf[p++];

  const r = {
    start,
    end: p,
    op,
    text: `opcode 0x${op.toString(16)}`
  };

  if (op === 0x20 ||
      op === 0x21 ||
      op === 0x22) {

    const a = uleb(p);
    if (!a) return null;

    r.end = a.next;
    r.local = a.value;

    r.text =
      op === 0x20
        ? `local.get local=${a.value}`
        : op === 0x21
          ? `local.set local=${a.value}`
          : `local.tee local=${a.value}`;

    return r;
  }

  if (op === 0x10) {

    const a = uleb(p);
    if (!a) return null;

    r.end = a.next;
    r.callTarget = a.value;
    r.text = `call ${a.value}`;

    return r;
  }

  if (op === 0x11) {

    const a = uleb(p);
    if (!a) return null;

    const b = uleb(a.next);
    if (!b) return null;

    r.end = b.next;

    r.text =
      `call_indirect type=${a.value} table=${b.value}`;

    return r;
  }

  if (
    op === 0x28 ||
    op === 0x29 ||
    op === 0x2a ||
    op === 0x2d
  ) {

    const a = uleb(p);
    if (!a) return null;

    const b = uleb(a.next);
    if (!b) return null;

    r.end = b.next;
    r.offset = b.value;
    r.isLoad = true;

    if (op === 0x28)
      r.text = `i32.load offset=${b.value}`;
    else if (op === 0x29)
      r.text = `i64.load offset=${b.value}`;
    else if (op === 0x2a)
      r.text = `f32.load offset=${b.value}`;
    else
      r.text = `i32.load8_u offset=${b.value}`;

    return r;
  }

  if (op === 0x36) {

    const a = uleb(p);
    if (!a) return null;

    const b = uleb(a.next);
    if (!b) return null;

    r.end = b.next;
    r.offset = b.value;
    r.isStore = true;

    r.text = `i32.store offset=${b.value}`;

    return r;
  }

  if (op === 0x41) {

    const a = sleb(p);
    if (!a) return null;

    r.end = a.next;
    r.value = a.value;
    r.text = `i32.const ${a.value}`;

    return r;
  }

  if (op === 0x6a) {
    r.text = "i32.add";
    return r;
  }

  if (op === 0x6b) {
    r.text = "i32.sub";
    return r;
  }

  if (op === 0x45) {
    r.text = "i32.eqz";
    return r;
  }

  if (op === 0x46) {
    r.text = "i32.eq";
    return r;
  }

  if (op === 0x47) {
    r.text = "i32.ne";
    return r;
  }

  if (op === 0x04) {

    if (p >= end)
      return null;

    p++;

    r.end = p;
    r.text = "if";

    return r;
  }

  if (op === 0x02) {

    if (p >= end)
      return null;

    p++;

    r.end = p;
    r.text = "block";

    return r;
  }

  if (op === 0x03) {

    if (p >= end)
      return null;

    p++;

    r.end = p;
    r.text = "loop";

    return r;
  }

  if (op === 0x05) {
    r.text = "else";
    return r;
  }

  if (op === 0x0b) {
    r.text = "end";
    return r;
  }

  if (op === 0x0c ||
      op === 0x0d) {

    const a = uleb(p);
    if (!a) return null;

    r.end = a.next;

    r.text =
      op === 0x0c
        ? `br ${a.value}`
        : `br_if ${a.value}`;

    return r;
  }

  return r;
}

/*
 * Locate code section.
 */

let p = 8;
let codeStart = -1;
let codeEnd = -1;

while (p < buf.length) {

  const section = buf[p++];

  const s = uleb(p);
  if (!s)
    throw new Error("bad section");

  p = s.next;

  const start = p;
  const end = start + s.value;

  if (section === 10) {
    codeStart = start;
    codeEnd = end;
    break;
  }

  p = end;
}

if (codeStart < 0)
  throw new Error("code section not found");

let q = codeStart;

const count = uleb(q);
q = count.next;

const bodyCount = count.value;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FIND FUNC 446 RESULT -> LOAD +4");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("WASM SIZE :", buf.length);
console.log("BODY COUNT:", bodyCount);
console.log("TARGET CALL: 446");
console.log("TARGET LOAD: offset=4");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let call446Count = 0;
let load4Count = 0;
let call248Count = 0;

for (let func = 0; func < bodyCount; func++) {

  const sizeInfo = uleb(q);
  if (!sizeInfo)
    break;

  const bodySize = sizeInfo.value;
  const bodyStart = sizeInfo.next;
  const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bodySize;

  q = bodyEnd;

  let x = bodyStart;

  const localInfo = uleb(x);
  if (!localInfo)
    continue;

  x = localInfo.next;

  for (
    let g = 0;
    g < localInfo.value;
    g++
  ) {

    const n = uleb(x);
    if (!n) {
      x = bodyEnd;
      break;
    }

    x = n.next + 1;
  }

  const ins = [];

  while (x < bodyEnd) {

    const z = decode(x, bodyEnd);

    if (!z)
      break;

    ins.push(z);
    x = z.end;
  }

  for (let i = 0; i < ins.length; i++) {

    if (ins[i].callTarget !== 446)
      continue;

    call446Count++;

    if (
      i + 1 >= ins.length ||
      !ins[i + 1].text.startsWith("local.tee")
    ) {
      continue;
    }

    const L = ins[i + 1].local;

    /*
     * Search forward for uses of L.
     */

    for (
      let j = i + 2;
      j < Math.min(ins.length, i + 150);
      j++
    ) {

      /*
       * Stop if L is overwritten.
       */
      if (
        (
          ins[j].text ===
          `local.set local=${L}`
        ) ||
        (
          ins[j].text ===
          `local.tee local=${L}`
        )
      ) {
        break;
      }

      /*
       * Exact:
       *
       * local.get L
       * i32.load offset=4
       */
      if (
        ins[j].isLoad &&
        ins[j].offset === 4 &&
        j > 0 &&
        ins[j - 1].text ===
          `local.get local=${L}`
      ) {

        load4Count++;

        console.log("");
        console.log("============================================================");
        console.log("MATCH: RESULT 446 -> LOAD +4");
        console.log("============================================================");
        console.log("FUNC :", func);
        console.log("CALL 446 :", hex(ins[i].start));
        console.log("LOCAL    :", L);
        console.log("LOAD +4  :", hex(ins[j].start));

        console.log("");
        console.log("CONTEXT:");

        const from = Math.max(i, j - 15);

        for (let k = from; k <= j; k++) {

          console.log(
            " ",
            hex(ins[k].start),
            ins[k].text
          );
        }
      }

      /*
       * Direct call 248 with L.
       */
      if (ins[j].callTarget === 248) {

        let found = false;

        for (
          let k = Math.max(i, j - 12);
          k < j;
          k++
        ) {

          if (
            ins[k].text ===
            `local.get local=${L}`
          ) {
            found = true;
            break;
          }
        }

        if (found) {

          call248Count++;

          console.log("");
          console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
          console.log("446 RESULT -> FUNC 248");
          console.log("FUNC :", func);
          console.log("CALL 446 :", hex(ins[i].start));
          console.log("LOCAL :", L);
          console.log("CALL 248 :", hex(ins[j].start));
          console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
        }
      }
    }
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SUMMARY");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TOTAL CALL 446 :", call446Count);
console.log("RESULT -> LOAD +4 :", load4Count);
console.log("RESULT -> CALL 248:", call248Count);
console.log("============================================================");

FILE: scripts\find446Type2Target.js

const fs = require("fs");

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm"
);

function u32(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[p++];
    v |= (b & 0x7f) << s;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };
    s += 7;
  }
}

function i32(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[p++];
    v |= (b & 0x7f) << s;
    s += 7;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) {
      if (s < 32 && (b & 0x40)) v |= (~0 << s);
      return { value: v | 0, next: p };
    }
  }
}

function hx(x) {
  return "0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Find CODE section
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let p = 8;
let codeStart = 0;
let codeEnd = 0;

while (p < wasm.length) {
  const id = wasm[p++];
  const s = u32(p);
  p = s.next;

  const start = p;
  const end = p + s.value;

  if (id === 10) {
    codeStart = start;
    codeEnd = end;
    break;
  }

  p = end;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Find exact body containing FUNC 446
// Imported funcs = 83
// ------------------------------------------------------------

p = codeStart;

const count = u32(p);
p = count.next;

let body446 = null;

for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
  const sz = u32(p);
  p = sz.next;

  const start = p;
  const end = p + sz.value;

  const fn = 83 + i;

  if (fn === 446) {
    body446 = { start, end };
    break;
  }

  p = end;
}

if (!body446)
  throw new Error("FUNC 446 not found");

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 446 : EXACT TYPE-2 DISPATCH");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`BODY : ${hx(body446.start)} .. ${hx(body446.end)}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Decode instructions while preserving stack expressions
// ------------------------------------------------------------

p = body446.start;

// local declarations
const localCount = u32(p);
p = localCount.next;

const locals = [];

for (let i = 0; i < localCount.value; i++) {
  const n = u32(p);
  p = n.next;

  const type = wasm[p++];
  for (let j = 0; j < n.value; j++)
    locals.push(type);
}

const stack = [];
const localExpr = new Map();

function push(x) {
  stack.push(x);
}

function pop() {
  return stack.pop() ?? "<?>"; 
}

function setLocal(n, x) {
  localExpr.set(n, x);
}

function getLocal(n) {
  return localExpr.get(n) ?? `local${n}`;
}

function dumpStack() {
  return stack.map((x, i) => `  [${i}] ${x}`).join("\n");
}

while (p < body446.end) {

  const off = p;
  const op = wasm[p++];

  // END
  if (op === 0x0b) {
    console.log(`${hx(off)} end`);
    continue;
  }

  // BLOCK
  if (op === 0x02) {
    const bt = wasm[p++];
    console.log(`${hx(off)} block type=0x${bt.toString(16)}`);
    continue;
  }

  // IF
  if (op === 0x04) {
    const bt = wasm[p++];
    const cond = pop();
    console.log(`${hx(off)} if (${cond})`);
    continue;
  }

  // BR_IF
  if (op === 0x0d) {
    const d = u32(p);
    p = d.next;

    const cond = pop();

    console.log(
      `${hx(off)} br_if ${d.value}  [condition=${cond}]`
    );
    continue;
  }

  // RETURN
  if (op === 0x0f) {
    console.log(`${hx(off)} return`);
    continue;
  }

  // LOCAL.GET
  if (op === 0x20) {
    const x = u32(p);
    p = x.next;

    const e = getLocal(x.value);
    push(e);

    console.log(`${hx(off)} local.get ${x.value} -> ${e}`);
    continue;
  }

  // LOCAL.SET
  if (op === 0x21) {
    const x = u32(p);
    p = x.next;

    const e = pop();
    setLocal(x.value, e);

    console.log(`${hx(off)} local.set ${x.value} = ${e}`);
    continue;
  }

  // LOCAL.TEE
  if (op === 0x22) {
    const x = u32(p);
    p = x.next;

    const e = pop();
    setLocal(x.value, e);
    push(e);

    console.log(`${hx(off)} local.tee ${x.value} = ${e}`);
    continue;
  }

  // I32.CONST
  if (op === 0x41) {
    const x = i32(p);
    p = x.next;

    push(String(x.value));

    console.log(`${hx(off)} i32.const ${x.value}`);
    continue;
  }

  // I32.LOAD
  if (op === 0x28) {
    const a = u32(p);
    p = a.next;

    const o = u32(p);
    p = o.next;

    const addr = pop();

    const expr =
      o.value === 0
        ? `MEM[${addr}]`
        : `MEM[${addr}+${o.value}]`;

    push(expr);

    console.log(
      `${hx(off)} i32.load offset=${o.value} : ${addr} -> ${expr}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  // I64.LOAD
  if (op === 0x29) {
    const a = u32(p);
    p = a.next;

    const o = u32(p);
    p = o.next;

    const addr = pop();

    const expr =
      o.value === 0
        ? `MEM64[${addr}]`
        : `MEM64[${addr}+${o.value}]`;

    push(expr);

    console.log(
      `${hx(off)} i64.load offset=${o.value}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  // I32.STORE
  if (op === 0x36) {
    const a = u32(p);
    p = a.next;

    const o = u32(p);
    p = o.next;

    const value = pop();
    const addr = pop();

    console.log(
      `${hx(off)} i32.store offset=${o.value} : MEM[${addr}+${o.value}] = ${value}`
    );

    continue;
  }

  // CALL
  if (op === 0x10) {
    const x = u32(p);
    p = x.next;

    console.log(
      `${hx(off)} CALL ${x.value}`
    );

    // Important for FUNC 446:
    // CALL 500 consumes one i32 and returns one i32.
    // CALL 122 returns one i32.
    if (x.value === 122) {
      pop();
      pop();
      pop();

      push("RET[122]");
    }
    else if (x.value === 500) {
      const arg = pop();
      push(`RET[500](${arg})`);
    }
    else if (x.value === 85 ||
             x.value === 84 ||
             x.value === 86 ||
             x.value === 91 ||
             x.value === 280) {
      // Most are void in the observed path.
    }

    continue;
  }

  // CALL INDIRECT
  if (op === 0x11) {

    const t = u32(p);
    p = t.next;

    const tab = u32(p);
    p = tab.next;

    console.log("");
    console.log("************************************************************");
    console.log(`CALL_INDIRECT TYPE=${t.value} TABLE=${tab.value}`);
    console.log(`AT ${hx(off)}`);
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

    console.log("STACK BEFORE INDIRECT CALL:");
    console.log(
      dumpStack() || "  <empty>"
    );

    // For FUNC 446, the final stack item is the dispatch index.
    const indexExpr = pop();

    console.log("");
    console.log("DISPATCH INDEX EXPRESSION:");
    console.log(`  ${indexExpr}`);

    console.log("");
    console.log("Remaining stack:");
    console.log(
      dumpStack() || "  <empty>"
    );

    console.log("************************************************************");
    console.log("");

    // TYPE-2 returns i32.
    push(`RET[TYPE2:${indexExpr}]`);

    continue;
  }

  // I32.EQZ
  if (op === 0x45) {
    const a = pop();
    push(`(${a}==0)`);

    console.log(`${hx(off)} i32.eqz ${a}`);
    continue;
  }

  // I32.WRAP_I64
  if (op === 0xa7) {
    const a = pop();
    push(`wrap_i32(${a})`);

    console.log(`${hx(off)} i32.wrap_i64`);
    continue;
  }

  // Unknown opcode
  console.log(
    `${hx(off)} UNKNOWN opcode=0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2,"0")}`
  );
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FINAL SYMBOLIC LOCALS");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const [k, v] of localExpr.entries()) {
  console.log(`local${k} = ${v}`);
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("END");
console.log("============================================================");

FILE: scripts\find4788Indirect.mjs

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function u(p) {
  let v=0,s=0;
  while(true){
    const x=b[p++];
    v+=(x&127)*2**s;
    if(!(x&128)) return [v>>>0,p];
    s+=7;
  }
}

let p=8, code=0;

while(p<b.length){
  const id=b[p++];
  const [n,q]=u(p); p=q;
  if(id===10){ code=p; break; }
  p+=n;
}

p=code;
const [count,q0]=u(p); p=q0;

for(let i=0;i<count;i++){
  const [size,q]=u(p); p=q;

  const func=83+i;
  const start=p, end=p+size;

  for(let x=start;x<end;x++){

    if(b[x]!==0x41) continue;

    const [v,qv]=u(x+1);
    if(v!==4788) continue;

    // Search until control-flow boundary / max 80 bytes
    let y=qv;

    for(let n=0;n<80 && y<end;n++,y++){

      // call_indirect
      if(b[y]===0x11){
        const [type,q1]=u(y+1);
        const [table]=u(q1);

        console.log(
          `FUNC ${func} CONST4788 @ 0x${x.toString(16)} `+
          `CALL_INDIRECT @ 0x${y.toString(16)} `+
          `TYPE=${type} TABLE=${table}`
        );

        console.log(
          [...b.subarray(
            Math.max(start,x-20),
            Math.min(end,y+10)
          )]
          .map(v=>v.toString(16).padStart(2,"0")).join(" ")
        );

        console.log("--------------------------------------------");
        break;
      }

      // stop at return / branch / end
      if(
        b[y]===0x0b ||
        b[y]===0x0c ||
        b[y]===0x0d ||
        b[y]===0x0f
      ) break;
    }
  }

  p=end;
}

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function u(p) {
  let v=0,s=0;
  while(true){
    const x=b[p++];
    v+=(x&127)*2**s;
    if(!(x&128)) return [v>>>0,p];
    s+=7;
  }
}

let p=8, code=0;

while(p<b.length){
  const id=b[p++];
  const [n,q]=u(p); p=q;
  if(id===10){ code=p; break; }
  p+=n;
}

p=code;
const [count,q0]=u(p); p=q0;

const TARGET=4788;

for(let i=0;i<count;i++){
  const [size,q]=u(p); p=q;

  const func=83+i;
  const start=p, end=p+size;

  for(let x=start;x<end;x++){

    // i32.const 4788
    if(b[x]!==0x41) continue;

    const [v,qv]=u(x+1);
    if(v!==TARGET) continue;

    // Search forward for a store within 24 bytes
    for(let y=qv;y<Math.min(end,qv+24);y++){

      if(b[y]!==0x36 && b[y]!==0x37 && b[y]!==0x38)
        continue;

      const [,qa]=u(y+1);
      const [off,qb]=u(qa);

      console.log(
        `FUNC ${func} CONST 4788 @ 0x${x.toString(16)} `+
        `STORE @ 0x${y.toString(16)} `+
        `OP=0x${b[y].toString(16)} OFFSET=${off}`
      );

      console.log(
        [...b.subarray(
          Math.max(start,x-20),
          Math.min(end,y+12)
        )]
        .map(v=>v.toString(16).padStart(2,"0")).join(" ")
      );

      console.log("--------------------------------------------");
      break;
    }
  }

  p=end;
}

FILE: scripts\find4815TableSlot.ts
====================================================================================================
import { readFileSync } from "fs";

const WASM_PATH =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_FUNC = 4815;

function readULEB(buf: Buffer, p: number) {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[p++];

        result += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            break;
        }

        shift += 7;
    }

    return {
        value: result,
        next: p,
    };
}

function readElementSection(buf: Buffer) {
    let p = 8;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const id = buf[p++];

        const s = readULEB(buf, p);
        const size = s.value;
        p = s.next;

        const payloadStart = p;
        const payloadEnd = p + size;

        if (id === 9) {
            return {
                start: payloadStart,
                end: payloadEnd,
            };
        }

        p = payloadEnd;
    }

    throw new Error("Element section not found.");
}

export async function run() {
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" FIND FUNCTION 4815 IN ELEMENT TABLE");
    console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log("");

    const wasm = readFileSync(WASM_PATH);

    console.log(`WASM size : ${wasm.length}`);
    console.log(`Target    : Function ${TARGET_FUNC}`);
    console.log("");

    const sec = readElementSection(wasm);

    console.log(
        `Element section : 0x${sec.start.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} .. 0x${sec.end.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
    );

    let p = sec.start;

    const segmentInfo = readULEB(wasm, p);
    const segmentCount = segmentInfo.value;
    p = segmentInfo.next;

    console.log(`Segments        : ${segmentCount}`);
    console.log("");

    let totalFound = 0;

    for (let seg = 0; seg < segmentCount; seg++) {
        const flagsInfo = readULEB(wasm, p);
        const flags = flagsInfo.value;
        p = flagsInfo.next;

        console.log(`Segment ${seg}: flags=${flags}`);

        if (flags !== 0) {
            console.log(
                `Unsupported flags=${flags}; stopping safely.`
            );
            break;
        }

        /*
         * flags = 0:
         *
         *   offset expression
         *   end
         *   vec(funcidx)
         *
         * There is NO elemkind byte here.
         */

        console.log(
            `  Offset expression starts at 0x${p.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
        );

        let offsetValue: number | null = null;

        const offsetOp = wasm[p++];

        if (offsetOp === 0x41) {
            const r = readULEB(wasm, p);
            offsetValue = r.value;
            p = r.next;

            console.log(`  Offset opcode : i32.const`);
            console.log(`  Table offset  : ${offsetValue}`);
        } else {
            throw new Error(
                `Unexpected offset opcode 0x${offsetOp.toString(16)} at 0x${(p - 1).toString(16)}`
            );
        }

        const endOp = wasm[p++];

        if (endOp !== 0x0b) {
            throw new Error(
                `Expected end (0x0b), got 0x${endOp.toString(16)} at 0x${(p - 1).toString(16)}`
            );
        }

        console.log(
            `  Offset expression ends at 0x${(p - 1).toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
        );

        /*
         * Now directly read vector length.
         */

        const countInfo = readULEB(wasm, p);
        const elementCount = countInfo.value;
        p = countInfo.next;

        console.log(`  Element count : ${elementCount}`);
        console.log(
            `  First funcidx : 0x${p.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
        );
        console.log("");

        let segmentFound = 0;

        for (let slot = 0; slot < elementCount; slot++) {
            const f = readULEB(wasm, p);
            const funcIndex = f.value;
            p = f.next;

            if (funcIndex === TARGET_FUNC) {
                segmentFound++;
                totalFound++;

                const actualTableIndex =
                    (offsetValue ?? 0) + slot;

                console.log(
                    `*** FOUND Function ${TARGET_FUNC} ***`
                );

                console.log(
                    `  Segment       : ${seg}`
                );

                console.log(
                    `  Vector slot   : ${slot}`
                );

                console.log(
                    `  Table offset  : ${offsetValue}`
                );

                console.log(
                    `  Table index   : ${actualTableIndex}`
                );

                console.log(
                    `  Function      : ${funcIndex}`
                );

                console.log("");
            }
        }

        console.log(
            `Segment matches : ${segmentFound}`
        );

        console.log(
            `Parser position : 0x${p.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
        );

        console.log(
            `Section end     : 0x${sec.end.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
        );

        console.log("");
    }

    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" SUMMARY");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log("");

    console.log(
        `Function ${TARGET_FUNC} occurrences : ${totalFound}`
    );

    if (totalFound === 0) {
        console.log("");
        console.log(
            "Function 4815 was NOT found in the Element Table."
        );
    } else {
        console.log("");
        console.log(
            `Function ${TARGET_FUNC} IS present in the Element Table.`
        );
        console.log("");
        console.log(
            "NEXT TARGET:"
        );
        console.log(
            "Find decoder-valid call_indirect instructions"
        );
        console.log(
            "whose runtime table index can resolve to the"
        );
        console.log(
            "table index reported above."
        );
    }
}

// ULEB128 of 5311 = 0xBF 0x29
const needle = [0xbf, 0x29];

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FIND 5311 NEAR CALL_INDIRECT TYPE 1");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

let hits = 0;

for (let i = 0; i < b.length - 2; i++) {
  if (b[i] !== 0x41) continue;
  if (b[i + 1] !== needle[0] || b[i + 2] !== needle[1]) continue;

  console.log("");
  console.log("i32.const 5311 @ 0x" + i.toString(16));

  const start = Math.max(0, i - 80);
  const end = Math.min(b.length, i + 100);

  let found = false;

  for (let j = i; j < end; j++) {
    if (b[j] === 0x11) {
      console.log(
        "CALL_INDIRECT candidate @ 0x" +
        j.toString(16)
      );
      found = true;
      break;
    }
  }

  if (found) {
    console.log(
      "RAW:",
      [...b.slice(start, end)]
        .map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
        .join(" ")
    );
    hits++;
  }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");

FILE: scripts\find7219004800V22.ts

import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const POOL =
    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf';

const TARGET_LT = '99809465000010';
const TARGET_RAW = '7219004800';

const URL =
    'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v3/transactions';

function walk(
    value: any,
    path: string,
    target: string,
    results: string[]
) {
    if (value === null || value === undefined) {
        return;
    }

    if (
        typeof value === 'string' ||
        typeof value === 'number'
    ) {
        if (String(value) === target) {
            results.push(path);
        }
        return;
    }

    if (Array.isArray(value)) {
        for (let i = 0; i < value.length; i++) {
            walk(
                value[i],
                `${path}[${i}]`,
                target,
                results
            );
        }
        return;
    }

    if (typeof value === 'object') {
        for (const [key, child] of Object.entries(value)) {
            walk(
                child,
                `${path}.${key}`,
                target,
                results
            );
        }
    }
}

export async function run(_provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('='.repeat(80));
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 FIND 7.219004800');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('='.repeat(80));

    console.log('\nPOOL:');
    console.log(POOL);

    console.log('\nTARGET LT:');
    console.log(TARGET_LT);

    console.log('\nTARGET TON:');
    console.log('7.219004800 TON');

    console.log('\nTARGET RAW:');
    console.log(TARGET_RAW);

    const url =
        `${URL}?account=${encodeURIComponent(POOL)}&limit=20`;

    console.log('\nFETCHING V3 TRANSACTIONS...');

    const response = await fetch(url);

    if (!response.ok) {
        console.log('\nHTTP ERROR:', response.status);
        console.log(await response.text());
        return;
    }

    const json: any = await response.json();

    const transactions =
        json.transactions ?? [];

    console.log(
        'Transactions returned:',
        transactions.length
    );

    const tx = transactions.find(
        (x: any) =>
            String(x.lt) === TARGET_LT
    );

    if (!tx) {
        console.log('\nTARGET TRANSACTION NOT FOUND');
        return;
    }

    console.log('\n' + '='.repeat(80));
    console.log('TARGET TRANSACTION FOUND');
    console.log('='.repeat(80));

    console.log('LT:', tx.lt);
    console.log('HASH:', tx.hash);

    const matches: string[] = [];

    walk(tx, 'tx', TARGET_RAW, matches);

    console.log('\nMATCH COUNT:', matches.length);

    if (matches.length === 0) {
        console.log(
            '\nNO FIELD CONTAINING 7219004800 WAS FOUND.'
        );
    } else {
        console.log('\nMATCHING JSON PATHS:');

        for (const path of matches) {
            console.log('\n', path);
        }
    }

    console.log('\n' + '='.repeat(80));
    console.log('IMPORTANT NUMERIC FIELDS');
    console.log('='.repeat(80));

    const keys = [
        'total_fees',
        'fee',
        'storage_fee',
        'other_fee',
        'compute_exit_code',
        'action_result_code',
        'aborted',
        'destroyed',
        'end_status',
        'orig_status'
    ];

    for (const key of keys) {
        if (tx[key] !== undefined) {
            console.log(
                `${key}:`,
                JSON.stringify(tx[key])
            );
        }
    }

    console.log('\n' + '='.repeat(80));
    console.log('TRANSACTION DESCRIPTION');
    console.log('='.repeat(80));

    if (tx.description !== undefined) {
        console.log(
            JSON.stringify(
                tx.description,
                null,
                2
            )
        );
    } else {
        console.log('No description field.');
    }

    console.log('\n' + '='.repeat(80));
    console.log('END');
    console.log('READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT');
    console.log('='.repeat(80));
}

import fs from "fs";

const WASM_PATH =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const FILE_START = 0x004d7c08;
const FILE_END   = 0x004d7c96;

const RUNTIME_START = 0x001bc480;
const TARGET_RUNTIME = 0x001bc4e0;

function hex(n: number): string {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

export async function run() {

    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" INSPECT FUNCTION INDEX 7969 DATA");
    console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log();

    // Read only the required small region.
    const fd = fs.openSync(WASM_PATH, "r");

    const size = FILE_END - FILE_START;
    const buf = Buffer.allocUnsafe(size);

    fs.readSync(
        fd,
        buf,
        0,
        size,
        FILE_START
    );

    fs.closeSync(fd);

    console.log("Segment file start :", hex(FILE_START));
    console.log("Segment file end   :", hex(FILE_END));
    console.log("Runtime start      :", hex(RUNTIME_START));
    console.log();

    const targetRelative =
        TARGET_RUNTIME - RUNTIME_START;

    const targetOffset =
        targetRelative;

    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" TARGET");
    console.log("============================================================");

    console.log("Runtime target :", hex(TARGET_RUNTIME));
    console.log("Relative       :", hex(targetRelative));
    console.log("Buffer offset  :", hex(targetOffset));

    if (
        targetOffset >= 0 &&
        targetOffset + 4 <= buf.length
    ) {
        const value =
            buf.readUInt32LE(targetOffset);

        console.log("Value          :", value);
        console.log("Value hex      :", hex(value));
    }

    console.log();

    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" COMPLETE SEGMENT");
    console.log("============================================================");

    for (
        let i = 0;
        i + 4 <= buf.length;
        i += 4
    ) {
        const file =
            FILE_START + i;

        const runtime =
            RUNTIME_START + i;

        const value =
            buf.readUInt32LE(i);

        const marker =
            runtime === TARGET_RUNTIME
                ? "  <<< TARGET 7969"
                : "";

        console.log(
            hex(file),
            "runtime",
            hex(runtime),
            "=>",
            value,
            "(" + hex(value) + ")",
            marker
        );
    }

    console.log();

    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" TARGET NEIGHBORHOOD");
    console.log("============================================================");

    for (
        let delta = -32;
        delta <= 32;
        delta += 4
    ) {
        const runtime =
            TARGET_RUNTIME + delta;

        const file =
            FILE_START +
            (runtime - RUNTIME_START);

        const offset =
            file - FILE_START;

        if (
            offset < 0 ||
            offset + 4 > buf.length
        ) {
            continue;
        }

        const value =
            buf.readUInt32LE(offset);

        console.log(
            (delta >= 0 ? "+" : "") +
            delta.toString().padStart(3),
            "runtime",
            hex(runtime),
            "=>",
            value,
            "(" + hex(value) + ")"
        );
    }

    console.log();

    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" END");
    console.log("============================================================");
}

FILE: scripts\findActualFunction87V1.ts

import fs from "fs";

const WASM_PATH =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_FUNCTION_INDEX = 87;

function hex(n: number): string {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function readULEB(
  buf: Buffer,
  pos: number
): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = buf[pos++];

    value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return { value, next: pos };
    }

    shift += 7;

    if (shift > 35) {
      throw new Error("ULEB128 too large");
    }
  }
}

function findSection(
  wasm: Buffer,
  wantedId: number
): { start: number; end: number } | null {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const id = wasm[p++];

    const sizeInfo = readULEB(wasm, p);
    p = sizeInfo.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + sizeInfo.value;

    if (id === wantedId) {
      return { start, end };
    }

    p = end;
  }

  return null;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FIND ACTUAL FUNCTION INDEX 87 v1");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM_PATH);

const importSection = findSection(wasm, 2);
const functionSection = findSection(wasm, 3);
const codeSection = findSection(wasm, 10);

if (!importSection) throw new Error("Import section not found");
if (!functionSection) throw new Error("Function section not found");
if (!codeSection) throw new Error("Code section not found");

//
// Count imported functions
//
let ip = importSection.start;

const importCountInfo = readULEB(wasm, ip);
const importCount = importCountInfo.value;
ip = importCountInfo.next;

let importedFunctions = 0;

for (let i = 0; i < importCount; i++) {
  const moduleLen = readULEB(wasm, ip);
  ip = moduleLen.next + moduleLen.value;

  const nameLen = readULEB(wasm, ip);
  ip = nameLen.next + nameLen.value;

  const kind = wasm[ip++];

  if (kind === 0x00) {
    const typeInfo = readULEB(wasm, ip);
    ip = typeInfo.next;
    importedFunctions++;
  } else if (kind === 0x01) {
    ip += 1;

    const flags = wasm[ip++];

    const min = readULEB(wasm, ip);
    ip = min.next;

    if (flags & 1) {
      const max = readULEB(wasm, ip);
      ip = max.next;
    }
  } else if (kind === 0x02) {
    const flags = wasm[ip++];

    const min = readULEB(wasm, ip);
    ip = min.next;

    if (flags & 1) {
      const max = readULEB(wasm, ip);
      ip = max.next;
    }
  } else if (kind === 0x03) {
    ip += 2;
  } else {
    throw new Error(`Unknown import kind: ${kind}`);
  }
}

console.log("Imported function count:", importedFunctions);
console.log();

if (TARGET_FUNCTION_INDEX < importedFunctions) {
  console.log(
    `Function ${TARGET_FUNCTION_INDEX} is IMPORTED.`
  );
  console.log(
    "There is no Code-section body for this function."
  );
  process.exit(0);
}

const bodyOrdinal =
  TARGET_FUNCTION_INDEX - importedFunctions;

console.log(
  `Function ${TARGET_FUNCTION_INDEX} -> body ordinal ${bodyOrdinal}`
);
console.log();

//
// Read Function section
//
let fp = functionSection.start;

const functionCountInfo = readULEB(wasm, fp);
const definedFunctionCount = functionCountInfo.value;
fp = functionCountInfo.next;

const typeIndices: number[] = [];

for (let i = 0; i < definedFunctionCount; i++) {
  const r = readULEB(wasm, fp);
  typeIndices.push(r.value);
  fp = r.next;
}

console.log(
  `Body #${bodyOrdinal} type index: ${typeIndices[bodyOrdinal]}`
);
console.log();

//
// Locate body
//
let cp = codeSection.start;

const bodyCountInfo = readULEB(wasm, cp);
const bodyCount = bodyCountInfo.value;
cp = bodyCountInfo.next;

if (bodyOrdinal >= bodyCount) {
  throw new Error("Body ordinal outside Code section");
}

let bodyStart = -1;
let bodyEnd = -1;
let bodySize = -1;

for (let i = 0; i < bodyCount; i++) {
  const sizeInfo = readULEB(wasm, cp);

  const size = sizeInfo.value;
  const start = sizeInfo.next;
  const end = start + size;

  if (i === bodyOrdinal) {
    bodyStart = start;
    bodyEnd = end;
    bodySize = size;
    break;
  }

  cp = end;
}

if (bodyStart < 0) {
  throw new Error("Target body not found");
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" ACTUAL FUNCTION 87");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();

console.log("Function index :", TARGET_FUNCTION_INDEX);
console.log("Body ordinal   :", bodyOrdinal);
console.log("Type index     :", typeIndices[bodyOrdinal]);
console.log("Body start     :", hex(bodyStart));
console.log("Body end       :", hex(bodyEnd));
console.log("Body size      :", bodySize);
console.log();

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" RAW BODY");
console.log("============================================================");

for (let p = bodyStart; p < bodyEnd; p += 16) {
  const e = Math.min(p + 16, bodyEnd);

  const bytes: string[] = [];

  for (let q = p; q < e; q++) {
    bytes.push(wasm[q].toString(16).padStart(2, "0"));
  }

  console.log(hex(p), bytes.join(" "));
}

console.log();

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" END");
console.log("============================================================");

FILE: scripts\findCall119.mjs

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function uleb(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 127) * 2 ** s;
    if (!(x & 128)) return [v >>> 0, p];
    s += 7;
  }
}

function findCode() {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < b.length) {
    const id = b[p++];
    const [size, q] = uleb(p);
    p = q;

    if (id === 10)
      return [p, p + size];

    p += size;
  }

  throw new Error("CODE SECTION NOT FOUND");
}

const [secStart, secEnd] = findCode();

let p = secStart;
const [count, q] = uleb(p);
p = q;

for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
  const [size, q2] = uleb(p);
  p = q2;

  const func = 83 + i;
  const start = p;
  const end = p + size;

  for (let x = start; x < end; x++) {
    if (b[x] === 0x10) {
      const [target, q3] = uleb(x + 1);

      if (target === 119) {
        console.log(
          `CALL119 FUNC ${func} @ 0x${x.toString(16)}`
        );

        const from = Math.max(start, x - 24);
        const to = Math.min(end, x + 4);

        console.log(
          [...b.subarray(from, to)]
            .map(v => v.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
            .join(" ")
        );
        console.log("--------------------------------------------");
      }
    }
  }

  p = end;
}

FILE: scripts\findCall1968.mjs

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGET = 1968;

function u(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, x;
  do {
    x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 127) * 2 ** s;
    s += 7;
  } while (x & 128);
  return [v >>> 0, p];
}

let p = 8;
let codeStart = 0;

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

let p = 8;

function u(): number {
  let v = 0, s = 0, x: number;
  do {
    x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 127) * 2 ** s;
    s += 7;
  } while (x & 128);
  return v >>> 0;
}

let codeStart = 0;

while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p++];
  const size = u();
  const end = p + size;

  if (id === 10) {
    codeStart = p;
    break;
  }
  p = end;
}

p = codeStart;
const count = u();

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FIND CALLS TO FUNC 1968");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

let hits = 0;

for (let f = 0; f < count; f++) {
  const bodySize = u();
  const bodyStart = p;
  const bodyEnd = p + bodySize;

  const groups = u();
  for (let g = 0; g < groups; g++) {
    u();
    p++;
  }

  for (let q = p; q < bodyEnd - 1; q++) {
    if (b[q] === 0x10 && b[q + 1] === 0xB0 && b[q + 2] === 0x0F) {
      console.log(
        "CALLER FUNC:",
        f + 91,
        "AT 0x" + q.toString(16)
      );
      hits++;
    }
  }

  p = bodyEnd;
}

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("TOTAL DIRECT CALLERS:", hits);
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
