import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function readULEB(p) {
  let v = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let n = 0;

  while (p < b.length) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;
    n++;

    if (!(x & 0x80)) return { value: v >>> 0, len: n };

    shift += 7;
    if (shift > 35) return null;
  }
  return null;
}

function hex(v) {
  return "0x" + v.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

const TARGET_FUNC = 2057;

console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log("CALLERS OF FUNC 2057");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("=".repeat(72));

let hits = 0;

for (let p = 0; p < b.length - 2; p++) {

  if (b[p] !== 0x10) continue;

  const r = readULEB(p + 1);
  if (!r || r.value !== TARGET_FUNC) continue;

  hits++;

  const s = Math.max(0, p - 32);
  const e = Math.min(b.length, p + 12);

  console.log("");
  console.log(`CALL ${hits} @ ${hex(p)}`);
  console.log(
    Array.from(b.slice(s, e))
      .map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
      .join(" ")
  );
}

console.log("");
console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log(`CALL SITES : ${hits}`);
console.log("=".repeat(72));

import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

const IMPORT_COUNT = 83;
const CODE_START = 0x6e7d;
const CODE_END = 0x324ef5;

function readULEB(offset: number): {
    value: number;
    next: number;
} {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = offset;

    while (true) {
        const x = b[p++];

        value += (x & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if ((x & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: p };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

type FuncRange = {
    bodyIndex: number;
    realIndex: number;
    sizeField: number;
    codeStart: number;
    bodyEnd: number;
};

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" CALL 205 OWNER SEARCH V1");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

let p = CODE_START;

const count = readULEB(p);
const functionCount = count.value;
p = count.next;

const funcs: FuncRange[] = [];

for (let bodyIndex = 0; bodyIndex < functionCount; bodyIndex++) {
    const sizeField = p;

    const sizeInfo = readULEB(p);
    const codeStart = sizeInfo.next;
    const bodyEnd = codeStart + sizeInfo.value;

    funcs.push({
        bodyIndex,
        realIndex: IMPORT_COUNT + bodyIndex,
        sizeField,
        codeStart,
        bodyEnd,
    });

    p = bodyEnd;
}

if (p !== CODE_END) {
    throw new Error(
        `Function map mismatch: ` +
        `0x${p.toString(16)} != 0x${CODE_END.toString(16)}`
    );
}

console.log(`Function bodies : ${functionCount}`);
console.log(`Map end         : 0x${p.toString(16)}`);
console.log("");

const hits: {
    offset: number;
    func: FuncRange;
}[] = [];

for (let i = CODE_START; i + 2 < CODE_END; i++) {
    if (
        b[i] === 0x10 &&
        b[i + 1] === 0xcd &&
        b[i + 2] === 0x01
    ) {
        const func = funcs.find(
            f => i >= f.codeStart && i < f.bodyEnd
        );

        if (func) {
            hits.push({
                offset: i,
                func,
            });
        }
    }
}

console.log("CALL 205 HITS");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (const h of hits) {
    console.log(
        `CALL @ 0x${h.offset.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} ` +
        `-> Function 205 ` +
        `owner Function ${h.func.realIndex} ` +
        `(body #${h.func.bodyIndex}) ` +
        `[0x${h.func.codeStart.toString(16)}..` +
        `0x${h.func.bodyEnd.toString(16)}]`
    );
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("RESULT");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`Raw CALL 205 hits : ${hits.length}`);

const selfCalls = hits.filter(
    h => h.func.realIndex === 205
).length;

console.log(`CALL 205 inside Function 205 : ${selfCalls}`);

import * as fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_FUNC = 2094;

const TARGETS = [
    { name: "G-528", offset: -528 },
    { name: "G-336", offset: -336 },
    { name: "G-272", offset: -272 },
    { name: "G-208", offset: -208 },
];

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 2094 CALL 205 PRODUCER SCAN V1");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();

const bytes = new Uint8Array(fs.readFileSync(WASM));

console.log(`WASM size : ${bytes.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log(`Function  : ${TARGET_FUNC}`);
console.log();

function uleb(pos: number): { value: number; end: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (true) {
        const b = bytes[p++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;

        shift += 7;
    }

    return { value, end: p };
}

function sleb32(pos: number): { value: number; end: number } {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;
    let b = 0;

    while (true) {
        b = bytes[p++];

        result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
        result |= ~0 << shift;
    }

    return {
        value: result,
        end: p,
    };
}

function readSections() {
    let p = 8;

    let codeStart = 0;
    let codeEnd = 0;

    while (p < bytes.length) {
        const id = bytes[p++];

        const size = uleb(p);
        p = size.end;

        const payload = p;
        const end = p + size.value;

        if (id === 10) {
            codeStart = payload;
            codeEnd = end;
        }

        p = end;
    }

    return {
        codeStart,
        codeEnd,
    };
}

function getImportedFunctionCount(): number {
    let p = 8;

    while (p < bytes.length) {
        const id = bytes[p++];

        const size = uleb(p);
        p = size.end;

        const end = p + size.value;

        if (id !== 2) {
            p = end;
            continue;
        }

        let q = p;

        const count = uleb(q);
        q = count.end;

        let importedFunctions = 0;

        for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {

            const moduleLen = uleb(q);
            q = moduleLen.end + moduleLen.value;

            const nameLen = uleb(q);
            q = nameLen.end + nameLen.value;

            const kind = bytes[q++];

            if (kind === 0) {
                const type = uleb(q);
                q = type.end;

                importedFunctions++;
            }
            else if (kind === 1) {
                q += 4;
            }
            else if (kind === 2) {
                q += 1;
            }
            else if (kind === 3) {
                q += 2;
            }
        }

        return importedFunctions;
    }

    return 0;
}

function getFunctionBody(target: number) {

    const sections = readSections();

    const imported = getImportedFunctionCount();

    const functionIndex = target - imported;

    let p = sections.codeStart;

    const count = uleb(p);
    p = count.end;

    for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {

        const size = uleb(p);

        const bodySize = size.value;

        const bodyStart = size.end;

        const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bodySize;

        if (i === functionIndex) {

            return {
                imported,
                functionIndex,
                bodyStart,
                bodyEnd,
            };
        }

        p = bodyEnd;
    }

    throw new Error("Function body not found");
}

function readOpcode(pc: number): {
    opcode: number;
    end: number;
} {

    const opcode = bytes[pc];

    let p = pc + 1;

    switch (opcode) {

        case 0x20:
        case 0x21:
        case 0x22: {
            const x = uleb(p);

            return {
                opcode,
                end: x.end,
            };
        }

        case 0x41: {
            const x = sleb32(p);

            return {
                opcode,
                end: x.end,
            };
        }

        case 0x42: {

            while (bytes[p++] & 0x80) {}

            return {
                opcode,
                end: p,
            };
        }

        case 0x28:
        case 0x29:
        case 0x2a:
        case 0x2b:
        case 0x36:
        case 0x37:
        case 0x38:
        case 0x39: {

            const align = uleb(p);

            const off = uleb(align.end);

            return {
                opcode,
                end: off.end,
            };
        }

        case 0x10: {

            const x = uleb(p);

            return {
                opcode,
                end: x.end,
            };
        }

        case 0x02:
        case 0x03:
        case 0x04: {

            p++;

            return {
                opcode,
                end: p,
            };
        }

        case 0x0c:
        case 0x0d: {

            const x = uleb(p);

            return {
                opcode,
                end: x.end,
            };
        }

        default:
            return {
                opcode,
                end: p,
            };
    }
}

const info = getFunctionBody(TARGET_FUNC);

console.log(`Imports   : ${info.imported}`);
console.log(
    `Body      : 0x${info.bodyStart.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} .. 0x${info.bodyEnd.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
);
console.log();

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" TARGET MEMORY CELLS");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const t of TARGETS) {
    console.log(`  ${t.name} = global0 ${t.offset}`);
}

console.log();

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" RAW STORE / LOAD SCAN");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();

let p = info.bodyStart;

/*
 * Skip locals declaration.
 *
 * bodyStart points to:
 *
 *   local decl count
 *   local decl groups
 *   instruction stream
 */

const localGroups = uleb(p);
p = localGroups.end;

for (let i = 0; i < localGroups.value; i++) {

    const n = uleb(p);
    p = n.end;

    p++;
}

let instructionCount = 0;

while (p < info.bodyEnd) {

    const current = p;

    const decoded = readOpcode(p);

    p = decoded.end;

    instructionCount++;

    /*
     * i64.store = 0x37
     * i32.store = 0x36
     * i64.load  = 0x29
     * i32.load  = 0x28
     */

    if (
        decoded.opcode === 0x37 ||
        decoded.opcode === 0x36
    ) {

        const name =
            decoded.opcode === 0x37
                ? "i64.store"
                : "i32.store";

        const align = uleb(current + 1);

        const off = uleb(align.end);

        console.log(
            `STORE @ 0x${current.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}` +
            `  ${name}` +
            `  offset=${off.value}`
        );
    }

    if (
        decoded.opcode === 0x29 ||
        decoded.opcode === 0x28
    ) {

        const name =
            decoded.opcode === 0x29
                ? "i64.load"
                : "i32.load";

        const align = uleb(current + 1);

        const off = uleb(align.end);

        console.log(
            `LOAD  @ 0x${current.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}` +
            `  ${name}` +
            `  offset=${off.value}`
        );
    }
}

);

);

import fs from "fs";

const file =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(file);

function uleb(p: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;

    if (!(x & 0x80))
      return { v: v >>> 0, p };

    s += 7;
  }
}

function hex(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(6, "0");
}

// Code section
let p = 0x6e7d;

const count = uleb(p);
p = count.p;

for (let fn = 0; fn < count.v; fn++) {

  const s = uleb(p);
  const size = s.v;
  p = s.p;

  const bodyStart = p;
  const bodyEnd = bodyStart + size;

  const locals = uleb(p);
  p = locals.p;

  for (let i = 0; i < locals.v; i++) {
    const n = uleb(p);
    p = n.p;
    p++;
  }

  const codeStart = p;

  while (p < bodyEnd) {

    const at = p;
    const op = b[p++];

    if (op === 0x10) {
      const r = uleb(p);
      p = r.p;

      if (r.v === 2094) {

        console.log();
        console.log("============================================================");
        console.log("CALL 2094 FOUND");
        console.log("============================================================");
        console.log(`Caller function : ${fn}`);
        console.log(`CALL address    : ${hex(at)}`);
        console.log(`Body start      : ${hex(bodyStart)}`);
        console.log(`Code start      : ${hex(codeStart)}`);
        console.log(`Body end        : ${hex(bodyEnd)}`);
        console.log("============================================================");

      }

      continue;
    }

    // Skip immediates

    if (op >= 0x20 && op <= 0x24) {
      const r = uleb(p);
      p = r.p;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x41 || op === 0x42) {
      let v = 0, shift = 0;

      while (true) {
        const x = b[p++];
        v |= (x & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if (!(x & 0x80))
          break;
      }

      continue;
    }

    if (op >= 0x28 && op <= 0x3e) {
      let r = uleb(p);
      p = r.p;

      r = uleb(p);
      p = r.p;

      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0xfc) {
      const r = uleb(p);
      p = r.p;

      if (r.v === 8 || r.v === 10) {
        let x = uleb(p);
        p = x.p;

        x = uleb(p);
        p = x.p;
      }

      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
      const r = uleb(p);
      p = r.p;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04) {
      if (b[p] === 0x40) {
        p++;
      } else {
        const r = uleb(p);
        p = r.p;
      }

      continue;
    }
  }

  p = bodyEnd;
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("END V30");
console.log("============================================================");

FILE: scripts\findCall26RealAddressConsumersV1.ts

import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const START = 0x012200;
const END   = 0x01295b;
const CALL26 = 0x0126b4;

const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function uleb(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
        const b = buf[pos++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            return { value, next: pos };

        shift += 7;
    }

    throw new Error("ULEB overflow");
}

function sleb(pos: number, bits: number) {
    let value = 0n;
    let shift = 0n;

    for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
        const b = buf[pos++];

        value |= BigInt(b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            if (
                shift + 7n < BigInt(bits) &&
                (b & 0x40)
            ) {
                value |=
                    (-1n) << (shift + 7n);
            }

            return { value, next: pos };
        }

        shift += 7n;
    }

    throw new Error("SLEB overflow");
}

function memarg(pos: number) {
    const a = uleb(pos);
    const b = uleb(a.next);

    return {
        align: a.value,
        offset: b.value,
        next: b.next
    };
}

interface Ins {
    start: number;
    end: number;
    op: number;
    text: string;
    kind: string;
    local?: number;
    value?: number | bigint;
    offset?: number;
    global?: number;
}

function decode(pos: number): Ins {
    const start = pos;
    const op = buf[pos++];

    if (op === 0x20) {
        const x = uleb(pos);

        return {
            start,
            end: x.next,
            op,
            text: `local.get ${x.value}`,
            kind: "local.get",
            local: x.value
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x21) {
        const x = uleb(pos);

        return {
            start,
            end: x.next,
            op,
            text: `local.set ${x.value}`,
            kind: "local.set",
            local: x.value
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x22) {
        const x = uleb(pos);

        return {
            start,
            end: x.next,
            op,
            text: `local.tee ${x.value}`,
            kind: "local.tee",
            local: x.value
        };
    }

    /*
     * global.get
     */
    if (op === 0x23) {
        const x = uleb(pos);

        return {
            start,
            end: x.next,
            op,
            text: `global.get ${x.value}`,
            kind: "global.get",
            global: x.value
        };
    }

    /*
     * global.set
     */
    if (op === 0x24) {
        const x = uleb(pos);

        return {
            start,
            end: x.next,
            op,
            text: `global.set ${x.value}`,
            kind: "global.set",
            global: x.value
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x41) {
        const x = sleb(pos, 32);

        return {
            start,
            end: x.next,
            op,
            text: `i32.const ${x.value}`,
            kind: "i32.const",
            value: Number(x.value)
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x42) {
        const x = sleb(pos, 64);

        return {
            start,
            end: x.next,
            op,
            text: `i64.const ${x.value}`,
            kind: "i64.const",
            value: x.value
        };
    }

    if (
        op === 0x28 ||
        op === 0x29 ||
        op === 0x2a ||
        op === 0x2b ||
        op === 0x36 ||
        op === 0x37 ||
        op === 0x38 ||
        op === 0x39
    ) {
        const m = memarg(pos);

        let name = `op=0x${op.toString(16)}`;

        if (op === 0x28) name = "i32.load";
        if (op === 0x29) name = "i64.load";
        if (op === 0x2a) name = "f32.load";
        if (op === 0x2b) name = "f64.load";
        if (op === 0x36) name = "i32.store";
        if (op === 0x37) name = "i64.store";
        if (op === 0x38) name = "f32.store";
        if (op === 0x39) name = "f64.store";

        return {
            start,
            end: m.next,
            op,
            text: `${name} offset=${m.offset}`,
            kind: name,
            offset: m.offset
        };
    }

    const names: Record<number, string> = {
        0x6a: "i32.add",
        0x6b: "i32.sub",

        0x7c: "i64.add",
        0x7d: "i64.sub",
        0x7e: "i64.mul",

        0x10: "call",
        0x0b: "end"
    };

    return {
        start,
        end: pos,
        op,
        text:
            names[op] ??
            `op=0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`,
        kind: "other"
    };
}

function H(n: number) {
    return "0x" +
        n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

const ins: Ins[] = [];

let p = START;

while (p < END) {
    const x = decode(p);
    ins.push(x);
    p = x.end;
}

if (p !== END) {
    throw new Error(
        `Decode mismatch: ${H(p)} != ${H(END)}`
    );
}

console.log(
    "============================================================"
);
console.log(
    " CALL #26 REAL ADDRESS CONSUMER SEARCH v1"
);
console.log(
    " READ ONLY - OFFLINE"
);
console.log(
    "============================================================"
);

console.log(`WASM size : ${buf.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log(`Decoded   : ${ins.length}`);

console.log(
    "\n============================================================"

console.log(
    "CALL #26 LOW  = global0 - 576"
);

console.log(
    "CALL #26 HIGH = global0 - 568"

 * ------------------------------------------------------------

const targetIndex =
    ins.findIndex(x => x.start === CALL26);

if (targetIndex < 0)
    throw new Error("CALL #26 not found");

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * Raw byte pattern around CALL #26
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 */

console.log(
    "\n============================================================"
);
console.log(
    " RAW CALL #26 DESTINATION BYTES"
);
console.log(
    "============================================================"
);

for (
    let i = targetIndex - 5;
    i <= targetIndex + 3;
    i++
) {
    if (i < 0 || i >= ins.length)
        continue;

    const x = ins[i];

    const bytes =
        Array.from(
            buf.subarray(x.start, x.end)
        )
        .map(
            b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")
        )
        .join(" ");

    console.log(
        `${H(x.start)}  ${bytes.padEnd(24)} ${x.text}`
    );
}

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * Search ALL memory accesses after CALL #26
 * ------------------------------------------------------------

    "\n============================================================"

console.log(
    " ALL MEMORY ACCESSES AFTER CALL #26"
);
console.log(
    "============================================================"

interface Access {
    index: number;
    ins: Ins;
}

const accesses: Access[] = [];

for (
    let i = targetIndex + 1;
    i < ins.length;
    i++
) {
    const x = ins[i];

    if (
        x.kind === "i32.load" ||
        x.kind === "i64.load" ||
        x.kind === "i32.store" ||
        x.kind === "i64.store"
    ) {
        accesses.push({
            index: i,
            ins: x
        });
    }
}

console.log(
    `Memory accesses after CALL #26 : ${accesses.length}`
);

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * Print every memory access with backward context.
 *
 * We don't try to execute the whole function.
 * Instead we inspect the address-producing instructions
 * immediately preceding each memory access.
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 */

console.log(
    "\n============================================================"
);
console.log(
    " ADDRESS-PROVENANCE CANDIDATES"
);
console.log(
    "============================================================"

for (const a of accesses) {
    const i = a.index;
    const x = a.ins;

    const from = Math.max(0, i - 8);

    let hasGlobal0 = false;
    let hasLocal2 = false;
    let hasMinus64 = false;
    let hasMinus568 = false;
    let hasMinus576 = false;
    let has512 = false;

    for (let j = from; j < i; j++) {
        const y = ins[j];

        if (
            y.kind === "global.get" &&
            y.global === 0
        ) {
            hasGlobal0 = true;
        }

        if (
            y.kind === "local.get" &&
            y.local === 2
        ) {
            hasLocal2 = true;
        }

        if (
            y.kind === "i32.const"
        ) {
            const n = Number(y.value);

            if (n === -64)
                hasMinus64 = true;

            if (n === -568)
                hasMinus568 = true;

            if (n === -576)
                hasMinus576 = true;

            if (n === 512)
                has512 = true;
        }
    }

    if (
        hasGlobal0 ||
        hasLocal2 ||
        hasMinus64 ||
        hasMinus568 ||
        hasMinus576
    ) {
        console.log(
            "\n------------------------------------------------------------"
        );

        console.log(
            `ACCESS @ ${H(x.start)}`
        );

        console.log(
            x.text
        );

        console.log(
            `flags: global0=${hasGlobal0} ` +
            `local2=${hasLocal2} ` +
            `-64=${hasMinus64} ` +
            `-568=${hasMinus568} ` +
            `-576=${hasMinus576}`
        );

        console.log(
            "Backward context:"
        );

        for (let j = from; j <= i; j++) {
            console.log(
                `  ${H(ins[j].start)}  ${ins[j].text}`
            );
        }
    }
}

import fs from "fs";

const file =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(file);

function uleb(p: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) return { v: v >>> 0, p };
    s += 7;
  }
}

function hex(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(6, "0");
}

let p = 0x6e7d;

const count = uleb(p);
p = count.p;

for (let fn = 0; fn < count.v; fn++) {

  const s = uleb(p);
  const size = s.v;
  p = s.p;

  const bodyStart = p;
  const bodyEnd = p + size;

  const locals = uleb(p);
  p = locals.p;

  for (let i = 0; i < locals.v; i++) {
    const n = uleb(p);
    p = n.p;
    p++;
  }

  const codeStart = p;

  while (p < bodyEnd) {

    const at = p;
    const op = b[p++];

    if (op === 0x10) {

      const r = uleb(p);
      p = r.p;

      if (r.v === 2827) {
        console.log();
        console.log("============================================================");
        console.log("CALL 2827 FOUND");
        console.log("============================================================");
        console.log(`Caller function : ${fn}`);
        console.log(`CALL address    : ${hex(at)}`);
        console.log(`Body start      : ${hex(bodyStart)}`);
        console.log(`Code start      : ${hex(codeStart)}`);
        console.log(`Body end        : ${hex(bodyEnd)}`);
        console.log("============================================================");
      }

      continue;
    }

    if (op >= 0x20 && op <= 0x24) {
      const r = uleb(p);
      p = r.p;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x41 || op === 0x42) {
      let x;
      do {
        x = b[p++];
      } while (x & 0x80);
      continue;
    }

    if (op >= 0x28 && op <= 0x3e) {
      let r = uleb(p);
      p = r.p;
      r = uleb(p);
      p = r.p;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0xfc) {
      const r = uleb(p);
      p = r.p;

      if (r.v === 8 || r.v === 10) {
        let x = uleb(p);
        p = x.p;
        x = uleb(p);
        p = x.p;
      }

      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
      const r = uleb(p);
      p = r.p;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04) {
      if (b[p] === 0x40) {
        p++;
      } else {
        const r = uleb(p);
        p = r.p;
      }
      continue;
    }
  }

  p = bodyEnd;
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("END V33");
console.log("============================================================");

import fs from "fs";

  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\findCall446Contexts.js
====================================================================================================
const fs = require("fs");

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm"
);

const TARGET_CALL = 446;

function readULEB(pos) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[p++];
    result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;

    shift += 7;
  }

  return {
    value: result >>> 0,
    next: p
  };
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Locate CODE section only
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let pos = 8;
let codeStart = null;

while (pos < wasm.length) {
  const sectionStart = pos;
  const id = wasm[pos++];

  const sz = readULEB(pos);
  const sectionSize = sz.value;
  pos = sz.next;

  if (id === 10) {
    codeStart = pos;
    break;
  }

  pos = sectionStart + 1 + (sz.next - (sectionStart + 1)) + sectionSize;
}

if (codeStart === null) {
  throw new Error("CODE section not found");
}

let p = codeStart;

const countInfo = readULEB(p);
const bodyCount = countInfo.value;
p = countInfo.next;

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Decode enough instructions to identify exact call 446
// and maintain instruction boundaries.
// ------------------------------------------------------------

function decodeBody(start, end) {
  let p = start;

  const decl = readULEB(p);
  const localDeclCount = decl.value;
  p = decl.next;

  // Skip local declarations.
  for (let i = 0; i < localDeclCount; i++) {
    const n = readULEB(p);
    p = n.next;

    // valtype
    p++;
  }

  const ins = [];

  while (p < end) {
    const at = p;
    const op = wasm[p++];

    // end
    if (op === 0x0b) {
      ins.push({
        at,
        op,
        text: "end"
      });
      break;
    }

    // local.get/set/tee
    if (op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) {
      const x = readULEB(p);
      p = x.next;

      const name =
        op === 0x20 ? "local.get" :
        op === 0x21 ? "local.set" :
        "local.tee";

      ins.push({
        at,
        op,
        text: `${name} local${x.value}`
      });

      continue;
    }

    // i32.const
    if (op === 0x41) {
      const x = readULEB(p);
      p = x.next;

      ins.push({
        at,
        op,
        text: `i32.const ${x.value | 0}`
      });

      continue;
    }

    // call
    if (op === 0x10) {
      const x = readULEB(p);
      p = x.next;

      ins.push({
        at,
        op,
        target: x.value,
        text: `call ${x.value}`
      });

      continue;
    }

    // call_indirect
    if (op === 0x11) {
      const t = readULEB(p);
      p = t.next;

      const table = readULEB(p);
      p = table.next;

      ins.push({
        at,
        op,
        text: `call_indirect type=${t.value} table=${table.value}`
      });

      continue;
    }

    // i32.load
    if (op === 0x28) {
      const a = readULEB(p);
      p = a.next;

      const o = readULEB(p);
      p = o.next;

      ins.push({
        at,
        op,
        text: `i32.load align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`
      });

      continue;
    }

    // i32.load8_u
    if (op === 0x2d) {
      const a = readULEB(p);
      p = a.next;

      const o = readULEB(p);
      p = o.next;

      ins.push({
        at,
        op,
        text: `i32.load8_u align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`
      });

      continue;
    }

    // i32.store
    if (op === 0x36) {
      const a = readULEB(p);
      p = a.next;

      const o = readULEB(p);
      p = o.next;

      ins.push({
        at,
        op,
        text: `i32.store align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`
      });

      continue;
    }

    // i64.load
    if (op === 0x29) {
      const a = readULEB(p);
      p = a.next;

      const o = readULEB(p);
      p = o.next;

      ins.push({
        at,
        op,
        text: `i64.load align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`
      });

      continue;
    }

    // block / loop / if
    if (op === 0x02) {
      p++;
      ins.push({ at, op, text: "block" });
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x03) {
      p++;
      ins.push({ at, op, text: "loop" });
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x04) {
      p++;
      ins.push({ at, op, text: "if" });
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x05) {
      ins.push({ at, op, text: "else" });
      continue;
    }

    // br / br_if
    if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
      const x = readULEB(p);
      p = x.next;

      ins.push({
        at,
        op,
        text: `${op === 0x0c ? "br" : "br_if"} ${x.value}`
      });

      continue;
    }

    // numeric one-byte instructions
    const names = {
      0x45: "i32.eqz",
      0x49: "i32.lt_u",
      0x4d: "i32.ge_u",
      0x4f: "i32.gt_u",
      0x51: "i64.eqz",
      0x67: "i32.clz",
      0x6a: "i32.add",
      0x6b: "i32.sub",
      0x6c: "i32.mul",
      0x71: "i32.and",
      0x72: "i32.or",
      0x73: "i32.xor",
      0x74: "i32.shl",
      0x75: "i32.shr_s",
      0x76: "i32.shr_u",
      0x79: "i64.shl",
      0x7d: "i64.extend_i32_u",
      0x83: "i64.and",
      0x84: "i64.or",
      0x85: "i64.xor",
      0x86: "i64.shr_u",
      0x88: "i64.shr_s",
      0xa7: "i32.wrap_i64",
      0xad: "i64.extend_i32_s"
    };

    ins.push({
      at,
      op,
      text: names[op] || `opcode 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
    });
  }

  return ins;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Scan every code body
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const matches = [];

p = codeStart + 1;

// Re-read count correctly
const c = readULEB(codeStart);
p = c.next;

for (let bodyIndex = 0; bodyIndex < bodyCount; bodyIndex++) {

  const size = readULEB(p);
  const bodySize = size.value;
  p = size.next;

  const bodyStart = p;
  const bodyEnd = p + bodySize;

  let ins;

  try {
    ins = decodeBody(bodyStart, bodyEnd);
  } catch {
    p = bodyEnd;
    continue;
  }

  for (let i = 0; i < ins.length; i++) {
    const x = ins[i];

    if (x.op === 0x10 && x.target === TARGET_CALL) {
      matches.push({
        bodyIndex,
        bodyStart,
        bodyEnd,
        ins,
        index: i
      });
    }
  }

  p = bodyEnd;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Output
// ------------------------------------------------------------

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FIND EXACT CALL 446 CONTEXTS");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("");
console.log(`WASM SIZE : ${wasm.length}`);
console.log(`TARGET CALL : ${TARGET_CALL}`);
console.log(`CODE BODY COUNT : ${bodyCount}`);
console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`EXACT CALL 446 COUNT : ${matches.length}`);
console.log("============================================================");

for (const m of matches) {

  console.log("");
  console.log(
    `BODY INDEX : ${m.bodyIndex}`
  );

  console.log(
    `BODY : 0x${m.bodyStart.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} .. 0x${m.bodyEnd.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
  );

  console.log(
    `CALL 446 @ 0x${m.ins[m.index].at.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
  );

  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

  const from = Math.max(0, m.index - 18);
  const to = Math.min(m.ins.length, m.index + 10);

  for (let i = from; i < to; i++) {
    const x = m.ins[i];

    const marker =
      i === m.index ? ">>>" : "   ";

    console.log(
      `${marker} 0x${x.at.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ${x.text}`
    );
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("END");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("READ ONLY : YES");
console.log("NETWORK : NO");
console.log("TRANSACTIONS : NO");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\findCall656.mjs
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function u(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 127) * 2 ** s;
    if (!(x & 128)) return [v >>> 0, p];
    s += 7;
  }
}

// Locate code section
let p = 8;
let code = 0;

while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p++];
  const [n, q] = u(p);
  p = q;

  if (id === 10) {
    code = p;
    break;
  }

  p += n;
}

p = code;

const [count, q0] = u(p);
p = q0;

console.log("CALLERS OF FUNC 656");
console.log("============================================");
console.log(`FUNCTION COUNT : ${count}`);
console.log("");

for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
  const [size, q] = u(p);
  p = q;

  const func = 83 + i;
  const start = p;
  const end = p + size;

  for (let x = start; x < end - 1; x++) {

    // call opcode
    if (b[x] !== 0x10) continue;

    const [target, q2] = u(x + 1);

    if (target !== 656) continue;

    console.log("============================================");
    console.log(`FUNC ${func}`);
    console.log(`BODY 0x${start.toString(16)} .. 0x${end.toString(16)}`);
    console.log(`CALL656 @ 0x${x.toString(16)}`);
    console.log("--------------------------------------------");

    // Print 80 bytes before and 30 after
    const a = Math.max(start, x - 80);
    const z = Math.min(end, q2 + 30);

    for (let j = a; j < z; j += 16) {
      let hex = "";
      for (let k = j; k < Math.min(j + 16, z); k++) {
        hex += b[k].toString(16).padStart(2, "0") + " ";
      }

      console.log(
        `0x${j.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ${hex}`
      );
    }

    console.log("");
  }

  p = end;
}

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function u(p: number) {
    let v = 0, s = 0, x = 0;
    do {
        x = b[p++];
        v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;
        s += 7;
    } while (x & 0x80);
    return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };
}

function hx(x: number) {
    return "0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

let p = 8;
let codeStart = 0;

while (p < b.length) {
    const id = b[p++];
    const r = u(p);
    p = r.next;

    if (id === 10) {
        codeStart = p;
        break;
    }

    p += r.value;
}

p = codeStart;

const cr = u(p);
const count = cr.value;
p = cr.next;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FIND ALL CALLS TO FUNC 6618");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

let hits = 0;

for (let ord = 0; ord < count; ord++) {

    const br = u(p);
    const bodySize = br.value;
    p = br.next;

    const bodyStart = p;
    const bodyEnd = p + bodySize;

    const lr = u(p);
    const groups = lr.value;
    p = lr.next;

    for (let g = 0; g < groups; g++) {
        const nr = u(p);
        p = nr.next;
        p++;
    }

    for (let q = p; q < bodyEnd - 3; q++) {

        // call opcode
        if (b[q] !== 0x10) continue;

        const r = u(q + 1);

        if (r.value === 6618) {

            console.log(
                "CALL 6618",
                "CALLER FUNC", ord + 83,
                "@", hx(q)
            );

            const a = Math.max(bodyStart, q - 40);
            const e = Math.min(bodyEnd, q + 8);

            console.log(
                " RAW:",
                [...b.subarray(a, e)]
                    .map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
                    .join(" ")
            );

            console.log();

            hits++;
        }
    }

    p = bodyEnd;
}

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("TOTAL CALLERS :", hits);
console.log("============================================================");

import { Address, Cell, TonClient } from '@ton/ton';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const TARGET_POOL = Address.parse(
    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf'
);

const OP_CALLBACK_ADD_LIQUIDITY = 0x06ecd527;

function opcodeHex(op: number | null): string {
    if (op === null) return 'UNKNOWN';
    return '0x' + op.toString(16).padStart(8, '0');
}

function getOpcode(cell: Cell): number | null {
    try {
        const s = cell.beginParse();

        if (s.remainingBits < 32) {
            return null;
        }

        return s.loadUint(32);
    } catch {
        return null;
    }
}

function cellInfo(cell: Cell): string {
    return `${cell.bits.length} bits / ${cell.refs.length} refs`;
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' STON.fi v2.2 CALLBACK ADD_LIQUIDITY FINDER');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log('READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('============================================================');

    console.log('');
    console.log('TARGET POOL:');
    console.log(TARGET_POOL.toString());

    const client = new TonClient({
        endpoint: 'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v2/jsonRPC',
    });

    console.log('');
    console.log('Fetching Pool transactions...');

    const transactions = await client.getTransactions(TARGET_POOL, {
        limit: 100,
    });

    console.log(`Fetched ${transactions.length} transactions.`);

    let callbackCount = 0;

    for (const tx of transactions) {
        const txHash = tx.hash().toString('base64');
        const lt = tx.lt.toString();

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // INPUT MESSAGE
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        const inMessage = tx.inMessage;

        if (inMessage) {
            const body = inMessage.body;
            const opcode = getOpcode(body);

            if (opcode === OP_CALLBACK_ADD_LIQUIDITY) {
                callbackCount++;

                console.log('');
                console.log('============================================================');
                console.log(` CALLBACK FOUND #${callbackCount} - INPUT`);
                console.log('============================================================');

                console.log('');
                console.log('LT:');
                console.log(lt);

                console.log('');
                console.log('TRANSACTION HASH:');
                console.log(txHash);

                console.log('');
                console.log('TIMESTAMP:');
                console.log(tx.now.toString());

                console.log('');
                console.log('SOURCE:');
                if (inMessage.info.type === 'internal') {
                    console.log(inMessage.info.src.toString());
                } else {
                    console.log('NON-INTERNAL');
                }

                console.log('');
                console.log('DESTINATION:');
                if (inMessage.info.type === 'internal') {
                    console.log(inMessage.info.dest.toString());
                } else {
                    console.log('NON-INTERNAL');
                }

                console.log('');
                console.log('BODY:');
                console.log(cellInfo(body));

                console.log('');
                console.log('OPCODE:');
                console.log(opcodeHex(opcode));

                console.log('');
                console.log('BOC:');
                console.log(body.toBoc().toString('base64'));

                console.log('');
                console.log('============================================================');
            }
        }

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // OUTGOING MESSAGES
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        for (const outMessage of tx.outMessages.values()) {
            const body = outMessage.body;
            const opcode = getOpcode(body);

            if (opcode === OP_CALLBACK_ADD_LIQUIDITY) {
                callbackCount++;

                console.log('');
                console.log('============================================================');
                console.log(` CALLBACK FOUND #${callbackCount} - OUTPUT`);
                console.log('============================================================');

                console.log('');
                console.log('LT:');
                console.log(lt);

                console.log('');
                console.log('TRANSACTION HASH:');
                console.log(txHash);

                console.log('');
                console.log('TIMESTAMP:');
                console.log(tx.now.toString());

                console.log('');
                console.log('SOURCE:');
                if (outMessage.info.type === 'internal') {
                    console.log(outMessage.info.src.toString());
                } else {
                    console.log('NON-INTERNAL');
                }

                console.log('');
                console.log('DESTINATION:');
                if (outMessage.info.type === 'internal') {
                    console.log(outMessage.info.dest.toString());
                } else {
                    console.log('NON-INTERNAL');
                }

                console.log('');
                console.log('BODY:');
                console.log(cellInfo(body));

                console.log('');
                console.log('OPCODE:');
                console.log(opcodeHex(opcode));

                console.log('');
                console.log('BOC:');
                console.log(body.toBoc().toString('base64'));

                console.log('');
                console.log('============================================================');
            }
        }
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' SEARCH RESULT');
    console.log('============================================================');

    console.log('');
    console.log('Callback messages found:');
    console.log(callbackCount);

    if (callbackCount === 0) {
        console.log('');
        console.log(
            'NO 0x06ecd527 MESSAGE FOUND IN THE FETCHED 100 POOL TRANSACTIONS.'
        );
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('Transaction submission: NONE');
    console.log('READ ONLY CHECK COMPLETE');
    console.log('============================================================');
}

const fs = require("fs");

const WASM =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGETS = new Set([
  8950,
  8951,
  8952,
  8953,
  8954
]);

const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function u8(p) {
  return buf[p];
}

function readULEB(pos) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = u8(pos++);
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if (!(b & 0x80)) {
      return { value: value >>> 0, next: pos };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function skipInitExpr(pos) {
  while (true) {
    const op = u8(pos++);

    if (op === 0x0b) {
      return pos;
    }

    if (op === 0x41) {
      pos = readULEB(pos).next;
    } else if (op === 0x42) {
      pos = readULEB(pos).next;
    } else if (op === 0x43) {
      pos += 4;
    } else if (op === 0x44) {
      pos += 8;
    } else if (op === 0x23 || op === 0xd2) {
      pos = readULEB(pos).next;
    }
  }
}

let p = 8;

const elementSegments = [];

while (p < buf.length) {
  const sectionId = u8(p++);

  const s = readULEB(p);
  const sectionSize = s.value;
  p = s.next;

  const end = p + sectionSize;

  if (sectionId === 9) {
    const n = readULEB(p);
    p = n.next;

    console.log("ELEMENT SEGMENTS :", n.value);

    for (let seg = 0; seg < n.value; seg++) {

      const flags = readULEB(p);
      p = flags.next;

      let tableIndex = 0;
      let offset = null;

      if (flags.value === 0) {
        offset = skipInitExpr(p);
        p = offset;

      } else if (flags.value === 1) {
        const elemKind = u8(p++);
        const count = readULEB(p);
        p = count.next;

        for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
          p = readULEB(p).next;
        }

        continue;

      } else if (flags.value === 2) {
        const ti = readULEB(p);
        tableIndex = ti.value;
        p = ti.next;

        p = skipInitExpr(p);

      } else if (flags.value === 3) {
        const elemKind = u8(p++);
        const count = readULEB(p);
        p = count.next;

        for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
          p = readULEB(p).next;
        }

        continue;

      } else if (flags.value === 4) {
        offset = skipInitExpr(p);
        p = offset;

      } else if (flags.value === 6) {
        const ti = readULEB(p);
        tableIndex = ti.value;
        p = ti.next;

        p = skipInitExpr(p);
      }

      const elemType = u8(p++);

      const count = readULEB(p);
      p = count.next;

      const funcs = [];

      for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
        const f = readULEB(p);
        p = f.next;
        funcs.push(f.value);
      }

      let base = null;

      // Re-read the offset expression is unnecessary for active
      // segments in this WASM because the known table is table 0.
      // We recover the base from the init expression bytes separately
      // only for flags 0/2/4/6 below.

      elementSegments.push({
        seg,
        flags: flags.value,
        tableIndex,
        count: funcs.length,
        funcs
      });
    }
  }

  p = end;
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" CALLBACK TABLE MEMBERSHIP");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();

for (const target of [...TARGETS].sort((a, b) => a - b)) {
  console.log(`FUNC ${target}`);

  let found = 0;

  for (const seg of elementSegments) {
    for (let i = 0; i < seg.funcs.length; i++) {
      if (seg.funcs[i] === target) {
        console.log(
          `  SEGMENT ${seg.seg}  relative=${i}  table=${seg.tableIndex}`
        );
        found++;
      }
    }
  }

  if (!found) {
    console.log("  NO TABLE ENTRY FOUND");
  }

  console.log();
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TARGET WINDOW 2045..2059");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();

for (const seg of elementSegments) {
  for (let i = 0; i < seg.funcs.length; i++) {
    const slot = i;

    if (slot >= 2045 && slot <= 2059) {
      console.log(
        `segment=${seg.seg} relative=${slot} func=${seg.funcs[i]}`
      );
    }
  }
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("END");
console.log("============================================================");

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function uleb(pos:number) {
  let v=0, shift=0, p=pos;
  while(p<wasm.length) {
    const b=wasm[p++];
    v += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;
    if(!(b & 0x80)) break;
    shift += 7;
  }
  return {value:v>>>0,next:p};
}

let p=8;
let imported=0;
let codeStart=0;

while(p<wasm.length) {
  const id=wasm[p++];
  const s=uleb(p); p=s.next;
  const end=p+s.value;

  if(id===2) {
    let q=p;
    const n=uleb(q); q=n.next;

    for(let i=0;i<n.value;i++) {
      const ml=uleb(q); q=ml.next+ml.value;
      const nl=uleb(q); q=nl.next+nl.value;
      const kind=wasm[q++];

      if(kind===0) {
        q=uleb(q).next;
        imported++;
      } else if(kind===1) {
        q++;
        q=uleb(q).next;
        q=uleb(q).next;
      } else if(kind===2) {
        q=uleb(q).next;
        q=uleb(q).next;
      } else if(kind===3) {
        q+=2;
      }
    }
  }

  if(id===10) {
    codeStart=p;
    break;
  }

  p=end;
}

let q=codeStart;
const n=uleb(q); q=n.next;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("CALLERS OF FUNC 113");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

for(let i=0;i<n.value;i++) {
  const bs=uleb(q); q=bs.next;
  const st=q;
  const en=q+bs.value;
  const fn=imported+i;

  for(let z=st;z<en;) {
    const pos=z;
    const op=wasm[z++];

    if(op===0x10) {
      const x=uleb(z);
      z=x.next;

      if(x.value===113) {
        const from=Math.max(st,pos-24);
        const to=Math.min(en,pos+1);

        console.log("");
        console.log(`CALLER FUNC : ${fn}`);
        console.log(`CALL OFFSET  : 0x${pos.toString(16)}`);
        console.log(`CONTEXT      : 0x${from.toString(16)} .. 0x${to.toString(16)}`);

        for(let r=from;r<to;) {
          const rop=wasm[r++];
          let imm="";

          if(
            rop===0x20 || rop===0x21 || rop===0x22 ||
            rop===0x10 || rop===0x41 || rop===0x42
          ) {
            const a=uleb(r);
            imm=` ${a.value}`;
            r=a.next;
          }
          else if(rop>=0x28 && rop<=0x3e) {
            const a=uleb(r); r=a.next;
            const o=uleb(r); r=o.next;
            imm=` align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`;
          }

          console.log(
            `  0x${(r-1).toString(16)}  ${rop.toString(16).padStart(2,"0")}${imm}`
          );
        }
      }
    }
    else if(
      op===0x20 || op===0x21 || op===0x22 ||
      op===0x10 || op===0x0c || op===0x0d ||
      op===0x41 || op===0x42
    ) {
      z=uleb(z).next;
    }
    else if(op>=0x28 && op<=0x3e) {
      z=uleb(z).next;
      z=uleb(z).next;
    }
    else if(op===0x11) {
      z=uleb(z).next;
      z=uleb(z).next;
    }
  }

  q=en;
}

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync(
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm"
);

const TARGET = 2055;

function uleb(p) {
  let v=0,s=0;
  while(true) {
    const x=b[p++];
    v += (x&0x7f)*2**s;
    if(!(x&0x80)) return {value:v,next:p};
    s+=7;
  }
}

/*
 * Locate CODE section
 */
let p=8;
let code=null;

while(p<b.length) {
  const id=b[p++];
  const z=uleb(p);
  const size=z.value;
  p=z.next;

  if(id===10) {
    code={start:p,end:p+size};
    break;
  }

  p+=size;
}

/*
 * Imported functions = 83 for this WASM,
 * already established in previous exact resolution.
 */
const IMPORTED=83;

const cc=uleb(code.start);
let cp=cc.next;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("ALL CALLERS OF FUNC 2055");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TARGET : FUNC ${TARGET}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for(let ordinal=0; ordinal<cc.value; ordinal++) {

  const z=uleb(cp);
  const size=z.value;
  cp=z.next;

  const start=cp;
  const end=cp+size;

  for(let q=start;q<end;) {

    const addr=q;
    const op=b[q++];

    /*
     * Decode enough to stay instruction-boundary aware.
     */
    if(op===0x10) {

      const x=uleb(q);
      q=x.next;

      if(x.value===TARGET) {

        const funcIndex=IMPORTED+ordinal;

        console.log("");
        console.log(`FUNC ${funcIndex}`);
        console.log(
          `CALL FUNC2055 @ 0x${addr.toString(16)}`
        );

        const from=Math.max(start,addr-40);
        const to=Math.min(end,addr+40);

        console.log(
          `RAW CONTEXT 0x${from.toString(16)} .. 0x${to.toString(16)}`
        );

        for(let r=from;r<to;r+=16) {

          let s="";
          for(let k=0;k<16 && r+k<to;k++)
            s+=b[r+k].toString(16).padStart(2,"0")+" ";

          console.log(
            `0x${r.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")}  ${s}`
          );
        }
      }

      continue;
    }

    /*
     * Instructions with immediates
     */
    if(op===0x41 || op===0x42) {
      q=uleb(q).next;
      continue;
    }

    if(
      op===0x20 || op===0x21 || op===0x22 ||
      op===0x23 || op===0x24 ||
      op===0x0c || op===0x0d
    ) {
      q=uleb(q).next;
      continue;
    }

    if(op===0x28 || op===0x29 ||
       op===0x2a || op===0x2b ||
       op===0x2c || op===0x2d ||
       op===0x2e || op===0x2f ||
       op===0x30 || op===0x31 ||
       op===0x32 || op===0x33 ||
       op===0x34 || op===0x35 ||
       op===0x36 || op===0x37 ||
       op===0x38 || op===0x39 ||
       op===0x3a || op===0x3b) {

      q=uleb(q).next;
      q=uleb(q).next;
      continue;
    }

    if(op===0x11) {
      q=uleb(q).next;
      q=uleb(q).next;
      continue;
    }

    if(op===0x02 || op===0x03 || op===0x04) {
      q++;
      continue;
    }
  }

  cp=end;
}

console.log("============================================================");

FILE: scripts\findCallers248.js

const fs = require("fs");

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET = 248;

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

function readULEB(pos) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[pos++];
    result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return { value: result >>> 0, next: pos };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function readSLEB(pos) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let b;

  while (true) {
    b = wasm[pos++];
    result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
    result |= (~0 << shift);
  }

  return { value: result | 0, next: pos };
}

function readName(pos) {
  const n = readULEB(pos);
  const len = n.value;
  const start = n.next;
  return {
    value: wasm.toString("utf8", start, start + len),
    next: start + len
  };
}

function skipLimits(pos) {
  const flags = wasm[pos++];
  const min = readULEB(pos);
  pos = min.next;

  if (flags & 1) {
    const max = readULEB(pos);
    pos = max.next;
  }

  return pos;
}

function skipBlockType(pos) {
  const b = wasm[pos];

  if (
    b === 0x40 ||
    b === 0x7f ||
    b === 0x7e ||
    b === 0x7d ||
    b === 0x7c ||
    b === 0x7b ||
    b === 0x70 ||
    b === 0x6f
  ) {
    return pos + 1;
  }

  return readSLEB(pos).next;
}

function skipMemArg(pos) {
  const align = readULEB(pos);
  const offset = readULEB(align.next);

  return {
    next: offset.next,
    align: align.value,
    offset: offset.value
  };
}

function decodeFunction(start, end) {
  const calls = [];

  let p = start;

  while (p < end) {
    const opPos = p;
    const op = wasm[p++];

    try {
      switch (op) {
        case 0x00: // unreachable
        case 0x01: // nop
        case 0x05: // else
        case 0x0b: // end
        case 0x0f: // return
          break;

        case 0x02: // block
        case 0x03: // loop
        case 0x04: // if
          p = skipBlockType(p);
          break;

        case 0x0c: // br
        case 0x0d: // br_if
          p = readULEB(p).next;
          break;

        case 0x0e: { // br_table
          const n = readULEB(p);
          p = n.next;

          for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
            p = readULEB(p).next;
          }

          p = readULEB(p).next;
          break;
        }

        case 0x10: { // call
          const r = readULEB(p);
          p = r.next;

          if (r.value === TARGET) {
            calls.push(opPos);
          }
          break;
        }

        case 0x11: { // call_indirect
          p = readULEB(p).next;
          p = readULEB(p).next;
          break;
        }

        case 0x20: // local.get
        case 0x21: // local.set
        case 0x22: // local.tee
        case 0x23: // global.get
        case 0x24: // global.set
          p = readULEB(p).next;
          break;

        case 0x25: // table.get
        case 0x26: // table.set
          p = readULEB(p).next;
          break;

        case 0x28: // i32.load
        case 0x29: // i64.load
        case 0x2a: // f32.load
        case 0x2b: // f64.load
        case 0x2c: // i32.load8_s
        case 0x2d: // i32.load8_u
        case 0x2e: // i32.load16_s
        case 0x2f: // i32.load16_u
        case 0x30: // i64.load8_s
        case 0x31: // i64.load8_u
        case 0x32: // i64.load16_s
        case 0x33: // i64.load16_u
        case 0x34: // i64.load32_s
        case 0x35: // i64.load32_u
        case 0x36: // i32.store
        case 0x37: // i64.store
        case 0x38: // f32.store
        case 0x39: // f64.store
        case 0x3a: // i32.store8
        case 0x3b: // i32.store16
        case 0x3c: // i64.store8
        case 0x3d: // i64.store16
        case 0x3e: // i64.store32
        {
          p = skipMemArg(p).next;
          break;
        }

        case 0x3f: // memory.size
        case 0x40: // memory.grow
          p = readULEB(p).next;
          break;

        case 0x41: // i32.const
          p = readSLEB(p).next;
          break;

        case 0x42: // i64.const
          p = readSLEB(p).next;
          break;

        case 0x43:
          p += 4;
          break;

        case 0x44:
          p += 8;
          break;

        case 0x45:
        case 0x46:
        case 0x47:
        case 0x48:
        case 0x49:
        case 0x4a:
        case 0x4b:
        case 0x4c:
        case 0x4d:
        case 0x4e:
        case 0x4f:
        case 0x50:
        case 0x51:
        case 0x52:
        case 0x53:
        case 0x54:
        case 0x55:
        case 0x56:
        case 0x57:
        case 0x58:
        case 0x59:
        case 0x5a:
        case 0x5b:
        case 0x5c:
        case 0x5d:
        case 0x5e:
        case 0x5f:
        case 0x60:
        case 0x61:
        case 0x62:
        case 0x63:
        case 0x64:
        case 0x65:
        case 0x66:
        case 0x67:
        case 0x68:
        case 0x69:
        case 0x6a:
        case 0x6b:
        case 0x6c:
        case 0x6d:
        case 0x6e:
        case 0x6f:
        case 0x70:
        case 0x71:
        case 0x72:
        case 0x73:
        case 0x74:
        case 0x75:
        case 0x76:
        case 0x77:
        case 0x78:
        case 0x79:
        case 0x7a:
        case 0x7b:
        case 0x7c:
        case 0x7d:
        case 0x7e:
        case 0x7f:
        case 0x80:
        case 0x81:
        case 0x82:
        case 0x83:
        case 0x84:
        case 0x85:
        case 0x86:
        case 0x87:
        case 0x88:
        case 0x89:
        case 0x8a:
        case 0x8b:
        case 0x8c:
        case 0x8d:
        case 0x8e:
        case 0x8f:
        case 0x90:
        case 0x91:
        case 0x92:
        case 0x93:
        case 0x94:
        case 0x95:
        case 0x96:
        case 0x97:
        case 0x98:
        case 0x99:
        case 0x9a:
        case 0x9b:
        case 0x9c:
        case 0x9d:
        case 0x9e:
        case 0x9f:
        case 0xa0:
        case 0xa1:
        case 0xa2:
        case 0xa3:
        case 0xa4:
        case 0xa5:
        case 0xa6:
        case 0xa7:
        case 0xa8:
        case 0xa9:
        case 0xaa:
        case 0xab:
        case 0xac:
        case 0xad:
        case 0xae:
        case 0xaf:
        case 0xb0:
        case 0xb1:
        case 0xb2:
        case 0xb3:
        case 0xb4:
        case 0xb5:
        case 0xb6:
        case 0xb7:
        case 0xb8:
        case 0xb9:
        case 0xba:
        case 0xbb:
        case 0xbc:
        case 0xbd:
        case 0xbe:
        case 0xbf:
        case 0xc0:
        case 0xc1:
        case 0xc2:
        case 0xc3:
        case 0xc4:
        case 0xc5:
        case 0xc6:
        case 0xc7:
        case 0xc8:
        case 0xc9:
        case 0xca:
        case 0xcb:
        case 0xcc:
        case 0xcd:
        case 0xce:
        case 0xcf:
        case 0xd0:
          p += 0;
          break;

        default:
          // Unknown opcode: stop this function.
          return calls;
      }
    } catch {
      return calls;
    }
  }

  return calls;
}

let pos = 8;

let functionBodies = [];
let functionBodyCount = 0;

while (pos < wasm.length) {
  const sectionId = wasm[pos++];

  const secSize = readULEB(pos);
  const size = secSize.value;
  pos = secSize.next;

  const secStart = pos;
  const secEnd = secStart + size;

  if (sectionId === 10) {
    const count = readULEB(pos);
    pos = count.next;
    functionBodyCount = count.value;

    for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
      const bodySize = readULEB(pos);
      pos = bodySize.next;

      const bodyStart = pos;
      const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bodySize.value;

      functionBodies.push({
        index: i,
        start: bodyStart,
        end: bodyEnd
      });

      pos = bodyEnd;
    }
  } else {
    pos = secEnd;
  }
}

console.log("============================================================");

console.log("TARGET FUNC:", TARGET);
console.log("CODE BODY COUNT:", functionBodyCount);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let total = 0;

for (const body of functionBodies) {
  const hits = decodeFunction(body.start, body.end);

  for (const offset of hits) {
    console.log(
      `FUNC ${body.index} -> CALL ${TARGET} @ 0x${offset
        .toString(16)
        .padStart(8, "0")}`
    );
    total++;
  }
}

FILE: scripts\findCallers656.ts

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGET = 656;
const IMPORTS = 83;

function uleb(p: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[p++];
    v += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) return { value: v, next: p };
    s += 7;
  }
}

function hex(v: number) {
  return "0x" + v.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function section(id: number) {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const sid = wasm[p++];
    const z = uleb(p);
    p = z.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + z.value;

    if (sid === id) return { start, end };

    p = end;
  }

  throw new Error(`section ${id} not found`);
}

const fsx = section(3);
let p = fsx.start;

const fc = uleb(p);
p = fc.next;

const funcTypes: number[] = [];

for (let i = 0; i < fc.value; i++) {
  const x = uleb(p);
  funcTypes.push(x.value);
  p = x.next;
}

const cs = section(10);
p = cs.start;

const cc = uleb(p);
p = cc.next;

console.log("FIND CALLERS OF FUNC 656");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

for (let i = 0; i < cc.value; i++) {
  const sz = uleb(p);
  p = sz.next;

  const start = p;
  const end = p + sz.value;
  const func = IMPORTS + i;

  for (let q = start; q < end;) {
    const pos = q;
    const op = wasm[q++];

    /*
     * Direct call
     */
    if (op === 0x10) {
      const x = uleb(q);
      q = x.next;

      if (x.value === TARGET) {
        console.log();
        console.log(`CALL 656`);
        console.log(`CALLER FUNC = ${func}`);
        console.log(`ADDRESS      = ${hex(pos)}`);
        console.log(`BODY         = ${hex(start)} .. ${hex(end - 1)}`);

        const from = Math.max(start, pos - 20);

        console.log("CONTEXT:");

        for (let z = from; z < Math.min(end, pos + 12); z++) {
          console.log(
            `${hex(z)}  ${wasm[z].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
          );
        }
      }

      continue;
    }

    /*
     * call_indirect:
     * immediate operands are type index + table index.
     */
    if (op === 0x11) {
      const t = uleb(q);
      q = t.next;

      const table = uleb(q);
      q = table.next;

      /*
       * We cannot know statically which table entry maps
       * to FUNC 656 yet. Record all indirect calls whose
       * type is compatible with FUNC 656.
       */
      if (t.value === funcTypes[TARGET - IMPORTS]) {
        console.log();
        console.log(`POSSIBLE INDIRECT CALL`);
        console.log(`CALLER FUNC = ${func}`);
        console.log(`ADDRESS      = ${hex(pos)}`);
        console.log(`TYPE INDEX   = ${t.value}`);
        console.log(`TABLE        = ${table.value}`);
      }

      continue;
    }

    /*
     * Skip immediates for common instructions.
     */
    if (
      op === 0x20 || // local.get
      op === 0x21 || // local.set
      op === 0x22    // local.tee
    ) {
      const x = uleb(q);
      q = x.next;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x41) { // i32.const
      const x = uleb(q);
      q = x.next;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x28) { // i32.load
      const a = uleb(q);
      q = a.next;

      const o = uleb(q);
      q = o.next;

      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
      const x = uleb(q);
      q = x.next;
      continue;
    }
  }

  p = end;
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("READ ONLY - NO EXECUTION - NO TRANSACTION");
console.log("============================================================");

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function uleb(p:number){
  let v=0,s=0;
  while(true){
    const x=b[p++];
    v |= (x&0x7f)<<s;
    if(!(x&0x80)) return {v:v>>>0,p};
    s+=7;
  }
}

function str(p:number){
  const x=uleb(p);
  return x.p+x.v;
}

let imported=0;
let funcs:{fn:number,code:number,end:number}[]=[];

// imports
let p=8;
while(p<b.length){
  const id=b[p++];
  const z=uleb(p);
  const start=z.p,end=start+z.v;

  if(id===2){
    let q=start;
    const n=uleb(q); q=n.p;

    for(let i=0;i<n.v;i++){
      q=str(q);
      q=str(q);
      const kind=b[q++];

      if(kind===0){
        q=uleb(q).p;
        imported++;
      }else if(kind===1){
        q++;
        const fl=uleb(q); q=fl.p;
        q=uleb(q).p;
        if(fl.v&1) q=uleb(q).p;
      }else if(kind===2){
        q=uleb(q).p;
      }else if(kind===3){
        q+=2;
      }
    }
  }

  if(id===10){
    let q=start;
    const n=uleb(q); q=n.p;

    for(let i=0;i<n.v;i++){
      const bs=uleb(q);
      const body=bs.p;
      const bend=body+bs.v;

      let x=body;
      const nl=uleb(x);
      x=nl.p;

      for(let j=0;j<nl.v;j++){
        const c=uleb(x);
        x=c.p+1;
      }

      funcs.push({
        fn:imported+i,
        code:x,
        end:bend
      });

      q=bend;
    }
  }

  p=end;
}

for(const target of [6615,7579]){

  console.log("");
  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log(`CALLERS OF FUNC ${target}`);
  console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
  console.log("============================================================");

  for(const f of funcs){

    let q=f.code;

    while(q<f.end-1){

      const off=q;
      const op=b[q++];

      try{

        if(op===0x10){
          const x=uleb(q);
          q=x.p;

          if(x.v===target){

            console.log("");
            console.log(
              `FUNC ${f.fn}  CALL ${target} @ 0x${off.toString(16)}`
            );

            // decode preceding ~12 instructions
            // restart from function beginning and collect offsets
            let r=f.code;
            const arr:any[]=[];

            while(r<off){
              const io=r;
              const oo=b[r++];

              if(oo===0x20){
                const z=uleb(r); r=z.p;
                arr.push([io,`local.get ${z.v}`]);
              }
              else if(oo===0x21){
                const z=uleb(r); r=z.p;
                arr.push([io,`local.set ${z.v}`]);
              }
              else if(oo===0x22){
                const z=uleb(r); r=z.p;
                arr.push([io,`local.tee ${z.v}`]);
              }
              else if(oo===0x41){
                const z=uleb(r); r=z.p;
                arr.push([io,`i32.const ${z.v}`]);
              }
              else if(oo===0x28){
                r=uleb(r).p;
                const z=uleb(r); r=z.p;
                arr.push([io,`i32.load offset=${z.v}`]);
              }
              else if(oo===0x29){
                r=uleb(r).p;
                const z=uleb(r); r=z.p;
                arr.push([io,`i64.load offset=${z.v}`]);
              }
              else if(oo===0x36){
                r=uleb(r).p;
                const z=uleb(r); r=z.p;
                arr.push([io,`i32.store offset=${z.v}`]);
              }
              else if(oo===0x6a){
                arr.push([io,"i32.add"]);
              }
              else if(oo===0x6b){
                arr.push([io,"i32.sub"]);
              }
              else if(oo===0x6c){
                arr.push([io,"i32.mul"]);
              }
              else if(oo===0x10){
                const z=uleb(r); r=z.p;
                arr.push([io,`call ${z.v}`]);
              }
              else if(oo===0x11){
                const z=uleb(r); r=z.p;
                const y=uleb(r); r=y.p;
                arr.push([io,`call_indirect type=${z.v}`]);
              }
              else if(oo===0x1a){
                arr.push([io,"drop"]);
              }
              else if(oo===0x45){
                arr.push([io,"i32.eqz"]);
              }
              else if(oo===0x47){
                arr.push([io,"i32.lt_u"]);
              }
              else if(oo===0x0b){
                arr.push([io,"end"]);
              }
              else if(oo===0x0c){
                const z=uleb(r); r=z.p;
                arr.push([io,`br ${z.v}`]);
              }
              else if(oo===0x0d){
                const z=uleb(r); r=z.p;
                arr.push([io,`br_if ${z.v}`]);
              }
              else {
                // skip unknown opcode without attempting semantic decode
              }
            }

            for(const [a,s] of arr.slice(-18)){
              console.log(
                `  0x${a.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")}  ${s}`
              );
            }
          }
        }

        else if(op===0x20 || op===0x21 || op===0x22){
          q=uleb(q).p;
        }

        else if(op===0x41){
          q=uleb(q).p;
        }

        else if(op===0x28 || op===0x29 || op===0x36 || op===0x37){
          q=uleb(q).p;
          q=uleb(q).p;
        }

        else if(op===0x10){
          q=uleb(q).p;
        }

        else if(op===0x11){
          q=uleb(q).p;
          q=uleb(q).p;
        }

        else if(op===0x0c || op===0x0d){
          q=uleb(q).p;
        }

      }catch{
        break;
      }
    }
  }
}
