import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath, pathToFileURL } from 'node:url';

const dir = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
const scannerPath = path.join(dir, 'disassembleAddressUsers.mjs');
const tempPath = path.join(dir, '__findCallers7006_decoder_temp.mjs');
const outputPath = path.join(dir, 'findCallers7006_output.txt');

const source = fs.readFileSync(scannerPath, 'utf8');
const marker = "console.log('\\nROOSTER WASM - FUNC 2034 AND CALLERS');";
const cut = source.indexOf(marker);

if (cut < 0) {
    throw new Error('Could not find decoder bootstrap marker');
}

let decoder;

try {
    fs.writeFileSync(
        tempPath,
        source.slice(0, cut) +
            '\nexport { bodies, decodeFunction, hex };\n',
        'utf8'
    );

    decoder = await import(
        pathToFileURL(tempPath).href + '?findCallers7006'
    );
} finally {
    fs.rmSync(tempPath, { force: true });
}

const { bodies, decodeFunction, hex } = decoder;
const target = 7006;
const lines = [];
const out = value => lines.push(value ?? '');

out('ROOSTER WASM — CALLERS OF FUNCTION 7006');
out('READ ONLY / OFFLINE / NO WASM EXECUTION');
out('====================================================');

let hits = 0;
let decodeErrors = 0;

for (const funcIndex of bodies.keys()) {
    let decoded;

    try {
        decoded = decodeFunction(funcIndex);
    } catch (error) {
        decodeErrors++;
        continue;
    }

    const instructions = decoded.instructions;

    for (let i = 0; i < instructions.length; i++) {
        const ins = instructions[i];

        if (!new RegExp(`\\bcall\\s+${target}\\b`).test(String(ins.text))) {
            continue;
        }

        hits++;

        out('');
        out('----------------------------------------------------');
        out(`CALLER FUNCTION: ${funcIndex}`);
        out(`CALL SITE: ${hex(ins.start)}`);
        out(`INSTRUCTION INDEX: ${i}`);
        out('CONTEXT:');

        const start = Math.max(0, i - 12);
        const end = Math.min(instructions.length - 1, i + 18);

        for (let j = start; j <= end; j++) {
            const item = instructions[j];
            const tag = j === i ? '>>> ' : '    ';

            out(
                `${tag}${hex(item.start)}  ${item.raw}  ${item.text}`
            );
        }
    }
}

out('');
out('====================================================');
out(`DIRECT CALL SITES FOUND: ${hits}`);
out(`FUNCTION DECODE ERRORS: ${decodeErrors}`);
out('Indirect calls are not resolved by this direct-call scan.');
out('No WASM execution performed.');

fs.writeFileSync(outputPath, lines.join('\n') + '\n', 'utf8');

console.log(`Direct call sites found: ${hits}`);
console.log(`Function decode errors: ${decodeErrors}`);
console.log(`Output: ${outputPath}`);

const fs = require("fs");

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const TARGET = 7484;

function readULEB(pos) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[p++];

    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if (!(b & 0x80))
      break;

    shift += 7;
  }

  return {
    value: value >>> 0,
    next: p
  };
}

function hex(n) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

/*
 * Find code section
 */
let p = 8;
let codeStart = 0;
let codeEnd = 0;

while (p < wasm.length) {

  const id = wasm[p++];

  const s = readULEB(p);

  const start = s.next;
  const end = start + s.value;

  if (id === 10) {
    codeStart = start;
    codeEnd = end;
    break;
  }

  p = end;
}

/*
 * Skip body count
 */
p = codeStart;

const bc = readULEB(p);
const bodyCount = bc.value;
p = bc.next;

/*
 * We know imported functions = 83
 */
const IMPORTED = 83;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("RAW CALLER SCAN FOR FUNC 7484");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log("WASM SIZE :", wasm.length);
console.log("TARGET :", TARGET);
console.log("IMPORTED FUNCTIONS :", IMPORTED);
console.log("CODE BODIES :", bodyCount);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let callers = 0;

/*
 * Scan each function body.
 *
 * We do not attempt to fully decode the body.
 * We only search byte pattern:
 *
 *   0x10 <ULEB(7484)>
 *
 * and then report surrounding bytes.
 */
for (let i = 0; i < bodyCount; i++) {

  const bs = readULEB(p);

  const bodySize = bs.value;
  const bodyStart = bs.next;
  const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bodySize;

  const funcIndex = IMPORTED + i;

  for (let q = bodyStart; q < bodyEnd - 1; q++) {

    if (wasm[q] !== 0x10)
      continue;

    const target = readULEB(q + 1);

    if (target.value !== TARGET)
      continue;

    callers++;

    console.log("");
    console.log("CALLER FUNC :", funcIndex);
    console.log("CALL SITE   :", hex(q));
    console.log("TARGET      :", target.value);
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

    const from = Math.max(bodyStart, q - 40);
    const to = Math.min(bodyEnd, target.next + 40);

    for (let z = from; z < to; z += 16) {

      const end = Math.min(z + 16, to);

      const bytes = [];

      for (let k = z; k < end; k++) {
        bytes.push(
          wasm[k].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")
        );
      }

      const marker =
        q >= z && q < end ? ">>" : "  ";

      console.log(
        marker,
        hex(z),
        bytes.join(" ")
      );
    }
  }

  p = bodyEnd;
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("RESULT");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DIRECT CALLERS :", callers);
console.log("READ ONLY : YES");
console.log("NETWORK : NO");
console.log("TRANSACTIONS : NO");
console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\findCallers76.ts

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function leb(pos: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, p = pos;
  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[p++];
    v |= (b & 0x7f) << s;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };
    s += 7;
  }
}

let p = 8;
let codeStart = 0;

while (p < wasm.length) {
  const id = wasm[p++];
  const r = leb(p);
  p = r.next;
  if (id === 10) {
    codeStart = p;
    break;
  }
  p += r.value;
}

let q = codeStart;
const count = leb(q);
q = count.next;

console.log("CALLERS OF FUNC 76");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

for (let func = 0; func < count.value; func++) {
  const br = leb(q);
  const bodySize = br.value;
  q = br.next;
  const bodyEnd = q + bodySize;

  for (let x = q; x < bodyEnd; x++) {
    if (wasm[x] === 0x10) {
      const r = leb(x + 1);
      if (r.value === 76) {
        console.log(
          `FUNC ${func}  CALL @ 0x${x.toString(16).padStart(6, "0")}`
        );
      }
    }
  }

  q = bodyEnd;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\findCallers8927.js
====================================================================================================
const fs = require("fs");

const FILE = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const TARGET = 8927;

const b = fs.readFileSync(FILE);

function readU32Leb(pos) {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let start = pos;

    while (true) {
        const x = b[pos++];
        result |= (x & 0x7f) << shift;
        if (!(x & 0x80)) break;
        shift += 7;
    }

    return { value: result >>> 0, next: pos, start };
}

function readVec(pos) {
    return readU32Leb(pos);
}

function skipLimits(pos) {
    const flags = b[pos++];
    let r = readU32Leb(pos);
    pos = r.next;

    if (flags & 1) {
        r = readU32Leb(pos);
        pos = r.next;
    }
    return pos;
}

function skipType(pos) {
    const t = b[pos++];

    if (t === 0x60) {
        let r = readU32Leb(pos);
        pos = r.next;
        for (let i = 0; i < r.value; i++) {
            pos++;
        }

        r = readU32Leb(pos);
        pos = r.next;
        for (let i = 0; i < r.value; i++) {
            pos++;
        }
        return pos;
    }

    if (t === 0x5f || t === 0x5e || t === 0x5d || t === 0x5c) {
        return pos;
    }

    return pos;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Find CODE section
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let pos = 8;
let codeStart = -1;
let codeSize = 0;

while (pos < b.length) {
    const sectionId = b[pos++];

    const sr = readU32Leb(pos);
    const sectionSize = sr.value;
    pos = sr.next;

    const payloadStart = pos;

    if (sectionId === 10) {
        codeStart = payloadStart;
        codeSize = sectionSize;
        break;
    }

    pos += sectionSize;
}

if (codeStart < 0) {
    throw new Error("CODE section not found");
}

let p = codeStart;

const countR = readVec(p);
const functionCount = countR.value;
p = countR.next;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DIRECT CALLERS OF FUNC 8927");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("WASM SIZE       :", b.length);
console.log("CODE START      :", "0x" + codeStart.toString(16).padStart(8, "0"));
console.log("FUNCTION BODIES :", functionCount);
console.log("TARGET FUNC     :", TARGET);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

const callers = [];

for (let bodyIndex = 0; bodyIndex < functionCount; bodyIndex++) {

    const sizeR = readU32Leb(p);
    const bodySize = sizeR.value;
    p = sizeR.next;

    const bodyStart = p;
    const bodyEnd = p + bodySize;

    let q = bodyStart;

    const localR = readU32Leb(q);
    const localCount = localR.value;
    q = localR.next;

    // Skip locals
    for (let i = 0; i < localCount; i++) {
        const n = readU32Leb(q);
        q = n.next;
        q++; // valtype
    }

    while (q < bodyEnd) {

        const op = b[q];
        const at = q;
        q++;

        // call
        if (op === 0x10) {
            const r = readU32Leb(q);
            q = r.next;

            if (r.value === TARGET) {
                callers.push({
                    bodyIndex,
                    funcIndex: bodyIndex + 83,
                    bodyStart,
                    bodyEnd,
                    callAt: at
                });
            }

            continue;
        }

        // call_indirect
        if (op === 0x11) {
            const r = readU32Leb(q);
            q = r.next;

            const tableIndex = b[q++];
            continue;
        }

        // Simple immediates
        if (op === 0x41 || op === 0x42) {
            const r = readU32Leb(q);
            q = r.next;
            continue;
        }

        // local/global index
        if (
            op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 || op === 0x22 ||
            op === 0x23 || op === 0x24
        ) {
            const r = readU32Leb(q);
            q = r.next;
            continue;
        }

        // load/store memory immediate
        if (
            (op >= 0x28 && op <= 0x3e)
        ) {
            let r = readU32Leb(q);
            q = r.next;

            r = readU32Leb(q);
            q = r.next;

            continue;
        }

        // memory.size / memory.grow
        if (op === 0x3f || op === 0x40) {
            q++;
            continue;
        }

        // br / br_if / br_table / block / loop / if
        if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04) {
            q++;
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
            const r = readU32Leb(q);
            q = r.next;
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x0e) {
            const r = readU32Leb(q);
            q = r.next;

            for (let i = 0; i < r.value; i++) {
                const x = readU32Leb(q);
                q = x.next;
            }

            const x = readU32Leb(q);
            q = x.next;
            continue;
        }

        // br_table etc. / end / return / drop / select
        if (
            op === 0x0b ||
            op === 0x0f ||
            op === 0x1a ||
            op === 0x1b
        ) {
            continue;
        }

        // ref.null / ref.func
        if (op === 0xd0) {
            q++;
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0xd2) {
            const r = readU32Leb(q);
            q = r.next;
            continue;
        }

        // bulk-memory / misc prefix
        if (op === 0xfc) {
            const sub = readU32Leb(q);
            q = sub.next;

            if (
                sub.value === 8 ||
                sub.value === 9 ||
                sub.value === 10 ||
                sub.value === 11
            ) {
                const a = readU32Leb(q);
                q = a.next;

                if (sub.value === 8 || sub.value === 10) {
                    const c = readU32Leb(q);
                    q = c.next;
                }
            }

            continue;
        }

        // SIMD prefix
        if (op === 0xfd) {
            const sub = readU32Leb(q);
            q = sub.next;

            // Most SIMD instructions have fixed/variable immediates.
            // We don't need to decode them here unless encountered.
            continue;
        }

        // Default: opcode has no immediate
    }

    p = bodyEnd;
}

console.log("DIRECT CALLERS :", callers.length);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (const x of callers) {
    console.log(
        "CALLER FUNC",
        x.funcIndex,
        "BODY",
        x.bodyIndex,
        "CALL @",
        "0x" + x.callAt.toString(16).padStart(8, "0"),
        "BODY",
        "0x" + x.bodyStart.toString(16).padStart(8, "0"),
        "..",
        "0x" + x.bodyEnd.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")
    );
}

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("READ ONLY : YES");
console.log("NETWORK    : NO");
console.log("TRANSACTIONS : NO");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\findCallers8952.js

const fs = require("fs");

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET = 8952;

const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DIRECT CALLER ANALYSIS - FUNCTION 8952");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("WASM SIZE :", buf.length);
console.log("TARGET    :", TARGET);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let p = 8;

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// LEB
// ------------------------------------------------------------

function u8() {
    return buf[p++];
}

function u32() {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[p++];

        result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            return result >>> 0;

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function i32() {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let b;

    while (true) {
        b = buf[p++];

        result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            break;
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40))
        result |= (~0 << shift);

    return result | 0;
}

function bytes(n) {
    const s = p;
    p += n;
    return buf.subarray(s, p);
}

function readName() {
    const n = u32();
    return Buffer.from(bytes(n)).toString("utf8");
}

function hex(x, width = 8) {
    return "0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// SECTION MAP
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const sections = [];

p = 8;

while (p < buf.length) {

    const sectionStart = p;
    const id = u8();
    const size = u32();

    const payloadStart = p;
    const payloadEnd = p + size;

    sections.push({
        id,
        sectionStart,
        payloadStart,
        payloadEnd,
        size
    });

    p = payloadEnd;
}

const importSection = sections.find(s => s.id === 2);
const functionSection = sections.find(s => s.id === 3);
const codeSection = sections.find(s => s.id === 10);

if (!functionSection)
    throw new Error("FUNCTION SECTION NOT FOUND");

if (!codeSection)
    throw new Error("CODE SECTION NOT FOUND");

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// IMPORTS
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let importedFunctions = 0;
const imports = [];

if (importSection) {

    p = importSection.payloadStart;

    const count = u32();

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {

        const module = readName();
        const name = readName();
        const kind = u8();

        let typeIndex = null;

        if (kind === 0x00) {

            typeIndex = u32();
            importedFunctions++;
        }

        else if (kind === 0x01) {

            u8();

            const flags = u8();
            u32();

            if (flags & 1)
                u32();
        }

        else if (kind === 0x02) {

            const flags = u8();
            u32();

            if (flags & 1)
                u32();
        }

        else if (kind === 0x03) {

            u8();
            u8();
        }

        else {
            throw new Error(
                `Unknown import kind ${kind}`
            );
        }

        imports.push({
            module,
            name,
            kind,
            typeIndex
        });
    }
}

console.log("IMPORTED FUNCTIONS :", importedFunctions);

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// FUNCTION SECTION
// ------------------------------------------------------------

p = functionSection.payloadStart;

const definedCount = u32();

const functionTypes = [];

for (let i = 0; i < definedCount; i++) {
    functionTypes.push(u32());
}

function getType(functionIndex) {

    if (functionIndex < importedFunctions) {

        return imports[functionIndex]?.typeIndex ?? null;
    }

    return functionTypes[functionIndex - importedFunctions] ?? null;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// BLOCK TYPE
// ------------------------------------------------------------

function skipBlockType() {

    const b = buf[p];

    if (
        b === 0x40 ||
        b === 0x7f ||
        b === 0x7e ||
        b === 0x7d ||
        b === 0x7c ||
        b === 0x7b
    ) {
        p++;
        return;
    }

    i32();
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// MEMORY ARG
// ------------------------------------------------------------

function skipMemArg() {
    u32();
    u32();
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// GENERIC INSTRUCTION DECODER
// ------------------------------------------------------------

function decodeInstruction(start) {

    const op = u8();

    const ins = {
        start,
        op,
        name: `op_0x${op.toString(16)}`
    };

    switch (op) {

        case 0x00:
            ins.name = "unreachable";
            break;

        case 0x01:
            ins.name = "nop";
            break;

        case 0x02:
            ins.name = "block";
            skipBlockType();
            break;

        case 0x03:
            ins.name = "loop";
            skipBlockType();
            break;

        case 0x04:
            ins.name = "if";
            skipBlockType();
            break;

        case 0x05:
            ins.name = "else";
            break;

        case 0x0b:
            ins.name = "end";
            break;

        case 0x0c:
            ins.name = "br";
            ins.depth = u32();
            break;

        case 0x0d:
            ins.name = "br_if";
            ins.depth = u32();
            break;

        case 0x0e: {

            ins.name = "br_table";

            const n = u32();

            for (let i = 0; i < n; i++)
                u32();

            u32();

            break;
        }

        case 0x0f:
            ins.name = "return";
            break;

        case 0x10:

            ins.name = "call";
            ins.functionIndex = u32();

            break;

        case 0x11:

            ins.name = "call_indirect";
            ins.typeIndex = u32();
            ins.tableIndex = u32();

            break;

        case 0x1a:
            ins.name = "drop";
            break;

        case 0x1b:
            ins.name = "select";
            break;

        case 0x20:
            ins.name = "local.get";
            ins.local = u32();
            break;

        case 0x21:
            ins.name = "local.set";
            ins.local = u32();
            break;

        case 0x22:
            ins.name = "local.tee";
            ins.local = u32();
            break;

        case 0x23:
            ins.name = "global.get";
            ins.global = u32();
            break;

        case 0x24:
            ins.name = "global.set";
            ins.global = u32();
            break;

        // loads
        case 0x28:
            ins.name = "i32.load";
            skipMemArg();
            break;

        case 0x29:
            ins.name = "i64.load";
            skipMemArg();
            break;

        case 0x2a:
            ins.name = "f32.load";
            skipMemArg();
            break;

        case 0x2b:
            ins.name = "f64.load";
            skipMemArg();
            break;

        case 0x2c:
            ins.name = "i32.load8_s";
            skipMemArg();
            break;

        case 0x2d:
            ins.name = "i32.load8_u";
            skipMemArg();
            break;

        case 0x2e:
            ins.name = "i32.load16_s";
            skipMemArg();
            break;

        case 0x2f:
            ins.name = "i32.load16_u";
            skipMemArg();
            break;

        case 0x30:
            ins.name = "i64.load8_s";
            skipMemArg();
            break;

        case 0x31:
            ins.name = "i64.load8_u";
            skipMemArg();
            break;

        case 0x32:
            ins.name = "i64.load16_s";
            skipMemArg();
            break;

        case 0x33:
            ins.name = "i64.load16_u";
            skipMemArg();
            break;

        case 0x34:
            ins.name = "i64.load32_s";
            skipMemArg();
            break;

        case 0x35:
            ins.name = "i64.load32_u";
            skipMemArg();
            break;

        // stores
        case 0x36:
            ins.name = "i32.store";
            skipMemArg();
            break;

        case 0x37:
            ins.name = "i64.store";
            skipMemArg();
            break;

        case 0x38:
            ins.name = "f32.store";
            skipMemArg();
            break;

        case 0x39:
            ins.name = "f64.store";

        case 0x3a:
            ins.name = "i32.store8";
            skipMemArg();
            break;

        case 0x3b:
            ins.name = "i32.store16";
            skipMemArg();
            break;

        case 0x3c:
            ins.name = "i64.store8";
            skipMemArg();
            break;

        case 0x3d:
            ins.name = "i64.store16";
            skipMemArg();
            break;

        case 0x3e:
            ins.name = "i64.store32";
            skipMemArg();
            break;

        case 0x3f:
            ins.name = "memory.size";
            u32();
            break;

        case 0x40:
            ins.name = "memory.grow";
            u32();
            break;

        // constants
        case 0x41:
            ins.name = "i32.const";
            ins.value = i32();
            break;

        case 0x42:

            ins.name = "i64.const";

            while (true) {

                const b = u8();

                if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
                    break;
            }

            break;

        case 0x43:
            ins.name = "f32.const";
            p += 4;
            break;

        case 0x44:
            ins.name = "f64.const";
            p += 8;
            break;

        // comparisons
        case 0x45:
            ins.name = "i32.eqz";
            break;

        case 0x46:
            ins.name = "i32.eq";
            break;

        case 0x47:
            ins.name = "i32.ne";
            break;

        case 0x48:
            ins.name = "i32.lt_s";
            break;

        case 0x49:
            ins.name = "i32.lt_u";
            break;

        case 0x4a:
            ins.name = "i32.gt_s";
            break;

        case 0x4b:
            ins.name = "i32.gt_u";

            ins.name = "i32.le_s";

            ins.name = "i32.ge_s";

            ins.name = "i32.popcnt";

            ins.name = "i32.div_u";

            ins.name = "i32.rem_s";

            ins.name = "i32.rem_u";

            ins.name = "i32.and";

            ins.name = "i64.rem_s";

        case 0x86:

        case 0x88:
            ins.name = "i64.shr_u";

            ins.name = "i32.wrap_i64";
            break;

        case 0xac:
            ins.name = "i64.extend_i32_s";
            break;

        case 0xad:
            ins.name = "i64.extend_i32_u";
            break;

        case 0xd0:
            ins.name = "ref.null";
            u8();
            break;

        case 0xd2:
            ins.name = "ref.func";
            ins.functionIndex = u32();
            break;

        case 0xfc: {

            ins.name = "prefix_fc";
            ins.subopcode = u32();

            if (
                ins.subopcode === 8 ||
                ins.subopcode === 9
            ) {
                u32();
                u32();
            }

            else if (
                ins.subopcode >= 10 &&
                ins.subopcode <= 17
            ) {
                u32();
            }

            break;
        }

        default:

            ins.unknown = true;

            break;
    }

    ins.end = p;

    return ins;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// BODY SCAN
// ------------------------------------------------------------

p = codeSection.payloadStart;

const bodyCount = u32();

if (bodyCount !== definedCount)
    throw new Error(
        `BODY COUNT ${bodyCount} != DEFINED COUNT ${definedCount}`
    );

const directCalls = [];
const targetContexts = [];

let decoderFailures = 0;

for (let bodyIndex = 0; bodyIndex < bodyCount; bodyIndex++) {

    const bodySize = u32();

    const bodyStart = p;
    const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bodySize;

    const functionIndex =
        importedFunctions + bodyIndex;

    const localGroups = u32();

    let declaredLocals = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < localGroups; i++) {

        declaredLocals += u32();
        u8();
    }

    const instructions = [];

    while (p < bodyEnd) {

        const insStart = p;

        const ins = decodeInstruction(insStart);

        if (ins.unknown) {

            decoderFailures++;

            p = bodyEnd;
            break;
        }

        instructions.push(ins);

        if (
            ins.op === 0x10 &&
            ins.functionIndex === TARGET
        ) {

            directCalls.push({
                caller: functionIndex,
                bodyIndex,
                address: ins.start,
                instructions: instructions.slice(
                    Math.max(0, instructions.length - 13),
                    instructions.length
                )
            });
        }

        if (
            ins.op === 0x0b &&
            p === bodyEnd
        ) {
            break;
        }
    }

    p = bodyEnd;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// RESULT
// ------------------------------------------------------------

console.log("\n============================================================");
console.log("RESULT");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log(
    "DIRECT CALLS TO 8952 :",
    directCalls.length
);

console.log(
    "DECODER FAILURES     :",
    decoderFailures
);

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (let i = 0; i < directCalls.length; i++) {

    const c = directCalls[i];

    console.log("");
    console.log(
        `CALLER #${i + 1}`
    );

    console.log(
        `FUNCTION : ${c.caller}`
    );

    console.log(
        `TYPE     : ${getType(c.caller)}`
    );

    console.log(
        `BODY     : ${c.bodyIndex}`
    );

    console.log(
        `CALL     : ${hex(c.address)}`
    );

    console.log(
        "CONTEXT:"
    );

    for (const ins of c.instructions) {

        let extra = "";

        if (ins.local !== undefined)
            extra = ` local=${ins.local}`;

        if (ins.functionIndex !== undefined)
            extra += ` func=${ins.functionIndex}`;

        if (ins.typeIndex !== undefined)
            extra +=
                ` type=${ins.typeIndex} table=${ins.tableIndex}`;

        if (ins.value !== undefined)
            extra += ` value=${ins.value}`;

        console.log(
            `  ${hex(ins.start)} ${ins.name}${extra}`
        );
    }
}

console.log("============================================================");

const fs = require("fs");
const wabtFactory = require("wabt");

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET = 96;

async function main() {

  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log("CALLERS OF FUNCTION 96");
  console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
  console.log("============================================================");

  const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

  console.log(
    `WASM SIZE : ${wasm.length.toLocaleString()}`
  );

  console.log(
    `TARGET    : call ${TARGET}`
  );

  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

  const wabt = await wabtFactory();

  const module = wabt.readWasm(
    wasm,
    {
      readDebugNames: true
    }
  );

  module.generateNames();
  module.applyNames();

  const wat = module.toText({
    foldExprs: false,
    inlineExport: false,
    inlineImports: false,
    showLabels: true
  });

  const lines = wat.split(/\r?\n/);

  let currentFunc = null;
  let total = 0;

  for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {

    const line = lines[i].trim();

    const funcMatch =
      line.match(/^\(func\s+(\$[^\s]+)?/);

    if (funcMatch) {
      currentFunc = funcMatch[1] || "<unnamed>";
    }

    const callMatch =
      line.match(/\bcall\s+(\$?)(\d+)\b/);

    if (callMatch) {

      const target =
        Number(callMatch[2]);

      if (target === TARGET) {

        total++;

        console.log("");
        console.log(`HIT #${total}`);
        console.log(`CALLER : ${currentFunc}`);
        console.log(`WAT    : ${line}`);
      }
    }
  }

  console.log("");
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
  console.log(`TOTAL CALLS TO 96 : ${total}`);
  console.log("============================================================");

  module.destroy();
}

main().catch(err => {

  console.error("");
  console.error("ERROR:");
  console.error(err.message);

  process.exit(1);
});

const fs = require("fs");

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET = 96;

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

function readULEB(buf, p) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (p < buf.length) {
    const b = buf[p++];
    result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return { value: result >>> 0, next: p };
    }

    shift += 7;
    if (shift > 35) return null;
  }

  return null;
}

function readSLEB(buf, p) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let b;

  while (p < buf.length) {
    b = buf[p++];

    result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
        result |= (~0 << shift);
      }
      return { value: result | 0, next: p };
    }

    if (shift > 35) return null;
  }

  return null;
}

function skipMemarg(buf, p) {
  let r = readULEB(buf, p);
  if (!r) return null;

  r = readULEB(buf, r.next);
  if (!r) return null;

  return r.next;
}

function skipInstruction(buf, p) {
  const op = buf[p++];

  if (op === undefined) return null;

  // control
  if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04) {
    if (p >= buf.length) return null;
    p++; // blocktype
    return p;
  }

  if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
    return readULEB(buf, p)?.next ?? null;
  }

  if (op === 0x0e) {
    let r = readULEB(buf, p);
    if (!r) return null;

    p = r.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < r.value; i++) {
      r = readULEB(buf, p);
      if (!r) return null;
      p = r.next;
    }

    r = readULEB(buf, p);
    return r ? r.next : null;
  }

  if (op === 0x10) {
    const r = readULEB(buf, p);
    if (!r) return null;

    if (r.value === TARGET) {
      return { next: r.next, call96: true };
    }

    return r.next;
  }

  if (op === 0x11) {
    let r = readULEB(buf, p);
    if (!r) return null;

    r = readULEB(buf, r.next);
    return r ? r.next : null;
  }

  // br_table
  if (op === 0x0e) {
    return null;
  }

  // constants
  if (op === 0x41) return readSLEB(buf, p)?.next ?? null;
  if (op === 0x42) {
    let r = readSLEB(buf, p);
    if (!r) return null;

    while (r.next < buf.length) {
      // i64.const can require up to 10 bytes;
      // use generic continuation handling
      if ((buf[r.next - 1] & 0x80) === 0) break;
      r = readSLEB(buf, r.next);
      if (!r) return null;
    }

    return r.next;
  }

  if (op === 0x43) return Math.min(p + 4, buf.length);
  if (op === 0x44) return Math.min(p + 8, buf.length);

  // local/global/table
  if (
    op === 0x20 ||
    op === 0x21 ||
    op === 0x22 ||
    op === 0x23 ||
    op === 0x24 ||
    op === 0xd2
  ) {
    return readULEB(buf, p)?.next ?? null;
  }

  // memory load/store
  if (
    (op >= 0x28 && op <= 0x3e)
  ) {
    return skipMemarg(buf, p);
  }

  // memory.size / memory.grow
  if (op === 0x3f || op === 0x40) {
    return readULEB(buf, p)?.next ?? null;
  }

  // reference instructions
  if (op === 0xd0) {
    if (p >= buf.length) return null;
    return p + 1;
  }

  if (op === 0xd1) return p;

  // 0xfc prefix: bulk memory / saturating conversions
  if (op === 0xfc) {
    const r = readULEB(buf, p);
    if (!r) return null;

    const sub = r.value;
    p = r.next;

    // memory.init
    if (sub === 8) {
      let x = readULEB(buf, p);
      if (!x) return null;
      x = readULEB(buf, x.next);
      return x ? x.next : null;
    }

    // data.drop
    if (sub === 9) {
      return readULEB(buf, p)?.next ?? null;
    }

    // memory.copy
    if (sub === 10) {
      let x = readULEB(buf, p);
      if (!x) return null;
      x = readULEB(buf, x.next);
      return x ? x.next : null;
    }

    // memory.fill
    if (sub === 11) {
      return readULEB(buf, p)?.next ?? null;
    }

    // table.init
    if (sub === 12) {
      let x = readULEB(buf, p);
      if (!x) return null;
      x = readULEB(buf, x.next);
      return x ? x.next : null;
    }

    // elem.drop
    if (sub === 13) {
      return readULEB(buf, p)?.next ?? null;
    }

    // table.copy
    if (sub === 14) {
      let x = readULEB(buf, p);
      if (!x) return null;
      x = readULEB(buf, x.next);
      return x ? x.next : null;
    }

    // table.grow/fill/size
    if (sub === 15 || sub === 16 || sub === 17) {
      return readULEB(buf, p)?.next ?? null;
    }

    return p;
  }

  // 0xfd prefix: SIMD
  if (op === 0xfd) {
    const r = readULEB(buf, p);
    if (!r) return null;

    p = r.next;

    // Most SIMD instructions have immediate widths.
    // For call scanning, skip conservatively.
    const sub = r.value;

    if (
      (sub >= 0 && sub <= 11) ||
      sub === 92 ||
      sub === 93 ||
      sub === 94 ||
      sub === 95 ||
      sub === 96 ||
      sub === 97 ||
      sub === 98 ||
      sub === 99 ||
      sub === 100 ||
      sub === 101 ||
      sub === 102 ||
      sub === 103 ||
      sub === 104 ||
      sub === 105 ||
      sub === 106 ||
      sub === 107 ||
      sub === 108 ||
      sub === 109 ||
      sub === 110 ||
      sub === 111 ||
      sub === 112 ||
      sub === 113 ||
      sub === 114 ||
      sub === 115 ||
      sub === 116 ||
      sub === 117 ||
      sub === 118 ||
      sub === 119 ||
      sub === 120 ||
      sub === 121 ||
      sub === 122 ||
      sub === 123 ||
      sub === 124 ||
      sub === 125 ||
      sub === 126 ||
      sub === 127 ||
      sub === 128 ||
      sub === 129 ||
      sub === 130 ||
      sub === 131 ||
      sub === 132 ||
      sub === 133 ||
      sub === 134 ||
      sub === 135 ||
      sub === 136 ||
      sub === 137 ||
      sub === 138 ||
      sub === 139 ||
      sub === 140 ||
      sub === 141 ||
      sub === 142 ||

      sub === 146 ||

      sub === 149 ||
      sub === 150 ||
      sub === 151 ||
      sub === 152 ||
      sub === 153 ||
      sub === 154 ||
      sub === 155 ||
      sub === 156 ||
      sub === 157 ||
      sub === 158 ||
      sub === 159 ||
      sub === 160 ||
      sub === 161 ||
      sub === 162 ||
      sub === 163 ||
      sub === 164 ||
      sub === 165 ||
      sub === 166 ||
      sub === 167 ||
      sub === 168 ||
      sub === 169 ||
      sub === 170 ||
      sub === 171 ||
      sub === 172 ||
      sub === 173 ||
      sub === 174 ||
      sub === 175 ||
      sub === 176 ||
      sub === 177 ||
      sub === 178 ||
      sub === 179 ||
      sub === 180 ||
      sub === 181 ||
      sub === 182 ||
      sub === 183 ||
      sub === 184 ||
      sub === 185 ||
      sub === 186 ||
      sub === 187 ||
      sub === 188 ||
      sub === 189 ||
      sub === 190 ||
      sub === 191 ||
      sub === 192 ||
      sub === 193 ||
      sub === 194 ||
      sub === 195 ||
      sub === 196 ||
      sub === 197 ||
      sub === 198 ||
      sub === 199 ||
      sub === 200 ||
      sub === 201 ||
      sub === 202 ||
      sub === 203 ||
      sub === 204 ||
      sub === 205 ||
      sub === 206 ||
      sub === 207 ||
      sub === 208 ||
      sub === 209 ||
      sub === 210 ||
      sub === 211 ||
      sub === 212 ||
      sub === 213 ||
      sub === 214 ||
      sub === 215 ||
      sub === 216 ||
      sub === 217 ||
      sub === 218 ||
      sub === 219 ||
      sub === 220 ||
      sub === 221 ||
      sub === 222 ||
      sub === 223 ||
      sub === 224 ||
      sub === 225 ||
      sub === 226 ||
      sub === 227 ||
      sub === 228 ||
      sub === 229 ||
      sub === 230 ||
      sub === 231 ||
      sub === 232 ||
      sub === 233 ||
      sub === 234 ||
      sub === 235 ||
      sub === 236 ||
      sub === 237 ||
      sub === 238 ||
      sub === 239 ||
      sub === 240 ||
      sub === 241 ||
      sub === 242 ||
      sub === 243 ||
      sub === 244 ||
      sub === 245 ||
      sub === 246 ||
      sub === 247 ||
      sub === 248 ||
      sub === 249 ||
      sub === 250 ||
      sub === 251 ||
      sub === 252 ||
      sub === 253 ||
      sub === 254 ||
      sub === 255
    ) {
      // Conservative fallback: SIMD immediates are not calls.
      // Advance minimally; function boundaries prevent false call matches.
      return p;
    }

    return p;
  }

  // 0xfe prefix: atomics / threads
  if (op === 0xfe) {
    const r = readULEB(buf, p);
    if (!r) return null;

    p = r.next;

    // Most 0xfe instructions use memarg.
    // All are non-call instructions.
    return skipMemarg(buf, p);
  }

  // 0x1c select_t
  if (op === 0x1c) {
    const r = readULEB(buf, p);
    if (!r) return null;

    p = r.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < r.value; i++) {
      if (p >= buf.length) return null;
      p++;
    }

    return p;
  }

  // Everything else with no immediate.
  return p;
}

function parseCodeSection() {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const sectionId = wasm[p++];

    const sr = readULEB(wasm, p);
    if (!sr) throw new Error("bad section size");

    const sectionSize = sr.value;
    p = sr.next;

    const sectionEnd = p + sectionSize;

    if (sectionId === 10) {
      const countR = readULEB(wasm, p);
      if (!countR) throw new Error("bad code count");

      const count = countR.value;
      p = countR.next;

      const hits = [];

      for (let fi = 0; fi < count; fi++) {
        const bodySizeR = readULEB(wasm, p);
        if (!bodySizeR) throw new Error("bad body size");

        const bodySize = bodySizeR.value;
        p = bodySizeR.next;

        const bodyStart = p;
        const bodyEnd = p + bodySize;

        const localR = readULEB(wasm, p);
        if (!localR) throw new Error("bad locals");

        const localCount = localR.value;
        p = localR.next;

        for (let i = 0; i < localCount; i++) {
          const n = readULEB(wasm, p);
          if (!n) throw new Error("bad local count");

          p = n.next + 1;
        }

        while (p < bodyEnd) {
          const r = skipInstruction(wasm, p);

          if (!r) {
            // stop this body rather than inventing instructions
            break;
          }

          if (typeof r === "object" && r.call96) {
            hits.push({
              functionIndex: fi,
              offset: p,
              bodyStart,
              bodyEnd
            });

            p = r.next;
          } else {
            p = r;
          }
        }

        p = bodyEnd;
      }

      return hits;
    }

    p = sectionEnd;
  }

  throw new Error("Code section not found");
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("EXACT CALLERS OF FUNCTION 96");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("WASM SIZE :", wasm.length.toLocaleString());
console.log("TARGET    : call 96");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

try {
  const hits = parseCodeSection();

  for (let i = 0; i < hits.length; i++) {
    const h = hits[i];

    console.log("");
    console.log(`HIT #${i + 1}`);
    console.log(`FUNCTION  : ${h.functionIndex}`);
    console.log(
      `OFFSET    : 0x${h.offset.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
    );
    console.log(
      `BODY      : 0x${h.bodyStart.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} .. 0x${h.bodyEnd
        .toString(16)
        .padStart(8, "0")}`
    );
  }

  console.log("");
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
  console.log("TOTAL EXACT CALLS TO 96 :", hits.length);
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
} catch (e) {
  console.log("");
  console.log("ERROR:");
  console.log(e.message);
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\findCallers96Safe.js
====================================================================================================
const fs = require("fs");

const WASM =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET = 96;
const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function uleb(p, end) {
  let v = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  for (let i = 0; i < 5 && p < end; i++) {
    const b = buf[p++];
    v += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if (!(b & 0x80)) {
      return { value: v, next: p };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }

  return null;
}

function sleb(p, end, maxBytes = 10) {
  let v = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  for (let i = 0; i < maxBytes && p < end; i++) {
    const b = buf[p++];

    v += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;
    shift += 7;

    if (!(b & 0x80)) {
      return { next: p };
    }
  }

  return null;
}

function memarg(p, end) {
  let a = uleb(p, end);
  if (!a) return null;

  return uleb(a.next, end);
}

function prefixed(p, end, prefix) {
  const r = uleb(p, end);
  if (!r) return null;

  p = r.next;
  const sub = r.value;

  /*
   * FE = threads/atomics.
   * Most instructions use memarg.
   * 0xfe 0x03 / 0xfe 0x04 etc. are special forms,
   * but none can be a direct call.
   */
  if (prefix === 0xfe) {
    if (sub === 0x03 || sub === 0x04) {
      return p < end ? p + 1 : null;
    }

    return memarg(p, end)?.next ?? null;
  }

  /*
   * FC = bulk memory / table / saturating conversions.
   */
  if (prefix === 0xfc) {
    if (sub <= 7) {
      return p;
    }

    if (sub === 8) {
      let a = uleb(p, end);
      if (!a) return null;
      a = uleb(a.next, end);
      return a?.next ?? null;
    }

    if (sub === 9) {
      return uleb(p, end)?.next ?? null;
    }

    if (sub === 10) {
      let a = uleb(p, end);
      if (!a) return null;
      a = uleb(a.next, end);
      return a?.next ?? null;
    }

    if (sub === 11) {
      return uleb(p, end)?.next ?? null;
    }

    if (sub === 12) {
      let a = uleb(p, end);
      if (!a) return null;
      a = uleb(a.next, end);
      return a?.next ?? null;
    }

    if (sub === 13) {
      return uleb(p, end)?.next ?? null;
    }

    if (sub === 14) {
      let a = uleb(p, end);
      if (!a) return null;
      a = uleb(a.next, end);
      return a?.next ?? null;
    }

    if (sub === 15 || sub === 16 || sub === 17) {
      return uleb(p, end)?.next ?? null;
    }

    return p;
  }

  /*
   * FD SIMD.
   *
   * We deliberately mark these as unknown rather than
   * guessing their immediate width.
   */
  if (prefix === 0xfd) {
    return null;
  }

  return null;
}

function nextInstruction(p, end) {
  if (p >= end) return null;

  const start = p;
  const op = buf[p++];

  // control
  if (op === 0x00) return { next: p };
  if (op === 0x01) return { next: p };

  if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04) {
    if (p >= end) return null;
    return { next: p + 1 };
  }

  if (op === 0x05) return { next: p };

  if (op === 0x0b) return { next: p, end: true };

  if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
    const r = uleb(p, end);
    return r ? { next: r.next } : null;
  }

  if (op === 0x0e) {
    let r = uleb(p, end);
    if (!r) return null;

    p = r.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < r.value; i++) {
      r = uleb(p, end);
      if (!r) return null;
      p = r.next;
    }

    r = uleb(p, end);
    return r ? { next: r.next } : null;
  }

  if (op === 0x0f) return { next: p };

  /*
   * call
   */
  if (op === 0x10) {
    const r = uleb(p, end);
    if (!r) return null;

    return {
      next: r.next,
      call: r.value,
      offset: start
    };
  }

  /*
   * call_indirect
   */
  if (op === 0x11) {
    let r = uleb(p, end);
    if (!r) return null;

    r = uleb(r.next, end);
    if (!r) return null;

    return { next: r.next };
  }

  /*
   * return_call / return_call_indirect
   */
  if (op === 0x12 || op === 0x13) {
    const r = uleb(p, end);
    if (!r) return null;

    if (op === 0x13) {
      const q = uleb(r.next, end);
      if (!q) return null;
      return { next: q.next };
    }

    return { next: r.next };
  }

  // drop / select / typed select
  if (op === 0x1a || op === 0x1b) {
    return { next: p };
  }

  if (op === 0x1c) {
    const r = uleb(p, end);
    if (!r) return null;

    p = r.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < r.value; i++) {
      if (p >= end) return null;
      p++;
    }

    return { next: p };
  }

  // locals / globals
  if (
    op === 0x20 ||
    op === 0x21 ||
    op === 0x22 ||
    op === 0x23 ||
    op === 0x24
  ) {
    const r = uleb(p, end);
    return r ? { next: r.next } : null;
  }

  // memory
  if (op >= 0x28 && op <= 0x3e) {
    const r = memarg(p, end);
    return r ? { next: r.next } : null;
  }

  // memory.size / grow
  if (op === 0x3f || op === 0x40) {
    const r = uleb(p, end);
    return r ? { next: r.next } : null;
  }

  // i32.const
  if (op === 0x41) {
    const r = sleb(p, end, 5);
    return r ? { next: r.next } : null;
  }

  // i64.const
  if (op === 0x42) {
    const r = sleb(p, end, 10);
    return r ? { next: r.next } : null;
  }

  // f32.const
  if (op === 0x43) {
    return p + 4 <= end ? { next: p + 4 } : null;
  }

  // f64.const
  if (op === 0x44) {
    return p + 8 <= end ? { next: p + 8 } : null;
  }

  // reference
  if (op === 0xd0) {
    const r = uleb(p, end);
    return r ? { next: r.next } : null;
  }

  if (op === 0xd1) return { next: p };

  // prefixed opcodes
  if (op === 0xfc || op === 0xfd || op === 0xfe) {
    const n = prefixed(p, end, op);

    if (n === null) return null;

    return { next: n };
  }

  // ordinary one-byte instruction
  return { next: p };
}

function getCodeSection() {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < buf.length) {
    const id = buf[p++];

    const size = uleb(p, buf.length);
    if (!size) throw new Error("Invalid section size");

    p = size.next;

    const end = p + size.value;

    if (id === 10) {
      return { start: p, end };
    }

    p = end;
  }

  throw new Error("Code section not found");
}

function scanFunction(start, end) {
  const queue = [start];
  const seen = new Set();
  const hits = [];

  while (queue.length) {
    const p = queue.pop();

    if (p < start || p >= end || seen.has(p)) {
      continue;
    }

    seen.add(p);

    const ins = nextInstruction(p, end);

    if (!ins) {
      continue;
    }

    if (ins.call === TARGET) {
      hits.push({
        offset: ins.offset,
        next: ins.next
      });
    }

    if (ins.next > p && ins.next <= end) {
      queue.push(ins.next);
    }
  }

  return {
    hits,
    coverage: seen.size
  };
}

function parse() {
  const code = getCodeSection();

  let p = code.start;

  const count = uleb(p, code.end);
  if (!count) throw new Error("Invalid function count");

  p = count.next;

  const all = [];

  for (let f = 0; f < count.value; f++) {
    const bodySize = uleb(p, code.end);
    if (!bodySize) throw new Error("Invalid body size");

    p = bodySize.next;

    const bodyStart = p;
    const bodyEnd = p + bodySize.value;

    /*
     * locals
     */
    const localGroups = uleb(p, bodyEnd);
    if (!localGroups) {
      p = bodyEnd;
      continue;
    }

    p = localGroups.next;

    let localsOK = true;

    for (let i = 0; i < localGroups.value; i++) {
      const n = uleb(p, bodyEnd);

      if (!n || n.next >= bodyEnd) {
        localsOK = false;
        break;
      }

      p = n.next + 1;
    }

    if (!localsOK) {
      p = bodyEnd;
      continue;
    }

    const result = scanFunction(p, bodyEnd);

    if (result.hits.length) {
      all.push({
        functionIndex: f,
        bodyStart,
        bodyEnd,
        coverage: result.coverage,
        hits: result.hits
      });
    }

    p = bodyEnd;
  }

  return all;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SAFE CALLERS OF FUNCTION 96");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("WASM SIZE :", buf.length.toLocaleString());
console.log("TARGET    : call 96");
console.log("METHOD    : BOUNDED MULTI-PATH DECODER");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

try {
  const results = parse();

  for (const r of results) {
    console.log("");
    console.log("FUNCTION :", r.functionIndex);
    console.log(
      "BODY     : 0x" +
        r.bodyStart.toString(16).padStart(8, "0") +
        " .. 0x" +
        r.bodyEnd.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")
    );
    console.log("COVERAGE :", r.coverage, "bytes");

    for (const h of r.hits) {
      console.log(
        "CALL 96  : 0x" +
          h.offset.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")
      );
    }
  }

  console.log("");
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
  console.log("CONFIRMED FUNCTIONS :", results.length);
  console.log(
    "CONFIRMED CALLS     :",
    results.reduce((n, x) => n + x.hits.length, 0)
  );
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
  console.log("");
  console.log("NOTE: Unknown instruction paths are discarded.");
  console.log("      No speculative CALL 96 is reported.");
} catch (e) {
  console.log("");
  console.log("ERROR:");
  console.log(e.stack || e.message);
}

import fs from "fs";

const buf = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGET_GLOBAL = 113;
const IMPORTED = 83;
const TARGET_ORDINAL = TARGET_GLOBAL - IMPORTED;

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[pos++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value: value >>> 0, next: pos };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function hex(n: number): string {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

//
// Locate sections
//
const sections: {
    id: number;
    start: number;
    end: number;
}[] = [];

let p = 8;

while (p < buf.length) {

    const id = buf[p++];

    const s = readULEB(p);
    p = s.next;

    sections.push({
        id,
        start: p,
        end: p + s.value
    });

    p += s.value;
}

const codeSec = sections.find(x => x.id === 10);

if (!codeSec)
    throw new Error("CODE SECTION NOT FOUND");

let c = codeSec.start;

const count = readULEB(c);
c = count.next;

const callers: {
    callerOrdinal: number;
    callerGlobal: number;
    callOffset: number;
    bodyStart: number;
    bodyEnd: number;
}[] = [];

for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < count.value; ordinal++) {

    const bodySize = readULEB(c);
    c = bodySize.next;

    const start = c;
    const end = c + bodySize.value;

    let x = start;

    //
    // Skip local declarations
    //
    const localGroups = readULEB(x);
    x = localGroups.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < localGroups.value; i++) {
        const n = readULEB(x);
        x = n.next;
        x++;
    }

    //
    // Scan instructions
    //
    while (x < end) {

        const pos = x;
        const op = buf[x++];

        if (op === 0x0b) {
            break;
        }

        //
        // direct call
        //
        if (op === 0x10) {

            const r = readULEB(x);
            x = r.next;

            if (r.value === TARGET_GLOBAL) {

                callers.push({
                    callerOrdinal: ordinal,
                    callerGlobal: ordinal + IMPORTED,
                    callOffset: pos,
                    bodyStart: start,
                    bodyEnd: end
                });
            }

            continue;
        }

        //
        // call_indirect
        //
        if (op === 0x11) {

            const r = readULEB(x);
            x = r.next;

            const table = readULEB(x);
            x = table.next;

            continue;
        }

        //
        // locals
        //
        if (
            op === 0x20 ||
            op === 0x21 ||
            op === 0x22 ||
            op === 0x23 ||
            op === 0x24
        ) {
            const r = readULEB(x);
            x = r.next;
            continue;
        }

        //
        // memory loads/stores
        //
        if (
            op === 0x28 ||
            op === 0x29 ||
            op === 0x2a ||
            op === 0x2b ||
            op === 0x2c ||
            op === 0x2d ||
            op === 0x2e ||
            op === 0x2f ||
            op === 0x30 ||
            op === 0x31 ||
            op === 0x32 ||
            op === 0x33 ||
            op === 0x34 ||
            op === 0x35 ||
            op === 0x36 ||
            op === 0x37 ||
            op === 0x38 ||
            op === 0x39 ||
            op === 0x3a ||
            op === 0x3b
        ) {
            const a = readULEB(x);
            x = a.next;

            const o = readULEB(x);
            x = o.next;

            continue;
        }

        //
        // i32.const
        //
        if (op === 0x41) {
            const r = readULEB(x);
            x = r.next;
            continue;
        }

        //
        // i64.const
        //
        if (op === 0x42) {
            const r = readULEB(x);
            x = r.next;
            continue;
        }

        //
        // block / loop / if
        //
        if (
            op === 0x02 ||
            op === 0x03 ||
            op === 0x04
        ) {
            x++;
            continue;
        }

        //
        // br / br_if
        //
        if (
            op === 0x0c ||
            op === 0x0d
        ) {
            const r = readULEB(x);
            x = r.next;
            continue;
        }

        //
        // memory.size / memory.grow
        //
        if (op === 0x3f || op === 0x40) {
            x++;
            continue;
        }

        //
        // prefixed instructions
        //
        if (op === 0xfc) {

            const r = readULEB(x);
            x = r.next;

            //
            // For this caller scan, skip the immediate payload conservatively.
            //
            if (
                r.value === 8 ||
                r.value === 9 ||
                r.value === 10 ||
                r.value === 11
            ) {
                const q = readULEB(x);
                x = q.next;
            }

            continue;
        }
    }

    c = end;
}

        `CALL @${hex(item.callOffset)} | ` +
        `BODY ${hex(item.bodyStart)}..${hex(item.bodyEnd)}`
    );
}

console.log(`TOTAL CALLERS = ${callers.length}`);

console.log("NO WASM EXECUTION");
console.log("============================================================");

            continue;

            x = r.next;

            x = r.next;
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0xfc) {
            const r = readULEB(x);
            x = r.next;
            continue;
        }

import * as fs from "fs";

const file =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(file);

const IMPORTS = 83;
const TARGET = 5079;

function uleb(p: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];

    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;

    if (!(x & 0x80))
      return { v, p };

    s += 7;
  }
}

let p = 8;
let codeStart = -1;

while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p++];

  const z = uleb(p);
  p = z.p;

  const start = p;
  const end = p + z.v;

  if (id === 10)
    codeStart = start;

  p = end;
}

p = codeStart;

const count = uleb(p);
p = count.p;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("CALLERS OF GLOBAL FUNCTION 5079 v41");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log(`Target global function : ${TARGET}`);
console.log(`Imported functions     : ${IMPORTS}`);
console.log(`Code bodies            : ${count.v}`);
console.log("");

let hits = 0;

for (let body = 0; body < count.v; body++) {

  const size = uleb(p);
  p = size.p;

  const start = p;
  const end = p + size.v;

  let q = start;

  // Skip local declarations
  const groups = uleb(q);
  q = groups.p;

  for (let i = 0; i < groups.v; i++) {
    const n = uleb(q);
    q = n.p;

    q += 1; // valtype
  }

  while (q < end) {

    const off = q;
    const op = b[q++];

    // CALL
    if (op === 0x10) {

      const x = uleb(q);
      q = x.p;

      if (x.v === TARGET) {

        hits++;

        const globalFunction =
          body + IMPORTS;

        console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
        console.log(`HIT #${hits}`);

        console.log(
          `Body ordinal       : ${body}`
        );

        console.log(
          `Global function    : ${globalFunction}`
        );

        console.log(
          `CALL address       : 0x${off.toString(16)}`
        );

        console.log(
          `Body start         : 0x${start.toString(16)}`
        );

        console.log(
          `Body end           : 0x${end.toString(16)}`
        );

        console.log("");
      }

      continue;
    }

    // local/global indexes
    if (
      op === 0x20 ||
      op === 0x21 ||
      op === 0x22 ||
      op === 0x23 ||
      op === 0x24 ||
      op === 0x0c ||
      op === 0x0d
    ) {
      const x = uleb(q);
      q = x.p;
      continue;
    }

    // i32/i64 constants
    if (op === 0x41 || op === 0x42) {
      const x = uleb(q);
      q = x.p;
      continue;
    }

    // memory instructions
    if (
      op === 0x28 ||
      op === 0x29 ||
      op === 0x2a ||
      op === 0x2b ||
      op === 0x2c ||
      op === 0x2d ||
      op === 0x2e ||
      op === 0x2f ||
      op === 0x36 ||
      op === 0x37 ||
      op === 0x38 ||
      op === 0x39
    ) {
      const a = uleb(q);
      q = a.p;

      const o = uleb(q);
      q = o.p;

      continue;
    }

    // block / loop / if
    if (
      op === 0x02 ||
      op === 0x03 ||
      op === 0x04
    ) {
      q += 1;
      continue;
    }

    // memory.copy
    if (op === 0xfc) {

      const sub = uleb(q);
      q = sub.p;

      if (sub.v === 10) {
        const a = uleb(q);
        q = a.p;

        const b2 = uleb(q);
        q = b2.p;
      }

      continue;
    }

    // memory.fill
    if (op === 0xfc) {
      continue;
    }
  }

  p = end;
}

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGET = 87;

function readULEB(pos: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[pos++];
    value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) return { value, next: pos };
    shift += 7;
  }
}

import * as fs from "fs";

const file =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(file);

const CODE_START = 0x00006e7d;
const CODE_END   = 0x00324ef5;

const TARGET_TYPE = 4;
const TARGET_TABLE = 0;

function uleb(p: number) {
  let v = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];

    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if (!(x & 0x80))
      return { v, p };

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function hex(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("CALL_INDIRECT TYPE 4 SEARCH");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log("Target signature : (i32,i32,i32,i32) -> i32");
console.log(`Type index       : ${TARGET_TYPE}`);
console.log(`Table index      : ${TARGET_TABLE}`);
console.log("");

let hits = 0;

for (let i = CODE_START; i < CODE_END - 2; i++) {

  if (b[i] !== 0x11)
    continue;

  try {
    const typeR = uleb(i + 1);
    const tableR = uleb(typeR.p);

    if (
      typeR.v === TARGET_TYPE &&
      tableR.v === TARGET_TABLE
    ) {
      hits++;

      console.log(
        `HIT #${hits} @ ${hex(i)}`
      );

      console.log(
        `  type=${typeR.v} table=${tableR.v}`
      );

      const from = Math.max(CODE_START, i - 32);
      const to = Math.min(CODE_END, tableR.p + 24);

      console.log(
        Array.from(b.slice(from, to))
          .map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
          .join(" ")
      );

      console.log("");
    }
  } catch {
    // ignore malformed byte sequences
  }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TOTAL TYPE-4 CALL_INDIRECT : ${hits}`);
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("END");
console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\findCallsInFunc259.ts
