import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGET = 259;

function readU32Leb(buf: Buffer, pos: number) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = buf[pos++];
    result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
    shift += 7;
  }

  return { value: result >>> 0, next: pos };
}

// Find Code section
let p = 8;
let codeStart = -1;

while (p < wasm.length) {
  const id = wasm[p++];
  const s = readU32Leb(wasm, p);
  p = s.next;

  if (id === 10) {
    codeStart = p;
    break;
  }

  p += s.value;
}

if (codeStart < 0) throw new Error("Code section not found");

// Locate function body
let q = codeStart;
const count = readU32Leb(wasm, q);
q = count.next;

let body: Buffer | null = null;

for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
  const s = readU32Leb(wasm, q);
  const size = s.value;
  q = s.next;

  if (i === TARGET) {
    body = wasm.subarray(q, q + size);
    break;
  }

  q += size;
}

if (!body) throw new Error(`FUNC ${TARGET} not found`);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 259 CALL TARGETS");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`BODY SIZE : ${body.length}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (let i = 0; i < body.length; i++) {

  // WebAssembly call opcode
  if (body[i] === 0x10) {

    const r = readU32Leb(body, i + 1);

    console.log(
      `CALL @ 0x${i.toString(16).padStart(4, "0")}  -> FUNC ${r.value}`
    );

    i = r.next - 1;
  }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\findCallsTo83_91.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[pos++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            break;
        }

        shift += 7;
    }

    return { value: value >>> 0, next: pos };
}

function hex(n: number): string {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FIND CALL REFERENCES TO FUNC 83..91");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

const TARGET_MIN = 83;
const TARGET_MAX = 91;

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Find code section
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let pos = 8;

let codeStart = -1;
let codeEnd = -1;

while (pos < wasm.length) {
    const sectionId = wasm[pos++];

    const sizeInfo = readULEB(pos);
    const size = sizeInfo.value;
    pos = sizeInfo.next;

    const payload = pos;
    const end = payload + size;

    if (sectionId === 10) {
        codeStart = payload;
        codeEnd = end;
        break;
    }

    pos = end;
}

if (codeStart < 0) {
    throw new Error("CODE SECTION NOT FOUND");
}

console.log(`CODE SECTION = ${hex(codeStart)} .. ${hex(codeEnd)}`);

let p = codeStart;

const countInfo = readULEB(p);
const definedCount = countInfo.value;
p = countInfo.next;

console.log(`DEFINED FUNCTIONS = ${definedCount}`);
console.log(`GLOBAL FUNCTION RANGE = 83 .. ${83 + definedCount - 1}`);

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

const references = new Map<number, {
    caller: number;
    ordinal: number;
    offset: number;
}[]>();

for (let target = TARGET_MIN; target <= TARGET_MAX; target++) {
    references.set(target, []);
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Scan every defined function
// ------------------------------------------------------------

for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < definedCount; ordinal++) {

    const globalFunc = 83 + ordinal;

    const bodySizeInfo = readULEB(p);
    const bodySize = bodySizeInfo.value;
    p = bodySizeInfo.next;

    const bodyStart = p;
    const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bodySize;

    let x = bodyStart;

    // locals declaration
    const localInfo = readULEB(x);
    const localGroups = localInfo.value;
    x = localInfo.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < localGroups; i++) {
        const count = readULEB(x);
        x = count.next;

        // valtype
        x++;
    }

    while (x < bodyEnd) {

        const opOffset = x;
        const op = wasm[x++];

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // direct call
        // ----------------------------------------------------
        if (op === 0x10) {

            const targetInfo = readULEB(x);
            x = targetInfo.next;

            const target = targetInfo.value;

            if (target >= TARGET_MIN && target <= TARGET_MAX) {

                references.get(target)!.push({
                    caller: globalFunc,
                    ordinal,
                    offset: opOffset
                });
            }

            continue;
        }

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // call_indirect
        // ----------------------------------------------------
        if (op === 0x11) {
            const typeInfo = readULEB(x);
            x = typeInfo.next;

            // table index / reserved byte
            x++;

            continue;
        }

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // local/global
        // ----------------------------------------------------
        if (
            op === 0x20 ||
            op === 0x21 ||
            op === 0x22 ||
            op === 0x23 ||
            op === 0x24
        ) {
            const r = readULEB(x);
            x = r.next;
            continue;
        }

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // memory load/store
        // ----------------------------------------------------
        if (op >= 0x28 && op <= 0x3e) {

            const align = readULEB(x);
            x = align.next;

            const offset = readULEB(x);
            x = offset.next;

            continue;
        }

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // memory.size / memory.grow
        // ----------------------------------------------------
        if (op === 0x3f || op === 0x40) {
            x++;
            continue;
        }

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // constants
        // ----------------------------------------------------
        if (op === 0x41) {
            const r = readULEB(x);
            x = r.next;
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x42) {
            while (true) {
                const b = wasm[x++];
                if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
            }
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x43) {
            x += 4;
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x44) {
            x += 8;
            continue;
        }

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // br / br_if
        // ----------------------------------------------------
        if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
            const r = readULEB(x);
            x = r.next;
            continue;
        }

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // br_table
        // ----------------------------------------------------
        if (op === 0x0e) {

            const countInfo = readULEB(x);
            const count = countInfo.value;
            x = countInfo.next;

            for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
                const r = readULEB(x);
                x = r.next;
            }

            const def = readULEB(x);
            x = def.next;

            continue;
        }

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // misc prefix
        // ----------------------------------------------------
        if (op === 0xfc) {

            const sub = readULEB(x);
            x = sub.next;

            // Handle common bulk-memory forms conservatively.
            if (
                sub.value === 8 ||
                sub.value === 9 ||
                sub.value === 10 ||
                sub.value === 11
            ) {
                const a = readULEB(x);
                x = a.next;

                const b = readULEB(x);
                x = b.next;
            }

            continue;
        }
    }

    p = bodyEnd;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Report
// ------------------------------------------------------------

console.log("CALL REFERENCES");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (let target = TARGET_MIN; target <= TARGET_MAX; target++) {

    const refs = references.get(target)!;

    console.log(`\nTARGET FUNC ${target}`);
    console.log(`CALLERS = ${refs.length}`);

    if (refs.length === 0) {
        console.log("  NONE");
        continue;
    }

    for (const r of refs) {
        console.log(
            `  caller FUNC ${r.caller}` +
            ` | ordinal ${r.ordinal}` +
            ` | call @ ${hex(r.offset)}`
        );
    }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("END");
console.log("NO WASM EXECUTION");
console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\findCleanupCallers.mjs
====================================================================================================

import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";

const FILE = path.resolve(
    process.cwd(),
    "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm"
);

const IMPORTS = 83;
const TARGETS = [1393, 2527, 2528, 2529];

function uleb(buf, s) {
    let n = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (s.pos < buf.length) {
        const b = buf[s.pos++];
        n += (b & 127) * 2 ** shift;

        if (!(b & 128)) return n;
        shift += 7;

        if (shift > 49) throw new Error("Invalid ULEB");
    }

    throw new Error("Unexpected EOF");
}

function encodeULEB(n) {
    const out = [];

    do {
        let b = n % 128;
        n = Math.floor(n / 128);
        if (n) b |= 128;
        out.push(b);
    } while (n);

    return out;
}

function hx(n) {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function readBodies(buf) {
    const s = { pos: 8 };

    while (s.pos < buf.length) {
        const id = buf[s.pos++];
        const size = uleb(buf, s);
        const start = s.pos;
        const end = start + size;

        if (id === 10) {
            const cs = { pos: start };
            const count = uleb(buf, cs);
            const bodies = [];

            for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
                const bodySize = uleb(buf, cs);
                const bodyStart = cs.pos;
                const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bodySize;

                bodies.push({
                    fn: IMPORTS + i,
                    start: bodyStart,
                    end: bodyEnd,
                    size: bodySize
                });

                cs.pos = bodyEnd;
            }

            return bodies;
        }

        s.pos = end;
    }

    throw new Error("Code section not found");
}

function findRaw(buf, start, end, pattern) {
    const hits = [];

    for (let p = start; p + pattern.length <= end; p++) {
        let ok = true;

        for (let j = 0; j < pattern.length; j++) {
            if (buf[p + j] !== pattern[j]) {
                ok = false;
                break;
            }
        }

        if (ok) hits.push(p);
    }

    return hits;
}

function dump(buf, start, end) {
    for (let p = start; p < end; p += 16) {
        const q = Math.min(p + 16, end);
        const bytes = [];

        for (let i = p; i < q; i++) {
            bytes.push(buf[i].toString(16).padStart(2, "0"));
        }

        console.log(`${hx(p)}  ${bytes.join(" ")}`);
    }
}

function main() {
    const buf = fs.readFileSync(FILE);
    const bodies = readBodies(buf);

    console.log("CLEANUP CALLER SEARCH");
    console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
    console.log("============================================================");

    for (const target of TARGETS) {
        const pattern = [0x10, ...encodeULEB(target)];

        console.log("");
        console.log(`TARGET FUNC ${target}`);
        console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

        let count = 0;

        for (const body of bodies) {
            const hits = findRaw(buf, body.start, body.end, pattern);

            for (const hit of hits) {
                count++;

                console.log(
                    `OWNER FUNC ${body.fn}` +
                    ` | BODY ${hx(body.start)}..${hx(body.end)}` +
                    ` | HIT ${hx(hit)}`
                );

                const start = Math.max(body.start, hit - 24);
                const end = Math.min(body.end, hit + 32);

                dump(buf, start, end);
                console.log("");
            }
        }

        console.log(`RAW HIT COUNT = ${count}`);
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log("DONE - NO WASM EXECUTION");
    console.log("Raw hits require instruction-level verification.");
}

try {
    main();
} catch (e) {
    console.error("FAILED:", e);
    process.exitCode = 1;
}

FILE: scripts\findConstOffset24Stores.js

const fs = require("fs");

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm"
);

function u32(p) {
  let v = 0;
  let s = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[p++];

    v |= (b & 0x7f) << s;

    if (!(b & 0x80))
      return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };

    s += 7;
  }
}

function hx(x) {
  return "0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function readI32Const(p) {
  if (wasm[p] !== 0x41)
    return null;

  const r = u32(p + 1);

  return {
    value: r.value,
    next: r.next
  };
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// CODE SECTION
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let p = 8;
let codeStart = 0;

while (p < wasm.length) {

  const id = wasm[p++];

  const s = u32(p);
  p = s.next;

  const start = p;
  const end = p + s.value;

  if (id === 10) {
    codeStart = start;
    break;
  }

  p = end;
}

let q = codeStart;

const count = u32(q);
q = count.next;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DIRECT CONSTANT -> OFFSET 24 STORE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`FUNCTION BODIES : ${count.value}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let hits = 0;

for (let bodyIndex = 0; bodyIndex < count.value; bodyIndex++) {

  const size = u32(q);
  q = size.next;

  const bodyStart = q;
  const bodyEnd = q + size.value;

  const func = 83 + bodyIndex;

  for (let x = bodyStart; x < bodyEnd; x++) {

    const c = readI32Const(x);

    if (!c)
      continue;

    let y = c.next;

    // i32.store
    if (wasm[y] !== 0x36)
      continue;

    // memarg alignment
    const align = wasm[y + 1];

    // memarg offset
    const off = u32(y + 2);

    if (off.value !== 24)
      continue;

    hits++;

    console.log("");
    console.log(`FUNC ${func} BODY ${bodyIndex}`);
    console.log(`CONST VALUE : ${c.value}`);
    console.log(`CONST HEX   : ${hx(c.value)}`);
    console.log(`STORE      : ${hx(y)} i32.store offset=24`);

    // show small raw neighborhood
    const a = Math.max(bodyStart, x - 16);
    const b = Math.min(bodyEnd, y + 8);

    console.log("CONTEXT:");

    for (let z = a; z < b; z++) {
      console.log(
        `  ${hx(z)} ${wasm[z].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
      );
    }
  }

  q = bodyEnd;
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`DIRECT CONST -> OFFSET24 COUNT : ${hits}`);
console.log("============================================================");

FILE: scripts\findData19534c.ts

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");
const TARGET = 0x19534c;

function uleb(pos: number) {
  let v = 0, shift = 0, p = pos;
  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const b = wasm[p++];
    v += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
    shift += 7;
  }
  return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };
}

function sleb(pos: number) {
  let v = 0, shift = 0, p = pos, b = 0;
  while (p < wasm.length) {
    b = wasm[p++];
    v |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
  }
  return { value: v | (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40) ? (-1 << shift) : 0), next: p };
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SEARCH DATA SEGMENTS FOR 0x19534C");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

let p = 8;
let seg = 0;
let found = 0;

while (p < wasm.length) {
  const id = wasm[p++];
  const s = uleb(p);
  p = s.next;

  const end = p + s.value;

  if (id !== 11) {
    p = end;
    continue;
  }

  const n = uleb(p);
  p = n.next;

  console.log(`DATA SEGMENTS : ${n.value}`);

  for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
    const flags = uleb(p);
    p = flags.next;

    let offset = 0;

    if (flags.value === 0) {
      if (wasm[p++] !== 0x41) throw new Error("unexpected offset expr");
      const x = sleb(p);
      offset = x.value >>> 0;
      p = x.next;
      if (wasm[p++] !== 0x0b) throw new Error("bad init expr");
    } else if (flags.value === 2) {
      uleb(p); // memory index
      p = uleb(p).next;

      if (wasm[p++] !== 0x41) throw new Error("unexpected offset expr");
      const x = sleb(p);
      offset = x.value >>> 0;
      p = x.next;
      if (wasm[p++] !== 0x0b) throw new Error("bad init expr");
    } else {
      // passive segment
      const len = uleb(p);
      p = len.next + len.value;
      seg++;
      continue;
    }

    const len = uleb(p);
    p = len.next;

    const dataStart = p;
    const dataEnd = p + len.value;

    if (
      TARGET >= offset &&
      TARGET < offset + len.value
    ) {
      const rel = TARGET - offset;

      console.log("");
      console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
      console.log(`SEGMENT : ${i}`);
      console.log(`START   : 0x${offset.toString(16)}`);
      console.log(`SIZE    : ${len.value}`);
      console.log(`TARGET  : 0x${TARGET.toString(16)}`);
      console.log(`OFFSET  : +0x${rel.toString(16)}`);

      const from = Math.max(0, rel - 16);
      const to = Math.min(len.value, rel + 32);

      console.log("DATA:");

      for (let j = from; j < to; j += 16) {
        const a = dataStart + j;
        const b = Math.min(dataStart + j + 16, dataEnd);

        let hex = "";
        for (let k = a; k < b; k++) {
          hex += wasm[k].toString(16).padStart(2, "0") + " ";
        }

        console.log(
          `0x${(offset + j).toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ${hex}`
        );
      }

      found++;
    }

    p = dataEnd;
  }

  break;
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TARGET DATA SEGMENTS : ${found}`);
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");

FILE: scripts\findDataAt266018.ts

import * as fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function u(p: number): [number, number] {
  let v = 0, s = 0, x = 0;
  do {
    x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;
    s += 7;
  } while (x & 0x80);
  return [v, p];
}

function section(id: number): [number, number] {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < b.length) {
    const sid = b[p++];

    let size: number;
    [size, p] = u(p);

    const start = p;
    const end = p + size;

    if (sid === id) return [start, end];

    p = end;
  }

  throw new Error("Section not found: " + id);
}

const [start, end] = section(11);
let p = start;

let count: number;
[count, p] = u(p);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FIND DATA SEGMENT COVERING 0x266018");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DATA SECTION :", "0x" + start.toString(16));
console.log("DATA END     :", "0x" + end.toString(16));
console.log("SEGMENT COUNT:", count);

for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {

  let flags: number;
  [flags, p] = u(p);

  let memidx = 0;
  let offset: number | null = null;

  if (flags === 0 || flags === 2) {

    if (flags === 2) {
      [memidx, p] = u(p);
    }

    const op = b[p++];

    if (op === 0x41) {
      [offset, p] = u(p);
    } else if (op === 0x23) {
      const [g, np] = u(p);
      p = np;
      offset = null;
      console.log(
        `SEG ${i}: global offset global${g}`
      );
    } else {
      throw new Error(
        `SEG ${i}: unsupported offset opcode 0x${op.toString(16)}`
      );
    }

    if (b[p++] !== 0x0b) {
      throw new Error(`SEG ${i}: missing offset END`);
    }
  }

  let size: number;
  [size, p] = u(p);

  const dataStart = p;

  if (
    offset !== null &&
    memidx === 0 &&
    offset <= 0x266018 &&
    offset + size > 0x266018
  ) {
    const rel = 0x266018 - offset;

    console.log("");
    console.log("******** TARGET HIT ********");
    console.log("SEGMENT     :", i);
    console.log("FLAGS       :", flags);
    console.log("OFFSET      : 0x" + offset.toString(16));
    console.log("SIZE        :", size);
    console.log("DATA START  : 0x" + dataStart.toString(16));
    console.log("TARGET REL  :", rel);

    const n = Math.min(32, size - rel);

    console.log(
      "TARGET BYTES:",
      [...b.slice(dataStart + rel, dataStart + rel + n)]
        .map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
        .join(" ")
    );
  }

  p += size;
}

console.log("");
console.log("DONE");

(async () => {
    const fs = require("fs");
    const wabt = await require("wabt")();

    const file =
        "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

    const targets = new Map([
        [1659720, "0x195348"],
        [1659724, "0x19534C"],
        [1659728, "0x195350"],
        [1659732, "0x195354"],
        [1659736, "0x195358"]
    ]);

    const features = {
        exceptions: true,
        mutable_globals: true,
        sat_float_to_int: true,
        sign_extension: true,
        simd: true,
        threads: true,
        function_references: true,
        multi_value: true,
        tail_call: true,
        bulk_memory: true,
        reference_types: true,
        annotations: true,
        code_metadata: true,
        gc: true,
        memory64: true,
        multi_memory: true,
        extended_const: true,
        relaxed_simd: true
    };

    const mod = wabt.readWasm(fs.readFileSync(file), {
        readDebugNames: false,
        ...features
    });

    mod.validate();

    const wat = mod.toText({
        foldExprs: false,
        inlineExport: false
    });

    const lines = wat.split(/\r?\n/);
    const history = [];
    let func = null;
    let stores = 0;
    let candidates = 0;

    const storePattern =
        /\b(?:i32|i64|f32|f64|v128)\.store(?:8|16|32|64)?\b/;

    function isInstruction(line) {
        const s = line.trim();
        return s !== "" &&
            !s.startsWith(";;") &&
            !s.startsWith("(") &&
            !s.startsWith(")") &&
            !s.startsWith("else") &&
            !s.startsWith("end") &&
            !s.startsWith("then") &&
            !s.startsWith("catch") &&
            !s.startsWith("delegate");
    }

    console.log("DIRECT DISPATCH STORE SCAN");
    console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
    console.log("================================================");

    for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
        const line = lines[i];

        const header = line.match(/^\s*\(func \(;(\d+);\)/);

        if (header) {
            func = Number(header[1]);
            history.length = 0;
        } else if (
            /^\s*\((?:data|elem|type|import|export|table|memory|global|start|custom|module)\b/.test(line)
        ) {
            func = null;
            history.length = 0;
            continue;
        }

        if (func === null || !isInstruction(line)) continue;

        if (storePattern.test(line)) {
            stores++;

            const recent = history.slice(-4);
            const targetHits = [];

            for (let k = 0; k < recent.length; k++) {
                const m = recent[k].line.match(
                    /\bi32\.const\s+(-?\d+)\b/
                );

                if (!m) continue;

                const value = Number(m[1]);
                if (targets.has(value)) {
                    targetHits.push({
                        value,
                        label: targets.get(value),
                        index: k,
                        line: recent[k]
                    });
                }
            }

            for (const hit of targetHits) {
                candidates++;

                const distance = recent.length - 1 - hit.index;
                const likelyDirect = distance <= 1;

                console.log("");
                console.log("-----------------------------------------------");
                console.log("TARGET:", hit.label);
                console.log("FUNCTION:", func);
                console.log("STORE WAT LINE:", i + 1);
                console.log(
                    "CLASSIFICATION:",
                    likelyDirect
                        ? "CLOSE STACK PATTERN - INSPECT"
                        : "WEAK CANDIDATE - NOT PROVEN"
                );

                console.log("PRECEDING INSTRUCTIONS:");
                for (const item of recent) {
                    console.log(
                        `${item.number}: ${item.line.trim()}`
                    );
                }

                console.log("STORE:");
                console.log(`${i + 1}: ${line.trim()}`);
            }
        }

        history.push({
            number: i + 1,
            line: line.trim()
        });

        if (history.length > 8) history.shift();
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log("================================================");
    console.log("STORE INSTRUCTIONS SCANNED:", stores);
    console.log("TARGET/STORE CANDIDATES:", candidates);
    console.log("A candidate is not proof of the effective address.");

    mod.destroy();
})().catch(error => {
    console.error(error);
    process.exitCode = 1;
});

FILE: scripts\findDispatchCallers.ts

import * as fs from "fs";

const file =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(file);

const CODE_START = 0x00006e7d;
const CODE_END   = 0x00324ef5;

const IMPORTED = 83;

const TARGETS = [
  8483,
  8509,
  8559,
  8637,
  8927,
  8952,
];

function readULEBAt(pos: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
    if (pos + i >= b.length)
      return null;

    const x = b[pos + i];

    value += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if ((x & 0x80) === 0) {
      return {
        value,
        end: pos + i + 1,
        bytes: i + 1,
      };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }

  return null;
}

function hex(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function bytes(from: number, to: number) {
  return Array.from(b.slice(from, to))
    .map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
    .join(" ");
}

type Body = {
  ordinal: number;
  global: number;
  start: number;
  end: number;
};

function getBodies(): Body[] {

  let p = CODE_START;

  const countR = readULEBAt(p);

  if (!countR)
    throw new Error("Cannot read code body count");

  p = countR.end;

  const bodies: Body[] = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < countR.value; i++) {

    const sizeR = readULEBAt(p);

    if (!sizeR)
      throw new Error(
        `Cannot read body size at ${hex(p)}`
      );

    p = sizeR.end;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + sizeR.value;

    bodies.push({
      ordinal: i,
      global: i + IMPORTED,
      start,
      end,
    });

    p = end;
  }

  if (p !== CODE_END) {

    console.log(
      `WARNING: code end mismatch ${hex(p)} != ${hex(CODE_END)}`
    );
  }

  return bodies;
}

const bodies = getBodies();

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("RAW DISPATCH CALLER SEARCH");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("");

console.log(`Bodies : ${bodies.length}`);
console.log(`Code   : ${hex(CODE_START)} .. ${hex(CODE_END)}`);
console.log("");

for (const target of TARGETS) {

  console.log("");
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
  console.log(`TARGET FUNCTION ${target}`);
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

  let hits = 0;

  for (const body of bodies) {

    for (let p = body.start; p < body.end; p++) {

      if (b[p] !== 0x10)
        continue;

      const r = readULEBAt(p + 1);

      if (!r)
        continue;

      if (r.value !== target)
        continue;

      hits++;

      console.log("");
      console.log(
        `CALLER global=${body.global} body=${body.ordinal}`
      );

      console.log(
        `CALL @ ${hex(p)}`
      );

      console.log(
        `Target : ${target}`
      );

      console.log(
        `CALL bytes : ${bytes(p, r.end)}`
      );

      const from = Math.max(body.start, p - 64);
      const to   = Math.min(body.end, r.end + 16);

      console.log(
        `Context ${hex(from)} .. ${hex(to)}`
      );

      console.log(
        bytes(from, to)
      );
    }
  }

  console.log("");
  console.log(`TOTAL RAW CALL PATTERNS = ${hits}`);
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("END");
console.log("============================================================");

FILE: scripts\findDispatcher122Selector.ts

import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET = 0x19534c;

const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function readU32(pos: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = buf[pos++];
    value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
      return { value, next: pos };

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function readS32(pos: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = buf[pos++];

    value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40))
        value -= 2 ** (shift + 7);

      return { value, next: pos };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function hex(n: number) {
  return "0x" + (n >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(6, "0");
}

function skipImm(pos: number, op: number): number {

  switch (op) {

    case 0x20:
    case 0x21:
    case 0x22:
    case 0x23:
    case 0x24:
    case 0x41:
    case 0x10:
      return readU32(pos).next;

    case 0x28:
    case 0x29:
    case 0x2a:
    case 0x2b:
    case 0x2c:
    case 0x2d:
    case 0x36:
    case 0x37:
    case 0x38:
    case 0x39:
    case 0x3a:
    case 0x3b:
      pos = readU32(pos).next;
      return readU32(pos).next;

    case 0x0c:
    case 0x0d:
      return readU32(pos).next;

    case 0x11:
      pos = readU32(pos).next;
      return readU32(pos).next;

    case 0x3f:
    case 0x40:
      return pos + 1;

    case 0xd0:
      return pos + 1;

    case 0xd2:
      return readU32(pos).next;

    default:
      return pos;
  }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FIND FUNC 122 DISPATCHER SELECTOR WRITES");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TARGET ADDRESS : ${hex(TARGET)}`);
console.log(`WASM SIZE      : ${buf.length}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let p = 8;

while (p < buf.length) {

  const sectionId = buf[p++];

  const sizeInfo = readU32(p);
  const sectionSize = sizeInfo.value;
  p = sizeInfo.next;

  const sectionEnd = p + sectionSize;

  if (sectionId !== 10) {
    p = sectionEnd;
    continue;
  }

  const countInfo = readU32(p);
  const bodyCount = countInfo.value;
  p = countInfo.next;

  console.log(`DEFINED BODIES : ${bodyCount}`);
  console.log("");

  for (let body = 0; body < bodyCount; body++) {

    const bodySizeInfo = readU32(p);
    const bodySize = bodySizeInfo.value;
    p = bodySizeInfo.next;

    const bodyStart = p;
    const bodyEnd = p + bodySize;

    const localInfo = readU32(p);
    const localCount = localInfo.value;
    p = localInfo.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < localCount; i++) {
      p = readU32(p).next;
      p++;
    }

    const codeStart = p;

    /*
     * Track simple stack expressions.
     *
     * Each item describes how an address was produced.
     */
    type Item = {
      kind: string;
      value?: number;
      at: number;
    };

    const stack: Item[] = [];

    while (p < bodyEnd) {

      const at = p;
      const op = buf[p++];

      /*
       * i32.const
       */
      if (op === 0x41) {

        const x = readS32(p);
        p = x.next;

        stack.push({
          kind: "const",
          value: x.value >>> 0,
          at
        });

        continue;
      }

      /*
       * local.get
       */
      if (op === 0x20) {

        const x = readU32(p);
        p = x.next;

        stack.push({
          kind: `local.get ${x.value}`,
          at
        });

        continue;
      }

      /*
       * global.get
       */
      if (op === 0x23) {

        const x = readU32(p);
        p = x.next;

        stack.push({
          kind: `global.get ${x.value}`,
          at
        });

        continue;
      }

      /*
       * i32.add
       */
      if (op === 0x6a) {

        const b = stack.pop();
        const a = stack.pop();

        if (
          a?.kind === "const" &&
          b?.kind === "const"
        ) {
          stack.push({
            kind: "const",
            value: ((a.value! + b.value!) >>> 0),
            at
          });
        } else {
          stack.push({
            kind: "computed",
            at
          });
        }

        continue;
      }

      /*
       * i32.sub
       */
      if (op === 0x6b) {

        const b = stack.pop();
        const a = stack.pop();

        if (
          a?.kind === "const" &&
          b?.kind === "const"
        ) {
          stack.push({
            kind: "const",
            value: ((a.value! - b.value!) >>> 0),
            at
          });
        } else {
          stack.push({
            kind: "computed",
            at
          });
        }

        continue;
      }

      /*
       * i32.store family
       */
      if (
        op === 0x36 ||
        op === 0x37 ||
        op === 0x38 ||
        op === 0x39 ||
        op === 0x3a ||
        op === 0x3b
      ) {

        const align = readU32(p);
        p = align.next;

        const off = readU32(p);
        p = off.next;

        const address = stack.pop();
        const value = stack.pop();

        /*
         * Wasm store stack order:
         *
         * address
         * value
         * store
         *
         * Because our symbolic stack is only approximate,
         * inspect both operands for TARGET.
         */

        let hit = false;

        if (
          address?.kind === "const" &&
          address.value === TARGET
        ) {
          hit = true;
        }

        if (
          value?.kind === "const" &&
          value.value === TARGET
        ) {
          hit = true;
        }

        /*
         * Also check:
         * const TARGET + store offset
         */
        if (
          address?.kind === "const" &&
          (
            ((address.value! + off.value) >>> 0) === TARGET
          )
        ) {
          hit = true;
        }

        if (hit) {

          console.log("############################################################");
          console.log("TARGET STORE");
          console.log("############################################################");
          console.log(`REAL FUNC    : ${body + 83}`);
          console.log(`BODY INDEX   : ${body}`);
          console.log(`STORE        : ${hex(at)}`);
          console.log(
            `OPCODE       : 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
          );
          console.log(`ALIGN        : ${align.value}`);
          console.log(`OFFSET       : ${off.value}`);
          console.log(
            `ADDRESS SRC  : ${address?.kind ?? "unknown"}`
          );
          console.log(
            `VALUE SRC    : ${value?.kind ?? "unknown"}`
          );

          if (address?.kind === "const")
            console.log(
              `ADDRESS VALUE: ${hex(address.value!)}`
            );

          if (value?.kind === "const")
            console.log(
              `VALUE        : ${hex(value.value!)}`
            );

          console.log("");
          console.log("RAW CONTEXT:");

          const from = Math.max(
            codeStart,
            at - 48
          );

          const to = Math.min(
            bodyEnd,
            at + 32
          );

          for (let q = from; q < to; q += 16) {

            const e = Math.min(
              to,
              q + 16
            );

            const bytes: string[] = [];

            for (let k = q; k < e; k++) {
              bytes.push(
                buf[k]
                  .toString(16)
                  .padStart(2, "0")
              );
            }

            console.log(
              `${hex(q)}  ${bytes.join(" ")}`
            );
          }

          console.log("");
        }

        continue;
      }

      /*
       * i32.load
       */
      if (
        op === 0x28 ||
        op === 0x29 ||
        op === 0x2a ||
        op === 0x2b ||
        op === 0x2c ||
        op === 0x2d
      ) {

        p = readU32(p).next;
        p = readU32(p).next;

        stack.pop();

        stack.push({
          kind: "load",
          at
        });

        continue;
      }

      /*
       * Calls consume unknown arguments.
       * Reset symbolic stack conservatively.
       */
      if (op === 0x10) {

        const fn = readU32(p);
        p = fn.next;

        stack.length = 0;

        continue;
      }

      /*
       * local.set
       */
      if (op === 0x21) {

        const x = readU32(p);
        p = x.next;

        stack.pop();

        continue;
      }

      /*
       * local.tee
       */
      if (op === 0x22) {

        const x = readU32(p);
        p = x.next;

        const top = stack[stack.length - 1];

        if (top) {
          stack.push({
            ...top
          });
        }

        continue;
      }

      /*
       * drop
       */
      if (op === 0x1a) {
        stack.pop();
        continue;
      }

      /*
       * Everything else:
       * skip immediate operands where known.
       */
      const next = skipImm(p, op);

      if (next <= p) {
        p++;
      } else {
        p = next;
      }

      /*
       * Avoid symbolic contamination.
       */
      if (
        op !== 0x6a &&
        op !== 0x6b
      ) {
        if (
          op >= 0x45 &&
          op <= 0xbf
        ) {
          stack.length = 0;
        }
      }
    }

    p = bodyEnd;
  }
}

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("============================================================");

import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET = 0x250fb8;

const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function readU32(pos: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = buf[pos++];
    value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
      return { value, next: pos };

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function readS32(pos: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = buf[pos++];

    value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40))
        value -= 2 ** (shift + 7);

      return { value, next: pos };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function hex(n: number) {
  return "0x" + (n >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(6, "0");
}

function skipBlockType(pos: number) {
  const b = buf[pos];

  if (b === 0x40)
    return pos + 1;

  if (
    b === 0x7f ||
    b === 0x7e ||
    b === 0x7d ||
    b === 0x7c
  )
    return pos + 1;

  const x = readS32(pos);
  return x.next;
}

function skipInstruction(pos: number): number {
  const op = buf[pos++];

  switch (op) {
    case 0x02:
    case 0x03:
    case 0x04:
      return skipBlockType(pos);

    case 0x0c:
    case 0x0d: {
      return readU32(pos).next;
    }

    case 0x0e: {
      const n = readU32(pos);
      pos = n.next;

      for (let i = 0; i <= n.value; i++)
        pos = readU32(pos).next;

      return pos;
    }

    case 0x10:
      return readU32(pos).next;

    case 0x11: {
      pos = readU32(pos).next;
      pos = readU32(pos).next;
      return pos;
    }

    case 0x20:
    case 0x21:
    case 0x22:
    case 0x23:
    case 0x24:
    case 0x41:
      return readU32(pos).next;

    case 0x42: {
      while (true) {
        const b = buf[pos++];
        if (!(b & 0x80))
          return pos;
      }
    }

    case 0x28:
    case 0x29:
    case 0x2a:
    case 0x2b:
    case 0x2c:
    case 0x2d:
    case 0x36:
    case 0x37:
    case 0x38:
    case 0x39:
    case 0x3a:
    case 0x3b:
      pos = readU32(pos).next;
      pos = readU32(pos).next;
      return pos;

    case 0x3f:
      return pos + 1;

    case 0x40:
      return pos;

    case 0xd0:
      return pos + 1;

    case 0xd2:
      return readU32(pos).next;

    case 0xfc: {
      const sub = readU32(pos);
      return sub.next;
    }

    default:
      return pos;
  }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FIND DISPATCHER FUNC122 WRITES");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TARGET ADDRESS : ${hex(TARGET)}`);
console.log(`WASM SIZE      : ${buf.length}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let p = 8;

while (p < buf.length) {

  const sectionId = buf[p++];

  const sizeInfo = readU32(p);
  const sectionSize = sizeInfo.value;
  p = sizeInfo.next;

  const sectionEnd = p + sectionSize;

  /*
   * Code section
   */
  if (sectionId === 10) {

    const bodyCountInfo = readU32(p);
    const bodyCount = bodyCountInfo.value;
    p = bodyCountInfo.next;

    console.log(`DEFINED BODIES : ${bodyCount}`);
    console.log("");

    for (let body = 0; body < bodyCount; body++) {

      const bodySizeInfo = readU32(p);
      const bodySize = bodySizeInfo.value;
      p = bodySizeInfo.next;

      const bodyStart = p;
      const bodyEnd = p + bodySize;

      const localInfo = readU32(p);
      const localCount = localInfo.value;
      p = localInfo.next;

      /*
       * Skip local declarations.
       */
      for (let i = 0; i < localCount; i++) {
        const n = readU32(p);
        p = n.next;

        p++;
      }

      const codeStart = p;

      /*
       * Lightweight stack/address tracking.
       *
       * We mainly want patterns:
       *
       * i32.const TARGET
       * i32.store...
       *
       * or
       *
       * i32.const BASE
       * i32.const OFFSET
       * i32.add
       * i32.store
       *
       * Also detect direct constants equal to TARGET.
       */

      const recent: {
        pos: number;
        op: number;
        imm?: number;
      }[] = [];

      while (p < bodyEnd) {

        const at = p;
        const op = buf[p];

        let imm: number | undefined;

        if (op === 0x41) {
          const x = readS32(p + 1);
          imm = x.value;
        }

        /*
         * Direct constant TARGET.
         */
        if (op === 0x41 && (imm! >>> 0) === TARGET) {

          console.log("############################################################");
          console.log("TARGET CONSTANT");
          console.log("############################################################");
          console.log(`REAL FUNC  : ${body + 83}`);
          console.log(`BODY INDEX : ${body}`);
          console.log(`OFFSET     : ${hex(at)}`);
          console.log(`VALUE      : ${hex(imm!)}`);
          console.log("CONTEXT:");
          
          const from = Math.max(codeStart, at - 40);
          const to = Math.min(bodyEnd, at + 60);

          for (let q = from; q < to; q += 16) {

            const end = Math.min(to, q + 16);

            const bytes: string[] = [];

            for (let k = q; k < end; k++)
              bytes.push(buf[k].toString(16).padStart(2, "0"));

            console.log(
              `${hex(q)}  ${bytes.join(" ")}`
            );
          }

          console.log("");
        }

        /*
         * Detect stores and inspect preceding instructions.
         */
        if (
          op === 0x36 ||
          op === 0x37 ||
          op === 0x38 ||
          op === 0x39 ||
          op === 0x3a ||
          op === 0x3b
        ) {

          const storeEnd = skipInstruction(p);

          /*
           * Examine previous ~80 bytes for:
           *
           * i32.const TARGET
           *
           * and
           * add
           */
          let q = Math.max(codeStart, p - 80);

          while (q < p) {

            if (buf[q] === 0x41) {

              const x = readS32(q + 1);

              if ((x.value >>> 0) === TARGET) {

                console.log("############################################################");
                console.log("POSSIBLE TARGET STORE");
                console.log("############################################################");
                console.log(`REAL FUNC  : ${body + 83}`);
                console.log(`BODY INDEX : ${body}`);
                console.log(`STORE      : ${hex(p)}`);
                console.log(
                  `STORE OPCODE: 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
                );
                console.log(`TARGET     : ${hex(TARGET)}`);
                console.log(`CONST @    : ${hex(q)}`);
                console.log("");
              }

              q = x.next;
              continue;
            }

            q = skipInstruction(q);
          }
        }

        const next = skipInstruction(p);

        if (next <= p) {
          console.log(
            `PARSER STOP @ ${hex(p)}`
          );
          break;
        }

        recent.push({
          pos: at,
          op,
          imm
        });

        if (recent.length > 20)
          recent.shift();

        p = next;
      }

      p = bodyEnd;
    }
  }

  p = sectionEnd;
}

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\findDispatchField4.js
====================================================================================================
const fs = require("fs");

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const buf = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FIND DISPATCH FIELD +4");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("WASM SIZE :", buf.length);
console.log("TARGET INDEX :", 4275);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

function readU32LE(p) {
  if (p + 4 > buf.length) return null;
  return (
    (buf[p] |
      (buf[p + 1] << 8) |
      (buf[p + 2] << 16) |
      (buf[p + 3] << 24)) >>>
    0
  );
}

function readI32LE(p) {
  const v = readU32LE(p);
  return v === null ? null : v | 0;
}

function readULEB(p) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (p < buf.length && shift < 35) {
    const b = buf[p++];
    result += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) {
      return { value: result, next: p };
    }
    shift += 7;
  }

  return null;
}

function readSLEB(p) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let byte = 0;

  while (p < buf.length && shift < 35) {
    byte = buf[p++];
    result |= (byte & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if (!(byte & 0x80)) {
      if (shift < 32 && (byte & 0x40)) {
        result |= (~0 << shift);
      }
      return { value: result | 0, next: p };
    }
  }

  return null;
}

/*
 * Parse WASM sections enough to obtain code bodies.
 */
let p = 8;
let codeSection = null;

while (p < buf.length) {
  const id = buf[p++];

  const sizeInfo = readULEB(p);
  if (!sizeInfo) break;

  const sectionSize = sizeInfo.value;
  const sectionStart = p;
  const sectionEnd = sectionStart + sectionSize;

  if (id === 10) {
    codeSection = {
      start: sectionStart,
      end: sectionEnd
    };
    break;
  }

  p = sectionEnd;
}

if (!codeSection) {
  console.log("CODE SECTION NOT FOUND");
  process.exit(1);
}

let q = codeSection.start;

const bodyCountInfo = readULEB(q);
if (!bodyCountInfo) {
  console.log("CODE BODY COUNT FAILED");
  process.exit(1);
}

const bodyCount = bodyCountInfo.value;
q = bodyCountInfo.next;

console.log("CODE BODY COUNT :", bodyCount);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let hits = 0;

function hex(n) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

/*
 * We specifically recognize:
 *
 *  i32.const 4275
 *      = 0x41 0xB3 0x21
 *
 *  followed somewhere shortly by:
 *
 *  i32.store offset=4
 *
 * Also print ALL offset=4 stores so we can inspect producers
 * even when the constant is not immediately adjacent.
 *
 * Correct opcode:
 *   0x36 = i32.store
 *
 * Memory immediate:
 *   align : ULEB
 *   offset: ULEB
 */

for (let func = 0; func < bodyCount; func++) {
  const bodySizeInfo = readULEB(q);
  if (!bodySizeInfo) break;

  const bodySize = bodySizeInfo.value;
  const bodyStart = bodySizeInfo.next;
  const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bodySize;

  let x = bodyStart;

  const localDeclInfo = readULEB(x);
  if (!localDeclInfo) {
    q = bodyEnd;
    continue;
  }

  const localGroups = localDeclInfo.value;
  x = localDeclInfo.next;

  for (let g = 0; g < localGroups; g++) {
    const n = readULEB(x);
    if (!n) break;
    x = n.next;

    if (x >= bodyEnd) break;

    x += 1; // value type byte
  }

  const instructionsStart = x;

  let lastConsts = [];

  while (x < bodyEnd) {
    const opPos = x;
    const op = buf[x++];

    /*
     * i32.const
     */
    if (op === 0x41) {
      const imm = readSLEB(x);
      if (!imm) break;

      x = imm.next;

      lastConsts.push({
        pos: opPos,
        value: imm.value
      });

      if (lastConsts.length > 12)
        lastConsts.shift();

      continue;
    }

    /*
     * i32.store
     */
    if (op === 0x36) {
      const align = readULEB(x);
      if (!align) break;
      x = align.next;

      const off = readULEB(x);
      if (!off) break;
      x = off.next;

      if (off.value === 4) {
        hits++;

        console.log("");
        console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
        console.log("FUNC", func);
        console.log("STORE +4 @", hex(opPos));

        console.log("RECENT i32.const VALUES:");

        for (const c of lastConsts) {
          console.log(
            "  ",
            hex(c.pos),
            "i32.const",
            c.value
          );
        }

        /*
         * Print raw bytes around the store.
         */
        const from = Math.max(bodyStart, opPos - 32);
        const to = Math.min(bodyEnd, opPos + 16);

        console.log("RAW CONTEXT:");
        let line = "";

        for (let z = from; z < to; z++) {
          line += buf[z].toString(16).padStart(2, "0") + " ";

          if ((z - from + 1) % 16 === 0) {
            console.log("  " + line);
            line = "";
          }
        }

        if (line.length) console.log("  " + line);
      }

      continue;
    }

    /*
     * Basic instructions whose immediates we know.
     *
     * The scanner intentionally stays conservative.
     * Unknown complex instructions are not interpreted.
     */

    if (
      op === 0x02 || // block
      op === 0x03 || // loop
      op === 0x04    // if
    ) {
      // blocktype: one byte for our purposes
      if (x < bodyEnd) x++;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x05 || op === 0x0b || op === 0x0f) {
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
      const a = readULEB(x);
      if (!a) break;
      x = a.next;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x10) {
      const a = readULEB(x);
      if (!a) break;
      x = a.next;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x11) {
      const t = readULEB(x);
      if (!t) break;
      x = t.next;

      const table = readULEB(x);
      if (!table) break;
      x = table.next;

      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) {
      const a = readULEB(x);
      if (!a) break;
      x = a.next;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x23 || op === 0x24) {
      const a = readULEB(x);
      if (!a) break;
      x = a.next;
      continue;
    }

    /*
     * Memory loads/stores.
     */
    if (
      op === 0x28 || op === 0x29 ||
      op === 0x2a || op === 0x2b ||
      op === 0x2c || op === 0x2d ||
      op === 0x2e || op === 0x2f ||
      op === 0x30 || op === 0x31 ||
      op === 0x32 || op === 0x33 ||
      op === 0x34 || op === 0x35 ||
      op === 0x36 || op === 0x37 ||
      op === 0x38 || op === 0x39 ||
      op === 0x3a || op === 0x3b ||
      op === 0x3c || op === 0x3d ||
      op === 0x3e
    ) {
      const align = readULEB(x);
      if (!align) break;
      x = align.next;

      const off = readULEB(x);
      if (!off) break;
      x = off.next;

      continue;
    }

    /*
     * Constants.
     */
    if (op === 0x42) {
      const imm = readSLEB(x);
      if (!imm) break;
      x = imm.next;
      continue;
    }

    /*
     * br_table
     */
    if (op === 0x0e) {
      const n = readULEB(x);
      if (!n) break;
      x = n.next;

      for (let i = 0; i < n.value + 1; i++) {
        const a = readULEB(x);
        if (!a) {
          x = bodyEnd;
          break;
        }
        x = a.next;
      }

      continue;
    }

    /*
     * Prefixed opcodes.
     * Do not try to decode them; stop this function scan
     * rather than corrupting alignment.
     */
    if (op === 0xfc || op === 0xfd || op === 0xfe) {
      break;
    }

    /*
     * Most numeric/control/simple instructions have no immediate.
     */
  }

  q = bodyEnd;
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("RESULT");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TOTAL STORE +4 HITS :", hits);
console.log("============================================================");

FILE: scripts\findDispatchObjectStores.js

const fs = require("fs");

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm"
);

const TARGET_OFFSETS = new Set([0, 4, 36]);

function readULEB(pos) {
  let r = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[p++];
    r += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
    shift += 7;
  }

  return { value: r >>> 0, next: p };
}

function readSLEB(pos) {
  let r = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;
  let b;

  while (true) {
    b = wasm[p++];
    r += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;
    shift += 7;

    if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
    r -= 2 ** shift;
  }

  return { value: r | 0, next: p };
}

function findCode() {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const id = wasm[p++];

    const s = readULEB(p);
    p = s.next;

    if (id === 10) {
      return p;
    }

    p += s.value;
  }

  throw new Error("CODE SECTION NOT FOUND");
}

const codeStart = findCode();

const count = readULEB(codeStart);
let p = count.next;

const results = [];

for (let func = 0; func < count.value; func++) {

  const sz = readULEB(p);
  p = sz.next;

  const start = p;
  const end = p + sz.value;

  let q = start;

  const ld = readULEB(q);
  q = ld.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < ld.value; i++) {
    const n = readULEB(q);
    q = n.next + 1;
  }

  while (q < end) {

    const at = q;
    const op = wasm[q++];

    // i32.store
    if (op === 0x36) {

      const align = readULEB(q);
      q = align.next;

      const off = readULEB(q);
      q = off.next;

      if (TARGET_OFFSETS.has(off.value)) {

        results.push({
          func,
          at,
          offset: off.value
        });
      }

      continue;
    }

    // i64.store
    if (op === 0x37) {

      const align = readULEB(q);
      q = align.next;

      const off = readULEB(q);
      q = off.next;

      continue;
    }

    // store8
    if (op === 0x3a) {

      const align = readULEB(q);
      q = align.next;

      const off = readULEB(q);
      q = off.next;

      continue;
    }

    // store16
    if (op === 0x3b) {

      const align = readULEB(q);
      q = align.next;

      const off = readULEB(q);
      q = off.next;

      continue;
    }

    // store32
    if (op === 0x3e) {

      const align = readULEB(q);
      q = align.next;

      const off = readULEB(q);
      q = off.next;

      continue;
    }

    // constants
    if (op === 0x41) {
      const x = readSLEB(q);
      q = x.next;
      continue;
    }

    // local
    if (op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) {
      const x = readULEB(q);
      q = x.next;
      continue;
    }

    // call
    if (op === 0x10) {
      const x = readULEB(q);
      q = x.next;
      continue;
    }

    // call_indirect
    if (op === 0x11) {
      const x = readULEB(q);
      q = x.next;

      const y = readULEB(q);
      q = y.next;

      continue;
    }

    // memory loads
    if (
      op === 0x28 ||
      op === 0x29 ||
      op === 0x2a ||
      op === 0x2b ||
      op === 0x2c ||
      op === 0x2d ||
      op === 0x2e ||
      op === 0x2f ||
      op === 0x30 ||
      op === 0x31 ||
      op === 0x32 ||
      op === 0x33 ||
      op === 0x34 ||
      op === 0x35
    ) {
      const a = readULEB(q);
      q = a.next;

      const o = readULEB(q);
      q = o.next;

      continue;
    }

    // block / loop / if
    if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04) {
      const b = wasm[q++];

      // MVP blocktype normally one byte in this binary
      if (b !== 0x40 &&
          b !== 0x7f &&
          b !== 0x7e &&
          b !== 0x7d &&
          b !== 0x7c) {
        q--;
        const x = readSLEB(q);
        q = x.next;
      }

      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
      const x = readULEB(q);
      q = x.next;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0xfc) {
      // skip subopcode
      const x = readULEB(q);
      q = x.next;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0xfd) {
      const x = readULEB(q);
      q = x.next;
      continue;
    }
  }

  p = end;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DISPATCH OBJECT STORE SCAN");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log("");
console.log(`TOTAL MATCHES : ${results.length}`);
console.log("");

const grouped = new Map();

for (const r of results) {

  if (!grouped.has(r.func)) {
    grouped.set(r.func, []);
  }

  grouped.get(r.func).push(r);
}

for (const [func, list] of grouped) {

  console.log(`FUNC ${func}`);

  for (const r of list) {

    console.log(
      `  0x${r.at.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")}  ` +
      `i32.store offset=${r.offset}`
    );
  }

  console.log("");
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("END");
console.log("============================================================");

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGET = 0x195354;

function uleb(pos: number) {
    let v = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const x = b[pos++];
        v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

        if (!(x & 0x80)) {
            return { value: v, next: pos };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function hex(v: number) {
    return "0x" + v.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DISPATCH SLOT STORE SCAN");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TARGET = " + hex(TARGET));
console.log("");

let p = 8;

while (p < b.length) {
    const id = b[p++];

    const r = uleb(p);
    const size = r.value;
    p = r.next;

    const end = p + size;

    if (id === 10) {
        const c = uleb(p);
        const count = c.value;
        p = c.next;

        for (let f = 0; f < count; f++) {
            const z = uleb(p);
            const bodySize = z.value;
            p = z.next;

            const s = p;
            const e = p + bodySize;
            const F = 83 + f;

            for (let x = s; x < e; x++) {
                if (b[x] !== 0x36) continue;

                // i32.store
                if (b[x + 1] !== 0x02) continue;

                const windowStart = Math.max(s, x - 40);
                const windowEnd = Math.min(e, x + 5);

                for (let q = windowStart; q < x; q++) {
                    if (b[q] !== 0x41) continue;

                    const a = uleb(q + 1);

                    if (a.value === TARGET) {
                        console.log(
                            "DIRECT STORE PATTERN IN FUNC " + F +
                            " @ " + hex(q)
                        );
                    }
                }
            }

            p = e;
        }

        break;
    }

    p = end;
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("============================================================");

FILE: scripts\findDispatchStateWriters.js

(async () => {
    const fs = require("fs");
    const wabt = await require("wabt")();

    const file =
        "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

    const targets = new Set([
        1659720, // 0x195348
        1659724, // 0x19534C
        1659728, // 0x195350
        1659732, // 0x195354
        1659736  // 0x195358
    ]);

    const features = {
        exceptions: true,
        mutable_globals: true,
        sat_float_to_int: true,
        sign_extension: true,
        simd: true,
        threads: true,
        function_references: true,
        multi_value: true,
        tail_call: true,
        bulk_memory: true,
        reference_types: true,
        annotations: true,
        code_metadata: true,
        gc: true,
        memory64: true,
        multi_memory: true,
        extended_const: true,
        relaxed_simd: true
    };

    const bytes = fs.readFileSync(file);
    const mod = wabt.readWasm(bytes, {
        readDebugNames: false,
        ...features
    });

    mod.validate();

    const wat = mod.toText({
        foldExprs: false,
        inlineExport: false
    });

    const lines = wat.split(/\r?\n/);
    const storePattern =
        /\b(?:i32|i64|f32|f64|v128)\.store(?:8|16|32|64)?\b/;

    let func = null;
    let refs = 0;
    let candidates = 0;

    console.log("DISPATCH STATE WRITER SCAN");
    console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
    console.log("================================================");

    for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
        const line = lines[i];

        const header = line.match(/^\s*\(func \(;(\d+);\)/);
        if (header) {
            func = Number(header[1]);
        } else if (/^\s*\((?:data|elem|type|import|export|table|memory|global|start|custom|module)\b/.test(line)) {
            func = null;
        }

        if (func === null) continue;

        const m = line.match(/\bi32\.const\s+(-?\d+)\b/);
        if (!m) continue;

        const value = Number(m[1]);
        if (!targets.has(value)) continue;

        refs++;

        const start = Math.max(0, i - 8);
        const end = Math.min(lines.length - 1, i + 20);

        const stores = [];
        for (let j = start; j <= end; j++) {
            if (storePattern.test(lines[j])) stores.push(j);
        }

        if (stores.length === 0) continue;

        candidates++;

        console.log("");
        console.log("------------------------------------------------");
        console.log(
            `TARGET 0x${value.toString(16).toUpperCase()} (${value})`
        );
        console.log(`FUNCTION ${func}`);
        console.log(`CONSTANT LINE ${i + 1}`);
        console.log("NEARBY STORE INSTRUCTIONS:");

        for (const j of stores) {
            console.log(`  WAT line ${j + 1}: ${lines[j].trim()}`);
        }

        console.log("CONTEXT:");
        for (let j = start; j <= end; j++) {
            if (j >= i - 4 && j <= i + 12) {
                console.log(
                    `${j + 1}: ${lines[j]}`
                );
            }
        }
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log("================================================");
    console.log("TARGET CONSTANT REFERENCES:", refs);
    console.log("STORE-NEARBY CANDIDATES:", candidates);
    console.log("NOTE: proximity does not prove the effective address.");

    mod.destroy();
})().catch(error => {
    console.error(error);
    process.exitCode = 1;
});


====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\findDispatchStores.ts

import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

const TARGETS = [
    0x0019534c,
    0x00195350,
    0x00195354,
    0x00195358,
];

const TARGET_NAMES = new Map<number, string>([
    [0x0019534c, "0x19534C"],
    [0x00195350, "0x195350"],
    [0x00195354, "0x195354"],
    [0x00195358, "0x195358"],
]);

function uleb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[pos++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: pos };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function readSections() {
    let p = 8;

    const sections: {
        id: number;
        start: number;
        end: number;
    }[] = [];

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const id = buf[p++];

        const s = uleb(p);
        p = s.next;

        const start = p;
        const end = p + s.value;

        sections.push({ id, start, end });

        p = end;
    }

    return sections;
}

const sections = readSections();

const funcSec = sections.find(x => x.id === 3);
const codeSec = sections.find(x => x.id === 10);

if (!funcSec || !codeSec) {
    throw new Error("Function/code section not found");
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Function section
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let p = funcSec.start;

const fc = uleb(p);
p = fc.next;

const definedCount = fc.value;

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Code section
// ------------------------------------------------------------

p = codeSec.start;

const cc = uleb(p);
p = cc.next;

if (cc.value !== definedCount) {
    throw new Error(
        `Function/code count mismatch: ${definedCount} vs ${cc.value}`
    );
}

const functions: {
    globalFunc: number;
    ordinal: number;
    start: number;
    end: number;
}[] = [];

for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < cc.value; ordinal++) {
    const bs = uleb(p);
    p = bs.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + bs.value;

    functions.push({
        globalFunc: 83 + ordinal,
        ordinal,
        start,
        end,
    });

    p = end;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FIND DISPATCH STORES");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TARGETS = ${TARGETS.map(x => TARGET_NAMES.get(x)).join(", ")}`);
console.log(
    `CODE SECTION = 0x${codeSec.start.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} .. 0x${codeSec.end.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// STORE opcode families
//
// i32.store      = 0x36
// i64.store      = 0x37
// f32.store      = 0x38
// f64.store      = 0x39
// i32.store8     = 0x3a
// i32.store16    = 0x3b
// i64.store8     = 0x3c
// i64.store16    = 0x3d
// i64.store32    = 0x3e
//
// We inspect every store and decode:
//   alignment
//   offset
//
// Absolute addresses generated as:
//
//   i32.const ADDRESS
//   i32.store offset=0
//
// will NOT necessarily have a useful offset field, so we also
// perform a local backward scan for target constants.
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const storeOpcodes = new Set([
    0x36, 0x37, 0x38, 0x39,
    0x3a, 0x3b, 0x3c, 0x3d, 0x3e,
]);

let directHits = 0;
let constStoreHits = 0;

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Direct encoded offset hits
// ------------------------------------------------------------

console.log();
console.log("DIRECT STORE-OFFSET HITS");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (const fn of functions) {
    for (let off = fn.start; off < fn.end; off++) {
        if (!storeOpcodes.has(buf[off])) continue;

        // memory instruction:
        // opcode
        // alignment ULEB
        // offset ULEB

        let q = off + 1;

        const align = uleb(q);
        q = align.next;

        const offset = uleb(q);

        if (TARGETS.includes(offset.value)) {
            directHits++;

            console.log(
                `HIT @ 0x${off.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} | ` +
                `FUNC ${fn.globalFunc} | ` +
                `STORE=0x${buf[off].toString(16)} | ` +
                `offset=0x${offset.value.toString(16)}`
            );
        }
    }
}

console.log(`COUNT = ${directHits}`);

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Search target constants and inspect nearby STORE
// ------------------------------------------------------------

console.log();
console.log("I32.CONST TARGET + NEARBY STORE");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (const fn of functions) {
    for (let off = fn.start; off < fn.end - 5; off++) {

        // i32.const
        if (buf[off] !== 0x41) continue;

        const x = uleb(off + 1);

        if (!TARGETS.includes(x.value)) continue;

        const targetName = TARGET_NAMES.get(x.value) ?? "UNKNOWN";

        console.log();
        console.log(
            `CONST HIT @ 0x${off.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} | ` +
            `FUNC ${fn.globalFunc} | TARGET ${targetName}`
        );

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // Search next 80 bytes for STORE
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        let foundForward = false;

        for (
            let s = x.next;
            s < Math.min(fn.end, x.next + 80);
            s++
        ) {
            if (!storeOpcodes.has(buf[s])) continue;

            console.log(
                `  FORWARD STORE @ 0x${s.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} | ` +
                `opcode=0x${buf[s].toString(16)} | ` +
                `delta=${s - off}`
            );

            foundForward = true;
            constStoreHits++;
            break;
        }

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // Search previous 80 bytes for STORE
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        for (
            let s = Math.max(fn.start, off - 80);
            s < off;
            s++
        ) {
            if (!storeOpcodes.has(buf[s])) continue;

            console.log(
                `  BACKWARD STORE @ 0x${s.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} | ` +
                `opcode=0x${buf[s].toString(16)} | ` +
                `delta=${s - off}`
            );

            foundForward = true;
            constStoreHits++;
        }

        if (!foundForward) {
            console.log("  NO NEARBY STORE");
        }

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // Raw context
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        const c0 = Math.max(fn.start, off - 24);
        const c1 = Math.min(fn.end, off + 64);

        for (let c = c0; c < c1; c += 16) {
            const e = Math.min(c + 16, c1);

            const bytes: string[] = [];

            for (let i = c; i < e; i++) {
                bytes.push(
                    buf[i].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")
                );
            }

            console.log(
                `  0x${c.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ${bytes.join(" ")}`
            );
        }
    }
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SUMMARY");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`DIRECT OFFSET HITS = ${directHits}`);
console.log(`CONST/NEARBY STORE HITS = ${constStoreHits}`);
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("END");
console.log("NO WASM EXECUTION");
console.log("============================================================");
