FILE: scripts\findDispatchTableWrites.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const START = 0x195348;
const END   = 0x195364;

function uleb(pos: number) {
  let v = 0, shift = 0, p = pos;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const b = wasm[p++];
    v += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
    shift += 7;
  }

  return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };
}

function sleb(pos: number) {
  let v = 0, shift = 0, p = pos, b = 0;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    b = wasm[p++];
    v |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
    v |= (-1 << shift);
  }

  return { value: v | 0, next: p };
}

function targetAddress(v: number) {
  return v >= START && v <= END;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DISPATCH TABLE WRITE SCAN");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(
  `TARGET RANGE : 0x${START.toString(16)} .. 0x${END.toString(16)}`
);
console.log("");

let hits = 0;

for (let p = 0; p < wasm.length - 8; p++) {

  if (wasm[p] !== 0x41) continue;

  const c = uleb(p + 1);

  if (!targetAddress(c.value)) continue;

  let q = c.next;

  console.log("");
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
  console.log(
    `CONST : 0x${p.toString(16)} = 0x${c.value.toString(16)}`
  );

  /*
   * Look ahead for a direct i32.store.
   * Stop if the address is modified or another value is pushed.
   */
  for (let n = 0; n < 16 && q < wasm.length; n++) {

    const opPos = q;
    const op = wasm[q++];

    if (op === 0x36) {
      const a = uleb(q);
      q = a.next;

      const o = uleb(q);
      q = o.next;

      console.log(
        `DIRECT STORE : 0x${opPos.toString(16)} ` +
        `align=${a.value} offset=0x${o.value.toString(16)}`
      );

      hits++;
      break;
    }

    if (op === 0x37) {
      const a = uleb(q);
      q = a.next;

      const o = uleb(q);
      q = o.next;

      console.log(
        `I64 STORE : 0x${opPos.toString(16)} ` +
        `align=${a.value} offset=0x${o.value.toString(16)}`
      );

      hits++;
      break;
    }

    if (
      op === 0x6a ||
      op === 0x6b ||
      op === 0x6c ||
      op === 0x74 ||
      op === 0x75 ||
      op === 0x76 ||
      op === 0x77 ||
      op === 0x78 ||
      op === 0x20 ||
      op === 0x21 ||
      op === 0x22 ||
      op === 0x41 ||
      op === 0x42
    ) {
      break;
    }

    if (op >= 0x28 && op <= 0x35) {
      break;
    }
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`DIRECT WRITE CANDIDATES : ${hits}`);
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");

FILE: scripts\findDispatchTargetsInTable.ts

import * as fs from "fs";

const file =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(file);

const ELEMENT_START = 0x00002b0d;
const ELEMENT_END   = 0x00006e74;

const TARGETS = [
  8637,
  8927,
  8952,

  5079,
  5078,
  5077,
  5076,
  5075,
  5074,
  5073,
  5072,
  5070,
  5069,
  5068,
];

function readULEB(pos: number) {

  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) {

    if (pos + i >= b.length)
      return null;

    const x = b[pos + i];

    value += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if ((x & 0x80) === 0) {

      return {
        value,
        end: pos + i + 1
      };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }

  return null;
}

function hex(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DISPATCH TARGET TABLE SEARCH");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("");

let p = ELEMENT_START;

const segmentCount = readULEB(p);

if (!segmentCount)
  throw new Error("Cannot read element segment count");

p = segmentCount.end;

console.log(
  `Element segments = ${segmentCount.value}`
);

console.log("");

const found = new Map<number, number[]>();

for (let segment = 0; segment < segmentCount.value; segment++) {

  const flagsR = readULEB(p);

  if (!flagsR)
    throw new Error(`Cannot read flags at ${hex(p)}`);

  const flags = flagsR.value;
  p = flagsR.end;

  console.log(
    `SEGMENT ${segment} flags=${flags} @ ${hex(p)}`
  );

  if (flags === 0) {

    /*
       Active element segment:

         flags
         offset expression
           i32.const 1
           end (0x0b)
         function_count
         function_indices...
    */

    if (b[p] !== 0x41) {

      throw new Error(
        `Expected i32.const at ${hex(p)}, ` +
        `got ${hex(b[p])}`
      );
    }

    p++;

    const offsetR = readULEB(p);

    if (!offsetR)
      throw new Error(
        `Cannot read table offset at ${hex(p)}`
      );

    const tableOffset = offsetR.value;
    p = offsetR.end;

    /*
       IMPORTANT:
       consume the end opcode of the offset expression.
    */

    if (b[p] !== 0x0b) {

      throw new Error(
        `Expected offset-expression end at ${hex(p)}, ` +
        `got ${hex(b[p])}`
      );
    }

    p++;

    const countR = readULEB(p);

    if (!countR)
      throw new Error(
        `Cannot read function count at ${hex(p)}`
      );

    p = countR.end;

    const count = countR.value;

    console.log(
      `  table offset = ${tableOffset}`
    );

    console.log(
      `  function count = ${count}`
    );

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {

      const f = readULEB(p);

      if (!f)
        throw new Error(
          `Cannot read function index at ${hex(p)}`
        );

      p = f.end;

      if (TARGETS.includes(f.value)) {

        const slot = tableOffset + i;

        if (!found.has(f.value))
          found.set(f.value, []);

        found.get(f.value)!.push(slot);

        console.log(
          `  FOUND function=${f.value} ` +
          `element=${i} table_slot=${slot}`
        );
      }
    }

    continue;
  }

  /*
     No other element segment type is expected here.
  */

  throw new Error(
    `Unsupported element flags=${flags}`
  );
}

console.log("");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("SUMMARY");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (const target of TARGETS) {

  const values = found.get(target);

  if (!values) {

    console.log(
      `function ${target} -> NOT FOUND`
    );

  } else {

    console.log(
      `function ${target} -> ` +
      values.join(", ")
    );
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log(
  `Final pointer = ${hex(p)}`
);

console.log(
  `Expected end  = ${hex(ELEMENT_END)}`
);

if (p !== ELEMENT_END) {

  console.log("");
  console.log(
    "WARNING: Element section end mismatch"
  );
} else {

  console.log("");
  console.log(
    "ELEMENT SECTION VERIFIED EXACTLY"
  );
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("END");
console.log("============================================================");

FILE: scripts\findExact19534CWriter.mjs
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGET = 0x19534c;

function uleb(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, n = 0;

  while (p < b.length) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;
    n++;

    if (!(x & 0x80))
      return { value: v >>> 0, len: n };

    s += 7;
  }

  return null;
}

function sleb(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, n = 0, x;

  do {
    x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;
    s += 7;
    n++;
  } while (x & 0x80);

  if (s < 32 && (x & 0x40))
    v |= (~0 << s);

  return { value: v | 0, len: n };
}

function hx(x) {
  return "0x" + (x >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

/*
 * Scan every i32.store / i32.store8 / i32.store16.
 *
 * Keep only stores whose immediately preceding address-producing
 * expression contains an exact i32.const TARGET.
 *
 * This is intentionally conservative.
 */

let hits = 0;

for (let p = 0; p < b.length - 5; p++) {

  const op = b[p];

  if (op !== 0x36 && op !== 0x3a && op !== 0x3b)
    continue;

  const a = p + 1;

  if (a >= b.length)
    continue;

  const align = b[a];

  const off = uleb(a + 1);

  if (!off)
    continue;

  /*
   * Search backwards only through a small expression window.
   *
   * We specifically require the exact byte sequence:
   *
   *   41 cc a6 e5 00
   *
   * which is:
   *
   *   i32.const 0x19534C
   */
  const lo = Math.max(0, p - 80);

  let found = -1;

  for (let q = lo; q < p; q++) {

    if (b[q] !== 0x41)
      continue;

    const r = sleb(q + 1);

    if (!r)
      continue;

    if ((r.value >>> 0) === TARGET) {
      found = q;
      break;
    }
  }

  if (found < 0)
    continue;

  hits++;

  console.log("");
  console.log("=".repeat(72));
  console.log(`HIT ${hits}`);
  console.log("=".repeat(72));
  console.log(`STORE : ${hx(p)}`);
  console.log(
    `TYPE  : ${
      op === 0x36 ? "i32.store" :
      op === 0x3a ? "i32.store8" :
      "i32.store16"
    }`
  );
  console.log(`ALIGN : ${align}`);
  console.log(`OFFSET: ${off.value}`);
  console.log(`TARGET CONST: ${hx(found)}`);
  console.log("");

  const start = Math.max(0, found - 16);
  const end = Math.min(b.length, p + 12);

  for (let q = start; q < end; q++) {

    let s = `${hx(q)}  ${b[q].toString(16).padStart(2,"0")}`;

    if (b[q] === 0x41) {
      const r = sleb(q + 1);

      if (r) {
        s += `  i32.const ${r.value} (${hx(r.value)})`;
      }

    } else if (b[q] === 0x20) {
      const r = uleb(q + 1);

      if (r)
        s += `  local.get ${r.value}`;

    } else if (b[q] === 0x21) {
      const r = uleb(q + 1);

      if (r)
        s += `  local.set ${r.value}`;

    } else if (b[q] === 0x22) {
      const r = uleb(q + 1);

      if (r)
        s += `  local.tee ${r.value}`;

    } else if (b[q] === 0x6a) {
      s += "  i32.add";

    } else if (b[q] === 0x6b) {
      s += "  i32.sub";

    } else if (b[q] === 0x36) {
      s += "  i32.store";

    } else if (b[q] === 0x3a) {
      s += "  i32.store8";

    } else if (b[q] === 0x3b) {
      s += "  i32.store16";
    }

    console.log(s);
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log(`TOTAL EXACT TARGET STORE CANDIDATES : ${hits}`);
console.log("=".repeat(72));

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\findExact195354Writers.mjs
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGET = 0x195354;

function readULEB(pos) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const b = wasm[p++];
    value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
    shift += 7;
  }

  return { value, next: p };
}

function findCodeSection() {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const id = wasm[p++];

    const r = readULEB(p);
    const size = r.value;
    p = r.next;

    if (id === 10) {
      return { start: p, end: p + size };
    }

    p += size;
  }

  throw new Error("Code section not found");
}

function getBodies() {
  const sec = findCodeSection();

  let p = sec.start;

  const count = readULEB(p);
  p = count.next;

  const bodies = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const r = readULEB(p);
    const size = r.value;
    p = r.next;

    bodies.push({
      func: 83 + i,
      start: p,
      end: p + size
    });

    p += size;
  }

  return bodies;
}

function isI32ConstTarget(pos) {
  if (wasm[pos] !== 0x41) return false;

  const r = readULEB(pos + 1);
  return r.value === TARGET;
}

function decodeStore(pos) {
  if (wasm[pos] !== 0x36) return null;

  const a = readULEB(pos + 1);
  const o = readULEB(a.next);

  return {
    align: a.value,
    offset: o.value,
    end: o.next
  };
}

const bodies = getBodies();

console.log("EXACT 0x195354 WRITER SEARCH");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("========================================================================");
console.log("TARGET : 0x195354");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------------------");

let hits = 0;

for (const body of bodies) {
  for (let p = body.start; p < body.end; p++) {

    if (!isI32ConstTarget(p)) continue;

    const targetConst = p;

    /*
     * Search forward only a short distance for a store.
     * We only accept cases where the TARGET constant is
     * still on the stack immediately before the store.
     *
     * We conservatively stop at control-flow boundaries
     * and calls.
     */

    let q = targetConst;
    const limit = Math.min(body.end, targetConst + 40);

    while (q < limit) {
      const op = wasm[q];

      if (op === 0x36) {
        const st = decodeStore(q);

        console.log(`HIT ${++hits}`);
        console.log(`FUNC  : ${body.func}`);
        console.log(`CONST : 0x${targetConst.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`);
        console.log(`STORE : 0x${q.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`);
        console.log(`ALIGN : ${st.align}`);
        console.log(`OFFSET: ${st.offset}`);
        console.log("------------------------------------------------------------------------");

        break;
      }

      /*
       * A call consumes stack values and may change the
       * value relationship, so don't classify through it.
       */
      if (op === 0x10 || op === 0x11) {
        break;
      }

      /*
       * Control flow boundaries.
       */
      if (
        op === 0x02 ||
        op === 0x03 ||
        op === 0x04 ||
        op === 0x05 ||
        op === 0x0b ||
        op === 0x0c ||
        op === 0x0d ||
        op === 0x0e ||
        op === 0x0f
      ) {
        break;
      }

      q++;

      if (op === 0x41) {
        const r = readULEB(q);
        q = r.next;
      }
    }
  }
}

console.log("========================================================================");
console.log(`TOTAL CANDIDATES : ${hits}`);
console.log("DONE");

FILE: scripts\findExactG528G272StoresV3.ts
====================================================================================================
import * as fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm"
);

const START = 0x95e67;
const END   = 0x96400;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" EXACT G-528 / G-272 STORE SEARCH v3");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`WASM size : ${wasm.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log(`Range     : 0x${START.toString(16)} .. 0x${END.toString(16)}`);
console.log("");
console.log("TARGET ADDRESS FORMS:");
console.log("  L7 - 64   = G-528");
console.log("  L7 + 192  = G-272");
console.log("============================================================");

function readUleb(pos: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[pos++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
      return { value: value >>> 0, next: pos };

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function readSleb(pos: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let b = 0;

  while (true) {
    b = wasm[pos++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
      break;
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40))
    value |= (~0 << shift);

  return { value: value | 0, next: pos };
}

function hex(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(6, "0");
}

function opname(op: number): string {
  switch (op) {
    case 0x20: return "local.get";
    case 0x21: return "local.set";
    case 0x22: return "local.tee";
    case 0x41: return "i32.const";
    case 0x42: return "i64.const";
    case 0x6a: return "i32.add";
    case 0x6b: return "i32.sub";
    case 0x37: return "i64.store";
    case 0x29: return "i64.load";
    case 0x10: return "call";
    default: return `op_0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;
  }
}

interface Ins {
  at: number;
  op: number;
  text: string;
}

const ins: Ins[] = [];

let p = START;

while (p < END) {
  const at = p;
  const op = wasm[p++];

  let text = opname(op);

  switch (op) {

    case 0x20:
    case 0x21:
    case 0x22: {
      const r = readUleb(p);
      p = r.next;
      text += ` ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x41: {
      const r = readSleb(p);
      p = r.next;
      text += ` ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x42: {
      const r = readSleb(p);
      p = r.next;
      text += ` ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x37:
    case 0x29: {
      const a = readUleb(p);
      p = a.next;

      const o = readUleb(p);
      p = o.next;

      text += ` align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x10: {
      const r = readUleb(p);
      p = r.next;
      text += ` ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    default:
      break;
  }

  ins.push({ at, op, text });
}

/*
 * Search for stores where the immediately preceding address-building
 * instructions contain:
 *
 *   local.get 7
 *   i32.const -64
 *   i32.sub
 *
 * OR
 *
 *   local.get 7
 *   i32.const 192
 *   i32.add
 *
 * We also allow an index addition:
 *
 *   ... + local.get 6
 *
 * because these are the limb-array stores.
 */

for (let i = 0; i < ins.length; i++) {

  const cur = ins[i];

  if (cur.op !== 0x37)
    continue;

  const w = ins.slice(Math.max(0, i - 12), i);

  const hasMinus64 =
    w.some((x, j) =>
      x.op === 0x20 &&
      x.text === "local.get 7" &&
      w[j + 1]?.text === "i32.const -64" &&
      w[j + 2]?.text === "i32.sub"
    );

  const hasPlus192 =
    w.some((x, j) =>
      x.op === 0x20 &&
      x.text === "local.get 7" &&
      w[j + 1]?.text === "i32.const 192" &&
      w[j + 2]?.text === "i32.add"
    );

  if (!hasMinus64 && !hasPlus192)
    continue;

  console.log("");
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
  console.log(`MATCH @ ${hex(cur.at)}`);
  console.log(
    hasMinus64
      ? "TARGET: L7 - 64 = G-528"
      : "TARGET: L7 + 192 = G-272"
  );
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

  for (const x of w)
    console.log(`${hex(x.at)} ${x.text}`);

  console.log(`${hex(cur.at)} ${cur.text}`);
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" EXACT SEARCH COMPLETE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("READ ONLY - NO NETWORK - NO TRANSACTION");

FILE: scripts\findExactLpAccountV22.ts

import fs from 'fs';
import path from 'path';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const ROOT = path.join(
    process.cwd(),
    'node_modules',
    '@ston-fi',
    'sdk',
    'dist'
);

const TARGETS = [
    'class LpAccountV2_2',
    'class LpAccountV2_1',
    'LpAccountV2_2 =',
    'LpAccountV2_1 =',
    'src/contracts/dex/v2_2/LpAccount/LpAccountV2_2.ts',
    'src/contracts/dex/v2_1/LpAccount/LpAccountV2_1.ts',
    '0x178d4519',
    '178d4519',
];

function scan(file: string) {
    let text: string;

    try {
        text = fs.readFileSync(file, 'utf8');
    } catch {
        return;
    }

    for (const target of TARGETS) {
        let from = 0;

        while (true) {
            const i = text.indexOf(target, from);
            if (i < 0) break;

            const start = Math.max(0, i - 1800);
            const end = Math.min(text.length, i + target.length + 5000);

            console.log('\n============================================================');
            console.log(`TARGET: ${target}`);
            console.log(`FILE  : ${path.relative(ROOT, file)}`);
            console.log('============================================================');
            console.log(text.slice(start, end));

            from = i + target.length;
        }
    }
}

export async function run(_provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' EXACT LP ACCOUNT V2.1 / V2.2 SEARCH');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - LOCAL SDK ONLY');
    console.log('============================================================');

    const preferred = [
        'chunk-G66DMSME.cjs',
        'chunk-G66DMSME.cjs.map',
    ];

    for (const name of preferred) {
        const file = path.join(ROOT, name);

        if (fs.existsSync(file)) {
            scan(file);
        } else {
            console.log(`NOT FOUND: ${name}`);
        }
    }

    console.log('\n============================================================');
    console.log(' TARGET CHUNK SEARCH');
    console.log('============================================================');

    for (const name of fs.readdirSync(ROOT)) {
        if (!/^chunk-.*\.(cjs|js|map)$/.test(name)) continue;

        if (preferred.includes(name)) continue;

        const file = path.join(ROOT, name);

        let text: string;

        try {
            text = fs.readFileSync(file, 'utf8');
        } catch {
            continue;
        }

        if (
            text.includes('LpAccountV2_2') ||
            text.includes('LpAccountV2_1') ||
            text.includes('0x178d4519') ||
            text.includes('178d4519')
        ) {
            scan(file);
        }
    }

    console.log('\n============================================================');
    console.log(' COMPLETE');
    console.log('============================================================');
}

}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\findExactStateStores.js
====================================================================================================
const fs = require("fs");

const file = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const buf = fs.readFileSync(file);

const IMPORTED = 83;
const TARGETS = new Set([
    0x250f84, 0x250f8c, 0x250f94,
    0x250f9c, 0x250fa0, 0x250fa8,
    0x250fb4, 0x250fb8,
    0x250fc0, 0x250fc4, 0x250fc8,
    0x250fd8
]);

function uleb(p) {
    let v = 0, s = 0;
    while (true) {
        const b = buf[p++];
        v += (b & 127) * 2 ** s;
        if (!(b & 128)) return { v, p };
        s += 7;
    }
}

function sleb(p) {
    let v = 0, s = 0, b;
    while (true) {
        b = buf[p++];
        v |= (b & 127) << s;
        s += 7;
        if (!(b & 128)) break;
    }
    if (s < 32 && (b & 64)) v |= (~0 << s);
    return { v: v | 0, p };
}

// code section
let p = 8, code = 0;

while (p < buf.length) {
    const id = buf[p++];
    const x = uleb(p);
    p = x.p;

    if (id === 10) {
        code = p;
        break;
    }

    p += x.v;
}

const n = uleb(code);
p = n.p;

const bodies = new Map();

for (let i = 0; i < n.v; i++) {
    const s = uleb(p);
    p = s.p;

    const f = IMPORTED + i;

    bodies.set(f, {
        start: p,
        end: p + s.v
    });

    p += s.v;
}

function decode(p) {
    const op = buf[p];

    if (op === 0x41) {
        const x = sleb(p + 1);
        return ["i32.const", x.v, x.p];
    }

    if (op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) {
        const x = uleb(p + 1);
        return [
            op === 0x20 ? "local.get" :
            op === 0x21 ? "local.set" : "local.tee",
            x.v,
            x.p
        ];
    }

    if (op === 0x28 || op === 0x36 ||
        op === 0x2d || op === 0x3a ||
        op === 0x3b) {

        const a = uleb(p + 1);
        const o = uleb(a.p);

        return [
            op === 0x28 ? "i32.load" :
            op === 0x36 ? "i32.store" :
            op === 0x2d ? "i32.load8_u" :
            op === 0x3a ? "i32.store8" :
                          "i32.store16",
            o.v,
            o.p
        ];
    }

    if (op === 0x6a) return ["i32.add", null, p + 1];
    if (op === 0x6b) return ["i32.sub", null, p + 1];
    if (op === 0x1a) return ["drop", null, p + 1];

    if (op === 0x10) {
        const x = uleb(p + 1);
        return ["call", x.v, x.p];
    }

    return ["?", op, p + 1];
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("EXACT GLOBAL STATE STORE SEARCH");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const [func, body] of bodies) {

    let p = body.start;
    const stack = [];

    while (p < body.end) {

        const [name, value, next] = decode(p);

        if (name === "i32.const") {
            stack.push({
                type: "const",
                value
            });
        }

        else if (name === "local.get") {
            stack.push({
                type: "local",
                value
            });
        }

        else if (name === "i32.add" || name === "i32.sub") {

            const b = stack.pop();
            const a = stack.pop();

            if (a?.type === "const" && b?.type === "const") {
                stack.push({
                    type: "const",
                    value: name === "i32.add"
                        ? (a.value + b.value) | 0
                        : (a.value - b.value) | 0
                });
            } else {
                stack.push({
                    type: "expr",
                    value: `${a?.value ?? "?"} ${name === "i32.add" ? "+" : "-"} ${b?.value ?? "?"}`
                });
            }
        }

        else if (name === "i32.store" ||
                 name === "i32.store8" ||
                 name === "i32.store16") {

            const valueItem = stack.pop();
            const addrItem = stack.pop();

            if (addrItem?.type === "const" &&
                TARGETS.has(addrItem.value)) {

                console.log(
                    `\nFUNC ${func} @ 0x${p.toString(16)}`
                );

                console.log(
                    `STORE ${name}`
                );

                console.log(
                    `ADDRESS = 0x${addrItem.value.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
                );

                console.log(
                    `VALUE   = ${valueItem?.type || "unknown"} ${valueItem?.value ?? ""}`
                );
            }
        }

        else if (name === "i32.load" ||
                 name === "i32.load8_u") {

            const addrItem = stack.pop();

            if (addrItem?.type === "const" &&
                TARGETS.has(addrItem.value)) {

                console.log(
                    `\nFUNC ${func} @ 0x${p.toString(16)}`
                );

                console.log(
                    `LOAD ${name}`
                );

                console.log(
                    `ADDRESS = 0x${addrItem.value.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
                );
            }

            stack.push({
                type: "load",
                value:
                    addrItem?.type === "const"
                        ? `mem[0x${addrItem.value.toString(16)}]`
                        : "mem[?]"
            });
        }

        else if (name === "drop") {
            stack.pop();
        }

        p = next;
    }
}

console.log("\n============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("============================================================");

import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const START = 0x0000d339;

function uleb(pos) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const x = b[pos++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) return { value: v, next: pos };
    s += 7;
  }
}

    found++;

    console.log("");

    console.log("RAW WINDOW:");

      );

    console.log("");

    const before = Math.max(START, p - 16);

    for (let q = before; q < p + 3; q++) {
      console.log(

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\findFunc122CallSites.js
====================================================================================================
const fs = require("fs");

const WASM_PATH =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET = 122;

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM_PATH);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FIND FUNC 122 CALL SITES");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("WASM SIZE :", wasm.length);
console.log("TARGET    : FUNC 122");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

function readU32Leb(pos) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (pos < wasm.length) {
    const b = wasm[pos++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if (!(b & 0x80))
      break;

    shift += 7;
  }

  return {
    value: value >>> 0,
    next: pos
  };
}

function readI32Leb(pos) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let b = 0;

  while (pos < wasm.length) {
    b = wasm[pos++];

    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if (!(b & 0x80))
      break;
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40))
    value |= (~0 << shift);

  return {
    value: value | 0,
    next: pos
  };
}

function decode(pos) {

  const start = pos;
  const op = wasm[pos++];

  let text =
    `0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;

  if (op === 0x10) {

    const x = readU32Leb(pos);
    pos = x.next;

    text = `call func=${x.value}`;
  }

  else if (op === 0x41) {

    const x = readI32Leb(pos);
    pos = x.next;

    text =
      `i32.const ${x.value}` +
      ` (0x${(x.value >>> 0).toString(16)})`;
  }

  else if (
    op === 0x20 ||
    op === 0x21 ||
    op === 0x22
  ) {

    const x = readU32Leb(pos);
    pos = x.next;

    const n =
      op === 0x20 ? "local.get" :
      op === 0x21 ? "local.set" :
      "local.tee";

    text = `${n} local=${x.value}`;
  }

  else if (
    op === 0x28 ||
    op === 0x29 ||
    op === 0x2c ||
    op === 0x2d ||
    op === 0x36 ||
    op === 0x37 ||
    op === 0x3a
  ) {

    const align = readU32Leb(pos);
    pos = align.next;

    const offset = readU32Leb(pos);
    pos = offset.next;

    const names = {
      0x28: "i32.load",
      0x29: "i64.load",
      0x2c: "i32.load8_s",
      0x2d: "i32.load8_u",
      0x36: "i32.store",
      0x37: "i64.store",
      0x3a: "i32.store8"
    };

    text =
      `${names[op]}` +
      ` align=${align.value}` +
      ` offset=0x${offset.value.toString(16)}`;
  }

  else if (
    op === 0x0c ||
    op === 0x0d
  ) {

    const x = readU32Leb(pos);
    pos = x.next;

    text =
      `${op === 0x0c ? "br" : "br_if"}` +
      ` depth=${x.value}`;
  }

  else {

    const names = {
      0x00: "unreachable",
      0x01: "nop",
      0x02: "block",
      0x03: "loop",
      0x04: "if",
      0x05: "else",
      0x0b: "end",
      0x1a: "drop",
      0x45: "i32.eqz",
      0x46: "i32.eq",
      0x47: "i32.ne",
      0x48: "i32.lt_s",
      0x49: "i32.lt_u",
      0x4a: "i32.gt_s",
      0x4b: "i32.gt_u",
      0x4c: "i32.le_s",
      0x4d: "i32.le_u",
      0x4e: "i32.ge_s",
      0x4f: "i32.ge_u",
      0x6a: "i32.add",
      0x6b: "i32.sub",
      0x6c: "i32.mul",
      0x71: "i32.and",
      0x72: "i32.or",
      0x73: "i32.xor"
    };

    text = names[op] || text;
  }

  return {
    start,
    end: pos,
    op,
    text
  };
}

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * Locate sections
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 */

let p = 8;
let importCount = 0;
let codeStart = -1;
let codeEnd = -1;

while (p < wasm.length) {

  const sectionId = wasm[p++];

  const sizeInfo = readU32Leb(p);
  const size = sizeInfo.value;
  p = sizeInfo.next;

  const start = p;
  const end = p + size;

  if (sectionId === 2) {

    const count = readU32Leb(start);
    importCount = count.value;
  }

  if (sectionId === 10) {

    codeStart = start;
    codeEnd = end;
    break;
  }

  p = end;
}

console.log("IMPORT FUNCTIONS :", importCount);

let q = codeStart;

const countInfo = readU32Leb(q);
const codeCount = countInfo.value;
q = countInfo.next;

console.log("CODE FUNCTIONS   :", codeCount);
console.log("TOTAL FUNCTIONS  :", importCount + codeCount);
console.log("");

/*

 * Scan every function body
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 */

for (let codeIndex = 0; codeIndex < codeCount; codeIndex++) {

  const sizeInfo = readU32Leb(q);
  const bodySize = sizeInfo.value;
  q = sizeInfo.next;

  const bodyStart = q;
  const bodyEnd = q + bodySize;

  const functionIndex = importCount + codeIndex;

  let ip = bodyStart;

  /*
   * locals
   */
  const localInfo = readU32Leb(ip);
  const localGroups = localInfo.value;
  ip = localInfo.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < localGroups; i++) {

    const count = readU32Leb(ip);
    ip = count.next;

    ip++;
  }

  const instructions = [];

  while (ip < bodyEnd) {

    const ins = decode(ip);

    instructions.push(ins);

    ip = ins.end;

    if (ins.op === 0x0b)
      break;
  }

  for (let i = 0; i < instructions.length; i++) {

    const ins = instructions[i];

    if (
      ins.op === 0x10 &&
      ins.text === "call func=122"
    ) {

      console.log("############################################################");
      console.log(
        `CALL FUNC 122  FROM FUNC ${functionIndex}`
      );
      console.log("############################################################");

      console.log(
        "CALL ADDRESS :",
        `0x${ins.start.toString(16)}`
      );

      console.log("");
      console.log("PREVIOUS INSTRUCTIONS");
      console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

      const from = Math.max(0, i - 10);

      for (let j = from; j < i; j++) {

        const x = instructions[j];

        console.log(
          `0x${x.start.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ${x.text}`
        );
      }

      console.log("");
      console.log(">>> TARGET");
      console.log(
        `0x${ins.start.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ${ins.text}`
      );

      console.log("");
      console.log("NEXT INSTRUCTIONS");
      console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

      const to =
        Math.min(instructions.length, i + 6);

      for (let j = i + 1; j < to; j++) {

        const x = instructions[j];

        console.log(
          `0x${x.start.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ${x.text}`
        );
      }

      console.log("");
    }
  }

  q = bodyEnd;
}

import fs from 'node:fs';

const file = 'G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm';
const wasm = fs.readFileSync(file);

function uleb(pos) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        if (pos >= wasm.length) throw new Error('Unexpected EOF');
        const byte = wasm[pos++];
        value += (byte & 0x7f) * (2 ** shift);
        if ((byte & 0x80) === 0) return { value, next: pos };
        shift += 7;
        if (shift > 35) throw new Error('Invalid ULEB');
    }
}

function hex(n) {
    return '0x' + n.toString(16).padStart(8, '0');
}

function bytesHex(start, end) {
    return [...wasm.subarray(start, end)]
        .map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0'))
        .join(' ');
}

let pos = 8;
let codeStart = -1;
let codeEnd = -1;

while (pos < wasm.length) {
    const id = wasm[pos++];
    const size = uleb(pos);
    const start = size.next;
    const end = start + size.value;

    if (id === 10) {
        codeStart = start;
        codeEnd = end;
        break;
    }

    pos = end;
}

if (codeStart < 0) throw new Error('Code section not found');

const count = uleb(codeStart);
pos = count.next;

const bodies = [];

for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < count.value; ordinal++) {
    const size = uleb(pos);
    const start = size.next;
    const end = start + size.value;

    bodies.push({
        func: 83 + ordinal,
        start,
        end,
        size: size.value
    });

    pos = end;
}

const targets = [
    {
        name: 'ADDR_2530A4',
        bytes: [0x41, 0xa4, 0xe1, 0x94, 0x01]
    },
    {
        name: 'ADDR_252D04',
        bytes: [0x41, 0x84, 0xda, 0x94, 0x01]
    }
];

console.log('FUNC 1769 ADDRESS REFERENCE SEARCH');
console.log('READ ONLY - OFFLINE');
console.log('============================================================');
console.log(`FILE SIZE = ${wasm.length}`);
console.log(`FUNCTION BODIES = ${bodies.length}`);
console.log('NOTE: RAW BYTE MATCHES ARE NOT PROOF OF INSTRUCTION BOUNDARIES.');

for (const target of targets) {
    console.log('\n============================================================');
    console.log(`${target.name} : ${target.bytes.map(b =>
        b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join(' ')}`);

    let hits = 0;

    for (const body of bodies) {
        for (let p = body.start; p + target.bytes.length <= body.end; p++) {
            let match = true;

            for (let j = 0; j < target.bytes.length; j++) {
                if (wasm[p + j] !== target.bytes[j]) {
                    match = false;
                    break;
                }
            }

            if (!match) continue;

            hits++;

            const from = Math.max(body.start, p - 12);
            const to = Math.min(body.end, p + target.bytes.length + 20);

            console.log(`\nHIT #${hits}`);
            console.log(`OWNER FUNC = ${body.func}`);
            console.log(`BODY = ${hex(body.start)} .. ${hex(body.end)}`);
            console.log(`MATCH = ${hex(p)}`);
            console.log(`CONTEXT ${hex(from)} .. ${hex(to)}`);
            console.log(bytesHex(from, to));
        }
    }

    console.log(`\nTOTAL RAW MATCHES = ${hits}`);
}

console.log('\n============================================================');
console.log('DONE - READ ONLY - NO WASM EXECUTION');

FILE: scripts\findFunc2034TableRefs.mjs

import fs from 'node:fs';

const wasmPath =
    'G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm';

const TARGET_FUNC = 2034;
const bytes = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

let pos = 8;
let elementSectionFound = false;
let matches = 0;

function readU32Leb() {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (pos < bytes.length) {
        const b = bytes[pos++];
        result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return result >>> 0;
        }

        shift += 7;

        if (shift > 35) {
            throw new Error('Invalid unsigned LEB128');
        }
    }

    throw new Error('Unexpected EOF reading ULEB128');
}

function readS32Leb() {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let b = 0;

    do {
        if (pos >= bytes.length) {
            throw new Error('Unexpected EOF reading SLEB128');
        }

        b = bytes[pos++];
        result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;
    } while (b & 0x80);

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
        result |= (~0 << shift);
    }

    return result | 0;
}

function skipConstExpr() {
    let result = null;

    while (pos < bytes.length) {
        const opcode = bytes[pos++];

        if (opcode === 0x0b) {
            return result;
        }

        switch (opcode) {
            case 0x41:
                result = readS32Leb();
                break;

            case 0x23:
                readU32Leb();
                result = null;
                break;

            case 0xd0:
                pos++; // heap type, MVP/reference-type encoding
                result = null;
                break;

            case 0xd2:
                result = readU32Leb();
                break;

            case 0x42:
                readS64Leb();
                result = null;
                break;

            case 0x43:
                pos += 4;
                result = null;
                break;

            case 0x44:
                pos += 8;
                result = null;
                break;

            default:
                throw new Error(
                    `Unsupported const-expression opcode 0x${opcode.toString(16)}`
                );
        }
    }

    throw new Error('Unterminated constant expression');
}

function readS64Leb() {
    let shift = 0;
    let b;

    do {
        if (pos >= bytes.length) {
            throw new Error('Unexpected EOF reading S64 LEB128');
        }

        b = bytes[pos++];
        shift += 7;

        if (shift > 70) {
            throw new Error('Invalid S64 LEB128');
        }
    } while (b & 0x80);
}

function readFunctionIndexVector(segmentInfo, activeOffset) {
    const count = readU32Leb();

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        const funcIndex = readU32Leb();

        if (funcIndex === TARGET_FUNC) {
            matches++;

            console.log(
                `MATCH: func=${funcIndex}, segment=${segmentInfo}, ` +
                `entry=${i}, tableSlot=${
                    activeOffset === null
                        ? 'not specified (passive/declarative)'
                        : activeOffset + i
                }`
            );
        }
    }
}

function readExpressionVector(segmentInfo, activeOffset) {
    const count = readU32Leb();

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        const exprResult = skipConstExpr();

        if (exprResult === TARGET_FUNC) {
            matches++;

            console.log(
                `MATCH: ref.func=${TARGET_FUNC}, segment=${segmentInfo}, ` +
                `entry=${i}, tableSlot=${
                    activeOffset === null
                        ? 'not specified (passive/declarative)'
                        : activeOffset + i
                }`
            );
        }
    }
}

function readElemKind() {
    const kind = bytes[pos++];

    if (kind !== 0) {
        throw new Error(`Unexpected elemkind ${kind}`);
    }
}

function readRefType() {
    const type = bytes[pos++];

    if (
        type !== 0x70 &&
        type !== 0x6f &&
        type !== 0x6e &&
        type !== 0x6d
    ) {
        throw new Error(
            `Unexpected reference type 0x${type.toString(16)}`
        );
    }
}

console.log('ROOSTER WASM - FUNCTION 2034 TABLE REFERENCE SCAN');
console.log('READ ONLY - OFFLINE - NO WASM EXECUTION');
console.log(`WASM SIZE: ${bytes.length}`);
console.log(`TARGET FUNCTION: ${TARGET_FUNC}`);

while (pos < bytes.length) {
    const sectionId = bytes[pos++];
    const sectionSize = readU32Leb();
    const sectionEnd = pos + sectionSize;

    if (sectionEnd > bytes.length) {
        throw new Error('Section exceeds file boundary');
    }

    if (sectionId !== 9) {
        pos = sectionEnd;
        continue;
    }

    elementSectionFound = true;

    console.log('\nELEMENT SECTION FOUND');
    console.log(`SECTION SIZE: ${sectionSize}`);

    const segmentCount = readU32Leb();
    console.log(`SEGMENTS: ${segmentCount}`);

    for (let seg = 0; seg < segmentCount; seg++) {
        const flags = readU32Leb();
        let activeOffset = null;

        console.log(`\nSEGMENT ${seg} FLAGS=${flags}`);

        switch (flags) {
            case 0:
                activeOffset = skipConstExpr();
                readFunctionIndexVector(seg, activeOffset);
                break;

            case 1:
                readElemKind();
                readFunctionIndexVector(seg, null);
                break;

            case 2:
                readU32Leb(); // table index
                activeOffset = skipConstExpr();
                readElemKind();
                readFunctionIndexVector(seg, activeOffset);
                break;

            case 3:
                readElemKind();
                readFunctionIndexVector(seg, null);
                break;

            case 4:
                activeOffset = skipConstExpr();
                readExpressionVector(seg, activeOffset);
                break;

            case 5:
                readRefType();
                readExpressionVector(seg, null);
                break;

            case 6:
                readU32Leb(); // table index
                activeOffset = skipConstExpr();
                readRefType();
                readExpressionVector(seg, activeOffset);
                break;

            case 7:
                readRefType();
                readExpressionVector(seg, null);
                break;

            default:
                throw new Error(`Unknown element segment flags ${flags}`);
        }

        if (pos > sectionEnd) {
            throw new Error('Segment parsing exceeded section boundary');
        }
    }

    pos = sectionEnd;
}

console.log('\n============================================================');

if (!elementSectionFound) {
    console.log('NO ELEMENT SECTION FOUND');
} else if (matches === 0) {
    console.log('NO REFERENCES TO FUNCTION 2034 FOUND IN ELEMENT SECTION');
} else {
    console.log(`TOTAL MATCHES: ${matches}`);
}

console.log('READ ONLY - NO WASM EXECUTION - NO TRANSACTIONS');

FILE: scripts\findFunc4095Callers.mjs

import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";

const FILE = path.resolve(
  process.cwd(),
  "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm"
);

const IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS = 83;
const TARGETS = [4095, 4097, 4098, 4099, 4100, 4101];

function readULEB(buf, state) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (state.pos < buf.length) {
    const b = buf[state.pos++];
    value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if (!(b & 0x80)) return value;
    shift += 7;

    if (shift > 49) throw new Error("Invalid ULEB");
  }

  throw new Error("Unexpected EOF");
}

function encodeULEB(value) {
  const out = [];

  do {
    let b = value & 0x7f;
    value = Math.floor(value / 128);

    if (value !== 0) b |= 0x80;
    out.push(b);
  } while (value !== 0);

  return out;
}

function getFunctionBodies(buf) {
  const state = { pos: 8 };

  while (state.pos < buf.length) {
    const id = buf[state.pos++];
    const size = readULEB(buf, state);
    const start = state.pos;
    const end = start + size;

    if (end > buf.length) throw new Error("Invalid section size");

    if (id === 10) {
      const cs = { pos: start };
      const count = readULEB(buf, cs);
      const bodies = [];

      for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        const bodySize = readULEB(buf, cs);
        const bodyStart = cs.pos;
        const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bodySize;

        if (bodyEnd > end) throw new Error("Invalid function body");

        bodies.push({
          fn: IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS + i,
          start: bodyStart,
          end: bodyEnd
        });

        cs.pos = bodyEnd;
      }

      return bodies;
    }

    state.pos = end;
  }

  throw new Error("Code section not found");
}

function hex(n) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function dump(buf, start, end) {
  for (let p = start; p < end; p += 16) {
    const q = Math.min(p + 16, end);
    const bytes = [];

    for (let i = p; i < q; i++) {
      bytes.push(buf[i].toString(16).padStart(2, "0"));
    }

    console.log(`${hex(p)}  ${bytes.join(" ")}`);
  }
}

function main() {
  const buf = fs.readFileSync(FILE);
  const bodies = getFunctionBodies(buf);

  console.log("DIRECT CALLER SEARCH");
  console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log(`FILE SIZE = ${buf.length}`);
  console.log(`FUNCTION BODIES = ${bodies.length}`);
  console.log("");

  for (const target of TARGETS) {
    const pattern = [0x10, ...encodeULEB(target)];

    console.log(`TARGET FUNCTION ${target}`);
    console.log(
      `CALL PATTERN = ${pattern.map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join(" ")}`
    );
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

    let hits = 0;

    for (const body of bodies) {
      for (let p = body.start; p + pattern.length <= body.end; p++) {
        let matches = true;

        for (let i = 0; i < pattern.length; i++) {
          if (buf[p + i] !== pattern[i]) {
            matches = false;
            break;
          }
        }

        if (!matches) continue;

        hits++;

        console.log(
          `OWNER FUNC ${body.fn} | BODY ${hex(body.start)}..${hex(body.end)} | HIT ${hex(p)}`
        );

        dump(
          buf,
          Math.max(body.start, p - 12),
          Math.min(body.end, p + 24)
        );
        console.log("");
      }
    }

    console.log(`RAW MATCH COUNT = ${hits}`);
    console.log("");
  }

  console.log("DONE - NO WASM EXECUTION");
  console.log("Raw byte matches are candidates and require instruction-boundary validation.");
}

try {
  main();
} catch (error) {
  console.error("FAILED:", error);
  process.exitCode = 1;
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\findFuncRefs8927_8952.js

const fs = require("fs");

const FILE =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGETS = new Set([8927, 8952]);

const b = fs.readFileSync(FILE);

function leb(pos) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const x = b[pos++];

        value |= (x & 0x7f) << shift;

        if (!(x & 0x80)) {
            return {
                value: value >>> 0,
                next: pos
            };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC REFERENCES 8927 / 8952");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("WASM SIZE :", b.length);
console.log("TARGETS   : 8927, 8952");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let pos = 8;
let codeStart = -1;
let codeSize = 0;

while (pos < b.length) {

    const sectionId = b[pos++];

    const sr = leb(pos);
    const size = sr.value;
    pos = sr.next;

    if (sectionId === 10) {
        codeStart = pos;
        codeSize = size;
        break;
    }

    pos += size;
}

if (codeStart < 0) {
    throw new Error("CODE section not found");
}

let p = codeStart;

const countR = leb(p);
const bodyCount = countR.value;
p = countR.next;

const hits = [];

for (let bodyIndex = 0; bodyIndex < bodyCount; bodyIndex++) {

    const sizeR = leb(p);
    const bodySize = sizeR.value;
    p = sizeR.next;

    const bodyStart = p;
    const bodyEnd = p + bodySize;

    let q = bodyStart;

    const localsR = leb(q);
    const localDeclCount = localsR.value;
    q = localsR.next;

    // Skip local declarations
    for (let i = 0; i < localDeclCount; i++) {
        const n = leb(q);
        q = n.next;
        q++;
    }

    while (q < bodyEnd) {

        const at = q;
        const op = b[q++];

        // ref.func
        if (op === 0xd2) {

            const r = leb(q);
            q = r.next;

            if (TARGETS.has(r.value)) {

                hits.push({
                    kind: "ref.func",
                    target: r.value,
                    caller: bodyIndex + 83,
                    bodyIndex,
                    at
                });
            }

            continue;
        }

        // call_ref
        if (op === 0x14) {
            hits.push({
                kind: "call_ref",
                target: null,
                caller: bodyIndex + 83,
                bodyIndex,
                at
            });
            continue;
        }

        // call_indirect
        if (op === 0x11) {

            const typeR = leb(q);
            q = typeR.next;

            // table index immediate
            q++;

            continue;
        }

        // direct call
        if (op === 0x10) {

            const r = leb(q);
            q = r.next;
            continue;
        }

        // local/global
        if (
            op === 0x20 ||
            op === 0x21 ||
            op === 0x22 ||
            op === 0x23 ||
            op === 0x24
        ) {
            const r = leb(q);
            q = r.next;
            continue;
        }

        // constants
        if (op === 0x41 || op === 0x42) {
            const r = leb(q);
            q = r.next;
            continue;
        }

        // loads/stores
        if (op >= 0x28 && op <= 0x3e) {

            let r = leb(q);
            q = r.next;

            r = leb(q);
            q = r.next;

            continue;
        }

        // memory.size / memory.grow
        if (op === 0x3f || op === 0x40) {
            q++;
            continue;
        }

        // blocks
        if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04) {
            q++;
            continue;
        }

        // branches
        if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
            const r = leb(q);
            q = r.next;
            continue;
        }

        // br_table
        if (op === 0x0e) {

            const r = leb(q);
            q = r.next;

            for (let i = 0; i < r.value; i++) {
                const x = leb(q);
                q = x.next;
            }

            const d = leb(q);
            q = d.next;

            continue;
        }

        // bulk memory
        if (op === 0xfc) {

            const sub = leb(q);
            q = sub.next;

            if (
                sub.value === 8 ||
                sub.value === 9 ||
                sub.value === 10 ||
                sub.value === 11
            ) {
                const a = leb(q);
                q = a.next;

                if (sub.value === 8 || sub.value === 10) {
                    const c = leb(q);
                    q = c.next;
                }
            }

            continue;
        }

        // SIMD
        if (op === 0xfd) {
            const sub = leb(q);
            q = sub.next;
            continue;
        }
    }

    p = bodyEnd;
}

console.log("REF.FUNC HITS :", hits.length);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (const h of hits) {

    console.log(
        h.kind.padEnd(12),
        "TARGET",
        h.target ?? "-",
        "CALLER FUNC",
        h.caller,
        "AT",
        "0x" + h.at.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")
    );
}

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("READ ONLY : YES");
console.log("NETWORK   : NO");
console.log("TRANSACTIONS : NO");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\findFunction122BranchTargetsV1.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const START = 0x1226d;
const END = 0x1295b;

const TARGET = 0x12680;

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function readU32LEB(buf: Buffer, p: { v: number }): number {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[p.v++];

        result += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;

        shift += 7;
    }

    return result;
}

function readS32LEB(buf: Buffer, p: { v: number }): number {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let b = 0;

    while (true) {
        b = buf[p.v++];

        result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;

        shift += 7;
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
        result |= (~0 << shift);
    }

    return result | 0;
}

function readBlockType(buf: Buffer, p: { v: number }): string {
    const b = buf[p.v++];

    if (b === 0x40) return "void";

    if (b === 0x7f) return "i32";
    if (b === 0x7e) return "i64";
    if (b === 0x7d) return "f32";
    if (b === 0x7c) return "f64";

    /*
     * blocktype can also be a signed type index.
     * We only need its byte consumption here.
     */
    if (b & 0x80) {
        while (true) {
            const x = wasm[p.v++];

            if ((x & 0x80) === 0) break;
        }
    }

    return `0x${b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;
}

function H(n: number): string {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

interface Frame {
    kind: "block" | "loop" | "if";
    start: number;
    depth: number;
}

/*
 * We cannot calculate a branch target from byte offset alone.
 * A branch target is a control-label depth.
 *
 * Therefore we first collect all control frames.
 */
const frames: Frame[] = [];

const branches: {
    pc: number;
    kind: string;
    depth: number;
    currentDepth: number;
}[] = [];

const control: Frame[] = [];

const p = { v: START };

let unknownCount = 0;

while (p.v < END) {
    const pc = p.v;
    const op = wasm[p.v++];

    try {

        switch (op) {

            /* ---------------- CONTROL ---------------- */

            case 0x00:
                break;

            case 0x01:
                break;

            case 0x02:
                readBlockType(wasm, p);

                control.push({
                    kind: "block",
                    start: pc,
                    depth: control.length
                });

                frames.push(control[control.length - 1]);
                break;

            case 0x03:
                readBlockType(wasm, p);

                control.push({
                    kind: "loop",
                    start: pc,
                    depth: control.length
                });

                frames.push(control[control.length - 1]);
                break;

            case 0x04:
                readBlockType(wasm, p);

                control.push({
                    kind: "if",
                    start: pc,
                    depth: control.length
                });

                frames.push(control[control.length - 1]);
                break;

            case 0x05:
                break;

            case 0x0b:
                control.pop();
                break;

            case 0x0c: {
                const depth = readU32LEB(wasm, p);

                branches.push({
                    pc,
                    kind: "br",
                    depth,
                    currentDepth: control.length
                });

                break;
            }

            case 0x0d: {
                const depth = readU32LEB(wasm, p);

                branches.push({
                    pc,
                    kind: "br_if",
                    depth,
                    currentDepth: control.length
                });

                break;
            }

            case 0x0e: {
                const count = readU32LEB(wasm, p);

                const depths: number[] = [];

                for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
                    depths.push(readU32LEB(wasm, p));
                }

                const defaultDepth = readU32LEB(wasm, p);

                for (const d of depths) {
                    branches.push({
                        pc,
                        kind: "br_table",
                        depth: d,
                        currentDepth: control.length
                    });
                }

                branches.push({
                    pc,
                    kind: "br_table_default",
                    depth: defaultDepth,
                    currentDepth: control.length
                });

                break;
            }

            case 0x0f:
                break;

            case 0x10:
                readU32LEB(wasm, p);
                break;

            case 0x11:
                readU32LEB(wasm, p);
                readU32LEB(wasm, p);
                break;

            /* ---------------- LOCALS ---------------- */

            case 0x20:
            case 0x21:
            case 0x22:
            case 0x23:
            case 0x24:
                readU32LEB(wasm, p);
                break;

            /* ---------------- MEMORY ---------------- */

            case 0x28:
            case 0x29:
            case 0x2a:
            case 0x2b:
            case 0x2c:
            case 0x2d:
            case 0x2e:
            case 0x2f:
            case 0x30:
            case 0x31:
            case 0x32:
            case 0x33:
            case 0x34:
            case 0x35: {
                readU32LEB(wasm, p);
                readU32LEB(wasm, p);
                break;
            }

            case 0x36:
            case 0x37:
            case 0x38:
            case 0x39:
            case 0x3a:
            case 0x3b:
            case 0x3c:
            case 0x3d:
            case 0x3e:
            case 0x3f:
            case 0x40:
                readU32LEB(wasm, p);
                readU32LEB(wasm, p);
                break;

            /* ---------------- CONST ---------------- */

            case 0x41:
                readS32LEB(wasm, p);
                break;

            case 0x42: {
                /*
                 * signed i64 LEB
                 */
                while (true) {
                    const b = wasm[p.v++];

                    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
                }

                break;
            }

            case 0x43:
                p.v += 4;
                break;

            case 0x44:
                p.v += 8;
                break;

            /* ---------------- PREFIXES ---------------- */

            case 0xfc: {
                /*
                 * Bulk-memory / misc prefix.
                 * Decode only common immediate shapes conservatively.
                 */
                const sub = readU32LEB(wasm, p);

                if (
                    sub === 8 ||
                    sub === 9 ||
                    sub === 10 ||
                    sub === 11
                ) {
                    /*
                     * Conservative handling:
                     * these forms have 1-2 immediates.
                     */
                    if (sub === 8 || sub === 10) {
                        readU32LEB(wasm, p);
                    }

                    if (sub === 8 || sub === 10) {
                        readU32LEB(wasm, p);
                    }
                }

                break;
            }

            default:
                /*
                 * Ordinary numeric instructions have no immediate.
                 */
                break;
        }

    } catch {
        unknownCount++;

        console.log(
            `${H(pc)}  PARSE_ERROR opcode=0x${op.toString(16)}`
        );

        break;
    }
}

/* ============================================================
   OUTPUT
   ============================================================ */

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 122 BRANCH TARGET ANALYSIS");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log(`Function range : ${H(START)} .. ${H(END)}`);
console.log(`Target region  : ${H(TARGET)}`);

console.log("");
console.log("CONTROL FRAMES:");
console.log("");

for (const f of frames) {
    console.log(
        `${H(f.start)}  ${f.kind.padEnd(6)} depth=${f.depth}`
    );
}

console.log("");
console.log("BRANCHES:");
console.log("");

for (const b of branches) {

    const targetFrame =
        b.depth < b.currentDepth
            ? frames.find(f => f.depth === b.currentDepth - 1 - b.depth)
            : undefined;

    console.log(
        `${H(b.pc)}  ${b.kind.padEnd(18)}` +
        ` depth=${b.depth}` +
        ` currentDepth=${b.currentDepth}` +
        ` targetFrame=${targetFrame ? H(targetFrame.start) : "UNKNOWN"}`
    );
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" BRANCHES NEAR 0x12680");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const b of branches) {

    if (
        b.pc >= 0x12500 &&
        b.pc <= 0x12680
    ) {
        console.log(
            `${H(b.pc)}  ${b.kind} depth=${b.depth} current=${b.currentDepth}`
        );
    }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" DONE");
console.log("============================================================");

import fs from "fs";

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const START = 0x0001226d;
const END   = 0x0001295c;

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 205 ALL STORE SEARCH v13");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();
console.log(`WASM size : ${wasm.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log(`Function 205 : 0x${START.toString(16)} .. 0x${END.toString(16)}`);
console.log();

function readU32Leb(pos: number) {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (pos < wasm.length) {
        const b = wasm[pos++];
        result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            return { value: result >>> 0, next: pos };

        shift += 7;
        if (shift > 35)
            throw new Error(`Invalid ULEB at 0x${pos.toString(16)}`);
    }

    throw new Error("EOF");
}

function readS32Leb(pos: number) {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let b = 0;

    while (pos < wasm.length) {
        b = wasm[pos++];
        result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40))
                result |= (-1 << shift);

            return { value: result | 0, next: pos };
        }

        if (shift > 35)
            throw new Error(`Invalid SLEB at 0x${pos.toString(16)}`);
    }

    throw new Error("EOF");
}

function hex(n: number) {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function dumpContext(at: number, radius = 18) {
    const a = Math.max(START, at - radius);
    const b = Math.min(END, at + radius + 1);

    console.log(`\nContext around ${hex(at)}:`);

    for (let p = a; p < b; p++) {
        const op = wasm[p];

        if (p === at)
            process.stdout.write(`>>> ${hex(p)} : `);
        else
            process.stdout.write(`    ${hex(p)} : `);

        console.log(`0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`);
    }
}

const stores = [
    { op: 0x37, name: "i64.store" },
    { op: 0x3b, name: "i64.store32" },
    { op: 0x3a, name: "i64.store16" },
    { op: 0x39, name: "i64.store8" },
    { op: 0x36, name: "i32.store" },
    { op: 0x3c, name: "i32.store8" },
    { op: 0x3d, name: "i32.store16" },
    { op: 0x38, name: "i32.store16?" }
];

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" STORE INSTRUCTIONS");
console.log("============================================================");

let count = 0;

for (let p = START; p < END;) {
    const op = wasm[p];

    if (stores.some(x => x.op === op)) {
        let name = stores.find(x => x.op === op)!.name;

        try {
            const align = readU32Leb(p + 1);
            const offset = readU32Leb(align.next);

            console.log(
                `STORE #${++count} @ ${hex(p)}  ` +
                `${name.padEnd(14)} ` +
                `align=${align.value} ` +
                `offset=${offset.value} (${hex(offset.value)})`
            );
        } catch {
            console.log(
                `STORE #${++count} @ ${hex(p)}  ${name}  [decode error]`
            );
        }
    }

    p++;
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TOTAL STORES : ${count}`);
console.log("============================================================");

import * as fs from "fs";

const WASM = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 205 CALL #26 CONSUMER SEARCH v1");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();
console.log(`WASM size : ${buf.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log(`CALL #26  : 0x126b4`);
console.log(`DEST      : L2 + 0x1b0`);
console.log(`LOW WORD  : L2 + 0x1b0`);
console.log(`HIGH WORD : L2 + 0x1b8`);
console.log();

function hex(n: number, w = 8) {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(w, "0");
}

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const b = buf[p++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value >>> 0, next: p };
        }

        shift += 7;
        if (shift > 35) break;
    }

    throw new Error(`Invalid ULEB at ${hex(pos)}`);
}

function isLocalGet2(p: number): boolean {
    // local.get 2 = 0x20 0x02
    return buf[p] === 0x20 && buf[p + 1] === 0x02;
}

function isI32Const432(p: number): boolean {
    // i32.const 432 = 0x41 0xb0 0x03
    return (
        buf[p] === 0x41 &&
        buf[p + 1] === 0xb0 &&
        buf[p + 2] === 0x03
    );
}

function isI32Const440(p: number): boolean {
    // i32.const 440 = 0x41 0xb8 0x03
    return (
        buf[p] === 0x41 &&
        buf[p + 1] === 0xb8 &&
        buf[p + 2] === 0x03
    );
}

function dump(p: number, radius = 32) {
    const a = Math.max(0, p - radius);
    const b = Math.min(buf.length, p + radius);

    console.log(`\nWINDOW around ${hex(p)}`);
    console.log("--------------------------------------------");

    for (let x = a; x < b;) {
        const byte = buf[x];

        let size = 1;
        let name = `op=0x${byte.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;

        if (byte === 0x20) {
            const u = readULEB(x + 1);
            size = u.next - x;
            name = `local.get ${u.value}`;
        }
        else if (byte === 0x22) {
            const u = readULEB(x + 1);
            size = u.next - x;
            name = `local.tee ${u.value}`;
        }
        else if (byte === 0x21) {
            const u = readULEB(x + 1);
            size = u.next - x;
            name = `local.set ${u.value}`;
        }
        else if (byte === 0x41) {
            const u = readULEB(x + 1);
            size = u.next - x;
            name = `i32.const ${u.value}`;
        }
        else if (byte === 0x42) {
            const u = readULEB(x + 1);
            size = u.next - x;
            name = `i64.const ${u.value}`;
        }
        else if (byte === 0x6a) {
            name = "i32.add";
        }
        else if (byte === 0x6b) {
            name = "i32.sub";
        }
        else if (byte === 0x29) {
            // i64.load
            let q = x + 1;
            const align = readULEB(q);
            q = align.next;
            const off = readULEB(q);
            size = off.next - x;
            name = `i64.load align=${align.value} offset=${off.value}`;
        }
        else if (byte === 0x37) {
            // i64.store
            let q = x + 1;
            const align = readULEB(q);
            q = align.next;
            const off = readULEB(q);
            size = off.next - x;
            name = `i64.store align=${align.value} offset=${off.value}`;
        }
        else if (byte === 0x10) {
            const u = readULEB(x + 1);
            size = u.next - x;
            name = `call ${u.value}`;
        }

        const mark = x === p ? " <<<" : "";
        console.log(`${hex(x)}  ${name}${mark}`);

        x += size;
    }
}

let hits432 = 0;
let hits440 = 0;

console.log("SEARCH 1: exact local2 + 432 pattern");
console.log("--------------------------------------------");

for (let p = 0; p + 5 < buf.length; p++) {
    if (isLocalGet2(p) && isI32Const432(p + 2)) {
        hits432++;

        console.log(
            `HIT #${hits432} @ ${hex(p)} : local.get 2 ; i32.const 432`
        );

        dump(p, 40);
    }
}

console.log();
console.log("SEARCH 2: exact local2 + 440 pattern");
console.log("--------------------------------------------");

for (let p = 0; p + 5 < buf.length; p++) {
    if (isLocalGet2(p) && isI32Const440(p + 2)) {
        hits440++;

        console.log(
            `HIT #${hits440} @ ${hex(p)} : local.get 2 ; i32.const 440`
        );

        dump(p, 40);
    }
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" SUMMARY");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`L2 + 0x1b0 (432) hits : ${hits432}`);
console.log(`L2 + 0x1b8 (440) hits : ${hits440}`);
console.log();

if (hits432 === 0 && hits440 === 0) {
    console.log("No direct local2+offset pattern found.");
    console.log("Next step will be symbolic tracking of the frame address.");
}
else {
    console.log("Direct frame-address references found.");
    console.log("Inspect the windows above for i64.load / i64.store consumers.");
}
