import * as fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 205 CALL #26 CONSUMER SEARCH v2");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();
console.log(`WASM size : ${buf.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log(`CALL #26  : 0x126b4`);
console.log(`DEST      : L2 + 0x1b0`);
console.log(`LOW WORD  : L2 + 0x1b0`);
console.log(`HIGH WORD : L2 + 0x1b8`);
console.log();

function hex(n: number, w = 8): string {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(w, "0");
}

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const b = buf[p++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return {
                value: value >>> 0,
                next: p,
            };
        }

        shift += 7;

        if (shift > 35) {
            throw new Error(`Invalid ULEB at ${hex(pos)}`);
        }
    }

    throw new Error(`Unexpected EOF while reading ULEB at ${hex(pos)}`);
}

function isLocalGet2(p: number): boolean {
    return buf[p] === 0x20 && buf[p + 1] === 0x02;
}

function isI32Const432(p: number): boolean {
    return (
        buf[p] === 0x41 &&
        buf[p + 1] === 0xb0 &&
        buf[p + 2] === 0x03
    );
}

function isI32Const440(p: number): boolean {
    return (
        buf[p] === 0x41 &&
        buf[p + 1] === 0xb8 &&
        buf[p + 2] === 0x03
    );
}

function dump(p: number, radius = 40): void {
    const a = Math.max(0, p - radius);
    const b = Math.min(buf.length, p + radius);

    console.log();
    console.log(`WINDOW around ${hex(p)}`);
    console.log("--------------------------------------------");

    let x = a;

    while (x < b) {
        const byte = buf[x];

        let size = 1;
        let name = `op=0x${byte.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;

        try {
            if (byte === 0x20) {
                const u = readULEB(x + 1);
                size = u.next - x;
                name = `local.get ${u.value}`;
            }

            else if (byte === 0x21) {
                const u = readULEB(x + 1);
                size = u.next - x;
                name = `local.set ${u.value}`;
            }

            else if (byte === 0x22) {
                const u = readULEB(x + 1);
                size = u.next - x;
                name = `local.tee ${u.value}`;
            }

            else if (byte === 0x41) {
                const u = readULEB(x + 1);
                size = u.next - x;
                name = `i32.const ${u.value}`;
            }

            else if (byte === 0x42) {
                const u = readULEB(x + 1);
                size = u.next - x;
                name = `i64.const ${u.value}`;
            }

            else if (byte === 0x6a) {
                name = "i32.add";
            }

            else if (byte === 0x6b) {
                name = "i32.sub";
            }

            else if (byte === 0x29) {
                let q = x + 1;

                const align = readULEB(q);
                q = align.next;

                const off = readULEB(q);

                size = off.next - x;

                name =
                    `i64.load align=${align.value} ` +
                    `offset=${off.value}`;
            }

            else if (byte === 0x37) {
                let q = x + 1;

                const align = readULEB(q);
                q = align.next;

                const off = readULEB(q);

                size = off.next - x;

                name =
                    `i64.store align=${align.value} ` +
                    `offset=${off.value}`;
            }

            else if (byte === 0x10) {
                const u = readULEB(x + 1);

                size = u.next - x;

                name = `call ${u.value}`;
            }
        }
        catch {
            name = `op=0x${byte.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")} [decode-error]`;
            size = 1;
        }

        const mark = x === p ? " <<< TARGET" : "";

        console.log(`${hex(x)}  ${name}${mark}`);

        x += size;
    }
}

let hits432 = 0;
let hits440 = 0;

    if (isLocalGet2(p) && isI32Const432(p + 2)) {
        hits432++;

        console.log(
            `HIT #${hits432} @ ${hex(p)} : ` +
            `local.get 2 ; i32.const 432`

    }

console.log(`L2 + 0x1b8 (440) hits : ${hits440}`);

    console.log("No direct local2+offset references found.");
    console.log("Need symbolic frame-address tracking next.");
}

    console.log("Direct frame-address references found.");

        "Inspect the windows for i64.load / i64.store consumers."

import * as fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

const START = 0x126b4 + 1;
const END   = 0x1295b;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 205 CALL #26 FRAME READ SEARCH v4");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();
console.log(`WASM size : ${buf.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log(`Range     : 0x${START.toString(16)} .. 0x${END.toString(16)}`);
console.log();

function hex(n: number): string {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function uleb(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const b = buf[p++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: p };
        }

        shift += 7;
        if (shift > 35) {
            throw new Error("ULEB too long");
        }
    }

    throw new Error("EOF");
}

function sleb(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const b = buf[p++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
                value -= 2 ** shift;
            }

            return { value, next: p };
        }

        if (shift > 35) {
            throw new Error("SLEB too long");
        }
    }

    throw new Error("EOF");
}

function dump(pos: number, radius = 16) {
    const a = Math.max(START, pos - radius);
    const z = Math.min(END, pos + radius);

    console.log(`\n${hex(pos)} surrounding bytes:`);

    for (let p = a; p < z; p += 16) {
        const e = Math.min(z, p + 16);

        const bytes = [];

        for (let q = p; q < e; q++) {
            bytes.push(buf[q].toString(16).padStart(2, "0"));
        }

        console.log(`${hex(p)}  ${bytes.join(" ")}`);
    }
}

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("A) DIRECT L2 + CONSTANT REFERENCES");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let hit = 0;

for (let p = START; p < END - 4; p++) {

    // local.get 2
    if (buf[p] !== 0x20 || buf[p + 1] !== 0x02) {
        continue;
    }

    // i32.const
    if (buf[p + 2] !== 0x41) {
        continue;
    }

    try {
        const c = sleb(p + 3);

        if (
            c.value === 432 ||
            c.value === 440 ||
            c.value === 424 ||
            c.value === 448
        ) {
            hit++;

            console.log();
            console.log(`HIT #${hit} @ ${hex(p)}`);
            console.log(
                `  local.get 2 + i32.const ${c.value}`
            );

            dump(p, 24);
        }
    } catch {
        // Ignore malformed/non-instruction bytes.
    }
}

console.log();
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("B) ALL i64.load INSTRUCTIONS");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let loads = 0;

for (let p = START; p < END - 4; p++) {

    // i64.load = 0x29
    if (buf[p] !== 0x29) {
        continue;
    }

    try {
        const a = uleb(p + 1);
        const o = uleb(a.next);

        loads++;

        console.log(
            `i64.load @ ${hex(p)} ` +
            `align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`
        );
    } catch {
        console.log(`i64.load candidate @ ${hex(p)} [decode failed]`);
    }
}

console.log();
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("C) POSSIBLE POINTER-COPY PATTERNS");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

// Search for local.get 2 followed by an i32 constant,
// regardless of whether the constant is one of our target offsets.

let ptrs = 0;

for (let p = START; p < END - 4; p++) {

    if (buf[p] !== 0x20 || buf[p + 1] !== 0x02) {
        continue;
    }

    if (buf[p + 2] !== 0x41) {
        continue;
    }

    try {
        const c = sleb(p + 3);

        ptrs++;

        console.log(
            `${hex(p)}  local.get 2 + i32.const ${c.value}`
        );
    } catch {
        // ignore
    }
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SUMMARY");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`Target frame refs : ${hit}`);
console.log(`i64.load count    : ${loads}`);
console.log(`L2 pointer refs   : ${ptrs}`);
console.log();
console.log("Target offsets:");
console.log("  432 = 0x1b0  CALL #26 LOW");
console.log("  440 = 0x1b8  CALL #26 HIGH");
console.log("  424 = 0x1a8");
console.log("  448 = 0x1c0");

FILE: scripts\findFunction205Frame1F0WritesV11.ts

import fs from "fs";

const path =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(path);

const START = 0x1226d;
const END   = 0x126b4;

const TARGET = 0x1f0; // L2 + 496

function uleb(p: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (p < b.length) {
    const x = b[p++];

    value |= (x & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((x & 0x80) === 0) {
      return { value: value >>> 0, next: p };
    }

    shift += 7;

    if (shift > 35) {
      throw new Error("Invalid ULEB");
    }
  }

  throw new Error("EOF");
}

function hex(x: number) {
  return "0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function decodeContext(pos: number) {
  const from = Math.max(START, pos - 55);
  const to   = Math.min(END, pos + 25);

  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

  let p = from;

  while (p < to) {
    const op = b[p];

    if (
      op === 0x20 ||
      op === 0x21 ||
      op === 0x22 ||
      op === 0x23
    ) {
      const r = uleb(p + 1);

      const name =
        op === 0x20 ? "local.get" :
        op === 0x21 ? "local.set" :
        op === 0x22 ? "local.tee" :
        "global.get";

      console.log(
        `${hex(p)} ${name} ${r.value}`
      );

      p = r.next;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x41) {
      const r = uleb(p + 1);

      console.log(
        `${hex(p)} i32.const ${r.value}`
      );

      p = r.next;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x42) {
      const r = uleb(p + 1);

      console.log(
        `${hex(p)} i64.const ${r.value}`
      );

      p = r.next;
      continue;
    }

    // i64.load
    if (op === 0x29) {
      const r = uleb(p + 2);

      console.log(
        `${hex(p)} i64.load offset=${r.value}`
      );

      p = r.next;
      continue;
    }

    // i64.store
    if (op === 0x37) {
      const r = uleb(p + 2);

      console.log(
        `${hex(p)} i64.store offset=${r.value}`
      );

      p = r.next;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x6a) {
      console.log(`${hex(p)} i32.add`);
      p++;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x6b) {
      console.log(`${hex(p)} i32.sub`);
      p++;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x7c) {
      console.log(`${hex(p)} i64.add`);
      p++;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x7d) {
      console.log(`${hex(p)} i64.sub`);
      p++;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x7e) {
      console.log(`${hex(p)} i64.mul`);
      p++;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x86) {
      console.log(`${hex(p)} i64.shl`);
      p++;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x88) {
      console.log(`${hex(p)} i64.shr_u`);
      p++;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x10) {
      const r = uleb(p + 1);

      console.log(
        `${hex(p)} call ${r.value}`
      );

      p = r.next;
      continue;
    }

    console.log(
      `${hex(p)} op=0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
    );

    p++;
  }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 205 FRAME +0x1F0 WRITE SEARCH v11");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("");
console.log(`WASM size : ${b.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log(`Range     : ${hex(START)} .. ${hex(END)}`);
console.log(`Target    : L2 + 0x1F0 (${TARGET})`);
console.log("");

let refs = 0;
let stores = 0;

for (let p = START; p < END - 5; p++) {

  // Exact address expression:
  //
  // local.get 2
  // i32.const 496
  // i32.add

  if (b[p] !== 0x20) continue;

  const r1 = uleb(p + 1);

  if (r1.value !== 2) continue;

  if (b[r1.next] !== 0x41) continue;

  const r2 = uleb(r1.next + 1);

  if (r2.value !== TARGET) continue;

  if (b[r2.next] !== 0x6a) continue;

  refs++;

  console.log(
    `FRAME REF #${refs} @ ${hex(p)}`
  );

  console.log(
    `  address = L2 + 0x1F0`
  );

  console.log(
    `  expression end = ${hex(r2.next)}`
  );

  // Inspect the next ~20 bytes to determine whether
  // the address feeds a store or something else.

  const after = r2.next;

  let foundStore = false;

  for (let q = after; q < Math.min(after + 20, END); q++) {
    if (b[q] === 0x37) {
      foundStore = true;
      stores++;
      console.log(
        `  POSSIBLE i64.store @ ${hex(q)}`
      );
      break;
    }
  }

  if (!foundStore) {
    console.log(
      `  No nearby i64.store detected`
    );
  }

  decodeContext(p);

  console.log("");
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`FRAME REFERENCES : ${refs}`);
console.log(`POSSIBLE STORES  : ${stores}`);
console.log("============================================================");

import fs from "fs";

const path =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(path);

const START = 0x1226d;
const END   = 0x126b4;

const TARGETS = [0x60, 0x170];

function uleb(p: number) {
  let v = 0;
  let s = 0;

  while (p < b.length) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };
    s += 7;
  }

  throw new Error("EOF in ULEB");
}

function hex(x: number) {
  return "0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 205 FRAME SLOT SEARCH v9");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("");
console.log(`WASM size : ${b.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log(`Range     : ${hex(START)} .. ${hex(END)}`);
console.log("Targets   : L2+0x60 , L2+0x170");
console.log("");

let hits = 0;

for (let p = START; p < END - 5; p++) {

  // Look specifically for:
  // local.get 2
  // i32.const OFFSET
  // i32.add
  //
  // This identifies actual frame-address construction.

  if (b[p] !== 0x20) continue;

  const r1 = uleb(p + 1);

  if (r1.value !== 2) continue;

  if (b[r1.next] !== 0x41) continue;

  const r2 = uleb(r1.next + 1);

  if (!TARGETS.includes(r2.value)) continue;

  if (b[r2.next] !== 0x6a) continue;

  hits++;

  console.log(
    `FRAME REF #${hits} @ ${hex(p)}`
  );

  console.log(
    `  L2 + 0x${r2.value.toString(16)}`
  );

  console.log(
    `  address expression ends @ ${hex(r2.next)}`
  );

  const a = Math.max(START, p - 20);
  const z = Math.min(END, r2.next + 35);

  console.log("  context:");

  for (let q = a; q < z; q++) {
    const op = b[q];

    if (op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) {
      const r = uleb(q + 1);

      const name =
        op === 0x20 ? "local.get" :
        op === 0x21 ? "local.set" :
        "local.tee";

      console.log(
        `    ${hex(q)} ${name} ${r.value}`
      );

      q = r.next - 1;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x41) {
      const r = uleb(q + 1);
      console.log(
        `    ${hex(q)} i32.const ${r.value}`
      );
      q = r.next - 1;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x42) {
      const r = uleb(q + 1);
      console.log(
        `    ${hex(q)} i64.const ${r.value}`
      );
      q = r.next - 1;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x29) {
      const r = uleb(q + 2);
      console.log(
        `    ${hex(q)} i64.load offset=${r.value}`
      );
      q = r.next - 1;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x36) {
      const r = uleb(q + 2);
      console.log(
        `    ${hex(q)} i64.store offset=${r.value}`
      );
      q = r.next - 1;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x6a) {
      console.log(`    ${hex(q)} i32.add`);
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x10) {
      const r = uleb(q + 1);
      console.log(`    ${hex(q)} call ${r.value}`);
      q = r.next - 1;
      continue;
    }
  }

  console.log("");
}

FILE: scripts\findFunction205L10ProducersV8.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const buf = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const START = 0x1226d;
const END   = 0x126b4;

// Function 205:
// local 10 is an i64 local.
// local.set 10  = 0x21 0x0a
// local.tee 10 = 0x22 0x0a

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (pos < buf.length) {
    const b = buf[pos++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return { value, next: pos };
    }

    shift += 7;
    if (shift > 35) throw new Error("Invalid ULEB");
  }

  throw new Error("EOF");
}

function hex(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function dumpAround(pos: number, before = 36, after = 44) {
  const a = Math.max(START, pos - before);
  const b = Math.min(END, pos + after);

  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

  let p = a;

  while (p < b) {
    const op = buf[p];

    if (op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) {
      const r = readULEB(p + 1);

      console.log(
        `${hex(p)}  ${op === 0x21 ? "local.set " : "local.tee "}${r.value}`
      );

      p = r.next;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x41) {
      const r = readULEB(p + 1);
      console.log(`${hex(p)}  i32.const ${r.value}`);
      p = r.next;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x42) {
      const r = readULEB(p + 1);
      console.log(`${hex(p)}  i64.const ${r.value}`);
      p = r.next;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x20 || op === 0x23) {
      const r = readULEB(p + 1);
      console.log(
        `${hex(p)}  ${op === 0x20 ? "local.get " : "global.get "}${r.value}`
      );
      p = r.next;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x6a) {
      console.log(`${hex(p)}  i32.add`);
      p++;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x6b) {
      console.log(`${hex(p)}  i32.sub`);
      p++;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x7c) {
      console.log(`${hex(p)}  i64.add`);
      p++;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x7d) {
      console.log(`${hex(p)}  i64.sub`);
      p++;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x86) {
      console.log(`${hex(p)}  i64.shl`);
      p++;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x88) {
      console.log(`${hex(p)}  i64.shr_u`);
      p++;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x94) {
      console.log(`${hex(p)}  i64.and`);
      p++;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x7e) {
      console.log(`${hex(p)}  i64.mul`);
      p++;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x29) {
      const align = buf[p + 1];
      const r = readULEB(p + 2);

      console.log(
        `${hex(p)}  i64.load align=${align} offset=${r.value}`
      );

      p = r.next;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x10) {
      const r = readULEB(p + 1);
      console.log(`${hex(p)}  call ${r.value}`);
      p = r.next;
      continue;
    }

    // Unknown opcode: do NOT try to interpret following bytes.
    console.log(`${hex(p)}  op=0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`);
    p++;
  }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 205 L10 PRODUCER SEARCH v8");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("");
console.log(`WASM size : ${buf.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log(`Range     : ${hex(START)} .. ${hex(END)}`);
console.log(`Target    : local 10`);
console.log("");

let hits = 0;

for (let p = START; p < END; p++) {
  const op = buf[p];

  if ((op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) && buf[p + 1] === 0x0a) {
    hits++;

    console.log("");
    console.log(
      `${op === 0x21 ? "local.set" : "local.tee"} 10 @ ${hex(p)}`
    );

    dumpAround(p);
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TOTAL L10 PRODUCERS : ${hits}`);
console.log("============================================================");

FILE: scripts\findFunction205L13L29ProducersV7.ts

import * as fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

const START = 0x1226d;
const END   = 0x126b4;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 205 L13 / L29 PRODUCER SEARCH v7");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();

function hex(n: number): string {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function uleb(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const b = buf[p++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: p };
        }

        shift += 7;

        if (shift > 35)
            throw new Error("ULEB");
    }

    throw new Error("EOF");
}

function sleb(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const b = buf[p++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {

            if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40))
                value -= 2 ** shift;

            return { value, next: p };
        }

        if (shift > 35)
            throw new Error("SLEB");
    }

    throw new Error("EOF");
}

const targets = new Set([13, 29]);

for (let p = START; p < END - 2; p++) {

    // local.tee / local.set
    if (buf[p] !== 0x22 && buf[p] !== 0x21)
        continue;

    let x;

    try {
        x = uleb(p + 1);
    } catch {
        continue;
    }

    if (!targets.has(x.value))
        continue;

    console.log();
    console.log("============================================================");

    console.log(
        `${buf[p] === 0x22 ? "local.tee" : "local.set"} ` +
        `${x.value} @ ${hex(p)}`
    );

    console.log(
        `TARGET = L${x.value}`
    );

    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

    const a = Math.max(START, p - 40);
    const z = Math.min(END, x.next + 20);

    let q = a;

    while (q < z) {

        const op = buf[q];

        if (op === 0x20) {
            try {
                const v = uleb(q + 1);

                console.log(
                    `${hex(q)}  local.get ${v.value}`
                );

                q = v.next;
                continue;
            } catch {}
        }

        if (op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) {
            try {
                const v = uleb(q + 1);

                console.log(
                    `${hex(q)}  ` +
                    `${op === 0x22 ? "local.tee" : "local.set"} ` +
                    `${v.value}`
                );

                q = v.next;
                continue;
            } catch {}
        }

        if (op === 0x41) {
            try {
                const v = sleb(q + 1);

                console.log(
                    `${hex(q)}  i32.const ${v.value}`
                );

                q = v.next;
                continue;
            } catch {}
        }

        if (op === 0x42) {
            try {
                const v = sleb(q + 1);

                console.log(
                    `${hex(q)}  i64.const ${v.value}`
                );

                q = v.next;
                continue;
            } catch {}
        }

        if (op === 0x10) {
            try {
                const v = uleb(q + 1);

                console.log(
                    `${hex(q)}  call ${v.value}`
                );

                q = v.next;
                continue;
            } catch {}
        }

        const names: Record<number,string> = {
            0x29: "i64.load",
            0x36: "i32.store",
            0x37: "i64.store",
            0x6a: "i32.add",
            0x6b: "i32.sub",
            0x7c: "i64.add",
            0x7d: "i64.sub",
            0x7e: "i64.mul",
            0x83: "i64.and",
            0x84: "i64.or",
            0x85: "i64.xor",
            0x86: "i64.shl",
            0x87: "i64.shr_s",
            0x88: "i64.shr_u",
            0x0d: "br_if",
            0x0b: "end"
        };

        console.log(
            `${hex(q)}  ${names[op] ?? "op=0x" + op.toString(16)}`
        );

        q++;
    }
}

console.log();

console.log("END");

import fs from "fs";

const path =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(path);

const START = 0x1226d;
const END   = 0x1242b;

function uleb(p: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (p < b.length) {
    const x = b[p++];

    value |= (x & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((x & 0x80) === 0) {
      return { value: value >>> 0, next: p };
    }

    shift += 7;
    if (shift > 35) throw new Error("Invalid ULEB");
  }

  throw new Error("EOF");
}

function hex(x: number) {
  return "0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function decodeAround(pos: number) {
  const from = Math.max(START, pos - 42);
  const to   = Math.min(END, pos + 50);

  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

  let p = from;

  while (p < to) {
    const op = b[p];

    if (op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) {
      const r = uleb(p + 1);

      const name =
        op === 0x20 ? "local.get" :
        op === 0x21 ? "local.set" :
        "local.tee";

      console.log(
        `${hex(p)} ${name} ${r.value}`
      );

      p = r.next;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x41) {
      const r = uleb(p + 1);
      console.log(
        `${hex(p)} i32.const ${r.value}`
      );
      p = r.next;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x42) {
      const r = uleb(p + 1);
      console.log(
        `${hex(p)} i64.const ${r.value}`
      );
      p = r.next;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x29) {
      const r = uleb(p + 2);

      console.log(
        `${hex(p)} i64.load offset=${r.value}`
      );

      p = r.next;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x36) {
      const r = uleb(p + 2);

      console.log(
        `${hex(p)} i64.store offset=${r.value}`
      );

      p = r.next;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x10) {
      const r = uleb(p + 1);

      console.log(
        `${hex(p)} call ${r.value}`
      );

      p = r.next;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x6a) {
      console.log(`${hex(p)} i32.add`);
      p++;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x6b) {
      console.log(`${hex(p)} i32.sub`);
      p++;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x7c) {
      console.log(`${hex(p)} i64.add`);
      p++;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x7d) {
      console.log(`${hex(p)} i64.sub`);
      p++;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x7e) {
      console.log(`${hex(p)} i64.mul`);
      p++;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x86) {
      console.log(`${hex(p)} i64.shl`);
      p++;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x88) {
      console.log(`${hex(p)} i64.shr_u`);
      p++;
      continue;
    }

    console.log(
      `${hex(p)} op=0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
    );

    p++;
  }
}

  );

}

import fs from "fs";

const path =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(path);

const START = 0x1226d;
const END   = 0x1295c;

function uleb(p: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (p < b.length) {
    const x = b[p++];

    value |= (x & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((x & 0x80) === 0) {
      return { value: value >>> 0, next: p };
    }

    shift += 7;

    if (shift > 35) {
      throw new Error("Invalid ULEB");
    }
  }

  throw new Error("EOF");
}

function hex(x: number) {
  return "0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function dump(pos: number) {
  const from = Math.max(START, pos - 60);
  const to   = Math.min(END, pos + 15);

  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

  let p = from;

  while (p < to) {
    const op = b[p];

    if (
      op === 0x20 ||
      op === 0x21 ||
      op === 0x22 ||
      op === 0x23
    ) {
      const r = uleb(p + 1);

      const name =
        op === 0x20 ? "local.get" :
        op === 0x21 ? "local.set" :
        op === 0x22 ? "local.tee" :
        "global.get";

      console.log(`${hex(p)} ${name} ${r.value}`);
      p = r.next;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x41) {
      const r = uleb(p + 1);
      console.log(`${hex(p)} i32.const ${r.value}`);
      p = r.next;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x42) {
      const r = uleb(p + 1);
      console.log(`${hex(p)} i64.const ${r.value}`);
      p = r.next;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x29) {
      const r = uleb(p + 2);
      console.log(`${hex(p)} i64.load offset=${r.value}`);
      p = r.next;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x37) {
      const r = uleb(p + 2);
      console.log(`${hex(p)} i64.store offset=${r.value}`);
      p = r.next;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x36) {
      const r = uleb(p + 1);
      console.log(`${hex(p)} store opcode=0x36 imm=${r.value}`);
      p = r.next;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x6a) {
      console.log(`${hex(p)} i32.add`);
      p++;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x6b) {
      console.log(`${hex(p)} i32.sub`);
      p++;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x7c) {
      console.log(`${hex(p)} i64.add`);
      p++;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x7d) {
      console.log(`${hex(p)} i64.sub`);
      p++;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x7e) {
      console.log(`${hex(p)} i64.mul`);
      p++;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x86) {
      console.log(`${hex(p)} i64.shl`);
      p++;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x88) {
      console.log(`${hex(p)} i64.shr_u`);
      p++;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x10) {
      const r = uleb(p + 1);
      console.log(`${hex(p)} call ${r.value}`);
      p = r.next;
      continue;
    }

    p++;
  }
}

const fs = require("fs");

const WASM = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

// Function 259 boundaries from the current reverse-engineering work.

const START = 0x14555;
const END   = 0x14950;

function readULEB(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[p++];

        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;

        shift += 7;
    }

    return {
        value: value >>> 0,
        next: p
    };
}

function hex(n: number) {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 259 - LOCAL 9 DATA-FLOW SEARCH v22");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`WASM size : ${wasm.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log(`RANGE     : ${hex(START)} - ${hex(END)}`);
console.log("");

let p = START;
let hits = 0;

while (p < END) {

    const at = p;
    const op = wasm[p];

    // local.get
    if (op === 0x20) {
        const r = readULEB(p + 1);

        if (r.value === 9) {
            hits++;

            const from = Math.max(START, at - 16);
            const to = Math.min(END, r.next + 24);

            console.log(`HIT #${hits} @ ${hex(at)}`);
            console.log(`TYPE: local.get 9`);
            console.log(`RAW : ${Array.from(wasm.slice(from, to))
                .map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
                .join(" ")}`);
            console.log("");
        }

        p = r.next;
        continue;
    }

    // local.set
    if (op === 0x21) {
        const r = readULEB(p + 1);

        if (r.value === 9) {
            hits++;

            const from = Math.max(START, at - 16);
            const to = Math.min(END, r.next + 24);

            console.log(`HIT #${hits} @ ${hex(at)}`);
            console.log(`TYPE: local.set 9`);
            console.log(`RAW : ${Array.from(wasm.slice(from, to))
                .map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
                .join(" ")}`);
            console.log("");
        }

        p = r.next;
        continue;
    }

    // local.tee
    if (op === 0x22) {
        const r = readULEB(p + 1);

        if (r.value === 9) {
            hits++;

            const from = Math.max(START, at - 16);
            const to = Math.min(END, r.next + 24);

            console.log(`HIT #${hits} @ ${hex(at)}`);
            console.log(`TYPE: local.tee 9`);

                .map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))

            console.log("");

        p = r.next;
        continue;

import fs from "fs";

const wasmPath =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const TARGET_FUNCTION = 3632;
const EXPECTED_SIZE = 5093309;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 3632 CALLER SEARCH v48");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("");
console.log(`WASM size : ${wasm.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log(`Target    : function ${TARGET_FUNCTION}`);
console.log("");

if (wasm.length !== EXPECTED_SIZE) {
    throw new Error(
        `Unexpected WASM size: ${wasm.length} (expected ${EXPECTED_SIZE})`
    );
}

function encodeULEB(value: number): number[] {
    const out: number[] = [];

    do {
        let b = value & 0x7f;
        value >>>= 7;

        if (value !== 0) {
            b |= 0x80;
        }

        out.push(b);
    } while (value !== 0);

    return out;
}

function hexByte(b: number): string {
    return b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0");
}

function hexAddr(n: number): string {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

const targetULEB = encodeULEB(TARGET_FUNCTION);

console.log(
    `Function index : ${TARGET_FUNCTION}`
);

console.log(
    `ULEB128        : ${targetULEB.map(hexByte).join(" ")}`
);

console.log(
    `CALL encoding   : 10 ${targetULEB.map(hexByte).join(" ")}`
);

console.log("");

const pattern = [
    0x10,
    ...targetULEB
];

console.log("Searching exact CALL encoding...");
console.log("");

const hits: number[] = [];

for (let i = 0; i <= wasm.length - pattern.length; i++) {
    let match = true;

    for (let j = 0; j < pattern.length; j++) {
        if (wasm[i + j] !== pattern[j]) {
            match = false;
            break;
        }
    }

    if (match) {
        hits.push(i);
    }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" EXACT CALL HITS");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log(`Total exact byte hits : ${hits.length}`);
console.log("");

for (const addr of hits) {
    const start = Math.max(0, addr - 12);
    const end = Math.min(wasm.length, addr + pattern.length + 12);

    const bytes: string[] = [];

    for (let p = start; p < end; p++) {
        const marker = p === addr ? ">" : " ";
        bytes.push(
            `${marker}${hexAddr(p)}:${hexByte(wasm[p])}`
        );
    }

    console.log("--------------------------------------------");
    console.log(`CALL 3632 @ ${hexAddr(addr)}`);
    console.log(bytes.join(" "));
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" CLASSIFICATION");
console.log("============================================================");

/*
 * A raw byte-pattern hit is not automatically a code reference.
 * We therefore report the surrounding bytes, but do not claim that
 * every hit is executable code.
 */

let likelyCode = 0;

for (const addr of hits) {
    const next = wasm[addr + pattern.length];

    /*
     * Common instruction opcodes following a call.
     * This is only a heuristic and is NOT treated as proof.
     */
    const likelyNextOpcodes = new Set([
        0x0b, // end
        0x0f, // return
        0x10, // call
        0x11, // call_indirect
        0x1a, // drop
        0x1b, // select
        0x20, // local.get
        0x21, // local.set
        0x22, // local.tee
        0x23, // global.get
        0x24, // global.set
        0x28, // i32.load
        0x29, // i64.load
        0x2a, // f32.load
        0x2b, // f64.load
        0x3c, // i64.store8
        0x3f, // memory.size
        0x40, // memory.grow
        0x41, // i32.const
        0x42, // i64.const
        0x43, // f32.const
        0x44, // f64.const
        0x45, // i32.eqz
        0x46, // i32.eq
        0x47, // i32.ne
        0x48, // i32.lt_s
        0x49, // i32.lt_u
        0x4a, // i32.gt_s
        0x4b, // i32.gt_u
        0x4c, // i32.le_s
        0x4d, // i32.le_u
        0x4e, // i32.ge_s
        0x4f, // i32.ge_u
        0x50, // i64.eqz
        0x51, // i64.eq
        0x52, // i64.ne
        0x53, // i64.lt_s
        0x54, // i64.lt_u
        0x55, // i64.gt_s
        0x56, // i64.gt_u
        0x57, // i64.le_s
        0x58, // i64.le_u
        0x59, // i64.ge_s
        0x5a, // i64.ge_u
        0x61, // f64.eq
        0x62, // f64.ne
        0x63, // f64.lt
        0x64, // f64.gt
        0x65, // f64.le
        0x66, // f64.ge
        0x6a, // i32.add
        0x6b, // i32.sub
        0x6c, // i32.mul
        0x71, // i32.and
        0x72, // i32.or
        0x73, // i32.xor
        0x76, // i32.shl
        0x77, // i32.shr_s
        0x78, // i32.shr_u
        0x7c, // i64.add
        0x7d, // i64.sub
        0x7e, // i64.mul
        0x83, // i64.and
        0x84, // i64.or
        0x86, // i64.shl
        0x88, // i64.shr_u
    ]);

    if (likelyNextOpcodes.has(next)) {
        likelyCode++;
    }
}

console.log(
    `Hits with code-like next byte : ${likelyCode}`
);

console.log(
    `Raw/non-classified hits        : ${hits.length - likelyCode}`
);

console.log("");
console.log("IMPORTANT:");
console.log(
    "A byte-pattern hit is not by itself proof of an executable call."
);
console.log(
    "Next step is to decode the surrounding caller function(s)."
);

import * as fs from "fs";

const file =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(file);

const TARGET = 5079;

function uleb(p: number) {
  let v = 0;
  let s = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];

    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;

    if (!(x & 0x80))
      return { v, p };

    s += 7;
  }
}

let p = 8;

let elementStart = -1;
let elementEnd = -1;

while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p++];

  const z = uleb(p);
  p = z.p;

  const start = p;
  const end = p + z.v;

  if (id === 9) {
    elementStart = start;
    elementEnd = end;
  }

  p = end;
}

if (elementStart < 0)
  throw new Error("Element section not found");

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNCTION 5079 ELEMENT/TABLE SEARCH v43");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log(
  `Element section : 0x${elementStart.toString(16)} .. 0x${elementEnd.toString(16)}`
);

console.log(`Target global function : ${TARGET}`);
console.log("");

/*
   We deliberately search only for the exact ULEB128 representation
   of function index 5079.

   5079 decimal = 0x13d7
   ULEB128 = d7 27
*/

const pattern = [0xd7, 0x27];

let hits = 0;

for (
  let i = elementStart;
  i <= elementEnd - pattern.length;
  i++
) {
  if (
    b[i] === pattern[0] &&
    b[i + 1] === pattern[1]
  ) {

    hits++;

    console.log(
      `RAW ULEB HIT #${hits} @ 0x${i.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
    );

    const from = Math.max(elementStart, i - 16);
    const to = Math.min(elementEnd, i + 18);

    console.log(
      Array.from(b.slice(from, to))
        .map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
        .join(" ")
    );

    console.log("");
  }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TOTAL ELEMENT RAW HITS : ${hits}`);
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("END V43");
console.log("============================================================");

  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function uleb(offset: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = offset;

  while (true) {
    const x = body[p++];

    value += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if ((x & 0x80) === 0) {
      return {
        value,
        next: p,
      };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function bytesHex(from: number, to: number) {
  return Array.from(body.subarray(from, to))
    .map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
    .join(" ");
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 87 CALL-SITE SEARCH IN FUNCTION 122 v1");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();

console.log(`Function 122 body : ${hex(START)} .. ${hex(END)}`);
console.log(`Body size         : ${body.length}`);
console.log(`Target function   : ${TARGET_FUNCTION}`);
console.log(`Call encoding     : 10 57`);
console.log();

let count = 0;

for (let p = 0; p < body.length; p++) {
  if (body[p] !== 0x10) continue;

  const x = uleb(p + 1);

  if (x.value !== TARGET_FUNCTION) continue;

  count++;

  const contextBefore = Math.max(0, p - 24);
  const contextAfter = Math.min(body.length, x.next + 24);

  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
  console.log(`CALL #${count}`);
  console.log(`WASM offset : ${hex(START + p)}`);
  console.log(`Body offset : ${hex(p)}`);
  console.log(`Function    : ${x.value}`);
  console.log();

  console.log(
    `Before/after bytes:`
  );

  console.log(
    `  ${hex(START + contextBefore)} .. ${hex(
      START + contextAfter
    )}`
  );

  console.log(
    `  ${bytesHex(contextBefore, contextAfter)}`
  );

  console.log();

  console.log("Immediate bytes:");
  console.log(
    `  ${bytesHex(p, x.next)}`
  );

  console.log();
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TOTAL CALL 87 = ${count}`);
console.log("============================================================");

const fs = require("fs");

const WASM =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET = 8952;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FIND FUNCTION 8952 + TABLE/ELEMENT REFERENCES");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

console.log("WASM SIZE :", buf.length);
console.log("TARGET    :", TARGET);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

function readU32(pos) {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[pos++];
        result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
        shift += 7;
    }

    return { value: result >>> 0, next: pos };
}

function sectionName(type) {
    return {
        0: "CUSTOM",
        1: "TYPE",
        2: "IMPORT",
        3: "FUNCTION",
        4: "TABLE",
        5: "MEMORY",
        6: "GLOBAL",
        7: "EXPORT",
        8: "START",
        9: "ELEMENT",
        10: "CODE",
        11: "DATA",
        12: "DATA_COUNT"
    }[type] || `SECTION_${type}`;
}

let pos = 8;

while (pos < buf.length) {

    const sectionStart = pos;
    const type = buf[pos++];

    const sizeInfo = readU32(pos);
    const sectionSize = sizeInfo.value;
    pos = sizeInfo.next;

    const payloadStart = pos;
    const payloadEnd = payloadStart + sectionSize;

    console.log(
        `SECTION ${type} ${sectionName(type)} START 0x${sectionStart.toString(16)} SIZE ${sectionSize}`
    );

    // ---------------------------------------------------------
    // FUNCTION SECTION
    // ---------------------------------------------------------
    if (type === 3) {

        const countInfo = readU32(pos);
        const count = countInfo.value;

        console.log("  FUNCTION COUNT :", count);
        console.log("  TARGET         :", TARGET);

        if (TARGET >= count) {
            console.log("  TARGET IS OUTSIDE FUNCTION SECTION");
        } else {
            console.log("  TARGET FUNCTION INDEX IS VALID");
        }
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------
    // ELEMENT SECTION
    // ---------------------------------------------------------
    if (type === 9) {

        let p = payloadStart;

        const segmentInfo = readU32(p);
        const segmentCount = segmentInfo.value;
        p = segmentInfo.next;

        console.log("  ELEMENT SEGMENTS :", segmentCount);

        for (let s = 0; s < segmentCount; s++) {

            const segStart = p;

            const flagsInfo = readU32(p);
            const flags = flagsInfo.value;
            p = flagsInfo.next;

            console.log(
                `\n  ELEMENT SEGMENT ${s} @ 0x${segStart.toString(16)} FLAGS ${flags}`
            );

            /*
             * We do not attempt to fully decode every possible
             * element-expression form here.
             *
             * Instead scan the raw element payload for the
             * little-endian ULEB128 representation of TARGET.
             */

            const scanStart = p;

            // Conservative local scan until next segment / section.
            // The exact semantic interpretation will be done after
            // locating candidate offsets.
            let scanEnd = payloadEnd;

            // Search raw bytes for ULEB128(TARGET)
            const targetBytes = [];

            let x = TARGET >>> 0;
            while (x >= 0x80) {
                targetBytes.push((x & 0x7f) | 0x80);
                x >>>= 7;
            }
            targetBytes.push(x);

            for (let q = scanStart; q + targetBytes.length <= scanEnd; q++) {

                let match = true;

                for (let k = 0; k < targetBytes.length; k++) {
                    if (buf[q + k] !== targetBytes[k]) {
                        match = false;
                        break;
                    }
                }

                if (match) {
                    console.log(
                        `    POSSIBLE FUNC ${TARGET} REF @ 0x${q.toString(16)}`
                    );

                    const a = Math.max(payloadStart, q - 12);
                    const b = Math.min(payloadEnd, q + targetBytes.length + 12);

                    console.log(
                        "    BYTES:",
                        [...buf.subarray(a, b)]
                            .map(v => v.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
                            .join(" ")
                    );
                }
            }

            /*
             * We intentionally do not advance using guessed
             * segment structure here. The raw scan above is only
             * a candidate finder.
             */
            p = payloadEnd;
            break;
        }
    }

    pos = payloadEnd;
}

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("END");
console.log("============================================================");

import fs from "fs";

const wasmPath =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const TARGET_FUNC = 122;

function readULEB(pos: { value: number }): number {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[pos.value++];

        result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return result >>> 0;
        }

        shift += 7;

        if (shift > 35) {
            throw new Error("ULEB128 too large");
        }
    }
}

function hex(n: number): string {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

        const b = wasm[pos.value++];

        result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    throw new Error(
        `Function ${TARGET_FUNC} is outside code-section function count`
    );
}

 * body vector after imported functions are accounted for.
 *
 * Therefore first report the raw body ordinal.
 */

let targetBodyEnd = -1;
let targetBodySize = -1;

    const bodySizePos = { value: q };
    const bodySize = readULEB(bodySizePos);
    const bodySizeFieldEnd = bodySizePos.value;

    const bodyStart = bodySizeFieldEnd;
    const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bodySize;

    if (i === TARGET_FUNC) {
        targetBodyStart = bodyStart;
        targetBodyEnd = bodyEnd;
        targetBodySize = bodySize;

        console.log("============================================================");
        console.log(" TARGET BODY FOUND");
        console.log("============================================================");
        console.log(`body ordinal : ${i}`);
        console.log(`body size    : ${bodySize}`);
        console.log(`body start   : ${hex(bodyStart)}`);
        console.log(`body end     : ${hex(bodyEnd)}`);
        console.log(
            `body range   : ${hex(bodyStart)} .. ${hex(bodyEnd - 1)}`
        );
        console.log(
            `size field   : ${hex(q)} .. ${hex(bodySizeFieldEnd - 1)}`
        );
        console.log();

        /*
         * Print first 32 bytes so we can independently verify
         * the body boundary and local declarations.
         */
        const previewEnd = Math.min(bodyStart + 32, bodyEnd);

        console.log("FIRST BYTES:");
        for (let x = bodyStart; x < previewEnd; x += 16) {
            const e = Math.min(x + 16, previewEnd);

            const bytes = [];
            for (let y = x; y < e; y++) {
                bytes.push(
                    wasm[y].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")
                );
            }

            console.log(
                `${hex(x)}  ${bytes.join(" ")}`
            );
        }

        console.log();
        break;
    }

    q = bodyEnd;
}

    throw new Error("Target function body was not found");

    );

import fs from "fs";

const WASM_PATH =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_FUNCTION = 504;

function hex(n: number): string {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function readULEB(
  buf: Buffer,
  pos: number
): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = buf[pos++];

    value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return { value, next: pos };
    }

    shift += 7;

    if (shift > 35) {
      throw new Error("ULEB128 too large");
    }
  }
}

function findSection(
  wasm: Buffer,
  wantedId: number
): { start: number; end: number } | null {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const id = wasm[p++];

    const sizeInfo = readULEB(wasm, p);
    const size = sizeInfo.value;
    p = sizeInfo.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + size;

    if (id === wantedId) {
      return { start, end };
    }

    p = end;
  }

  return null;
}

import fs from "fs";

const WASM_PATH =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_FUNCTION = 87;

function hex(n: number): string {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function readULEB(
  buf: Buffer,
  pos: number
): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = buf[pos++];

    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return { value, next: pos };
    }

    shift += 7;

    if (shift > 35) {
      throw new Error("ULEB128 too large");
    }
  }
}

function findSection(
  wasm: Buffer,
  wantedId: number
): { start: number; end: number } | null {
  if (
    wasm[0] !== 0x00 ||
    wasm[1] !== 0x61 ||
    wasm[2] !== 0x73 ||
    wasm[3] !== 0x6d
  ) {
    throw new Error("Not a valid WASM file");
  }

  let p = 8;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const id = wasm[p++];

    const sizeInfo = readULEB(wasm, p);
    const size = sizeInfo.value;
    p = sizeInfo.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + size;

    if (id === wantedId) {
      return { start, end };
    }

    p = end;
  }

  return null;
}

import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

const START = 0x964d1;
const END   = 0x96654;

function hx(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(6, "0");
}

function uleb(p: number) {
  let v = 0;
  let s = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = buf[p++];
    v |= (b & 0x7f) << s;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
    s += 7;
  }

  return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };
}

function sleb32(p: number) {
  let v = 0;
  let s = 0;
  let b = 0;

  while (true) {
    b = buf[p++];
    v |= (b & 0x7f) << s;
    s += 7;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
  }

  if (s < 32 && (b & 0x40))
    v |= (~0 << s);

  return { value: v | 0, next: p };
}

function memarg(p: number) {
  const a = uleb(p);
  const o = uleb(a.next);

  return {
    align: a.value,
    offset: o.value,
    next: o.next
  };
}

function opName(op: number) {
  const m: Record<number, string> = {
    0x20: "local.get",
    0x21: "local.set",
    0x22: "local.tee",
    0x23: "global.get",
    0x24: "global.set",
    0x29: "i64.load",
    0x37: "i64.store",
    0x41: "i32.const",
    0x6a: "i32.add",
    0x6b: "i32.sub",
    0x74: "i32.shl",
    0x7c: "i64.add",
    0x7d: "i64.sub",
    0x7e: "i64.mul",
    0x03: "loop",
    0x02: "block",
    0x0b: "end",
    0x0d: "br_if",
    0x0c: "br",
    0x10: "call"
  };

  return m[op] ?? `UNKNOWN(0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")})`;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" G208 / G144 STORE SEARCH V9");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log(" REAL LEB128");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();

console.log("Range:", hx(START), "..", hx(END));
console.log();

let p = START;

while (p < END) {
  const at = p;
  const op = buf[p++];

  let extra = "";

  if (op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 || op === 0x22 ||
      op === 0x23 || op === 0x24) {

    const r = uleb(p);
    p = r.next;
    extra = ` ${r.value}`;

  } else if (op === 0x41) {

    const r = sleb32(p);
    p = r.next;
    extra = ` ${r.value}`;

  } else if (op === 0x29 || op === 0x37) {

    const m = memarg(p);
    p = m.next;
    extra = ` align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`;

    if (op === 0x37) {
      console.log(
        `${hx(at)}  i64.store${extra}  <<< STORE CANDIDATE`
      );
      continue;
    }

  } else if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d || op === 0x10) {

    const r = uleb(p);
    p = r.next;
    extra = ` ${r.value}`;

  } else if (op === 0x42) {

    let r = 0n;
    let shift = 0n;
    let b = 0;

    while (true) {
      b = buf[p++];
      r |= BigInt(b & 0x7f) << shift;
      shift += 7n;
      if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
    }

    extra = ` ${r}`;

  }

  console.log(`${hx(at)}  ${opName(op)}${extra}`);
}

import fs from "fs";
import path from "path";

const ROOT = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin";

const EXT = new Set([
    ".js",
    ".mjs",
    ".cjs",
    ".ts",
    ".html",
]);

const SKIP_DIRS = new Set([
    "node_modules",
    ".git",
    "dist",
    "build",
    "coverage",
    ".cache",
    "temp",
    "scripts",
]);

const PATTERNS = [
    "ga:",
    '"ga":',
    "'ga':",
    "a.ga",
    '["ga"]',
    "['ga']",
    ".ga =",
    ".ga=",
];

function walk(dir: string): string[] {
    const out: string[] = [];

    let entries: fs.Dirent[];

    try {
        entries = fs.readdirSync(dir, { withFileTypes: true });
    } catch {
        return out;
    }

    for (const entry of entries) {
        if (entry.isDirectory() && SKIP_DIRS.has(entry.name)) {
            continue;
        }

        const full = path.join(dir, entry.name);

        if (entry.isDirectory()) {
            out.push(...walk(full));
        } else if (
            entry.isFile() &&
            EXT.has(path.extname(entry.name).toLowerCase())
        ) {
            out.push(full);
        }
    }

    return out;
}

function printContext(lines: string[], index: number) {
    const start = Math.max(0, index - 10);
    const end = Math.min(lines.length, index + 15);

    for (let i = start; i < end; i++) {
        console.log(
            `${String(i + 1).padStart(7)} | ${lines[i]}`
        );
    }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" EXACT GA BINDING SEARCH v35");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log(" scripts/ excluded");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();

const files = walk(ROOT);

console.log(`Files scanned: ${files.length}`);
console.log();

let total = 0;

for (const file of files) {
    let source: string;

    try {
        source = fs.readFileSync(file, "utf8");
    } catch {
        continue;
    }

    const lines = source.split(/\r?\n/);

    for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
        const matches = PATTERNS.filter(p =>
            lines[i].includes(p)
        );

        if (matches.length === 0) {
            continue;
        }

        total++;

        console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
        console.log(file);
        console.log(`line ${i + 1}`);
        console.log(`MATCH: ${matches.join(", ")}`);
        console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

        printContext(lines, i);
    }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TOTAL EXACT GA MATCHES: ${total}`);
console.log("============================================================");

            continue;

            out.push(...walk(full));

            entry.isFile() &&

            out.push(full);

    return out;

    for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
        const matches = EXACT_PATTERNS.filter(p =>

const fs = require("fs");

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm"
);

function u32(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[p++];

    v |= (b & 0x7f) << s;

    if (!(b & 0x80))
      return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };

    s += 7;
  }
}

function hx(x) {
  return "0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Find code section
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let p = 8;
let codeStart = 0;
let codeEnd = 0;

while (p < wasm.length) {

  const id = wasm[p++];

  const s = u32(p);
  p = s.next;

  const start = p;
  const end = p + s.value;

  if (id === 10) {
    codeStart = start;
    codeEnd = end;
    break;
  }

  p = end;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Parse bodies
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let q = codeStart;

const count = u32(q);
q = count.next;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("GLOBAL[0] SET SCAN");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`FUNCTION BODIES : ${count.value}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let hits = 0;

for (let bodyIndex = 0; bodyIndex < count.value; bodyIndex++) {

  const sz = u32(q);
  q = sz.next;

  const start = q;
  const end = q + sz.value;

  // Search raw opcode:
  // global.set = 0x24
  // global index 0 = 0x00

  for (let x = start; x + 1 < end; x++) {

    if (wasm[x] === 0x24 && wasm[x + 1] === 0x00) {

      hits++;

      const actualFunction = 83 + bodyIndex;

      console.log("");

        `FUNC ${actualFunction} BODY ${bodyIndex}`
      );

      console.log(
        `ADDRESS : ${hx(x)}`
      );

      console.log(
        `BYTES   : ${wasm.slice(x, Math.min(x + 12, end))
          .toString("hex")
          .match(/.{1,2}/g)
          .join(" ")}`
      );

      // ------------------------------------------------------
      // Show 24 bytes before and after
      // ------------------------------------------------------

      const a = Math.max(start, x - 24);
      const b = Math.min(end, x + 24);

      console.log("CONTEXT:");

      for (let z = a; z < b; z++) {

        console.log(
          `  ${hx(z)} ${wasm[z].toString(16).padStart(2,"0")}`
        );
      }
    }
  }

  q = end;
}

FILE: scripts\findGlobal250F9C_250FA0.js

const fs = require("fs");

const file =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const buf = fs.readFileSync(file);

const IMPORTED = 83;

const TARGETS = [
    0x250f9c,
    0x250fa0
];

function readULEB(pos) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[pos++];
        value += (b & 0x7f) * (2 ** shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            break;

        shift += 7;
    }

    return {
        value,
        next: pos
    };
}

function readSLEB(pos) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let b;

    while (true) {
        b = buf[pos++];

        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            break;
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40))
        value |= (~0 << shift);

    return {
        value: value | 0,
        next: pos
    };
}

function hex(n, w = 8) {
    return "0x" + (n >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(w, "0");
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Locate code section
// ------------------------------------------------------------
let pos = 8;
let codeStart = 0;

while (pos < buf.length) {

    const id = buf[pos++];

    const s = readULEB(pos);
    const size = s.value;
    pos = s.next;

    const start = pos;
    const end = start + size;

    if (id === 10) {
        codeStart = start;
        break;
    }

    pos = end;
}

if (!codeStart)
    throw new Error("Code section not found");

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Read function bodies
// ------------------------------------------------------------
let p = codeStart;

const countInfo = readULEB(p);
const count = countInfo.value;
p = countInfo.next;

const bodies = [];

for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {

    const sizeInfo = readULEB(p);
    const size = sizeInfo.value;
    p = sizeInfo.next;

    bodies.push({
        func: IMPORTED + i,
        start: p,
        end: p + size
    });

    p += size;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Encode target as signed LEB bytes
// ------------------------------------------------------------
function encodeSLEB32(value) {

    const out = [];

    let more = true;

    while (more) {

        let byte = value & 0x7f;

        value >>= 7;

        const signBit = byte & 0x40;

        if (
            (value === 0 && signBit === 0) ||
            (value === -1 && signBit !== 0)
        ) {
            more = false;
        } else {
            byte |= 0x80;
        }

        out.push(byte);
    }

    return Buffer.from(out);
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Dump context
// ------------------------------------------------------------
function dump(start, end) {

    start = Math.max(0, start);
    end = Math.min(buf.length, end);

    for (let p = start; p < end; p += 16) {

        const e = Math.min(end, p + 16);

        const bytes = [];

        for (let q = p; q < e; q++) {
            bytes.push(
                buf[q].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")
            );
        }

        console.log(
            `${hex(p)}  ${bytes.join(" ")}`
        );
    }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Scan i32.const TARGET
// ------------------------------------------------------------
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("GLOBAL STATE SCAN");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TARGET A : 0x250F9C");
console.log("TARGET B : 0x250FA0");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (const target of TARGETS) {

    const encoded = encodeSLEB32(target);

    console.log("\n############################################################");
    console.log(`TARGET ${hex(target)}`);
    console.log(
        "SLEB   : " +
        [...encoded]
            .map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
            .join(" ")
    );
    console.log("############################################################");

    let total = 0;

    for (const body of bodies) {

        for (
            let p = body.start;
            p < body.end - encoded.length;
            p++
        ) {

            if (
                buf.subarray(
                    p,
                    p + encoded.length
                ).equals(encoded)
            ) {

                // Check whether immediately before this is
                // i32.const opcode.
                if (p === body.start || buf[p - 1] !== 0x41)
                    continue;

                total++;

                console.log(
                    `\nHIT FUNC ${body.func} @ ${hex(p - 1)}`
                );

                dump(
                    Math.max(body.start, p - 12),
                    Math.min(body.end, p + encoded.length + 35)
                );
            }
        }
    }

    console.log(
        `\nTOTAL CONST HITS FOR ${hex(target)} : ${total}`
    );
}

console.log("\n============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("============================================================");

import fs from "fs";

const file =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(file);

const START = 0x95e67;
const END   = 0x96265;

function hex(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(6, "0");
}

function uleb(p: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) return { v: v >>> 0, p };
    s += 7;
  }
}

function sleb(p: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, x = 0;
  while (true) {
    x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;
    s += 7;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) break;
  }
  if (s < 32 && (x & 0x40)) v |= (-1 << s);
  return { v: v | 0, p };
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" G-300 STORE / ALIAS SEARCH V23");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();
console.log(`Range : ${hex(START)} .. ${hex(END)}`);
console.log("Target: MEM[G-300] = MEM[local6 + 68]");
console.log();

let p = START;
let lastWasLocal6 = false;
let lastConst: number | null = null;

while (p < END) {
  const at = p;
  const op = b[p++];

  // local.get
  if (op === 0x20) {
    const r = uleb(p);
    p = r.p;

    lastWasLocal6 = r.v === 6;

    if (r.v === 6) {
      console.log(`${hex(at)} local.get 6`);
    } else {
      lastWasLocal6 = false;
    }

    continue;
  }

  // local.set / tee
  if (op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) {
    const r = uleb(p);
    p = r.p;
    lastWasLocal6 = false;
    continue;
  }

  // i32.const
  if (op === 0x41) {
    const r = sleb(p);
    p = r.p;
    lastConst = r.v;

    if (r.v === 68 || r.v === -300) {
      console.log(`${hex(at)} i32.const ${r.v}`);
    }

    continue;
  }

  // i32.add / sub
  if (op === 0x6a || op === 0x6b) {
    if (lastWasLocal6 && lastConst !== null) {
      const result =
        op === 0x6a
          ? lastConst
          : -lastConst;

      console.log(
        `${hex(at)} ${op === 0x6a ? "i32.add" : "i32.sub"} ` +
        `=> local6 ${result >= 0 ? "+" : ""}${result}`
      );

      if (result === 68 || result === -300) {
        console.log(
          `*** TARGET ADDRESS CANDIDATE: local6 ${result >= 0 ? "+" : ""}${result} ***`
        );
      }
    }

    lastWasLocal6 = false;
    continue;
  }

  // memory store/load
  if (op >= 0x36 && op <= 0x3e) {
    const a = uleb(p);
    p = a.p;

    const o = uleb(p);
    p = o.p;

    console.log(
      `${hex(at)} store/load op=0x${op.toString(16)} ` +
      `align=${a.v} offset=${o.v}`
    );

    lastWasLocal6 = false;
    lastConst = null;
    continue;
  }

  // memory loads
  if (op >= 0x28 && op <= 0x35) {
    const a = uleb(p);
    p = a.p;

    const o = uleb(p);
    p = o.p;

    lastWasLocal6 = false;
    lastConst = null;
    continue;
  }

  // calls
  if (op === 0x10) {
    const r = uleb(p);
    p = r.p;

    if (
      r.v === 116 ||
      r.v === 136 ||
      r.v === 3425 ||
      r.v === 85 ||
      r.v === 84 ||
      r.v === 86
    ) {
      console.log(`${hex(at)} CALL ${r.v}`);
    }

    lastWasLocal6 = false;
    lastConst = null;
    continue;
  }

  // memory.copy / fill
  if (op === 0xfc) {
    const r = uleb(p);
    p = r.p;

    console.log(`${hex(at)} 0xfc subop=${r.v}`);

    if (r.v === 8 || r.v === 10) {
      const a = uleb(p);
      p = a.p;
      const c = uleb(p);
      p = c.p;
    }

    lastWasLocal6 = false;
    lastConst = null;
    continue;
  }

  lastWasLocal6 = false;

  if (op !== 0x0b && op !== 0x05 && op !== 0x1a) {
    lastConst = null;
  }
}

console.log();

import * as fs from "fs";
import * as path from "path";

const ROOT = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin";

const TARGETS = [
  "ga",
  "a.ga",
  '"ga"',
  "'ga'",
  "WebAssembly.instantiate",
  "WebAssembly.instantiateStreaming",
  "wasmImports",
  "imports"
];

const SKIP = new Set([
  "node_modules",
  ".git",
  "dist",
  "build",
  "coverage",
  ".cache"
]);

function walk(dir: string, files: string[] = []): string[] {
  for (const name of fs.readdirSync(dir)) {
    if (SKIP.has(name)) continue;

    const full = path.join(dir, name);

    let st;
    try {
      st = fs.statSync(full);
    } catch {
      continue;
    }

    if (st.isDirectory()) {
      walk(full, files);
    } else {
      const ext = path.extname(name).toLowerCase();

      if (
        ext === ".js" ||
        ext === ".mjs" ||
        ext === ".cjs" ||
        ext === ".ts" ||
        ext === ".html" ||
        ext === ".json"
      ) {
        files.push(full);
      }
    }
  }

  return files;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FIND IMPORT #60 BINDING v31");
console.log(" READ ONLY - LOCAL FILES");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`ROOT : ${ROOT}`);
console.log();

const files = walk(ROOT);

console.log(`Files scanned : ${files.length}`);
console.log();

for (const file of files) {
  let text: string;

  try {
    text = fs.readFileSync(file, "utf8");
  } catch {
    continue;
  }

  const lines = text.split(/\r?\n/);

  for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
    const line = lines[i];

    let matched = false;

    for (const target of TARGETS) {
      if (line.includes(target)) {
        matched = true;
        break;
      }
    }

    if (!matched) continue;

    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
    console.log(file);
    console.log(`line ${i + 1}`);

    const from = Math.max(0, i - 3);
    const to = Math.min(lines.length, i + 4);

    for (let j = from; j < to; j++) {
      console.log(
        `${String(j + 1).padStart(7, " ")} | ${lines[j]}`
      );
    }
  }
}

console.log("END");
console.log("============================================================");
