import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

function readULEB(buf: Buffer, pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[p++];

        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: p };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function readString(buf: Buffer, pos: number) {
    const x = readULEB(buf, pos);
    const start = x.next;
    const end = start + x.value;

    return {
        value: buf.subarray(start, end).toString("utf8"),
        next: end,
    };
}

const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" IMPORTS USING TYPE #2 v38");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();

let pos = 8;

while (pos < buf.length) {
    const sectionId = buf[pos++];

    const sizeInfo = readULEB(buf, pos);
    const size = sizeInfo.value;
    pos = sizeInfo.next;

    const payloadStart = pos;
    const payloadEnd = pos + size;

    if (sectionId !== 2) {
        pos = payloadEnd;
        continue;
    }

    let p = payloadStart;

    const countInfo = readULEB(buf, p);
    const count = countInfo.value;
    p = countInfo.next;

    let functionIndex = 0;

    for (let entry = 0; entry < count; entry++) {

        const moduleInfo = readString(buf, p);
        p = moduleInfo.next;

        const nameInfo = readString(buf, p);
        p = nameInfo.next;

        const kind = buf[p++];

        if (kind === 0x00) {

            const typeInfo = readULEB(buf, p);
            p = typeInfo.next;

            if (typeInfo.value === 2) {
                console.log(
                    `function #${functionIndex}` +
                    ` | importEntry=${entry}` +
                    ` | module="${moduleInfo.value}"` +
                    ` | name="${nameInfo.value}"` +
                    ` | type=2`
                );
            }

            functionIndex++;

        } else if (kind === 0x01) {

            p++; // elem type

            const flags = buf[p++];

            const min = readULEB(buf, p);
            p = min.next;

            if (flags & 1) {
                const max = readULEB(buf, p);
                p = max.next;
            }

        } else if (kind === 0x02) {

            const flags = buf[p++];

            const min = readULEB(buf, p);
            p = min.next;

            if (flags & 1) {
                const max = readULEB(buf, p);
                p = max.next;
            }

        } else if (kind === 0x03) {

            p += 2;
        }
    }

    console.log();
    console.log(`Total imports: ${count}`);
    console.log(`Function imports: ${functionIndex}`);
    console.log("============================================================");

    break;
}

FILE: scripts\findIndex114Writers.ts

import * as fs from "fs";

const WASM_PATH =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET = 114;

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM_PATH);

interface Ins {
    off: number;
    op: number;
    text: string;
    imm?: number;
    memOffset?: number;
}

interface FuncBody {
    index: number;
    start: number;
    end: number;
    code: Uint8Array;
}

function hex(x: number): string {
    return "0x" + (x >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function readULEB(pos: number): {
    value: number;
    next: number;
} {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[pos++];

        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return {
                value: value >>> 0,
                next: pos
            };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function readSLEB(pos: number): {
    value: number;
    next: number;
} {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let b = 0;

    while (true) {
        b = wasm[pos++];

        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            break;
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40))
        value |= (~0 << shift);

    return {
        value: value | 0,
        next: pos
    };
}

function parseCodeBodies(): FuncBody[] {
    let p = 8;

    while (p < wasm.length) {
        const sectionId = wasm[p++];

        const s = readULEB(p);
        const sectionSize = s.value;
        p = s.next;

        const payloadStart = p;
        const payloadEnd = payloadStart + sectionSize;

        if (sectionId !== 10) {
            p = payloadEnd;
            continue;
        }

        const countInfo = readULEB(p);
        const count = countInfo.value;
        p = countInfo.next;

        const bodies: FuncBody[] = [];

        for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
            const bodySizeInfo = readULEB(p);
            const bodySize = bodySizeInfo.value;
            p = bodySizeInfo.next;

            const bodyStart = p;
            const bodyEnd = p + bodySize;

            const localInfo = readULEB(p);
            const localDeclCount = localInfo.value;
            p = localInfo.next;

            for (let j = 0; j < localDeclCount; j++) {
                const n = readULEB(p);
                p = n.next;

                // valtype
                p++;
            }

            const codeStart = p;

            bodies.push({
                index: 83 + i,
                start: codeStart,
                end: bodyEnd,
                code: wasm.subarray(codeStart, bodyEnd)
            });

            p = bodyEnd;
        }

        return bodies;
    }

    throw new Error("Code section not found");
}

function decodeInstruction(
    body: FuncBody,
    rel: number
): Ins | null {
    const b = body.code;

    if (rel >= b.length)
        return null;

    const op = b[rel];

    function simple(text: string): Ins {
        return {
            off: body.start + rel,
            op,
            text
        };
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // Control
    // ------------------------------------------------------------

    if (op === 0x00)
        return simple("unreachable");

    if (op === 0x01)
        return simple("nop");

    if (op === 0x02)
        return simple("block");

    if (op === 0x03)
        return simple("loop");

    if (op === 0x04)
        return simple("if");

    if (op === 0x05)
        return simple("else");

    if (op === 0x0b)
        return simple("end");

    if (op === 0x0c) {
        const x = readULEB(body.start + rel + 1);

        return {
            off: body.start + rel,
            op,
            text: `br ${x.value}`,
            imm: x.value
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x0d) {
        const x = readULEB(body.start + rel + 1);

        return {
            off: body.start + rel,
            op,
            text: `br_if ${x.value}`,
            imm: x.value
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x0e) {
        let q = body.start + rel + 1;

        const n = readULEB(q);
        q = n.next;

        for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
            const x = readULEB(q);
            q = x.next;
        }

        const d = readULEB(q);

        return {
            off: body.start + rel,
            op,
            text: `br_table count=${n.value} default=${d.value}`,
            imm: d.value
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x0f)
        return simple("return");

    if (op === 0x10) {
        const x = readULEB(body.start + rel + 1);

        return {
            off: body.start + rel,
            op,
            text: `call ${x.value}`,
            imm: x.value
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x11) {
        const t = readULEB(body.start + rel + 1);
        const table = readULEB(t.next);

        return {
            off: body.start + rel,
            op,
            text:
                `call_indirect type=${t.value} table=${table.value}`,
            imm: t.value
        };
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // Parametric
    // ------------------------------------------------------------

    if (op === 0x1a)
        return simple("drop");

    if (op === 0x1b)
        return simple("select");

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // Locals / globals
    // ------------------------------------------------------------

    if (op === 0x20) {
        const x = readULEB(body.start + rel + 1);

        return {
            off: body.start + rel,
            op,
            text: `local.get ${x.value}`,
            imm: x.value
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x21) {
        const x = readULEB(body.start + rel + 1);

        return {
            off: body.start + rel,
            op,
            text: `local.set ${x.value}`,
            imm: x.value
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x22) {
        const x = readULEB(body.start + rel + 1);

        return {
            off: body.start + rel,
            op,
            text: `local.tee ${x.value}`,
            imm: x.value
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x23) {
        const x = readULEB(body.start + rel + 1);

        return {
            off: body.start + rel,
            op,
            text: `global.get ${x.value}`,
            imm: x.value
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x24) {
        const x = readULEB(body.start + rel + 1);

        return {
            off: body.start + rel,
            op,
            text: `global.set ${x.value}`,
            imm: x.value
        };
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // Constants
    // ------------------------------------------------------------

    if (op === 0x41) {
        const x = readSLEB(body.start + rel + 1);

        return {
            off: body.start + rel,
            op,
            text: `i32.const ${x.value}`,
            imm: x.value
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x42) {
        const x = readSLEB(body.start + rel + 1);

        return {
            off: body.start + rel,
            op,
            text: `i64.const ${x.value}`,
            imm: x.value
        };
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // Memory loads
    // ------------------------------------------------------------

    const loadNames: Record<number, string> = {
        0x28: "i32.load",
        0x29: "i64.load",
        0x2a: "f32.load",
        0x2b: "f64.load",

        0x2c: "i32.load8_s",
        0x2d: "i32.load8_u",
        0x2e: "i32.load16_s",
        0x2f: "i32.load16_u",

        0x30: "i64.load8_s",
        0x31: "i64.load8_u",
        0x32: "i64.load16_s",
        0x33: "i64.load16_u",
        0x34: "i64.load32_s",
        0x35: "i64.load32_u"
    };

    if (loadNames[op]) {
        const align = readULEB(body.start + rel + 1);
        const offset = readULEB(align.next);

        return {
            off: body.start + rel,
            op,
            text:
                `${loadNames[op]} offset=${offset.value}`,
            memOffset: offset.value
        };
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // Memory stores
    // ------------------------------------------------------------

    const storeNames: Record<number, string> = {
        0x36: "i32.store",
        0x37: "i64.store",
        0x38: "f32.store",
        0x39: "f64.store",

        0x3a: "i32.store8",
        0x3b: "i32.store16",

        0x3c: "i64.store8",
        0x3d: "i64.store16",
        0x3e: "i64.store32"
    };

    if (storeNames[op]) {
        const align = readULEB(body.start + rel + 1);
        const offset = readULEB(align.next);

        return {
            off: body.start + rel,
            op,
            text:
                `${storeNames[op]} offset=${offset.value}`,
            memOffset: offset.value
        };
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // Numeric operations
    // ------------------------------------------------------------

    const names: Record<number, string> = {
        0x45: "i32.eqz",
        0x46: "i32.eq",
        0x47: "i32.ne",
        0x48: "i32.lt_s",
        0x49: "i32.lt_u",
        0x4a: "i32.gt_s",
        0x4b: "i32.gt_u",
        0x4c: "i32.le_s",
        0x4d: "i32.le_u",
        0x4e: "i32.ge_s",
        0x4f: "i32.ge_u",

        0x67: "i32.clz",
        0x68: "i32.ctz",
        0x69: "i32.popcnt",

        0x6a: "i32.add",
        0x6b: "i32.sub",
        0x6c: "i32.mul",

        0x6d: "i32.div_s",
        0x6e: "i32.div_u",
        0x6f: "i32.rem_s",
        0x70: "i32.rem_u",

        0x71: "i32.and",
        0x72: "i32.or",
        0x73: "i32.xor",
        0x74: "i32.shl",
        0x75: "i32.shr_s",
        0x76: "i32.shr_u",
        0x77: "i32.rotl",
        0x78: "i32.rotr"
    };

    if (names[op])
        return simple(names[op]);

    return simple(
        `op=0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
    );
}

function instructionLength(
    body: FuncBody,
    rel: number,
    ins: Ins
): number {
    const op = body.code[rel];

    if (
        op === 0x0c ||
        op === 0x0d ||
        op === 0x10 ||
        op === 0x20 ||
        op === 0x21 ||
        op === 0x22 ||
        op === 0x23 ||
        op === 0x24 ||
        op === 0x41 ||
        op === 0x42
    ) {
        const x = readULEB(body.start + rel + 1);
        return x.next - (body.start + rel);
    }

    if (op === 0x11) {
        const a = readULEB(body.start + rel + 1);
        const b = readULEB(a.next);

        return b.next - (body.start + rel);
    }

    if (op >= 0x28 && op <= 0x3e) {
        const a = readULEB(body.start + rel + 1);
        const b = readULEB(a.next);

        return b.next - (body.start + rel);
    }

    if (op === 0x0e) {
        let q = body.start + rel + 1;

        const n = readULEB(q);
        q = n.next;

        for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++)
            q = readULEB(q).next;

        q = readULEB(q).next;

        return q - (body.start + rel);
    }

    return 1;
}

function decodeBody(body: FuncBody): Ins[] {
    const result: Ins[] = [];

    let rel = 0;

    while (rel < body.code.length) {
        const ins = decodeInstruction(body, rel);

        if (!ins)
            break;

        result.push(ins);

        const len = instructionLength(body, rel, ins);

        if (len <= 0)
            break;

        rel += len;
    }

    return result;
}

function showWindow(
    ins: Ins[],
    center: number,
    radius = 12
) {
    const idx = ins.findIndex(
        x => x.off === center
    );

    if (idx < 0)
        return;

    const from = Math.max(0, idx - radius);
    const to = Math.min(
        ins.length,
        idx + radius + 1
    );

    for (let i = from; i < to; i++) {
        const x = ins[i];

        console.log(
            `    ${hex(x.off)} ${x.text}`
        );
    }
}

function findPreviousConst114(
    ins: Ins[],
    index: number,
    maxDistance = 30
): number {
    const start = Math.max(
        0,
        index - maxDistance
    );

    for (let i = index - 1; i >= start; i--) {
        if (
            ins[i].text ===
            `i32.const ${TARGET}`
        ) {
            return i;
        }

        /*
         * Stop at another store/call/control boundary.
         */
        if (
            ins[i].text.startsWith("call ") ||
            ins[i].text.startsWith("call_indirect") ||
            ins[i].text.includes("store") ||
            ins[i].text === "return"
        ) {
            break;
        }
    }

    return -1;
}

function findNextStore(
    ins: Ins[],
    index: number,
    maxDistance = 30
): number {
    const end = Math.min(
        ins.length,
        index + maxDistance + 1
    );

    for (let i = index + 1; i < end; i++) {
        if (
            ins[i].text.includes("store")
        ) {
            return i;
        }

        if (
            ins[i].text.startsWith("call_indirect") ||
            ins[i].text === "return"
        ) {
            break;
        }
    }

    return -1;
}

export async function run() {
    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );
    console.log(
        " FIND WRITERS OF TABLE SLOT 114"
    );
    console.log(
        " READ ONLY - OFFLINE"
    );
    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );
    console.log("");

    console.log(
        "WASM size :",
        wasm.length
    );

    console.log(
        "Target value :",
        TARGET
    );

    console.log(
        "Target hex :",
        hex(TARGET)
    );

    console.log("");

    const bodies = parseCodeBodies();

    console.log(
        "Code bodies :",
        bodies.length
    );

    console.log("");

    let decodedBodies = 0;
    let decodeErrors = 0;

    let const114Count = 0;
    let storeCount = 0;

    let constToStore = 0;

    let store8Count = 0;
    let store16Count = 0;
    let store32Count = 0;
    let store64Count = 0;

    const constSites: {
        func: number;
        ins: Ins[];
        index: number;
    }[] = [];

    const storeSites: {
        func: number;
        ins: Ins[];
        index: number;
    }[] = [];

    for (const body of bodies) {
        try {
            const ins = decodeBody(body);

            decodedBodies++;

            for (let i = 0; i < ins.length; i++) {
                const x = ins[i];

                if (
                    x.text ===
                    `i32.const ${TARGET}`
                ) {
                    const114Count++;

                    constSites.push({
                        func: body.index,
                        ins,
                        index: i
                    });
                }

                if (
                    x.text.includes("store")
                ) {
                    storeCount++;

                    storeSites.push({
                        func: body.index,
                        ins,
                        index: i
                    });

                    if (
                        x.text.startsWith(
                            "i32.store8"
                        )
                    )
                        store8Count++;

                    else if (
                        x.text.startsWith(
                            "i32.store16"
                        )
                    )
                        store16Count++;

                    else if (
                        x.text.startsWith(
                            "i32.store "
                        )
                    )
                        store32Count++;

                    else if (
                        x.text.startsWith(
                            "i64.store"
                        )
                    )
                        store64Count++;
                }
            }
        }
        catch {
            decodeErrors++;
        }
    }

    console.log(
        "Decoded bodies :",
        decodedBodies
    );

    console.log(
        "Decode errors :",
        decodeErrors
    );

    console.log("");

    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );
    console.log(
        " CONST(114) SITES"
    );
    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );
    console.log("");

    console.log(
        "CONST(114) count :",
        const114Count
    );

    console.log("");

    let printedConst = 0;

    for (const site of constSites) {
        const x = site.ins[site.index];

        const nextStore = findNextStore(
            site.ins,
            site.index
        );

        if (nextStore >= 0) {
            const114Count;

            console.log(
                `CONST->STORE candidate: func=${site.func}`
            );

            console.log(
                `  CONST @ ${hex(x.off)}`
            );

            console.log(
                `  STORE @ ${hex(site.ins[nextStore].off)}`
            );

            console.log(
                `  ${site.ins[nextStore].text}`
            );

            showWindow(
                site.ins,
                site.ins[nextStore].off
            );

            console.log("");

            constToStore++;
        }
        else if (printedConst < 30) {
            console.log(
                `CONST(114): func=${site.func} @ ${hex(x.off)}`
            );

            showWindow(
                site.ins,
                x.off,
                8
            );

            console.log("");

            printedConst++;
        }
    }

    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );
    console.log(
        " STORE STATISTICS"
    );
    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );
    console.log("");

    console.log(
        "All stores :",
        storeCount
    );

    console.log(
        "i32.store :",
        store32Count
    );

    console.log(
        "i32.store8 :",
        store8Count
    );

    console.log(
        "i32.store16 :",
        store16Count
    );

    console.log(
        "i64.store family :",
        store64Count
    );

    console.log("");

    /*
     * ----------------------------------------------------------
     * Look for stores whose nearby source contains CONST(114).
     * This is intentionally conservative.
     * ----------------------------------------------------------
     */

    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );
    console.log(
        " STORE SITES WITH NEARBY CONST(114)"
    );
    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );
    console.log("");

    let nearbyMatches = 0;

    for (const site of storeSites) {
        const constIndex = findPreviousConst114(
            site.ins,
            site.index
        );

        if (constIndex < 0)
            continue;

        nearbyMatches++;

        console.log(
            `MATCH #${nearbyMatches}: func=${site.func}`
        );

        console.log(
            `  CONST @ ${hex(site.ins[constIndex].off)}`
        );

        console.log(
            `  STORE @ ${hex(site.ins[site.index].off)}`
        );

        console.log(
            `  STORE instruction: ${site.ins[site.index].text}`
        );

        showWindow(
            site.ins,
            site.ins[site.index].off,
            14
        );

        console.log("");

        if (nearbyMatches >= 100) {
            console.log(
                "... output limited to first 100 matches ..."
            );
            break;
        }
    }

    console.log(
        "Nearby CONST->STORE matches :",
        nearbyMatches
    );

    console.log("");

    /*
     * ----------------------------------------------------------
     * Search for immediate constant 114 followed by store
     * with only simple value-preserving instructions between.
     * ----------------------------------------------------------
     */

    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );
    console.log(
        " STRONG CONST(114) -> STORE CANDIDATES"
    );
    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );
    console.log("");

    let strong = 0;

    for (const site of constSites) {
        const start = site.index;

        for (
            let i = start + 1;
            i < Math.min(
                site.ins.length,
                start + 12
            );
            i++
        ) {
            const x = site.ins[i];

            if (
                x.text === "drop" ||
                x.text === "nop"
            )
                continue;

            if (
                x.text.startsWith("local.tee") ||
                x.text.startsWith("local.set") ||
                x.text.startsWith("local.get")
            )
                continue;

            if (
                x.text.includes("store")
            ) {
                strong++;

                console.log(
                    `STRONG #${strong}: func=${site.func}`
                );

                console.log(
                    `  CONST @ ${hex(site.ins[start].off)}`
                );

                console.log(
                    `  STORE @ ${hex(x.off)}`
                );

                console.log(
                    `  ${x.text}`
                );

                showWindow(
                    site.ins,
                    x.off,
                    10
                );

                console.log("");

                break;
            }

            /*
             * Anything arithmetic/control/call between CONST
             * and STORE makes the path non-trivial.
             */
            if (
                x.text.startsWith("call ") ||
                x.text.startsWith("call_indirect") ||
                x.text.startsWith("i32.") ||
                x.text.startsWith("i64.") ||
                x.text === "if" ||
                x.text === "else" ||
                x.text === "block" ||
                x.text === "loop" ||
                x.text === "return"
            ) {
                break;
            }
        }

        if (strong >= 100) {
            console.log(
                "... strong output limited to first 100 ..."
            );
            break;
        }
    }

    console.log(
        "Strong candidates :",
        strong
    );

    console.log("");

    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );
    console.log(
        " FINAL SUMMARY"
    );
    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );
    console.log("");

    console.log(
        "Decoded bodies          :",
        decodedBodies
    );

    console.log(
        "Decode errors           :",
        decodeErrors
    );

    console.log(
        "CONST(114)              :",
        constSites.length
    );

    console.log(
        "All memory stores       :",
        storeSites.length
    );

    console.log(
        "Nearby CONST->STORE     :",
        nearbyMatches
    );

    console.log(
        "Strong CONST->STORE     :",
        strong
    );

    console.log("");

    if (strong > 0) {
        console.log(
            ">>> POTENTIAL WRITER OF TABLE SLOT 114 FOUND <<<"
        );
    }
    else {
        console.log(
            "No direct strong CONST(114)->STORE path found."
        );

        console.log(
            "Next step: trace local/call/memory producers of 114."
        );
    }

    console.log("");
}

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGETS = new Set([5220, 5232, 5235]);

function u(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, x;
  do {
    x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 127) * 2 ** s;
    s += 7;
  } while (x & 128);
  return [v >>> 0, p];
}

function hex(x) {
  return "0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

let p = 8;
let codeStart = 0;

while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p++];
  let size;
  [size, p] = u(p);

  if (id === 10) {
    codeStart = p;
    break;
  }

  p += size;
}

let q = codeStart;
let count;
[count, q] = u(q);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("INDIRECT SELECTOR SEARCH : 5220 / 5232 / 5235");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
  let size;
  [size, q] = u(q);

  const start = q;
  const end = q + size;
  const func = i + 91;

  for (let x = start; x < end; x++) {

    // i32.const
    if (b[x] === 0x41) {
      let v = 0, s = 0, y = x + 1;

      while (y < end) {
        const z = b[y++];
        v += (z & 127) * 2 ** s;
        s += 7;

        if (!(z & 128)) break;
      }

      if (TARGETS.has(v >>> 0)) {
        const from = Math.max(start, x - 16);
        const to = Math.min(end, x + 24);

        console.log("");
        console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
        console.log("FUNC", func);
        console.log("CONST", hex(x), "=", v);
        console.log(
          "RAW:",
          [...b.slice(from, to)]
            .map(z => z.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
            .join(" ")
        );
      }
    }
  }

  q = end;
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGET = 5311;

let p = 8;

function uleb(): number {
  let v = 0, s = 0, x = 0;
  do {
    x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 127) * 2 ** s;
    s += 7;
  } while (x & 128);
  return v >>> 0;
}

function skipLimits() {
  const flags = uleb();
  uleb();
  if (flags & 1) uleb();
}

function skipType() {
  const form = b[p++];
  if (form !== 0x60) throw new Error("bad type form");
  const np = uleb();
  p += np;
  const nr = uleb();
  p += nr;
}

let types: { params: number; results: number }[] = [];
let table0: number[] = [];
let codeStart = 0;
let codeCount = 0;

while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p++];
  const size = uleb();
  const end = p + size;

  if (id === 1) {
    const n = uleb();

    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
      const form = b[p++];
      if (form !== 0x60) throw new Error("bad function type");

      const np = uleb();
      p += np;

      const nr = uleb();
      p += nr;

      types.push({ params: np, results: nr });
    }
  }

  if (id === 9) {
    const n = uleb();

    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
      const flags = uleb();

      if (flags === 0) {
        if (b[p++] !== 0x41) throw new Error("bad elem offset");
        uleb();
        if (b[p++] !== 0x0b) throw new Error("bad elem end");
      } else if (flags === 2) {
        uleb();
        if (b[p++] !== 0x41) throw new Error("bad elem offset");
        uleb();
        if (b[p++] !== 0x0b) throw new Error("bad elem end");
      } else {
        throw new Error("unsupported elem flags " + flags);
      }

      const count = uleb();

      for (let j = 0; j < count; j++) {
        table0.push(uleb());
      }
    }
  }

  if (id === 10) {
    codeStart = p;
    codeCount = uleb();
  }

  p = end;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FIND CALL_INDIRECT SELECTOR 5311");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TABLE INDEX TARGET :", TARGET);
console.log("FUNC TARGET        :", table0[TARGET - 1]);
console.log("TYPE COUNT         :", types.length);
console.log("CODE COUNT         :", codeCount);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let q = codeStart;

function readLocalDecls(): number {
  const groups = uleb();
  let total = 0;

  for (let i = 0; i < groups; i++) {
    const n = uleb();
    p = p; // keep parser state explicit
    total += n;
    p += 1;
  }

  return total;
}

// Reparse code section cleanly
p = codeStart;
const funcBodies = uleb();

for (let f = 0; f < funcBodies; f++) {
  const bodySize = uleb();
  const bodyStart = p;
  const bodyEnd = p + bodySize;

  const localGroups = uleb();

  for (let g = 0; g < localGroups; g++) {
    uleb();
    p++;
  }

  let lastConst: number | null = null;
  let lastConstPos = -1;

  while (p < bodyEnd) {
    const pos = p;
    const op = b[p++];

    if (op === 0x41) {
      lastConst = uleb();
      lastConstPos = pos;
      continue;
    }

    // call_indirect
    if (op === 0x11) {
      const typeIndex = uleb();
      const tableIndex = uleb();

      console.log(
        "FUNC",
        f + 91,
        "CALL_INDIRECT @",
        "0x" + pos.toString(16),
        "TYPE",
        typeIndex,
        "TABLE",
        tableIndex,
        "LAST_CONST",
        lastConst === null ? "-" : lastConst,
        "CONST_POS",
        lastConstPos < 0 ? "-" : "0x" + lastConstPos.toString(16)
      );

      lastConst = null;
      continue;
    }

    // Reset constant tracking on most instructions.
    if (op !== 0x0b) {
      lastConst = null;
      lastConstPos = -1;
    }

    // Conservative immediate-byte skipping.
    if (
      op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 || op === 0x22 ||
      op === 0x10 || op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d ||
      op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04
    ) {
      uleb();
    } else if (op === 0x28 || op === 0x29 ||
               op === 0x2c || op === 0x2d ||
               op === 0x36 || op === 0x37 ||
               op === 0x3a || op === 0x3b) {
      uleb();
      uleb();
    } else if (op === 0x41) {
      uleb();
    } else if (op === 0x42) {
      uleb();
    }
  }

  p = bodyEnd;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");

FILE: scripts\findIndirectCallsFor7006.mjs

import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath, pathToFileURL } from 'node:url';

const dir = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
const wasmPath = path.resolve(dir, '..', 'emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm');
const scannerPath = path.join(dir, 'disassembleAddressUsers.mjs');
const tempPath = path.join(dir, '__findIndirectCallsFor7006_decoder_temp.mjs');
const outputPath = path.join(dir, 'findIndirectCallsFor7006_output.txt');

const TARGET = 7006;
const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);
const lines = [];
const out = value => lines.push(String(value ?? ''));

function uleb(s) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (s.p < s.end) {
        const b = wasm[s.p++];
        value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;
        if (!(b & 0x80)) return value;
        shift += 7;
        if (shift > 35) throw new Error('ULEB too large');
    }

    throw new Error('Unexpected end of ULEB');
}

function byte(s) {
    if (s.p >= s.end) throw new Error('Unexpected end of section');
    return wasm[s.p++];
}

function name(s) {
    const n = uleb(s);
    const start = s.p;
    s.p += n;
    if (s.p > s.end) throw new Error('Invalid name length');
    return wasm.toString('utf8', start, s.p);
}

function skipLimits(s) {
    const flags = uleb(s);
    uleb(s); // minimum
    if (flags & 1) uleb(s); // maximum
}

function skipGlobalType(s) {
    byte(s); // value type
    byte(s); // mutability
}

function readSections() {
    if (wasm.subarray(0, 4).toString('hex') !== '0061736d') {
        throw new Error('Invalid WASM magic header');
    }

    const sections = new Map();
    let p = 8;

    while (p < wasm.length) {
        const id = wasm[p++];
        const state = { p, end: wasm.length };
        const size = uleb(state);
        const start = state.p;
        const end = start + size;

        if (end > wasm.length) throw new Error('Invalid section size');

        sections.set(id, { p: start, end });
        p = end;
    }

    return sections;
}

function readTypes(section) {
    const s = { ...section };
    const count = uleb(s);
    const types = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        const form = byte(s);

        if (form !== 0x60) {
            throw new Error(`Unsupported function type form ${form}`);
        }

        const paramCount = uleb(s);
        const params = [];

        for (let j = 0; j < paramCount; j++) {
            params.push(byte(s));
        }

        const resultCount = uleb(s);
        const results = [];

        for (let j = 0; j < resultCount; j++) {
            results.push(byte(s));
        }

        types.push({ params, results });
    }

    return types;
}

function readImportedFunctionTypes(section) {
    const s = { ...section };
    const count = uleb(s);
    const functionTypes = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        name(s); // module
        name(s); // import name

        const kind = byte(s);

        if (kind === 0) {
            functionTypes.push(uleb(s));
        } else if (kind === 1) {
            byte(s); // reference type
            skipLimits(s);
        } else if (kind === 2) {
            skipLimits(s);
        } else if (kind === 3) {
            skipGlobalType(s);
        } else if (kind === 4) {
            byte(s); // tag attribute
            uleb(s); // tag type index
        } else {
            throw new Error(`Unknown import kind ${kind}`);
        }
    }

    return functionTypes;
}

function readDefinedFunctionTypes(section) {
    const s = { ...section };
    const count = uleb(s);
    const result = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        result.push(uleb(s));
    }

    return result;
}

const sections = readSections();

if (!sections.has(1) || !sections.has(3) || !sections.has(10)) {
    throw new Error('Required WASM sections are missing');
}

const types = readTypes(sections.get(1));
const importedTypes = sections.has(2)
    ? readImportedFunctionTypes(sections.get(2))
    : [];
const definedTypes = readDefinedFunctionTypes(sections.get(3));

const definedOrdinal = TARGET - importedTypes.length;

if (definedOrdinal < 0 || definedOrdinal >= definedTypes.length) {
    throw new Error(
        `Function ${TARGET} does not match the parsed defined-function index`
    );
}

const targetTypeIndex = definedTypes[definedOrdinal];
const targetType = types[targetTypeIndex];

if (!targetType) {
    throw new Error(`Missing type ${targetTypeIndex} for function ${TARGET}`);
}

function typeDescription(t) {
    const names = new Map([
        [0x7f, 'i32'],
        [0x7e, 'i64'],
        [0x7d, 'f32'],
        [0x7c, 'f64'],
        [0x7b, 'v128'],
        [0x70, 'funcref'],
        [0x6f, 'externref']
    ]);

    const fmt = list => list.map(v => names.get(v) ?? `0x${v.toString(16)}`);

    return `(${fmt(t.params).join(', ')}) -> (${fmt(t.results).join(', ')})`;
}

const source = fs.readFileSync(scannerPath, 'utf8');
const marker = "console.log('\\nROOSTER WASM - FUNC 2034 AND CALLERS');";
const cut = source.indexOf(marker);

if (cut < 0) throw new Error('Could not find decoder bootstrap marker');

let decoder;

try {
    fs.writeFileSync(
        tempPath,
        source.slice(0, cut) +
            '\nexport { bodies, decodeFunction, hex };\n',
        'utf8'
    );

    decoder = await import(
        pathToFileURL(tempPath).href + '?findIndirectCallsFor7006'
    );
} finally {
    fs.rmSync(tempPath, { force: true });
}

const { bodies, decodeFunction, hex } = decoder;

let decoded = 0;
let decodeErrors = 0;
let indirectTotal = 0;
let matchingSites = 0;

out('ROOSTER WASM — INDIRECT CALLS COMPATIBLE WITH FUNCTION 7006');
out('READ ONLY / OFFLINE / NO WASM EXECUTION');
out('====================================================');
out(`TARGET FUNCTION: ${TARGET}`);
out(`IMPORTED FUNCTIONS: ${importedTypes.length}`);
out(`DEFINED FUNCTIONS: ${definedTypes.length}`);
out(`TARGET TYPE INDEX: ${targetTypeIndex}`);
out(`TARGET SIGNATURE: ${typeDescription(targetType)}`);
out('');
out('MATCHING CALL SITES');
out('====================================================');

for (const funcIndex of bodies.keys()) {
    let decodedFunction;

    try {
        decodedFunction = decodeFunction(funcIndex);
        decoded++;
    } catch {
        decodeErrors++;
        continue;
    }

    const instructions = decodedFunction.instructions;

    for (let i = 0; i < instructions.length; i++) {
        const ins = instructions[i];
        const text = String(ins.text);

        if (!/\bcall_indirect\b/.test(text)) continue;
        indirectTotal++;

        const match = text.match(/\bcall_indirect\s+type=(\d+)\s+table=(\d+)/);
        if (!match) continue;

        const typeIndex = Number(match[1]);
        const tableIndex = Number(match[2]);

        if (typeIndex !== targetTypeIndex || tableIndex !== 0) continue;

        matchingSites++;

        out('');
        out('----------------------------------------------------');
        out(`CALLER FUNCTION: ${funcIndex}`);
        out(`CALL SITE: ${hex(ins.start)}`);
        out(`INDIRECT TYPE: ${typeIndex}`);
        out(`TABLE: ${tableIndex}`);

        const start = Math.max(0, i - 8);
        const end = Math.min(instructions.length - 1, i + 5);

        for (let j = start; j <= end; j++) {
            const item = instructions[j];
            const prefix = j === i ? '>>> ' : '    ';
            out(`${prefix}${hex(item.start)}  ${item.raw}  ${item.text}`);
        }
    }
}

out('');
out('====================================================');
out('SUMMARY');
out(`DECODED FUNCTIONS: ${decoded}`);
out(`DECODE ERRORS: ${decodeErrors}`);
out(`INDIRECT CALL SITES SCANNED: ${indirectTotal}`);
out(`CALL SITES WITH TARGET TYPE AND TABLE 0: ${matchingSites}`);
out('');
out('A matching type is necessary but does not prove the table index is 4769.');
out('Runtime index provenance still needs to be established.');
out('No WASM execution performed.');

fs.writeFileSync(outputPath, lines.join('\n') + '\n', 'utf8');

console.log(`Target type index: ${targetTypeIndex}`);
console.log(`Matching indirect call sites: ${matchingSites}`);
console.log(`Decode errors: ${decodeErrors}`);
console.log(`Output: ${outputPath}`);

FILE: scripts\findIndirectDispatcherCallers.ts

import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

const CODE_SECTION_HEADER = 0x00006e78;
const CODE_PAYLOAD_START  = 0x00006e7d;
const CODE_SECTION_END    = 0x00324ef5;

const IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS = 83;

const TARGETS = new Map<number, string>([
  [8952, "Dispatcher A"],
  [8927, "Dispatcher B"],
  [8637, "Dispatcher C"],
]);

function hex(n: number): string {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = buf[pos++];

    value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return { value, next: pos };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

/*
 * Code section layout:
 *
 * 0x6e78 = section id 10
 * 0x6e79 = section size ULEB
 * 0x6e7d = function-count ULEB
 * followed by function bodies
 */
function parseFunctionBodies() {
  let p = CODE_PAYLOAD_START;

  const countInfo = readULEB(p);
  const functionCount = countInfo.value;
  p = countInfo.next;

  const result: Array<{
    body: number;
    global: number;
    start: number;
    end: number;
  }> = [];

  for (let body = 0; body < functionCount; body++) {
    const sizeInfo = readULEB(p);

    const start = sizeInfo.next;
    const end = start + sizeInfo.value;

    if (end > CODE_SECTION_END) {
      throw new Error(
        `Function body exceeds code section at body ${body}\n` +
        `start=${hex(start)} end=${hex(end)} sectionEnd=${hex(CODE_SECTION_END)}`
      );
    }

    result.push({
      body,
      global: IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS + body,
      start,
      end
    });

    p = end;
  }

  console.log(`Function count in code section = ${functionCount}`);
  console.log(`Final body pointer = ${hex(p)}`);
  console.log(`Expected section end = ${hex(CODE_SECTION_END)}`);

  if (p !== CODE_SECTION_END) {
    throw new Error(
      `Code body parser mismatch: ${hex(p)} != ${hex(CODE_SECTION_END)}`
    );
  }

  return result;
}

/*
 * Raw call_indirect:
 *
 * 11 04 00
 *
 * = call_indirect
 *   type index 4
 *   table index 0
 */
function scanRawCallIndirect() {
  const hits: number[] = [];

  for (
    let p = CODE_PAYLOAD_START;
    p + 2 < CODE_SECTION_END;
    p++
  ) {
    if (
      buf[p] === 0x11 &&
      buf[p + 1] === 0x04 &&
      buf[p + 2] === 0x00
    ) {
      hits.push(p);
    }
  }

  return hits;
}

function containingFunction(
  pos: number,
  functions: Array<{
    body: number;
    global: number;
    start: number;
    end: number;
  }>
) {
  let lo = 0;
  let hi = functions.length - 1;

  while (lo <= hi) {
    const mid = (lo + hi) >> 1;
    const fn = functions[mid];

    if (pos < fn.start) {
      hi = mid - 1;
    } else if (pos >= fn.end) {
      lo = mid + 1;
    } else {
      return fn;
    }
  }

  return null;
}

function previousBytes(pos: number, count = 32): string {
  const start = Math.max(CODE_PAYLOAD_START, pos - count);

  let s = "";

  for (let p = start; p < pos; p++) {
    s += buf[p].toString(16).padStart(2, "0") + " ";
  }

  return s.trim();
}

const functions = parseFunctionBodies();
const indirects = scanRawCallIndirect();

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log("");

console.log(`Defined function bodies = ${functions.length}`);
console.log(`Expected defined bodies = 9398`);

console.log("");
console.log(`Raw call_indirect(type=4) = ${indirects.length}`);

const byFunction = new Map<number, number[]>();

for (const pos of indirects) {
  const fn = containingFunction(pos, functions);

  if (!fn) {
    console.log(
      `WARNING: no containing function for ${hex(pos)}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  const list = byFunction.get(fn.global) ?? [];
  list.push(pos);
  byFunction.set(fn.global, list);
}

console.log("");

console.log("============================================================");

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

let p = 8;

function u(): number {
  let v = 0, s = 0, x: number;
  do {
    x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 127) * 2 ** s;
    s += 7;
  } while (x & 128);
  return v >>> 0;
}

let codeStart = 0;

while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p++];
  const size = u();
  const end = p + size;

  if (id === 10) {
    codeStart = p;
    break;
  }

  p = end;
}

p = codeStart;
const count = u();

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TYPE-1 INDIRECT WITH DIRECT MEMORY SELECTOR");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

for (let f = 0; f < count; f++) {
  const bodySize = u();
  const bodyStart = p;
  const bodyEnd = p + bodySize;

  const groups = u();

  for (let g = 0; g < groups; g++) {
    u();
    p++;
  }

  let prevLoad = "";

  while (p < bodyEnd) {
    const pos = p;
    const op = b[p++];

    if (op === 0x28) {
      u();
      const off = u();
      prevLoad = `i32.load offset=${off} @ 0x${pos.toString(16)}`;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x11) {
      const type = u();
      const table = u();

      if (type === 1 && table === 0 && prevLoad) {
        console.log(
          `FUNC ${f + 91} CALL_INDIRECT @ 0x${pos.toString(16)}`
        );
        console.log(`  SELECTOR SOURCE: ${prevLoad}`);
      }

      prevLoad = "";
      continue;
    }

    // Preserve only immediately preceding load.
    if (op !== 0x0b) {
      prevLoad = "";
    }

    if (
      op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 || op === 0x22 ||
      op === 0x10 || op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d ||
      op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04
    ) {
      u();
    } else if (
      op === 0x36 || op === 0x37 ||
      op === 0x3a || op === 0x3b
    ) {
      u();
      u();
    } else if (op === 0x41 || op === 0x42) {
      u();
    }
  }

  p = bodyEnd;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");

function u(p) {
  let v=0,s=0;
  while(true){
    const x=b[p++];
    v+=(x&127)*2**s;
    if(!(x&128)) return [v>>>0,p];
    s+=7;
  }
}

let p=8, code=0;

  const id=b[p++];
  const [n,q]=u(p); p=q;

  p+=n;

for(let i=0;i<count;i++){
  const [size,q]=u(p); p=q;
  const func=83+i, start=p, end=p+size;

  for(let x=start;x<end;x++){
    if(b[x]!==0x36) continue;

    const [,q1]=u(x+1);
    const [off,q2]=u(q1);

    // Look backward for:
    // local.get N
    // i32.const small
    // i32.add
    // i32.store
    for(let z=Math.max(start,x-20);z<x;z++){
      if(b[z]!==0x20) continue;

      const [local,q3]=u(z+1);
      let y=q3;

      if(b[y]!==0x41) continue;
      const [c,q4]=u(y+1);
      y=q4;

      if(b[y]!==0x6a) continue;

          `FUNC ${func} STORE @ 0x${x.toString(16)} `+
          `ADDR=L${local}+${c} OFFSET=${off}`
        );

        console.log(
          [...b.subarray(Math.max(start,z-12),Math.min(end,x+8))]
          .map(v=>v.toString(16).padStart(2,"0")).join(" ")
        );
        console.log("--------------------------------------------");
      }
    }
  }

  p=end;
}

import * as fs from "fs";

const file =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(file);

const TARGET = 7697;

function uleb(p: number) {
  let v = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];

    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if (!(x & 0x80))
      return { v, p };

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function hex(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

  /*
     call_indirect = 0x11
     Immediate:
       type index : ULEB
       table index: ULEB
  */

  if (b[i] !== 0x11)
    continue;

  const typeR = uleb(i + 1);
  const tableR = uleb(typeR.p);

  const to = Math.min(b.length, tableR.p + 24);

  console.log("  bytes:");

  for (let p = from; p < to; p += 16) {

    const lineEnd = Math.min(to, p + 16);

    console.log(
      `  ${hex(p)}  ` +
      Array.from(b.slice(p, lineEnd))
        .map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))

FILE: scripts\findInitialG528G272V1.ts

import * as fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_FUNC = 2094;

// First important consumer points
const START_POINTS = [
    0x9670d,
    0x967c8,
    0x96883,
    0x96baf,
    0x969de,
    0x96aa1,
    0x96aad,
    0x96ab4,
];

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 2094 INITIAL G-528 / G-272 BACKWARD SCAN V1");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();

const bytes = new Uint8Array(fs.readFileSync(WASM));

function uleb(pos: number): { value: number; end: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (true) {
        const b = bytes[p++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;

        shift += 7;
    }

    return { value, end: p };
}

function sleb32(pos: number): { value: number; end: number } {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;
    let b = 0;

    while (true) {
        b = bytes[p++];

        result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
        result |= ~0 << shift;
    }

    return {
        value: result,
        end: p,
    };
}

function getCodeSection() {

    let p = 8;

    while (p < bytes.length) {

        const id = bytes[p++];

        const s = uleb(p);
        p = s.end;

        const end = p + s.value;

        if (id === 10) {
            return {
                start: p,
                end,
            };
        }

        p = end;
    }

    throw new Error("Code section not found");
}

function getImportedFunctionCount() {

    let p = 8;

    while (p < bytes.length) {

        const id = bytes[p++];

        const s = uleb(p);
        p = s.end;

        const end = p + s.value;

        if (id !== 2) {
            p = end;
            continue;
        }

        let q = p;

        const c = uleb(q);
        q = c.end;

        let count = 0;

        for (let i = 0; i < c.value; i++) {

            const ml = uleb(q);
            q = ml.end + ml.value;

            const nl = uleb(q);
            q = nl.end + nl.value;

            const kind = bytes[q++];

            if (kind === 0) {

                const t = uleb(q);
                q = t.end;

                count++;
            }
            else if (kind === 1) {
                q += 4;
            }
            else if (kind === 2) {
                q += 1;
            }
            else if (kind === 3) {
                q += 2;
            }
        }

        return count;
    }

    return 0;
}

function getFunctionBody(func: number) {

    const sec = getCodeSection();

    const imported = getImportedFunctionCount();

    const bodyIndex = func - imported;

    let p = sec.start;

    const count = uleb(p);
    p = count.end;

    for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {

        const s = uleb(p);

        const start = s.end;
        const end = start + s.value;

        if (i === bodyIndex) {

            return {
                start,
                end,
                imported,
            };
        }

        p = end;
    }

    throw new Error("Function body not found");
}

function decode(pc: number) {

    const op = bytes[pc];

    let p = pc + 1;

    switch (op) {

        case 0x20: {
            const x = uleb(p);
            return {
                text: `local.get ${x.value}`,
                end: x.end,
            };
        }

        case 0x21: {
            const x = uleb(p);
            return {
                text: `local.set ${x.value}`,
                end: x.end,
            };
        }

        case 0x22: {
            const x = uleb(p);
            return {
                text: `local.tee ${x.value}`,
                end: x.end,
            };
        }

        case 0x41: {
            const x = sleb32(p);

            return {
                text: `i32.const ${x.value}`,
                end: x.end,
            };
        }

        case 0x42: {

            while (bytes[p++] & 0x80) {}

            return {
                text: "i64.const",
                end: p,
            };
        }

        case 0x10: {

            const x = uleb(p);

            return {
                text: `call ${x.value}`,
                end: x.end,
            };
        }

        case 0x28:
        case 0x29:
        case 0x2a:
        case 0x2b:
        case 0x36:
        case 0x37:
        case 0x38:
        case 0x39: {

            const a = uleb(p);
            const o = uleb(a.end);

            const names: Record<number, string> = {
                0x28: "i32.load",
                0x29: "i64.load",
                0x2a: "f32.load",
                0x2b: "f64.load",
                0x36: "i32.store",
                0x37: "i64.store",
                0x38: "f32.store",
                0x39: "f64.store",
            };

            return {
                text:
                    `${names[op] ?? "memory-op"} offset=${o.value}`,
                end: o.end,
            };
        }

        case 0x6a:
            return {
                text: "i32.add",
                end: p,
            };

        case 0x6b:
            return {
                text: "i32.sub",
                end: p,
            };

        case 0x74:
            return {
                text: "i32.shl",
                end: p,
            };

        case 0x7c:
            return {
                text: "i64.add",
                end: p,
            };

        case 0x7d:
            return {
                text: "i64.sub",
                end: p,
            };

        case 0x83:
            return {
                text: "i64.mul",
                end: p,
            };

        case 0x0b:
            return {
                text: "end",
                end: p,
            };

        case 0x0c: {
            const x = uleb(p);

            return {
                text: `br ${x.value}`,
                end: x.end,
            };
        }

        case 0x0d: {
            const x = uleb(p);

            return {
                text: `br_if ${x.value}`,
                end: x.end,
            };
        }

        case 0x02:
        case 0x03:
        case 0x04: {

            const bt = bytes[p++];

            const name =
                op === 0x02 ? "block" :
                op === 0x03 ? "loop" :
                "if";

            return {
                text:
                    `${name} blocktype=0x${bt.toString(16)}`,
                end: p,
            };
        }

        default:
            return {
                text:
                    `op_0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`,
                end: p,
            };
    }
}

const body = getFunctionBody(TARGET_FUNC);

);

import * as fs from "fs";

const WASM =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const START = 0x964d1;
const END = 0x96853;

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function u8(p: number): number {
  return wasm[p];
}

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (true) {
    const b = u8(p++);
    value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if (!(b & 0x80)) return { value, next: p };

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function readSLEB(pos: number, bits = 32): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;
  let b = 0;

  while (true) {
    b = u8(p++);
    value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;
    shift += 7;

    if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
  }

  if (shift < bits && (b & 0x40)) {
    value -= 2 ** shift;
  }

  return { value, next: p };
}

function hex(n: number): string {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function decode(pos: number): { next: number; text: string } {
  const op = u8(pos++);

  switch (op) {
    case 0x00: return { next: pos, text: "unreachable" };
    case 0x01: return { next: pos, text: "nop" };
    case 0x05: return { next: pos, text: "else" };
    case 0x0b: return { next: pos, text: "end" };
    case 0x0f: return { next: pos, text: "return" };

    case 0x02:
    case 0x03:
    case 0x04: {
      const x = readSLEB(pos, 33);
      const name =
        op === 0x02 ? "block" :
        op === 0x03 ? "loop" : "if";

      return {
        next: x.next,
        text: `${name} ${x.value}`,
      };
    }

    case 0x0c:
    case 0x0d: {
      const x = readULEB(pos);

      return {
        next: x.next,
        text: `${op === 0x0c ? "br" : "br_if"} ${x.value}`,
      };
    }

    case 0x10: {
      const x = readULEB(pos);
      return {
        next: x.next,
        text: `call ${x.value}`,
      };
    }

    case 0x1a:
      return { next: pos, text: "drop" };

    case 0x1b:
      return { next: pos, text: "select" };

    case 0x20:
    case 0x21:
    case 0x22: {
      const x = readULEB(pos);

      const name =
        op === 0x20 ? "local.get" :
        op === 0x21 ? "local.set" :
        "local.tee";

      return {
        next: x.next,
        text: `${name} ${x.value}`,
      };
    }

    case 0x23:
    case 0x24: {
      const x = readULEB(pos);

      return {
        next: x.next,
        text: `${op === 0x23 ? "global.get" : "global.set"} ${x.value}`,
      };
    }

    case 0x28:
    case 0x29:
    case 0x2c:
    case 0x2d:
    case 0x2e:
    case 0x2f:
    case 0x30:
    case 0x31:
    case 0x32:
    case 0x33:
    case 0x34:
    case 0x35:
    case 0x36:
    case 0x37:
    case 0x3a:
    case 0x3b:
    case 0x3c:
    case 0x3d:
    case 0x3e: {
      const align = readULEB(pos);
      pos = align.next;

      const off = readULEB(pos);
      pos = off.next;

      const names: Record<number, string> = {
        0x28: "i32.load",
        0x29: "i64.load",

        0x2c: "i32.load8_s",
        0x2d: "i32.load8_u",
        0x2e: "i32.load16_s",
        0x2f: "i32.load16_u",

        0x30: "i64.load8_s",
        0x31: "i64.load8_u",
        0x32: "i64.load16_s",
        0x33: "i64.load16_u",
        0x34: "i64.load32_s",
        0x35: "i64.load32_u",

        0x36: "i32.store",
        0x37: "i64.store",

        0x3a: "i32.store8",
        0x3b: "i32.store16",
        0x3c: "i64.store8",
        0x3d: "i64.store16",
        0x3e: "i64.store32",
      };

      return {
        next: pos,
        text:
          `${names[op]} align=${align.value} offset=${off.value}`,
      };
    }

    case 0x3f:
    case 0x40: {
      const x = readULEB(pos);

      return {
        next: x.next,
        text:
          `${op === 0x3f ? "memory.size" : "memory.grow"} ${x.value}`,
      };
    }

    case 0x41: {
      const x = readSLEB(pos, 32);

      return {
        next: x.next,
        text: `i32.const ${x.value}`,
      };
    }

    case 0x42: {
      const x = readSLEB(pos, 64);

      return {
        next: x.next,
        text: `i64.const ${x.value}`,
      };
    }

    case 0x45: return { next: pos, text: "i32.eqz" };
    case 0x46: return { next: pos, text: "i32.eq" };
    case 0x47: return { next: pos, text: "i32.ne" };
    case 0x48: return { next: pos, text: "i32.lt_s" };
    case 0x49: return { next: pos, text: "i32.lt_u" };
    case 0x4a: return { next: pos, text: "i32.gt_s" };
    case 0x4b: return { next: pos, text: "i32.gt_u" };
    case 0x4c: return { next: pos, text: "i32.le_s" };
    case 0x4d: return { next: pos, text: "i32.le_u" };
    case 0x4e: return { next: pos, text: "i32.ge_s" };
    case 0x4f: return { next: pos, text: "i32.ge_u" };

    case 0x6a: return { next: pos, text: "i32.add" };
    case 0x6b: return { next: pos, text: "i32.sub" };
    case 0x6c: return { next: pos, text: "i32.mul" };
    case 0x71: return { next: pos, text: "i32.and" };
    case 0x72: return { next: pos, text: "i32.or" };
    case 0x73: return { next: pos, text: "i32.xor" };
    case 0x74: return { next: pos, text: "i32.shl" };
    case 0x75: return { next: pos, text: "i32.shr_s" };
    case 0x76: return { next: pos, text: "i32.shr_u" };

    case 0x7c: return { next: pos, text: "i64.add" };
    case 0x7d: return { next: pos, text: "i64.sub" };
    case 0x7e: return { next: pos, text: "i64.mul" };
    case 0x83: return { next: pos, text: "i64.and" };
    case 0x84: return { next: pos, text: "i64.or" };
    case 0x85: return { next: pos, text: "i64.xor" };
    case 0x86: return { next: pos, text: "i64.shl" };
    case 0x87: return { next: pos, text: "i64.shr_s" };
    case 0x88: return { next: pos, text: "i64.shr_u" };

    case 0xad:
      return { next: pos, text: "i64.extend_i32_u" };

    case 0xac:
      return { next: pos, text: "i64.extend_i32_s" };

    default:
      return {
        next: pos,
        text: `UNKNOWN 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`,
      };
  }
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
 * Decode the selected range
 * ------------------------------------------------------------ */

const instructions: {
  start: number;
  next: number;
  text: string;
}[] = [];

let p = START;

while (p < END) {
  const start = p;
  const d = decode(p);

  instructions.push({
    start,
    next: d.next,
    text: d.text,
  });

  p = d.next;
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
 * Symbolic expression tracker
 *
 * Track only i32 pointer expressions.
 * We deliberately keep the symbolic form instead of evaluating it.
 * ------------------------------------------------------------ */

type Expr = string;

const stack: Expr[] = [];
const locals = new Map<number, Expr>();

function pop(): Expr {
  return stack.length ? stack.pop()! : "?";
}

function push(x: Expr) {
  stack.push(x);
}

function simplify(x: string): string {
  return x
    .replace(/\(\(([^()]*)\)\)/g, "($1)")
    .replace(/\+\s*0\b/g, "")
    .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
    .trim();
}

function isG528(x: string): boolean {
  const s = simplify(x);

  return (
    s === "G-528" ||
    s === "(G-528)" ||
    s === "L7-64" ||
    s === "(L7-64)" ||
    s.includes("L7-64")
  );
}

function symbolicLocal(n: number): Expr {
  if (locals.has(n)) return locals.get(n)!;

  if (n === 7) return "G-464";
  if (n === 6) return "L6";
  if (n === 8) return "L8";

  return `L${n}`;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" INITIAL G-528 PRODUCER SEARCH v4");
console.log(" FUNCTION 2094");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();
console.log(`Range : ${hex(START)} .. ${hex(END)}`);
console.log("Known : L7 = G-464");
console.log("Target: G-528 = L7 - 64");
console.log();

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
 * Seed known locals
 * ------------------------------------------------------------ */

locals.set(7, "G-464");
locals.set(6, "L6");
locals.set(8, "L8");

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
 * Walk instructions
 * ------------------------------------------------------------ */

for (const ins of instructions) {
  const t = ins.text;

  if (t.startsWith("local.get ")) {
    const n = Number(t.split(" ")[1]);
    push(symbolicLocal(n));
    continue;
  }

  if (t.startsWith("local.set ")) {
    const n = Number(t.split(" ")[1]);
    const v = pop();

    locals.set(n, simplify(v));

    continue;
  }

  if (t.startsWith("local.tee ")) {
    const n = Number(t.split(" ")[1]);
    const v = stack.length ? stack[stack.length - 1] : "?";

    locals.set(n, simplify(v));

    continue;
  }

  if (t.startsWith("i32.const ")) {
    const n = Number(t.split(" ")[1]);

    push(`${n}`);

    continue;
  }

  if (t === "i32.add" || t === "i32.sub") {
    const rhs = pop();
    const lhs = pop();

    const op = t === "i32.add" ? "+" : "-";

    push(`(${lhs}${op}${rhs})`);

    continue;
  }

  if (t === "i32.shl") {
    const rhs = pop();
    const lhs = pop();

    push(`(${lhs}<<${rhs})`);

    continue;
  }

  if (t === "i32.mul") {
    const rhs = pop();
    const lhs = pop();

    push(`(${lhs}*${rhs})`);

    continue;
  }

  /* ----------------------------------------------------------
   * STORE detection
   * Stack order:
   *
   *   address
   *   value
   *
   * The value is popped first.
   * ---------------------------------------------------------- */

  if (
    t.startsWith("i64.store") ||
    t.startsWith("i32.store")
  ) {
    const value = pop();
    const address = pop();

    const addr = simplify(address);

    console.log(
      `${hex(ins.start)}  ${t}`
    );
    console.log(
      `    ADDRESS = ${addr}`
    );
    console.log(
      `    VALUE   = ${simplify(value)}`
    );

    if (isG528(addr)) {
      console.log();
      console.log("!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!");
      console.log("!!! POSSIBLE G-528 WRITE FOUND !!!");
      console.log("!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!");
      console.log(
        `WRITE @ ${hex(ins.start)}`
      );
      console.log(
        `ADDRESS = ${addr}`
      );
      console.log(
        `VALUE   = ${simplify(value)}`
      );
      console.log();
    }

    continue;
  }

  /* ----------------------------------------------------------
   * Calls:
   *
   * We cannot fully pop arguments without the complete type
   * table here, so preserve the current symbolic stack and
   * print it. This is useful for identifying a call whose
   * first argument is G-528.
   * ---------------------------------------------------------- */

  if (t.startsWith("call ")) {
    const fn = Number(t.split(" ")[1]);

    console.log();
    console.log(
      `${hex(ins.start)}  call ${fn}`
    );

    console.log(
      `    STACK = [${stack.join(", ")}]`
    );

    if (stack.some(isG528)) {
      console.log(
        "    >>> G-528 EXPRESSION PRESENT ON CALL STACK <<<"
      );
    }

    continue;
  }

  /*
   * Loads consume an address and produce an unknown value.
   * Keep stack shape approximately correct.
   */
  if (t.startsWith("i64.load") || t.startsWith("i32.load")) {
    const address = pop();

    console.log(
      `${hex(ins.start)}  ${t}`
    );
    console.log(
      `    LOAD ADDRESS = ${simplify(address)}`
    );

    push("MEM");
    continue;
  }

  /*
   * i64 arithmetic.
   */
  if (
    t === "i64.add" ||
    t === "i64.sub" ||
    t === "i64.mul" ||
    t === "i64.and" ||
    t === "i64.or" ||
    t === "i64.xor"
  ) {
    const rhs = pop();
    const lhs = pop();

    push(`(${lhs} ${t.slice(4)} ${rhs})`);
    continue;
  }

  if (
    t === "i64.shl" ||
    t === "i64.shr_s" ||
    t === "i64.shr_u"
  ) {
    const rhs = pop();
    const lhs = pop();

    push(`(${lhs} ${t.slice(4)} ${rhs})`);
    continue;
  }

  if (t.startsWith("i64.const ")) {
    push("CONST64");
    continue;
  }

  if (t === "drop") {
    pop();
    continue;
  }

  if (t === "select") {
    const cond = pop();
    const b = pop();
    const a = pop();

    push(`select(${a},${b},${cond})`);
    continue;
  }

  if (t.startsWith("global.get")) {
    push("G");
    continue;
  }

  if (t.startsWith("global.set")) {
    pop();
    continue;
  }
}

console.log();

console.log(" SEARCH COMPLETE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("Target symbolic address: G-528 = L7-64");
console.log("READ ONLY - NO NETWORK - NO TRANSACTION");
