import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const START = 0x000964d1;
const END   = 0x00096853;

const TARGET_OFF = -528;
const L7_OFF = -464;

const bytes = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function u32leb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  while (true) {
    const b = bytes[pos++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) return { value, next: pos };
    shift += 7;
  }
}

function i32leb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let b = 0;
  while (true) {
    b = bytes[pos++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
  }
  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) value |= (~0 << shift);
  return { value, next: pos };
}

function hex(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function signed(n: number) {
  return n >= 0 ? `+${n}` : `${n}`;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" INITIAL G-528 PRODUCER SEARCH v5");
console.log(" FUNCTION 2094");
console.log(" TARGETED STORE ADDRESS TRACE");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("");
console.log(`Range : ${hex(START)} .. ${hex(END)}`);
console.log(`Target: G-528 = L7 - 64`);
console.log("");

/*
 * V5 intentionally does not attempt to emulate the complete function.
 * It performs a raw instruction/data-flow inspection around the three
 * critical stores and tracks local.get/set/tee plus i32 address arithmetic.
 */

const OPC = {
  END: 0x0b,

  BLOCK: 0x02,
  LOOP: 0x03,
  IF: 0x04,
  ELSE: 0x05,

  LOCAL_GET: 0x20,
  LOCAL_SET: 0x21,
  LOCAL_TEE: 0x22,

  GLOBAL_GET: 0x23,
  GLOBAL_SET: 0x24,

  I32_LOAD: 0x28,
  I64_LOAD: 0x29,
  I64_STORE: 0x37,

  I32_CONST: 0x41,
  I64_CONST: 0x42,

  I32_ADD: 0x6a,
  I32_SUB: 0x6b,
  I32_SHL: 0x74,
  I32_MUL: 0x6c,

  CALL: 0x10
};

type Expr =
  | { k: "unknown"; s?: string }
  | { k: "const"; v: number }
  | { k: "local"; n: number }
  | { k: "global"; n: number }
  | { k: "add"; a: Expr; b: Expr }
  | { k: "sub"; a: Expr; b: Expr }
  | { k: "shl"; a: Expr; b: Expr }
  | { k: "mem"; a: Expr; off: number };

function E(s: string): Expr {
  return { k: "unknown", s };
}

function simplify(x: Expr): Expr {
  if (x.k === "add" || x.k === "sub") {
    const a = simplify(x.a);
    const b = simplify(x.b);

    if (a.k === "const" && a.v === 0) return x.k === "add" ? b : { k: "sub", a: { k: "const", v: 0 }, b };
    if (b.k === "const" && b.v === 0) return a;

    if (a.k === "const" && b.k === "const") {
      return {
        k: "const",
        v: x.k === "add" ? a.v + b.v : a.v - b.v
      };
    }

    return { ...x, a, b };
  }

  if (x.k === "shl") {
    const a = simplify(x.a);
    const b = simplify(x.b);
    if (a.k === "const" && b.k === "const")
      return { k: "const", v: a.v << b.v };
    return { k: "shl", a, b };
  }

  return x;
}

function show(x: Expr): string {
  x = simplify(x);

  switch (x.k) {
    case "unknown":
      return x.s ?? "?";

    case "const":
      return `${x.v}`;

    case "local":
      return `L${x.n}`;

    case "global":
      return `G${x.n}`;

    case "mem":
      return `MEM[${show(x.a)}${x.off ? signed(x.off) : ""}]`;

    case "add":
      return `(${show(x.a)}+${show(x.b)})`;

    case "sub":
      return `(${show(x.a)}-${show(x.b)})`;

    case "shl":
      return `(${show(x.a)}<<${show(x.b)})`;
  }
}

function isTarget(x: Expr): boolean {
  const s = show(x)
    .replace(/\s+/g, "")
    .replace(/\+0/g, "");

  return (
    s === "L7-64" ||
    s === "G0-528" ||
    s === "(L7-64)" ||
    s.includes("L7-64")
  );
}

function readMemArg(pos: number): { off: number; next: number } {
  const align = bytes[pos++];
  const r = u32leb(pos);
  return { off: r.value | 0, next: r.next };
}

const stores = [0x00096653, 0x0009670d, 0x000967c8];

let p = START;

const local: Record<number, Expr> = {};

local[7] = E("G-464");
local[6] = E("L6");
local[8] = E("L8");

const stack: Expr[] = [];

function pop(): Expr {
  return stack.length ? stack.pop()! : E("STACK_UNDERFLOW");
}

function push(x: Expr) {
  stack.push(simplify(x));
}

function reportStore(addr: number, address: Expr, value: Expr, off: number) {
  const finalAddress = simplify(
    off
      ? { k: "add", a: address, b: { k: "const", v: off } }
      : address
  );

  console.log("");
  console.log(`STORE @ ${hex(addr)}`);
  console.log(`  raw offset = ${off}`);
  console.log(`  ADDRESS    = ${show(finalAddress)}`);
  console.log(`  VALUE      = ${show(value)}`);

  if (isTarget(finalAddress)) {
    console.log("  >>> TARGET G-528 HIT <<<");
  }
}

while (p < END) {
  const addr = p;
  const op = bytes[p++];

  try {
    switch (op) {
      case OPC.LOCAL_GET: {
        const r = u32leb(p);
        p = r.next;
        push(local[r.value] ?? E(`L${r.value}`));
        break;
      }

      case OPC.LOCAL_SET: {
        const r = u32leb(p);
        p = r.next;
        local[r.value] = pop();
        break;
      }

      case OPC.LOCAL_TEE: {
        const r = u32leb(p);
        p = r.next;
        const x = pop();
        local[r.value] = x;
        push(x);
        break;
      }

      case OPC.GLOBAL_GET: {
        const r = u32leb(p);
        p = r.next;
        push(E(r.value === 0 ? "G" : `G${r.value}`));
        break;
      }

      case OPC.GLOBAL_SET: {
        const r = u32leb(p);
        p = r.next;
        pop();
        break;
      }

      case OPC.I32_CONST: {
        const r = i32leb(p);
        p = r.next;
        push({ k: "const", v: r.value });
        break;
      }

      case OPC.I64_CONST: {
        const r = u32leb(p);
        p = r.next;
        push(E(`CONST64@${hex(addr)}`));
        break;
      }

      case OPC.I32_ADD: {
        const b = pop();
        const a = pop();
        push({ k: "add", a, b });
        break;
      }

      case OPC.I32_SUB: {
        const b = pop();
        const a = pop();
        push({ k: "sub", a, b });
        break;
      }

      case OPC.I32_SHL: {
        const b = pop();
        const a = pop();
        push({ k: "shl", a, b });
        break;
      }

      case OPC.I32_MUL: {
        const b = pop();
        const a = pop();
        push({ k: "unknown", s: `(${show(a)}*${show(b)})` });
        break;
      }

      case OPC.I64_LOAD: {
        const m = readMemArg(p);
        p = m.next;
        const a = pop();
        push({ k: "mem", a, off: m.off });
        break;
      }

      case OPC.I32_LOAD: {
        const m = readMemArg(p);
        p = m.next;
        const a = pop();
        push(E(`MEM32[${show(a)}${m.off ? signed(m.off) : ""}]`));
        break;
      }

      case OPC.I64_STORE: {
        const m = readMemArg(p);
        p = m.next;

        const value = pop();
        const address = pop();

        if (stores.includes(addr)) {
          reportStore(addr, address, value, m.off);
        }

        break;
      }

      case OPC.CALL: {
        const r = u32leb(p);
        p = r.next;

        console.log(`CALL @ ${hex(addr)} -> function ${r.value}`);

        /*
         * Keep stack conservative after calls.
         * Function 185/205 results are not modeled here.
         */
        stack.length = 0;
        break;
      }

      case OPC.BLOCK:
      case OPC.LOOP:
      case OPC.IF: {
        p += 1;
        break;
      }

      case OPC.ELSE:
      case OPC.END:
        break;

      default:
        /*
         * Unknown opcode: preserve synchronization only for this targeted
         * scanner by stopping rather than producing misleading addresses.
         */
        if (addr >= 0x96600 && addr <= 0x96853) {
          console.log(`UNKNOWN OPCODE @ ${hex(addr)} = 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`);
        }
        break;
    }
  } catch {
    console.log(`TRACE ERROR @ ${hex(addr)}`);
    break;
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" V5 SEARCH COMPLETE");
console.log(" TARGET = G-528 = L7-64");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE - NO NETWORK - NO TRANSACTION");

FILE: scripts\findInitialLocal13V16.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

const START = 0x964d1;
const END   = 0x9652b;

function hx(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(6, "0");
}

function uleb(p: number) {
  let v = 0;
  let s = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;

    if (!(x & 0x80))
      break;

    s += 7;
  }

  return { value: v, next: p };
}

function sleb32(p: number) {
  let v = 0;
  let s = 0;
  let x = 0;

  while (true) {
    x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;
    s += 7;

    if (!(x & 0x80))
      break;
  }

  if (s < 32 && (x & 0x40))
    v |= ~0 << s;

  return { value: v | 0, next: p };
}

function memarg(p: number) {
  const a = uleb(p);
  const o = uleb(a.next);

  return {
    align: a.value,
    offset: o.value,
    next: o.next
  };
}

const names: Record<number,string> = {
  0x20: "local.get",
  0x21: "local.set",
  0x22: "local.tee",

  0x23: "global.get",
  0x24: "global.set",

  0x28: "i32.load",
  0x29: "i64.load",

  0x41: "i32.const",
  0x42: "i64.const",

  0x6a: "i32.add",
  0x6b: "i32.sub",

  0x7c: "i64.add",
  0x7d: "i64.sub",
  0x7e: "i64.mul"
};

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" INITIAL LOCAL13 SEARCH V16");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();

console.log(`Range: ${hx(START)} .. ${hx(END)}`);
console.log();

let p = START;

while (p < END) {

  const at = p;
  const op = b[p++];

  let name =
    names[op] ??
    `UNKNOWN(0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2,"0")})`;

  let extra = "";

  if (
    op === 0x20 ||
    op === 0x21 ||
    op === 0x22 ||
    op === 0x23 ||
    op === 0x24
  ) {
    const r = uleb(p);
    p = r.next;
    extra = ` ${r.value}`;

    if (
      (op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) &&
      r.value === 13
    ) {
      console.log(
        `>>> FOUND ${
          op === 0x21 ? "local.set" : "local.tee"
        } 13 @ ${hx(at)}`
      );
    }
  }

  else if (op === 0x41) {
    const r = sleb32(p);
    p = r.next;
    extra = ` ${r.value}`;
  }

  else if (op === 0x28 || op === 0x29) {
    const m = memarg(p);
    p = m.next;
    extra =
      ` align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`;
  }

  console.log(`${hx(at)}  ${name}${extra}`);
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" TARGET");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();

console.log("At 0x9652b:");
console.log("  local13 is read as the initial state.");
console.log();

console.log("Need the last local.set/tee 13 before 0x9652b.");
console.log();

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" END V16");
console.log("============================================================");

import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

const START = 0x95e67;
const TARGET = 0x9652b;

function hx(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(6, "0");
}

function uleb(p: number) {
  let v = 0;
  let s = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];

    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;

    if (!(x & 0x80))
      break;

    s += 7;
  }

  return { value: v, next: p };
}

const hits: {
  offset: number;
  kind: string;
}[] = [];

let p = START;

while (p < TARGET) {

  const at = p;
  const op = b[p++];

  if (
    op === 0x21 ||
    op === 0x22 ||
    op === 0x20
  ) {

    const r = uleb(p);
    p = r.next;

    if (r.value === 13) {

      hits.push({
        offset: at,
        kind:
          op === 0x20
            ? "local.get 13"
            : op === 0x21
              ? "local.set 13"
              : "local.tee 13"
      });
    }

    continue;
  }

  // Skip immediates of common instructions.
  if (op === 0x41) {

    const r = uleb(p);
    p = r.next;
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x42) {

    // signed LEB64
    while (true) {
      const x = b[p++];
      if (!(x & 0x80))
        break;
    }

    continue;
  }

  if (
    op === 0x28 ||
    op === 0x29 ||
    op === 0x2a ||
    op === 0x2b ||
    op === 0x2c ||
    op === 0x2d ||
    op === 0x2e ||
    op === 0x2f ||
    op === 0x30 ||
    op === 0x31 ||
    op === 0x32 ||
    op === 0x33 ||
    op === 0x34 ||
    op === 0x35 ||
    op === 0x36 ||
    op === 0x37 ||
    op === 0x38 ||
    op === 0x39 ||
    op === 0x3a ||
    op === 0x3b ||
    op === 0x3c ||
    op === 0x3d ||
    op === 0x3e
  ) {

    const a = uleb(p);
    const o = uleb(a.next);

    p = o.next;
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x10) {

    const r = uleb(p);
    p = r.next;
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {

    const r = uleb(p);
    p = r.next;
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0xfc) {

    const sub = uleb(p);
    p = sub.next;

    // memory.copy / memory.fill
    if (sub.value === 10) {

      const a = uleb(p);
      const c = uleb(a.next);

      p = c.next;

    } else if (sub.value === 11) {

      const a = uleb(p);
      p = a.next;
    }

    continue;
  }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" LAST LOCAL13 PRODUCER V17");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();

console.log("Function range:");
console.log(`  ${hx(START)} .. ${hx(TARGET)}`);

console.log();
console.log("ALL local13 REFERENCES BEFORE TARGET:");
console.log();

for (const h of hits) {
  console.log(`${hx(h.offset)}  ${h.kind}`);
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");

const producers = hits.filter(
  x =>
    x.kind === "local.set 13" ||
    x.kind === "local.tee 13"
);

console.log();

if (producers.length === 0) {

  console.log("NO local.set/tee 13 FOUND.");

} else {

  const last = producers[producers.length - 1];

  console.log("LAST PRODUCER:");
  console.log(`  ${hx(last.offset)}  ${last.kind}`);

  console.log();
  console.log("INITIAL local13 USED AT:");
  console.log(`  ${hx(TARGET)}  local.get 13`);

  console.log();
  console.log("DISTANCE:");
  console.log(
    `  ${TARGET - last.offset} bytes`
  );
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" END V17");
console.log("============================================================");

import fs from 'fs';
import path from 'path';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const ROOT = 'G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin';

const SEARCH_DIRS = [
    path.join(ROOT, 'node_modules', '@ston-fi'),
    path.join(ROOT, 'node_modules', '@ton'),
    path.join(ROOT, 'contracts'),
    path.join(ROOT, 'wrappers'),
];

const TARGET =
    '02C95A2ED22AB516F77F9D4898DC4578E72F18A2448E8F6832334B0B4BF501BC79';

const TEXT_EXTENSIONS = new Set([
    '.ts',
    '.js',
    '.mjs',
    '.cjs',
    '.json',
    '.map',
    '.md',
    '.tolk',
    '.func',
    '.fc',
    '.txt',
]);

let filesScanned = 0;
let matches = 0;

function scanFile(file: string) {
    const ext = path.extname(file).toLowerCase();

    if (!TEXT_EXTENSIONS.has(ext)) {
        return;
    }

    filesScanned++;

    let text: string;

    try {
        text = fs.readFileSync(file, 'utf8');
    } catch {
        return;
    }

    const lower = text.toLowerCase();
    const target = TARGET.toLowerCase();

    let offset = lower.indexOf(target);

    while (offset !== -1) {
        matches++;

        console.log();
        console.log('============================================================');
        console.log('*** REAL LIBRARY HASH MATCH ***');
        console.log('============================================================');

        console.log('FILE:');
        console.log(file);

        console.log();
        console.log('OFFSET:');
        console.log(offset);

        console.log();
        console.log('CONTEXT:');

        const start = Math.max(0, offset - 500);
        const end = Math.min(
            text.length,
            offset + TARGET.length + 500
        );

        console.log(text.slice(start, end));

        offset = lower.indexOf(
            target,
            offset + TARGET.length
        );
    }
}

function scanDirectory(dir: string) {
    if (!fs.existsSync(dir)) {
        console.log('SKIP - directory not found:');
        console.log(dir);
        return;
    }

    let entries: fs.Dirent[];

    try {
        entries = fs.readdirSync(dir, {
            withFileTypes: true,
        });
    } catch {
        return;
    }

    for (const entry of entries) {
        const full = path.join(dir, entry.name);

        if (entry.isDirectory()) {
            scanDirectory(full);
        } else if (entry.isFile()) {
            scanFile(full);
        }
    }
}

async function main() {
    console.log('='.repeat(72));
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 LIBRARY HASH SEARCH - REAL');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - LOCAL SDK/PROJECT FILES');
    console.log('='.repeat(72));

    console.log();
    console.log('TARGET:');
    console.log(TARGET);

    console.log();

    for (const dir of SEARCH_DIRS) {
        console.log('Scanning:');
        console.log(dir);
        scanDirectory(dir);
        console.log();
    }

    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log('SUMMARY');
    console.log('============================================================');

    console.log('files scanned:', filesScanned);
    console.log('real matches:', matches);

    if (matches === 0) {
        console.log();
        console.log('NO REAL LOCAL MATCH FOUND.');
        console.log();
        console.log(
            'The Library hash is not present in the local SDK/project sources.'
        );
        console.log();
        console.log(
            'Next step: identify the Library on-chain.'
        );
    } else {
        console.log();
        console.log(
            'REAL LIBRARY SOURCE MATCH FOUND.'
        );
    }

    console.log();
    console.log('No transaction was sent.');
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    await main();
}

FILE: scripts\findLpAccountSchemaV22.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from 'fs';
import path from 'path';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const ROOT = path.join(
    process.cwd(),
    'node_modules',
    '@ston-fi',
    'sdk',
    'dist'
);

const TARGETS = [
    'LpAccountV2_2',
    'LpAccountV2_1',
    '0x178d4519',
    '178d4519',
    'LP_ACCOUNT',
    'createTransfer',
    'createMint',
    'createBurn',
    'transfer',
    'mint',
    'burn',
];

const FILE_PRIORITY = [
    'chunk-G66DMSME.cjs',
    'chunk-G66DMSME.cjs.map',
];

function allFiles(dir: string): string[] {
    const out: string[] = [];

    for (const name of fs.readdirSync(dir)) {
        const full = path.join(dir, name);

        let st;
        try {
            st = fs.statSync(full);
        } catch {
            continue;
        }

        if (st.isDirectory()) {
            out.push(...allFiles(full));
        } else {
            out.push(full);
        }
    }

    return out;
}

function printContext(
    file: string,
    content: string,
    index: number,
    term: string,
) {
    const start = Math.max(0, index - 1000);
    const end = Math.min(content.length, index + term.length + 2500);

    console.log('\n------------------------------------------------------------');
    console.log(`MATCH: ${term}`);
    console.log(`FILE : ${path.relative(ROOT, file)}`);
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------');
    console.log(content.slice(start, end));
}

export async function run(_provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 LP ACCOUNT SCHEMA SEARCH');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - LOCAL SDK FILES ONLY');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(`SDK root: ${ROOT}`);

    if (!fs.existsSync(ROOT)) {
        console.log('\nERROR: SDK dist directory not found.');
        return;
    }

    const files = allFiles(ROOT).filter((f) =>
        /\.(cjs|js|map|d\.ts)$/.test(f)
    );

    console.log(`Files scanned: ${files.length}`);

    const priority = new Set(
        FILE_PRIORITY.map((x) => path.normalize(path.join(ROOT, x)))
    );

    const ordered = [
        ...files.filter((f) => priority.has(path.normalize(f))),
        ...files.filter((f) => !priority.has(path.normalize(f))),
    ];

    const shown = new Set<string>();
    let total = 0;

    for (const file of ordered) {
        let content: string;

        try {
            content = fs.readFileSync(file, 'utf8');
        } catch {
            continue;
        }

        for (const term of TARGETS) {
            let pos = 0;

            while (true) {
                const index = content.indexOf(term, pos);

                if (index === -1) break;

                total++;

                const key = `${file}|${term}|${index}`;

                if (!shown.has(key)) {
                    shown.add(key);

                    printContext(
                        file,
                        content,
                        index,
                        term,
                    );
                }

                pos = index + term.length;

                // Prevent enormous duplicate output.
                if (shown.size >= 80) {
                    console.log('\n[OUTPUT LIMIT REACHED]');
                    console.log('Stopping after 80 matches.');
                    console.log('============================================================');
                    console.log(`Total displayed matches: ${shown.size}`);
                    return;
                }
            }
        }
    }

    console.log('\n============================================================');
    console.log(' INSPECTION COMPLETE');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(`Total displayed matches: ${shown.size}`);
}

import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const POOL =
    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf';

const LP_WALLET =
    'EQCNnXhynq5G7RZ8glhXSNg6eXby1kzqD-p8HNxu6PN5SIgd';

const USER =
    'EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix';

const API =
    'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v3';

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('==============================================================');
    console.log(' TONCENTER v3 LP SUPPLY DIFFERENCE INSPECTOR');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('==============================================================');
    console.log('');

    console.log('POOL:');
    console.log(POOL);
    console.log('');

    console.log('LP WALLET:');
    console.log(LP_WALLET);
    console.log('');

    console.log('USER:');
    console.log(USER);
    console.log('');

    const url =
        `${API}/jetton/transfers` +
        `?jetton_master=${encodeURIComponent(POOL)}` +
        `&owner_address=${encodeURIComponent(USER)}` +
        `&limit=100`;

    console.log('REQUEST:');
    console.log(url);
    console.log('');

    try {
        const response = await fetch(url);

        console.log('HTTP STATUS:', response.status);
        console.log('HTTP STATUS TEXT:', response.statusText);
        console.log('');

        const text = await response.text();

        if (!response.ok) {
            console.log('API RESPONSE:');
            console.log(text);
            console.log('');
            console.log('READ ONLY: YES');
            console.log('TRANSACTION SENT: NO');
            return;
        }

        const json = JSON.parse(text);

        console.log('==============================================================');
        console.log(' JETTON TRANSFERS');
        console.log('==============================================================');
        console.log('');

        console.log(JSON.stringify(json, null, 2));

    } catch (error) {
        console.log('');
        console.log('REQUEST ERROR:');
        console.log(error);
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('==============================================================');
    console.log(' READ ONLY: YES');
    console.log(' TRANSACTION SENT: NO');
    console.log('==============================================================');
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\findLpDustV22.ts

import { Address, TonClient4 } from '@ton/ton';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const POOL =
    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf';

const TARGET_AMOUNT = 1001n;

function shortAddress(address: any): string {
    try {
        return address.toString();
    } catch {
        return String(address);
    }
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('==============================================================');
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 LP DUST / SUPPLY DIFFERENCE INSPECTOR');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('==============================================================');
    console.log('');

    const client = new TonClient4({
        endpoint: 'https://testnet-v4.tonhubapi.com',
    });

    const poolAddress = Address.parse(POOL);

    const latest = await client.getLastBlock();

    console.log('MASTERCHAIN SEQNO:', latest.last.seqno);
    console.log('');
    console.log('POOL:');
    console.log(POOL);
    console.log('');
    console.log('TARGET DIFFERENCE:');
    console.log('1001 base LP units');
    console.log('0.000001001 LP');
    console.log('');

    const transactions = await client.getAccountTransactions(
        poolAddress,
        0n,
        Buffer.alloc(32)
    );

    console.log('TRANSACTIONS RETURNED:', transactions.length);
    console.log('');

    let found = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < transactions.length; i++) {
        const tx: any = transactions[i].tx;

        const txHash =
            typeof tx.hash === 'function'
                ? tx.hash().toString('base64')
                : tx.hash
                    ? Buffer.from(tx.hash).toString('base64')
                    : 'N/A';

        const lt = tx.lt?.toString() ?? 'N/A';

        const outMessages: any = tx.outMessages;

        if (!outMessages?._map) {
            continue;
        }

        const internalMap: Map<any, any> = outMessages._map;

        for (const [key, message] of internalMap.entries()) {
            try {
                if (!message?.body) {
                    continue;
                }

                const slice = message.body.beginParse();

                if (slice.remainingBits < 32) {
                    continue;
                }

                const op = slice.loadUint(32);

                if (op !== 0x178d4519) {
                    continue;
                }

                const queryId = slice.loadUintBig(64);
                const amount = slice.loadCoins();

                const from = slice.loadAddress();

                let responseAddress: any = null;
                let forwardTonAmount = 0n;

                try {
                    responseAddress = slice.loadAddress();
                    forwardTonAmount = slice.loadCoins();
                } catch {
                    // Ignore optional parsing differences.
                }

                const destination =
                    message.info?.dest ??
                    message.destination ??
                    key;

                console.log('----------------------------------------');
                console.log('TX LT:', lt);
                console.log('TX HASH:', txHash);
                console.log('DESTINATION:', shortAddress(destination));
                console.log('OPCODE: 0x178d4519');
                console.log('QUERY ID:', queryId.toString());
                console.log('LP AMOUNT:', amount.toString());
                console.log(
                    'LP AMOUNT DISPLAY:',
                    Number(amount) / 1e9
                );
                console.log('FROM:', shortAddress(from));

                if (responseAddress) {
                    console.log(
                        'RESPONSE:',
                        shortAddress(responseAddress)
                    );
                }

                console.log(
                    'FORWARD TON:',
                    forwardTonAmount.toString()
                );

                if (amount === TARGET_AMOUNT) {
                    console.log('');
                    console.log('******** TARGET MATCH ********');
                    console.log('FOUND EXACT 1001 LP BASE UNITS');
                    console.log('*******************************');
                    found++;
                }
            } catch {
                // Ignore messages that are not parseable.
            }
        }
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('==============================================================');
    console.log('SEARCH RESULT');
    console.log('==============================================================');
    console.log('');

    if (found > 0) {
        console.log('EXACT 1001-UNIT LP TRANSFER FOUND:', found);
    } else {
        console.log('NO EXACT 1001-UNIT LP TRANSFER FOUND');
        console.log('');
        console.log(
            'This does NOT mean the 1001 units are missing.'
        );
        console.log(
            'They may originate from mint/burn/accounting state rather than'
        );
        console.log(
            'a direct 1001-unit internal_transfer message.'
        );
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('READ ONLY: YES');
    console.log('TRANSACTION SENT: NO');
    console.log('==============================================================');
}

FILE: scripts\findMem28Writers.ts

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function u(pos: number) {
    let v = 0, s = 0, x = 0;
    do {
        x = b[pos++];
        v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;
        s += 7;
    } while (x & 0x80);
    return { value: v, next: pos };
}

function hex(x: number) {
    return "0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

// Locate code section
let p = 8;
let codeStart = 0;
let codeEnd = 0;

while (p < b.length) {
    const id = b[p++];
    const r = u(p);
    p = r.next;

    if (id === 10) {
        codeStart = p;
        codeEnd = p + r.value;
        break;
    }

    p += r.value;
}

if (!codeStart) throw new Error("CODE SECTION NOT FOUND");

p = codeStart;

const cr = u(p);
const count = cr.value;
p = cr.next;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FIND WRITERS FOR MEM[0x28]");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNCTIONS :", count);
console.log("TARGET    : MEM[0x28]");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let found = 0;

for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < count; ordinal++) {

    const br = u(p);
    const bodySize = br.value;
    p = br.next;

    const bodyStart = p;
    const bodyEnd = p + bodySize;

    // locals
    const lr = u(p);
    const groups = lr.value;
    p = lr.next;

    for (let j = 0; j < groups; j++) {
        const nr = u(p);
        p = nr.next;
        p++; // valtype
    }

    // Scan instructions.
    for (let q = p; q < bodyEnd; q++) {

        // i32.store = 0x36
        if (b[q] !== 0x36) continue;

        const align = b[q + 1];
        const r = u(q + 2);
        const offset = r.value;

        // offset 40 is interesting because:
        // local.get 3 (= 0)
        // i32.load offset=40
        // => MEM[0x28]
        if (offset === 40) {

            console.log(
                "FUNC",
                ordinal + 83,
                "STORE @",
                hex(q),
                "offset=40"
            );

            // Show 16 bytes before the store.
            const a = Math.max(bodyStart, q - 16);
            const bytes = [...b.subarray(a, Math.min(bodyEnd, q + 8))]
                .map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
                .join(" ");

            console.log("  RAW:", bytes);
            console.log();

            found++;
        }
    }

    p = bodyEnd;
}

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("TOTAL OFFSET=40 STORES :", found);
console.log("============================================================");

FILE: scripts\findMemory1659724V22.ts

import fs from 'fs';

const WASM_PATH =
    'G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm';

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM_PATH);

const TARGET = 1659724; // 0x19534C

function readU32(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[pos++];

        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: pos };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function readI32(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let b = 0;

    while (true) {
        b = wasm[pos++];

        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
                value |= (~0 << shift);
            }

            return { value, next: pos };
        }
    }
}

function readString(pos: number) {
    const n = readU32(pos);
    const start = n.next;
    const end = start + n.value;

    return {
        value: wasm.subarray(start, end).toString('utf8'),
        next: end,
    };
}

function readInitExpr(pos: number) {
    const op = wasm[pos++];

    if (op === 0x41) {
        const x = readI32(pos);

        pos = x.next;

        if (wasm[pos] !== 0x0b) {
            throw new Error(
                `Expected end of init expr at ${pos}`
            );
        }

        pos++;

        return {
            offset: x.value,
            next: pos,
        };
    }

    throw new Error(
        `Unsupported data offset expression opcode 0x${op
            .toString(16)
            .padStart(2, '0')} at ${pos - 1}`
    );
}

console.log('');
console.log('============================================================');
console.log(' WASM MEMORY DATA SEGMENT SEARCH v22');
console.log(' READ ONLY - OFFLINE');
console.log('============================================================');
console.log('');

console.log(`WASM size : ${wasm.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log(`TARGET    : ${TARGET}`);
console.log(`TARGET HEX: 0x${TARGET.toString(16)}`);
console.log('');

let p = 8;

let foundSection = false;

while (p < wasm.length) {

    const sectionId = wasm[p++];

    const sizeInfo = readU32(p);
    p = sizeInfo.next;

    const sectionStart = p;
    const sectionEnd = sectionStart + sizeInfo.value;

    if (sectionId !== 11) {
        p = sectionEnd;
        continue;
    }

    foundSection = true;

    console.log(
        `Data section found: offset=${sectionStart} size=${sizeInfo.value}`
    );
    console.log('');

    const countInfo = readU32(p);
    p = countInfo.next;

    console.log(`Data segments: ${countInfo.value}`);
    console.log('');

    for (let seg = 0; seg < countInfo.value; seg++) {

        const flagsInfo = readU32(p);
        p = flagsInfo.next;

        const flags = flagsInfo.value;

        let memoryIndex = 0;
        let offset: number | null = null;

        /*
         * flags:
         * 0 = active, memory 0, offset expr follows
         * 1 = passive, no offset expr
         * 2 = active, explicit memory index, offset expr follows
         */

        if (flags === 0) {

            const expr = readInitExpr(p);
            p = expr.next;

            offset = expr.offset;

        } else if (flags === 1) {

            offset = null;

        } else if (flags === 2) {

            const mem = readU32(p);
            p = mem.next;

            memoryIndex = mem.value;

            const expr = readInitExpr(p);
            p = expr.next;

            offset = expr.offset;

        } else {

            console.log(
                `Segment ${seg}: unsupported flags=${flags}`
            );

            break;
        }

        const dataSizeInfo = readU32(p);
        p = dataSizeInfo.next;

        const dataStart = p;
        const dataEnd = dataStart + dataSizeInfo.value;

        const dataSize = dataSizeInfo.value;

        const containsTarget =
            offset !== null &&
            TARGET >= offset &&
            TARGET < offset + dataSize;

        if (containsTarget) {

            const relative = TARGET - offset;

            console.log('============================================================');
            console.log(' TARGET FOUND');
            console.log('============================================================');
            console.log('');

            console.log(`segment       : ${seg}`);
            console.log(`flags         : ${flags}`);
            console.log(`memory index  : ${memoryIndex}`);
            console.log(`segment offset: ${offset}`);
            console.log(
                `segment offset hex: 0x${offset.toString(16)}`
            );
            console.log(`data size     : ${dataSize}`);
            console.log(`target        : ${TARGET}`);
            console.log(
                `target hex    : 0x${TARGET.toString(16)}`
            );
            console.log(`relative      : ${relative}`);
            console.log('');

            const from = Math.max(0, relative - 32);
            const to = Math.min(dataSize, relative + 32);

            console.log('DATA AROUND TARGET:');
            console.log('');

            for (let i = from; i < to; i++) {

                const absoluteAddress = offset! + i;

                const mark =
                    absoluteAddress === TARGET
                        ? ' <<< TARGET'
                        : '';

                console.log(
                    `addr=${absoluteAddress
                        .toString()
                        .padStart(9)} ` +
                    `0x${absoluteAddress
                        .toString(16)
                        .padStart(8, '0')} ` +
                    `byte=0x${wasm[dataStart + i]
                        .toString(16)
                        .padStart(2, '0')}${mark}`
                );
            }

            console.log('');

            if (relative + 4 <= dataSize) {

                const b0 = wasm[dataStart + relative];
                const b1 = wasm[dataStart + relative + 1];
                const b2 = wasm[dataStart + relative + 2];
                const b3 = wasm[dataStart + relative + 3];

                const value =
                    (b0 |
                        (b1 << 8) |
                        (b2 << 16) |
                        (b3 << 24)) >>> 0;

                console.log('32-BIT VALUE AT TARGET:');
                console.log('');

                console.log(`bytes : ${[
                    b0,
                    b1,
                    b2,
                    b3,
                ]
                    .map(x =>
                        x.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')
                    )
                    .join(' ')}`);

                console.log(`uint32: ${value}`);
                console.log(
                    `hex   : 0x${value.toString(16)}`
                );

                console.log('');

                if (value > 4788) {
                    console.log(
                        `CHECK : ${value} > 4788 = TRUE`
                    );
                } else {
                    console.log(
                        `CHECK : ${value} > 4788 = FALSE`
                    );
                }
            }

            console.log('');
        }

        p = dataEnd;
    }

    break;
}

if (!foundSection) {
    console.log('Data section (11) was not found.');
}

console.log('');
console.log('============================================================');
console.log(' DONE');
console.log('============================================================');

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\findObject80Consumers.mjs
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function u(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, x;
  do {
    x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 127) * 2 ** s;
    s += 7;
  } while (x & 128);
  return [v >>> 0, p];
}

function sleb(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, x;
  do {
    x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 127) << s;
    s += 7;
  } while (x & 128);

  if (s < 32 && (x & 0x40)) v |= (~0 << s);
  return [v | 0, p];
}

function hex(x) {
  return "0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

// Find code section
let p = 8;
let codeStart = 0;

while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p++];
  let size;
  [size, p] = u(p);

  if (id === 10) {
    codeStart = p;
    break;
  }

  p += size;
}

let q = codeStart;
let bodyCount;
[bodyCount, q] = u(q);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC OBJECT +80 / +84 CONSUMERS");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("BODY COUNT :", bodyCount);
console.log("");

for (let i = 0; i < bodyCount; i++) {
  let bodySize;
  [bodySize, q] = u(q);

  const bodyStart = q;
  const bodyEnd = q + bodySize;
  const func = i + 91;

  let p2 = bodyStart;

  // locals
  let groups;
  [groups, p2] = u(p2);

  for (let g = 0; g < groups; g++) {
    let n;
    [n, p2] = u(p2);
    p2++; // valtype
  }

  const hits = [];

  while (p2 < bodyEnd) {
    const opPos = p2;
    const op = b[p2++];

    // i32.load / i32.store
    if (op === 0x28 || op === 0x36) {
      let align, offset;
      [align, p2] = u(p2);
      [offset, p2] = u(p2);

      if (offset === 80 || offset === 84) {
        hits.push({
          pos: opPos,
          op,
          offset
        });
      }

      continue;
    }

    // Skip immediates for common instructions
    if (
      op === 0x20 || // local.get
      op === 0x21 || // local.set
      op === 0x22 || // local.tee
      op === 0x10 || // call
      op === 0x0c || // br
      op === 0x0d || // br_if
      op === 0x41 || // i32.const
      op === 0x42 || // i64.const
      op === 0x43 || // f32.const
      op === 0x44    // f64.const
    ) {
      if (op === 0x43) p2 += 4;
      else if (op === 0x44) p2 += 8;
      else {
        let dummy;
        [dummy, p2] = u(p2);
      }
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x11) {
      let typeidx;
      [typeidx, p2] = u(p2);
      p2++; // table index
      continue;
    }

    // block / loop / if
    if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04) {
      p2++;
      continue;
    }

    // memory.size / memory.grow
    if (op === 0x3f || op === 0x40) {
      p2++;
      continue;
    }

    // br_table
    if (op === 0x0e) {
      let n;
      [n, p2] = u(p2);
      for (let z = 0; z < n + 1; z++) {
        let dummy;
        [dummy, p2] = u(p2);
      }
      continue;
    }

    // ref.func
    if (op === 0xd2) {
      let dummy;
      [dummy, p2] = u(p2);
      continue;
    }

    // table.get / table.set
    if (op === 0x25 || op === 0x26) {
      let dummy;
      [dummy, p2] = u(p2);
      continue;
    }

    // otherwise no immediate
  }

  if (hits.length) {
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
    console.log("FUNC", func);
    console.log("BODY", hex(bodyStart), "..", hex(bodyEnd));

    for (const h of hits) {
      console.log(
        hex(h.pos),
        h.op === 0x28 ? "i32.load" : "i32.store",
        "offset=" + h.offset
      );

      const from = Math.max(bodyStart, h.pos - 10);
      const to = Math.min(bodyEnd, h.pos + 12);

      console.log(
        "  RAW:",
        [...b.slice(from, to)]
          .map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
          .join(" ")
      );
    }
  }

  q = bodyEnd;
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");

function u(p) {

  do {
    x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 127) * 2 ** s;
    s += 7;
  } while (x & 128);
  return [v >>> 0, p];
}

let p = 8;
let codeStart = 0;

while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p++];
  let size;
  [size, p] = u(p);

  if (id === 10) {
    codeStart = p;
    break;
  }

  p += size;
}

console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");

let hits = 0;

for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
  let size;

  const start = q;
  const end = q + size;
  const func = i + 91;

  for (let x = start; x < end - 3; x++) {

    let align, z;
    [align, z] = u(x + 1);

    let offset;
    [offset, z] = u(z);

    if (offset !== 0) continue;

    const from = Math.max(start, x - 20);
    const to = Math.min(end, x + 12);

    console.log("");
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
    console.log("FUNC  :", func);
    console.log("STORE :", "0x" + x.toString(16));
    console.log("OFFSET: 0");

    console.log(
      "RAW:",
      [...b.slice(from, to)]
        .map(v => v.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
        .join(" ")
    );

    hits++;
  }

  q = end;

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\findOffset128Stores.js
====================================================================================================
const fs = require("fs");

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const TARGET_OFFSET = 128;

function readULEB(pos) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[p++];
    result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if (!(b & 0x80)) break;

    shift += 7;
  }

  return {
    value: result >>> 0,
    next: p
  };
}

function readSLEB(pos) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let b;

  do {
    b = wasm[pos++];
    result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;
  } while (b & 0x80);

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
    result |= (~0 << shift);
  }

  return {
    value: result | 0,
    next: pos
  };
}

function hex(n) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function readName(pos) {
  const x = readULEB(pos);
  const start = x.next;
  const end = start + x.value;

  return {
    next: end
  };
}

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * Locate sections
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 */

let p = 8;

let importSection = null;
let functionSection = null;
let codeSection = null;

while (p < wasm.length) {
  const sectionId = wasm[p++];

  const sizeInfo = readULEB(p);
  const size = sizeInfo.value;

  const start = sizeInfo.next;
  const end = start + size;

  if (sectionId === 2)
    importSection = { start, end };

  if (sectionId === 3)
    functionSection = { start, end };

  if (sectionId === 10)
    codeSection = { start, end };

  p = end;
}

if (!importSection || !functionSection || !codeSection) {
  throw new Error("Required WASM sections not found");
}

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * Count imported functions
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 */

p = importSection.start;

let x = readULEB(p);
let importCount = x.value;
p = x.next;

let importedFunctions = 0;

for (let i = 0; i < importCount; i++) {

  p = readName(p).next;
  p = readName(p).next;

  const kind = wasm[p++];

  if (kind === 0) {
    p = readULEB(p).next;
    importedFunctions++;
  }

  else if (kind === 1) {
    p++; // elemtype

    const flags = wasm[p++];

    p = readULEB(p).next;

    if (flags & 1)
      p = readULEB(p).next;
  }

  else if (kind === 2) {
    const flags = readULEB(p);
    p = flags.next;

    p = readULEB(p).next;

    if (flags.value & 1)
      p = readULEB(p).next;
  }

  else if (kind === 3) {
    p++;
    p++;
  }
}

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * Function section
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 */

p = functionSection.start;

const fc = readULEB(p);
const definedCount = fc.value;
p = fc.next;

const functionTypes = [];

for (let i = 0; i < definedCount; i++) {
  const t = readULEB(p);
  p = t.next;

  functionTypes.push(t.value);
}

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * Code bodies
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 */

p = codeSection.start;

const bc = readULEB(p);
const bodyCount = bc.value;
p = bc.next;

const bodies = [];

for (let i = 0; i < bodyCount; i++) {

  const bs = readULEB(p);

  const bodySize = bs.value;
  const bodyStart = bs.next;
  const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bodySize;

  bodies.push({
    func: importedFunctions + i,
    start: bodyStart,
    end: bodyEnd
  });

  p = bodyEnd;
}

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * Raw instruction scanner
 *
 * We only need to reliably recognize:
 *
 *   0x36 i32.store
 *   0x37 i64.store
 *   0x38 f32.store
 *   0x39 f64.store
 *
 * and memory offset immediates.
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 */

function scanStoreInstructions(body) {

  let p = body.start;

  /*
   * local declarations
   */
  const locals = readULEB(p);
  p = locals.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < locals.value; i++) {

    const count = readULEB(p);
    p = count.next;

    p++; // value type
  }

  const results = [];

  while (p < body.end) {

    const start = p;
    const op = wasm[p++];

    /*
     * i32.store
     */
    if (op === 0x36) {

      const align = readULEB(p);
      p = align.next;

      const off = readULEB(p);
      p = off.next;

      if (off.value === TARGET_OFFSET) {
        results.push({
          func: body.func,
          addr: start,
          opcode: "i32.store",
          align: align.value,
          offset: off.value
        });
      }

      continue;
    }

    /*
     * i64.store
     */
    if (op === 0x37) {

      const align = readULEB(p);
      p = align.next;

      const off = readULEB(p);
      p = off.next;

      if (off.value === TARGET_OFFSET) {
        results.push({
          func: body.func,
          addr: start,
          opcode: "i64.store",
          align: align.value,
          offset: off.value
        });
      }

      continue;
    }

    /*
     * f32.store
     */
    if (op === 0x38) {

      const align = readULEB(p);
      p = align.next;

      const off = readULEB(p);
      p = off.next;

      if (off.value === TARGET_OFFSET) {
        results.push({
          func: body.func,
          addr: start,
          opcode: "f32.store",
          align: align.value,
          offset: off.value
        });
      }

      continue;
    }

    /*
     * f64.store
     */
    if (op === 0x39) {

      const align = readULEB(p);
      p = align.next;

      const off = readULEB(p);
      p = off.next;

      if (off.value === TARGET_OFFSET) {
        results.push({
          func: body.func,
          addr: start,
          opcode: "f64.store",
          align: align.value,
          offset: off.value
        });
      }

      continue;
    }

    /*
     * immediates for common instructions
     */
    if (
      op === 0x20 ||
      op === 0x21 ||
      op === 0x22 ||
      op === 0x10 ||
      op === 0x0c ||
      op === 0x0d
    ) {
      p = readULEB(p).next;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x41) {
      p = readSLEB(p).next;
      continue;
    }

    /*
     * Loads.
     */
    if (
      op === 0x28 ||
      op === 0x29 ||
      op === 0x2a ||
      op === 0x2b ||
      op === 0x2c ||
      op === 0x2d
    ) {
      p = readULEB(p).next;
      p = readULEB(p).next;
      continue;
    }

    /*
     * call_indirect
     */
    if (op === 0x11) {
      p = readULEB(p).next;
      p = readULEB(p).next;
      continue;
    }

    /*
     * br_table
     */
    if (op === 0x0e) {
      const n = readULEB(p);
      p = n.next;

      for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++)
        p = readULEB(p).next;

      p = readULEB(p).next;
      continue;
    }

    /*
     * Memory.size / memory.grow
     */
    if (op === 0x3f || op === 0x40) {
      p++;
      continue;
    }

    /*
     * Other opcodes are one byte.
     */
  }

  return results;
}

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * Execute scan
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 */

const hits = [];

for (const body of bodies) {

  const found = scanStoreInstructions(body);

  for (const x of found)
    hits.push(x);
}

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * Output
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 */

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FIND OFFSET 128 STORES");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log("WASM SIZE :", wasm.length);
console.log("TARGET OFFSET :", TARGET_OFFSET);
console.log("IMPORTED FUNCTIONS :", importedFunctions);
console.log("DEFINED FUNCTIONS :", bodyCount);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

console.log("RESULT");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (const hit of hits) {

  console.log(
    `FUNC ${hit.func} @ ${hex(hit.addr)} ` +
    `${hit.opcode} align=${hit.align} offset=${hit.offset}`
  );
}

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("TOTAL HITS :", hits.length);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FINAL");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("READ ONLY : YES");
console.log("NETWORK : NO");
console.log("TRANSACTIONS : NO");

const fs = require("fs");

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm"
);

function u32(p) {
  let v = 0;
  let s = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[p++];

    v |= (b & 0x7f) << s;

    if (!(b & 0x80)) {
      return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };
    }

    s += 7;
  }
}

function hx(x) {
  return "0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function readLeb(p) {
  return u32(p);
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Find CODE section
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let p = 8;
let codeStart = 0;
let codeEnd = 0;

while (p < wasm.length) {

  const sectionId = wasm[p++];

  const s = u32(p);
  p = s.next;

  const start = p;
  const end = p + s.value;

  if (sectionId === 10) {
    codeStart = start;
    codeEnd = end;
    break;
  }

  p = end;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Parse function bodies
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let q = codeStart;

const count = u32(q);
q = count.next;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FIND i32.store OFFSET=24");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`FUNCTION BODIES : ${count.value}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let hits = 0;

for (let bodyIndex = 0; bodyIndex < count.value; bodyIndex++) {

  const sizeInfo = u32(q);
  q = sizeInfo.next;

  const bodyStart = q;
  const bodyEnd = q + sizeInfo.value;

  const actualFunction = 83 + bodyIndex;

  for (let x = bodyStart; x + 2 < bodyEnd; x++) {

    // i32.store = 0x36
    if (wasm[x] !== 0x36)
      continue;

    // memarg:
    // alignment
    // offset
    const align = wasm[x + 1];

    const off = u32(x + 2);

    if (off.value !== 24)
      continue;

    hits++;

    console.log("");
    console.log(
      `FUNC ${actualFunction} BODY ${bodyIndex}`
    );

    console.log(
      `ADDRESS : ${hx(x)}`
    );

    console.log(
      `STORE   : i32.store align=${align} offset=24`
    );

    const a = Math.max(bodyStart, x - 20);
    const b = Math.min(bodyEnd, x + 24);

    console.log("RAW CONTEXT:");

    for (let z = a; z < b; z++) {

      console.log(
        `  ${hx(z)} ${wasm[z].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
      );
    }
  }

  q = bodyEnd;
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`OFFSET=24 STORE COUNT : ${hits}`);
console.log("============================================================");

FILE: scripts\findOffset4Stores.js

const fs = require("fs");

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

function readULEB(buf, pos) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = buf[pos++];
    value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if (!(b & 0x80))
      return { value, next: pos };

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function readName(buf, pos) {
  const n = readULEB(buf, pos);
  const end = n.next + n.value;

  return {
    next: end
  };
}

function skipLimits(buf, pos) {
  const flags = buf[pos++];

  const min = readULEB(buf, pos);
  pos = min.next;

  if (flags & 1) {
    const max = readULEB(buf, pos);
    pos = max.next;
  }

  return pos;
}

function countImports(buf, sec) {
  let pos = sec.start;

  const n = readULEB(buf, pos);
  pos = n.next;

  let funcs = 0;

  for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
    pos = readName(buf, pos).next;
    pos = readName(buf, pos).next;

    const kind = buf[pos++];

    if (kind === 0) {
      const x = readULEB(buf, pos);
      pos = x.next;
      funcs++;
    } else if (kind === 1) {
      pos++; // elemtype
      pos = skipLimits(buf, pos);
    } else if (kind === 2) {
      pos = skipLimits(buf, pos);
    } else if (kind === 3) {
      pos += 2;
    } else if (kind === 4) {
      const x = readULEB(buf, pos);
      pos = x.next;
    } else {
      throw new Error("Unknown import kind " + kind);
    }
  }

  return funcs;
}

function sections(buf) {
  let pos = 8;
  const out = [];

  while (pos < buf.length) {
    const id = buf[pos++];

    const s = readULEB(buf, pos);
    pos = s.next;

    out.push({
      id,
      start: pos,
      end: pos + s.value
    });

    pos += s.value;
  }

  return out;
}

function skipOpcode(buf, pos, op) {
  let x;

  if (
    op === 0x20 ||
    op === 0x21 ||
    op === 0x22 ||
    op === 0x23 ||
    op === 0x24 ||
    op === 0x10 ||
    op === 0x0c ||
    op === 0x0d ||
    op === 0x25 ||
    op === 0x26
  ) {
    x = readULEB(buf, pos);
    return x.next;
  }

  if (op === 0x11) {
    x = readULEB(buf, pos);
    x = readULEB(buf, x.next);
    return x.next;
  }

  if (
    op === 0x28 ||
    op === 0x29 ||
    op === 0x2a ||
    op === 0x2b ||
    op === 0x2c ||
    op === 0x2d ||
    op === 0x2e ||
    op === 0x2f ||
    op === 0x30 ||
    op === 0x31 ||
    op === 0x32 ||
    op === 0x33 ||
    op === 0x34 ||
    op === 0x35 ||
    op === 0x36 ||
    op === 0x37 ||
    op === 0x38 ||
    op === 0x39 ||
    op === 0x3a ||
    op === 0x3b
  ) {
    x = readULEB(buf, pos);
    x = readULEB(buf, x.next);
    return x.next;
  }

  if (op === 0x41) {
    x = readULEB(buf, pos);
    return x.next;
  }

  if (op === 0x42) {
    x = readULEB(buf, pos);
    return x.next;
  }

  if (op === 0x43) return pos + 4;
  if (op === 0x44) return pos + 8;

  if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04) {
    return pos + 1;
  }

  if (op === 0x0e) {
    const n = readULEB(buf, pos);
    pos = n.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
      const y = readULEB(buf, pos);
      pos = y.next;
    }

    const d = readULEB(buf, pos);
    return d.next;
  }

  return pos;
}

const sec = sections(wasm);

const importSec = sec.find(x => x.id === 2);
const functionSec = sec.find(x => x.id === 3);
const codeSec = sec.find(x => x.id === 10);

const importedFunctions =
  countImports(wasm, importSec);

let fp = functionSec.start;

const fnCount = readULEB(wasm, fp);
fp = fnCount.next;

const typeIndices = [];

for (let i = 0; i < fnCount.value; i++) {
  const x = readULEB(wasm, fp);
  fp = x.next;
  typeIndices.push(x.value);
}

let cp = codeSec.start;

const bodyCount = readULEB(wasm, cp);
cp = bodyCount.next;

const results = [];

for (let bodyIndex = 0; bodyIndex < bodyCount.value; bodyIndex++) {
  const bodySize = readULEB(wasm, cp);
  cp = bodySize.next;

  const bodyStart = cp;
  const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bodySize.value;

  const funcIndex = importedFunctions + bodyIndex;

  let pos = bodyStart;

  // locals
  const localCount = readULEB(wasm, pos);
  pos = localCount.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < localCount.value; i++) {
    const n = readULEB(wasm, pos);
    pos = n.next;
    pos++;
  }

  while (pos < bodyEnd) {
    const addr = pos;
    const op = wasm[pos++];

    if (
      op === 0x36 ||
      op === 0x37 ||
      op === 0x3a ||
      op === 0x3b
    ) {
      const a = readULEB(wasm, pos);
      const o = readULEB(wasm, a.next);

      if (o.value === 4) {
        results.push({
          func: funcIndex,
          addr,
          op,
          align: a.value
        });
      }

      pos = o.next;
      continue;
    }

    pos = skipOpcode(wasm, pos, op);

    if (pos > bodyEnd)
      break;
  }

  cp = bodyEnd;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FIND OFFSET 4 STORES");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("WASM SIZE :", wasm.length);
console.log("IMPORTED FUNCTIONS :", importedFunctions);
console.log("DEFINED FUNCTIONS :", bodyCount.value);
console.log("OFFSET TARGET : 4");
console.log("HITS :", results.length);

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("RESULTS");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const r of results) {
  const name =
    r.op === 0x36 ? "i32.store" :
    r.op === 0x37 ? "i64.store" :
    r.op === 0x3a ? "i32.store8" :
    "i32.store16";

  console.log(
    "FUNC " + r.func +
    "  @ 0x" +
    r.addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0") +
    "  " + name +
    " offset=4"
  );
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FINAL");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("READ ONLY : YES");
console.log("NETWORK : NO");
console.log("TRANSACTIONS : NO");

import * as fs from 'fs';
import * as path from 'path';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const ROOT = path.resolve(
    'G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\node_modules\\@ston-fi\\sdk'
);

const TARGETS = [
    '0x178d4519',
    '178d4519',
    '395134233',
    '0x50c6a654',
    '50c6a654',
    '1355195988',
    'MINT',
    'mint',
    'transfer',
    'LP_JETTON',
    'JETTON'
];

function walk(dir: string): string[] {
    const out: string[] = [];

    if (!fs.existsSync(dir)) return out;

    for (const entry of fs.readdirSync(dir, { withFileTypes: true })) {
        const full = path.join(dir, entry.name);

        if (entry.isDirectory()) {
            out.push(...walk(full));
        } else {
            out.push(full);
        }
    }

    return out;
}

function isTextFile(file: string): boolean {
    const ext = path.extname(file).toLowerCase();

    return [
        '.js',
        '.cjs',
        '.mjs',
        '.ts',
        '.mts',
        '.d.ts',
        '.map',
        '.json'
    ].includes(ext);
}

function context(text: string, index: number): string {
    const start = Math.max(0, index - 220);
    const end = Math.min(text.length, index + 420);

    return text
        .slice(start, end)
        .replace(/\r?\n/g, ' ')
        .replace(/\s+/g, ' ');
}

async function main() {
    console.log('');
    console.log('================================================================');
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 GLOBAL OPCODE SEARCH');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - LOCAL node_modules ONLY');
    console.log('================================================================');
    console.log('');
    console.log('SDK root:');
    console.log(ROOT);
    console.log('');

    const files = walk(ROOT).filter(isTextFile);

    console.log(`Text files scanned: ${files.length}`);
    console.log('');

    let totalMatches = 0;

    for (const target of TARGETS) {
        console.log('');
        console.log('------------------------------------------------------------');
        console.log(`TARGET: ${target}`);
        console.log('------------------------------------------------------------');

        let targetMatches = 0;

        for (const file of files) {
            let text: string;

            try {
                text = fs.readFileSync(file, 'utf8');
            } catch {
                continue;
            }

            let pos = 0;

            while (true) {
                const index = text.indexOf(target, pos);

                if (index === -1) break;

                targetMatches++;
                totalMatches++;

                const relative = path.relative(ROOT, file);

                console.log('');
                console.log(`FILE: ${relative}`);
                console.log(`OFFSET: ${index}`);
                console.log(`CONTEXT: ${context(text, index)}`);

                pos = index + Math.max(target.length, 1);

                // جلوگیری از خروجی بسیار بزرگ
                if (targetMatches >= 50) {
                    console.log('');
                    console.log('... maximum 50 matches shown for this target ...');
                    break;
                }
            }
        }

        console.log('');
        console.log(`MATCHES FOR TARGET: ${targetMatches}`);
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('================================================================');
    console.log(`TOTAL MATCHES: ${totalMatches}`);
    console.log('================================================================');
    console.log('');
    console.log('No transaction was created or sent.');
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    await main();
}

import * as fs from 'fs';
import * as path from 'path';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const ROOT =
    'G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\node_modules\\@tact-lang\\opcode\\dist';

function scanFile(file: string) {
    console.log();
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(`FILE: ${file}`);
    console.log('============================================================');

    if (!fs.existsSync(file)) {
        console.log('[MISSING]');
        return;
    }

    const text = fs.readFileSync(file, 'utf8');
    const lines = text.split(/\r?\n/);

    const patterns = [
        'decodeInstruction',
        'Bad operand',
        'PUSHCONT',
        'operand.type',
        'operand',
        'parseInstructions',
    ];

    for (const pattern of patterns) {
        console.log();
        console.log(`PATTERN: ${pattern}`);

        let matches = 0;

        for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
            if (lines[i].toLowerCase().includes(pattern.toLowerCase())) {
                matches++;

                const start = Math.max(0, i - 8);
                const end = Math.min(lines.length, i + 20);

                console.log();
                console.log(`--- lines ${start + 1}-${end} ---`);

                for (let j = start; j < end; j++) {
                    console.log(
                        `${String(j + 1).padStart(4, ' ')} | ${lines[j]}`
                    );
                }
            }
        }

        console.log(`Matches: ${matches}`);
    }
}

export async function run(_provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('========================================================================');
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 OPCODE PARSER INSPECTOR');
    console.log(' LOCAL NODE_MODULES ONLY');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION');
    console.log('========================================================================');

    console.log();
    console.log(`Package root: ${ROOT}`);

    const files = [
        path.join(ROOT, 'decompiler', 'operand-loader.js'),
        path.join(ROOT, 'decompiler', 'disasm.js'),
        path.join(ROOT, 'decompiler', 'opcode-loader.js'),
        path.join(ROOT, 'decompiler', 'instruction-loader.js'),
        path.join(ROOT, 'decompiler', 'decoder.js'),
        path.join(ROOT, 'decompiler', 'parser.js'),
        path.join(ROOT, 'decompiler', 'helpers.js'),
        path.join(ROOT, 'opcodes.js'),
    ];

    for (const file of files) {
        scanFile(file);
    }

    console.log();
    console.log('========================================================================');
    console.log(' INSPECTION COMPLETE');
    console.log('========================================================================');
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\findOwnerOf126b4V1.ts
