import * as fs from "fs";

const wasmPath = process.argv[2];

if (!wasmPath) {
    console.error("Usage:");
    console.error("  npx.cmd tsx scripts/findOwnerOf126b4V1.ts <wasm>");
    process.exit(1);
}

const buf = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const TARGET = 0x126b4;

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[pos++];

        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: pos };
        }

        shift += 7;

        if (shift > 35) {
            throw new Error("Invalid ULEB128");
        }
    }
}

function sectionName(id: number): string {
    switch (id) {
        case 0: return "custom";
        case 1: return "type";
        case 2: return "import";
        case 3: return "function";
        case 4: return "table";
        case 5: return "memory";
        case 6: return "global";
        case 7: return "export";
        case 8: return "start";
        case 9: return "element";
        case 10: return "code";
        case 11: return "data";
        default: return `section-${id}`;
    }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FIND OWNER OF 0x126B4");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();
console.log(`WASM size : ${buf.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log(`TARGET    : 0x${TARGET.toString(16)}`);
console.log();

let pos = 8;

let functionTypes: number[] = [];
let importFunctionCount = 0;

let codeSectionStart = -1;
let codeSectionEnd = -1;
let codeCount = 0;
let codeEntries: {
    definedIndex: number;
    realIndex: number;
    bodyStart: number;
    codeStart: number;
    bodyEnd: number;
    bodySize: number;
}[] = [];

while (pos < buf.length) {
    const sectionStart = pos;

    const id = buf[pos++];

    const sizeInfo = readULEB(pos);
    const sectionSize = sizeInfo.value;
    pos = sizeInfo.next;

    const contentStart = pos;
    const contentEnd = contentStart + sectionSize;

    console.log(
        `section ${id.toString().padStart(2)} ` +
        `${sectionName(id).padEnd(8)} ` +
        `0x${sectionStart.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} .. ` +
        `0x${(contentEnd - 1).toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
    );

    if (id === 2) {
        const countInfo = readULEB(pos);
        let p = countInfo.next;

        for (let i = 0; i < countInfo.value; i++) {
            const moduleLen = readULEB(p);
            p = moduleLen.next + moduleLen.value;

            const nameLen = readULEB(p);
            p = nameLen.next + nameLen.value;

            const kind = buf[p++];

            if (kind === 0) {
                // function import
                importFunctionCount++;
                const typeInfo = readULEB(p);
                p = typeInfo.next;
            } else if (kind === 1) {
                // table
                const elemType = buf[p++];
                const flags = readULEB(p);
                p = flags.next;

                const min = readULEB(p);
                p = min.next;

                if (flags.value & 1) {
                    const max = readULEB(p);
                    p = max.next;
                }
            } else if (kind === 2) {
                // memory
                const flags = readULEB(p);
                p = flags.next;

                const min = readULEB(p);
                p = min.next;

                if (flags.value & 1) {
                    const max = readULEB(p);
                    p = max.next;
                }
            } else if (kind === 3) {
                // global
                p += 2;
            } else {
                throw new Error(`Unknown import kind ${kind}`);
            }
        }

        console.log(`  imported functions : ${importFunctionCount}`);
    }

    if (id === 3) {
        const countInfo = readULEB(pos);
        let p = countInfo.next;

        functionTypes = [];

        for (let i = 0; i < countInfo.value; i++) {
            const t = readULEB(p);
            functionTypes.push(t.value);
            p = t.next;
        }

        console.log(`  defined functions  : ${functionTypes.length}`);
    }

    if (id === 10) {
        codeSectionStart = contentStart;
        codeSectionEnd = contentEnd;

        const countInfo = readULEB(pos);
        let p = countInfo.next;

        codeCount = countInfo.value;

        console.log(`  code functions     : ${codeCount}`);

        if (functionTypes.length !== codeCount) {
            console.log();
            console.log("WARNING:");
            console.log(
                `  function section = ${functionTypes.length}, ` +
                `code section = ${codeCount}`
            );
        }

        for (let definedIndex = 0; definedIndex < codeCount; definedIndex++) {
            const bodyStart = p;

            const sizeInfo = readULEB(p);
            const bodySize = sizeInfo.value;
            p = sizeInfo.next;

            const codeStart = p;
            const bodyEnd = codeStart + bodySize;

            const realIndex = importFunctionCount + definedIndex;

            codeEntries.push({
                definedIndex,
                realIndex,
                bodyStart,
                codeStart,
                bodyEnd,
                bodySize
            });

            if (TARGET >= codeStart && TARGET < bodyEnd) {
                console.log();
                console.log("============================================================");
                console.log(" TARGET OWNER FOUND");
                console.log("============================================================");
                console.log();
                console.log(`defined index : ${definedIndex}`);
                console.log(`real index    : ${realIndex}`);
                console.log(`type index    : ${functionTypes[definedIndex] ?? "?"}`);
                console.log(`body start    : 0x${bodyStart.toString(16)}`);
                console.log(`code start    : 0x${codeStart.toString(16)}`);
                console.log(`body end      : 0x${bodyEnd.toString(16)}`);
                console.log(`body size     : ${bodySize}`);
                console.log();

                const from = Math.max(codeStart, TARGET - 96);
                const to = Math.min(bodyEnd, TARGET + 96);

                console.log("RAW WINDOW");
                console.log(
                    `0x${from.toString(16)} .. 0x${to.toString(16)}`
                );
                console.log();

                for (let a = from; a < to; a += 16) {
                    const end = Math.min(to, a + 16);

                    const bytes: string[] = [];

                    for (let x = a; x < end; x++) {
                        bytes.push(
                            buf[x].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")
                        );
                    }

                    console.log(
                        `0x${a.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ` +
                        bytes.join(" ")
                    );
                }
            }

            p = bodyEnd;
        }
    }

    pos = contentEnd;
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" RESULT");
console.log("============================================================");

const owner = codeEntries.find(
    x => TARGET >= x.codeStart && TARGET < x.bodyEnd
);

if (!owner) {
    console.log("TARGET OWNER NOT FOUND");
    process.exit(2);
}

console.log(`real function index : ${owner.realIndex}`);
console.log(`defined index       : ${owner.definedIndex}`);
console.log(`type index          : ${functionTypes[owner.definedIndex]}`);
console.log(`code start          : 0x${owner.codeStart.toString(16)}`);
console.log(`body end            : 0x${owner.bodyEnd.toString(16)}`);
console.log(`body size           : ${owner.bodySize}`);

import { StonApiClient } from '@ston-fi/api';
import { dexFactory } from '@ston-fi/sdk';

export async function run() {
    console.log('===== FIND POOL-CREATION ROUTERS =====');

    const api = new StonApiClient({
        baseUrl: 'https://api.ston.fi',
    });

    try {
        const routers = await api.getRouters({
            dexV2: true,
        });

        const candidates = routers.filter((r: any) =>
            r.majorVersion === 2 &&
            r.minorVersion === 2 &&
            r.routerType === 'ConstantProduct' &&
            r.poolCreationEnabled === true
        );

        console.log('TOTAL ROUTERS:', routers.length);
        console.log('CANDIDATES:', candidates.length);

        for (const r of candidates) {
            console.log('\n===== CANDIDATE =====');

            console.log('Address:', r.address);
            console.log('Version:', `${r.majorVersion}.${r.minorVersion}`);
            console.log('Type:', r.routerType);
            console.log('Pool creation:', r.poolCreationEnabled);
            console.log('pTON version:', r.ptonVersion);
            console.log('pTON master:', r.ptonMasterAddress);
            console.log('pTON wallet:', r.ptonWalletAddress);

            try {
                const dex = dexFactory(r);

                console.log('SDK Router:', dex.Router.name);
                console.log('SDK Pool:', dex.Pool.name);
                console.log('SDK pTON:', dex.pTON.name);
            } catch (e) {
                console.log(
                    'dexFactory ERROR:',
                    e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e)
                );
            }
        }

    } catch (e) {
        console.log(
            'API ERROR:',
            e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e)
        );
    }
}

import { Address, Cell } from '@ton/core';
import { TonClient } from '@ton/ton';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    const pool = Address.parse(
        'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf'
    );

    const client = new TonClient({
        endpoint: 'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v2/jsonRPC',
    });

    console.log('===== RECENT V2.2 POOL TRANSACTIONS =====');
    console.log('Pool:', pool.toString());
    console.log('');

    try {
        const transactions = await client.getTransactions(pool, {
            limit: 20,
        });

        console.log('Transactions found:', transactions.length);
        console.log('');

        for (let i = 0; i < transactions.length; i++) {
            const tx = transactions[i];

            console.log('----------------------------------------');
            console.log(`Transaction #${i + 1}`);
            console.log('LT        :', tx.lt.toString());
            console.log('Hash      :', tx.hash().toString('hex'));
            console.log(
                'Timestamp :',
                tx.now ? new Date(tx.now * 1000).toISOString() : 'N/A'
            );

            if (tx.inMessage) {
                console.log(
                    'InMessage :',
                    tx.inMessage.info.type
                );

                if (tx.inMessage.body) {
                    try {
                        const slice = tx.inMessage.body.beginParse();

                        if (slice.remainingBits >= 32) {
                            const op = slice.loadUint(32);

                            console.log(
                                'In opcode  :',
                                '0x' + op.toString(16).padStart(8, '0')
                            );
                        }
                    } catch {
                        console.log('In opcode  : unable to decode');
                    }
                }
            } else {
                console.log('InMessage : none');
            }

            console.log(
                'OutMessages:',
                tx.outMessages.size
            );
        }

        console.log('');
        console.log('===== END =====');

    } catch (e) {
        console.log('FAILED TO READ TRANSACTIONS');
        console.log(e);
    }
}

FILE: scripts\findPreCall205ProducersV2.ts
====================================================================================================
import * as fs from "fs";

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const START = 0x96400;
const END   = 0x96a00;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" PRE-CALL205 PRODUCER SCAN v2");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`WASM size : ${wasm.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log(`Window    : 0x${START.toString(16)} .. 0x${END.toString(16)}`);
console.log("");
console.log("TARGETS:");
console.log("  G-528 = L7 - 64");
console.log("  G-272 = L7 + 192");
console.log("============================================================");

function u32(p: number): number {
  return (
    (wasm[p] |
      (wasm[p + 1] << 8) |
      (wasm[p + 2] << 16) |
      (wasm[p + 3] << 24)) >>> 0
  );
}

function readUleb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[pos++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
      return { value: value >>> 0, next: pos };

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function readSleb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let b = 0;

  while (true) {
    b = wasm[pos++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
      break;
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40))
    value |= (~0 << shift);

  return { value: value | 0, next: pos };
}

function hex(n: number): string {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(6, "0");
}

function localName(n: number): string {
  return `L${n}`;
}

/*
 * We decode enough opcodes to recognize:
 *
 * local.get
 * local.set
 * local.tee
 * i32.const
 * i32.add
 * i32.sub
 * i64.store
 *
 * Everything else is printed as raw opcode.
 */

let p = START;

const recent: string[] = [];

while (p < END) {
  const at = p;
  const op = wasm[p++];

  let text = `${hex(at)} `;

  switch (op) {

    case 0x20: { // local.get
      const r = readUleb(p);
      p = r.next;
      text += `local.get ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x21: { // local.set
      const r = readUleb(p);
      p = r.next;
      text += `local.set ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x22: { // local.tee
      const r = readUleb(p);
      p = r.next;
      text += `local.tee ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x41: { // i32.const
      const r = readSleb(p);
      p = r.next;
      text += `i32.const ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x42: { // i64.const
      const r = readSleb(p);
      p = r.next;
      text += `i64.const ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x6a:
      text += "i32.add";
      break;

    case 0x6b:
      text += "i32.sub";
      break;

    case 0x37: { // i64.store
      const align = readUleb(p);
      p = align.next;

      const off = readUleb(p);
      p = off.next;

      text += `i64.store align=${align.value} offset=${off.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x29: { // i64.load
      const align = readUleb(p);
      p = align.next;

      const off = readUleb(p);
      p = off.next;

      text += `i64.load align=${align.value} offset=${off.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x10: { // call
      const r = readUleb(p);
      p = r.next;
      text += `call ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    default:
      text += `op_0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;
      break;
  }

  recent.push(text);
  if (recent.length > 10)
    recent.shift();

  /*
   * Highlight every i64.store.
   * We inspect its preceding stack-building instructions manually.
   */
  if (op === 0x37) {
    console.log("");
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
    console.log(`STORE @ ${hex(at)}`);
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

    for (const x of recent)
      console.log(x);

    console.log("");
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" END OF PRE-CALL SCAN");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("No transaction / network access performed.");
console.log("");
console.log("NOTE:");
console.log("Blueprint may append 'Error: Function run is missing!'");
console.log("after the offline script output. That is unrelated.");

FILE: scripts\findRawCall106Func2088.mjs

import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const IMPORTED = 83;
const TARGET_FUNC = 2088;
const TARGET_CALL = 106;

const b = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function uleb(pos) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[pos++];

    value += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if (!(x & 0x80)) {
      return { value, next: pos };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function findCode() {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < b.length) {
    const id = b[p++];

    const s = uleb(p);
    p = s.next;

    if (id === 10) {
      return {
        start: p,
        end: p + s.value
      };
    }

    p += s.value;
  }

  throw new Error("CODE section not found");
}

function getFunction(index) {
  const code = findCode();

  let p = code.start;

  const count = uleb(p);
  p = count.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {

    const size = uleb(p);
    p = size.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + size.value;

    const funcIndex = IMPORTED + i;

    if (funcIndex === index) {
      return {
        index: funcIndex,
        start,
        end,
        size: size.value
      };
    }

    p = end;
  }

  throw new Error(`FUNC ${index} not found`);
}

const fn = getFunction(TARGET_FUNC);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 2088 RAW SEARCH FOR CALL FUNC106");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log(
  `BODY : 0x${fn.start.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} .. 0x${fn.end
    .toString(16)
    .padStart(8, "0")}`
);

console.log(`SIZE : ${fn.size}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let found = 0;

for (let p = fn.start; p < fn.end - 1; p++) {

  // WASM call opcode = 0x10
  // FUNC106 ULEB128 = 0x6A
  if (b[p] === 0x10 && b[p + 1] === 0x6a) {

    found++;

    console.log("");
    console.log(
      `FOUND CALL FUNC106 @ 0x${p.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
    );

    console.log("RAW WINDOW:");

    const from = Math.max(fn.start, p - 32);
    const to = Math.min(fn.end, p + 12);

    for (let q = from; q < to; q += 16) {

      const lineEnd = Math.min(q + 16, to);

      const bytes = [];

      for (let r = q; r < lineEnd; r++) {
        bytes.push(
          b[r].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")
        );
      }

      console.log(
        `0x${q.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ${bytes.join(" ")}`
      );
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log("IMMEDIATE BYTES BEFORE CALL:");

    const from2 = Math.max(fn.start, p - 16);

    for (let q = from2; q < p + 2; q++) {

      console.log(
        `0x${q.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  0x${b[q]
          .toString(16)
          .padStart(2, "0")}`
      );
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log("KNOWN DECODING OF THE RELEVANT RAW SEQUENCE:");
    console.log("");
    console.log("  20 03       local.get 3");
    console.log("  41 10       i32.const 16");
    console.log("  10 6a       call FUNC106");
    console.log("");
    console.log("Therefore:");
    console.log("  FUNC106.arg0 = local3");
    console.log("  FUNC106.arg1 = 16");
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log(`TOTAL RAW CALL FUNC106 FOUND : ${found}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

if (found === 0) {
  console.log(
    "WARNING: RAW pattern 10 6a was not found inside FUNC2088."
  );
}

console.log("");

console.log("  FUNC2088.local3");
console.log("       ↓");
console.log("  FUNC106(local3, 16)");
console.log("       ↓");
console.log("  FUNC2431(local3, 16)");
console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("============================================================");

FILE: scripts\findRawCallOwners.mjs

import fs from "node:fs";

const file = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const wasm = fs.readFileSync(file);

function readU32(pos) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const byte = wasm[pos++];
        value |= (byte & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((byte & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value: value >>> 0, next: pos };
        }

        shift += 7;
        if (shift > 35) throw new Error("Invalid ULEB128");
    }
}

function findCodeSection() {
    let pos = 8;

    while (pos < wasm.length) {
        const id = wasm[pos++];
        const sizeInfo = readU32(pos);
        const sectionStart = sizeInfo.next;
        const sectionEnd = sectionStart + sizeInfo.value;

        if (id === 10) {
            return { start: sectionStart, end: sectionEnd };
        }

        pos = sectionEnd;
    }

    throw new Error("Code section not found");
}

const targets = [
    { name: "call_2530", bytes: [0x10, 0xe2, 0x13] },
    { name: "call_1746", bytes: [0x10, 0xd2, 0x0d] },
];

const code = findCodeSection();
let pos = code.start;
const countInfo = readU32(pos);
const bodyCount = countInfo.value;
pos = countInfo.next;

console.log("WASM RAW CALL OWNER SCAN");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("================================================");
console.log("Defined functions :", bodyCount);
console.log("Imported functions: 83");
console.log("Code section      : 0x" + code.start.toString(16));
console.log();

for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < bodyCount; ordinal++) {
    const sizeInfo = readU32(pos);
    const bodyStart = sizeInfo.next;
    const bodyEnd = bodyStart + sizeInfo.value;

    for (const target of targets) {
        for (
            let i = bodyStart;
            i <= bodyEnd - target.bytes.length;
            i++
        ) {
            let match = true;

            for (let j = 0; j < target.bytes.length; j++) {
                if (wasm[i + j] !== target.bytes[j]) {
                    match = false;
                    break;
                }
            }

            if (match) {
                console.log(target.name);
                console.log("  Global function :", 83 + ordinal);
                console.log("  Code ordinal    :", ordinal);
                console.log(
                    "  Match offset    : 0x" +
                    i.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")
                );
                console.log(
                    "  Body range      : 0x" +
                    bodyStart.toString(16).padStart(8, "0") +
                    " .. 0x" +
                    (bodyEnd - 1).toString(16).padStart(8, "0")
                );
                console.log();
            }
        }
    }

    pos = bodyEnd;
}

console.log("================================================");
console.log("DONE");

import fs from 'node:fs';

const wasmPath =
    'G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm';

const bytes = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);
const pattern = [0x10, 0x72, 0x0f];

console.log('ROOSTER WASM - RAW FUNC 2034 REFERENCE SCAN');
console.log('READ ONLY - OFFLINE - NO WASM EXECUTION');
console.log(`WASM SIZE: ${bytes.length}`);

    if (
        bytes[i] === pattern[0] &&
        bytes[i + 1] === pattern[1] &&
        bytes[i + 2] === pattern[2]
    ) {
        matches++;

        const from = Math.max(0, i - 12);
        const to = Math.min(bytes.length, i + 15);

        const context = [];

        for (let j = from; j < to; j++) {
            context.push(
                `${j === i ? '[' : ''}` +
                bytes[j].toString(16).padStart(2, '0') +
                `${j === i + 2 ? ']' : ''}`
            );
        }

        console.log('\n----------------------------------------');
        console.log(`RAW OFFSET: 0x${i.toString(16).padStart(8, '0')}`);
        console.log(`CONTEXT START: 0x${from.toString(16).padStart(8, '0')}`);
        console.log(context.join(' '));
    }
}

console.log('\n========================================');
console.log(`RAW MATCHES: ${matches}`);

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\findRealField4Writers.js

const fs = require("fs");

const FILE = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const IMPORTS = 83;

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(FILE);

function uleb(p) {
    let v = 0, s = 0;
    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[p++];
        v |= (b & 0x7f) << s;
        if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
        s += 7;
    }
    return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };
}

function sleb(p) {
    let v = 0, s = 0, b;
    while (true) {
        b = wasm[p++];
        v |= (b & 0x7f) << s;
        s += 7;
        if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
    }
    if (s < 32 && (b & 0x40))
        v |= (~0 << s);
    return { value: v | 0, next: p };
}

function section(type) {
    let p = 8;

    while (p < wasm.length) {
        const t = wasm[p++];

        const z = uleb(p);
        p = z.next;

        if (t === type)
            return { start: p, end: p + z.value };

        p += z.value;
    }

    return null;
}

function decodeBody(start, end) {
    let p = start;

    const lg = uleb(p);
    p = lg.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < lg.value; i++) {
        const n = uleb(p);
        p = n.next;
        p++;
    }

    const out = [];

    while (p < end) {
        const addr = p;
        const op = wasm[p++];

        let text = `opcode_0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;

        switch (op) {

            case 0x20: {
                const x = uleb(p);
                p = x.next;
                text = `local.get ${x.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x21: {
                const x = uleb(p);
                p = x.next;
                text = `local.set ${x.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x22: {
                const x = uleb(p);
                p = x.next;
                text = `local.tee ${x.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x41: {
                const x = sleb(p);
                p = x.next;
                text = `i32.const ${x.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x28: {
                const a = uleb(p);
                p = a.next;

                const o = uleb(p);
                p = o.next;

                text = `i32.load align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x29: {
                const a = uleb(p);
                p = a.next;

                const o = uleb(p);
                p = o.next;

                text = `i64.load align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x2d: {
                const a = uleb(p);
                p = a.next;

                const o = uleb(p);
                p = o.next;

                text = `i32.load8_u align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x36: {
                const a = uleb(p);
                p = a.next;

                const o = uleb(p);
                p = o.next;

                text = `i32.store align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x37: {
                const a = uleb(p);
                p = a.next;

                const o = uleb(p);
                p = o.next;

                text = `i64.store align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x10: {
                const x = uleb(p);
                p = x.next;
                text = `call ${x.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x11: {
                const t = uleb(p);
                p = t.next;

                const tab = uleb(p);
                p = tab.next;

                text = `call_indirect type=${t.value} table=${tab.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x1a:
                text = "drop";
                break;

            case 0x45:
                text = "i32.eqz";
                break;

            case 0x46:
                text = "i32.eq";
                break;

            case 0x47:
                text = "i32.ne";
                break;

            case 0x6a:
                text = "i32.add";
                break;

            case 0x6b:
                text = "i32.sub";
                break;

            case 0x71:
                text = "i32.and";
                break;

            case 0x72:
                text = "i32.or";
                break;

            case 0x73:
                text = "i32.xor";
                break;

            case 0x0b:
                text = "end";
                break;

            case 0x0c: {
                const x = uleb(p);
                p = x.next;
                text = `br ${x.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x0d: {
                const x = uleb(p);
                p = x.next;
                text = `br_if ${x.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x04:
                text = "if";
                break;

            case 0x05:
                text = "else";
                break;

            case 0x02:
            case 0x03:
                text = `${op === 2 ? "block" : "loop"} type=0x${wasm[p++].toString(16)}`;
                break;

            case 0x23: {
                const x = uleb(p);
                p = x.next;
                text = `global.get ${x.value}`;
                break;
            }

            case 0x24: {
                const x = uleb(p);
                p = x.next;
                text = `global.set ${x.value}`;
                break;
            }
        }

        out.push({ addr, text });
    }

    return out;
}

function getBodies() {
    const sec = section(10);
    if (!sec) throw new Error("CODE SECTION NOT FOUND");

    let p = sec.start;

    const n = uleb(p);
    p = n.next;

    const bodies = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
        const z = uleb(p);
        p = z.next;

        const start = p;
        const end = p + z.value;

        bodies.push({
            body: i,
            func: IMPORTS + i,
            start,
            end
        });

        p = end;
    }

    return bodies;
}

function hx(x) {
    return "0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

const bodies = getBodies();

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("REAL WRITERS OF FIELD +4");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("WASM SIZE :", wasm.length);
console.log("BODIES    :", bodies.length);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let total = 0;
let decrement = 0;
let real = 0;

for (const b of bodies) {

    const ins = decodeBody(b.start, b.end);

    for (let i = 0; i < ins.length; i++) {

        if (!ins[i].text.includes("i32.store") ||
            !ins[i].text.includes("offset=4"))
            continue;

        total++;

        /*
         * Look backwards for:
         *
         * i32.const 1
         * i32.sub
         *
         * immediately before store.
         */
        const prev1 = ins[i - 1]?.text || "";
        const prev2 = ins[i - 2]?.text || "";

        const isDecrement =
            prev1 === "i32.sub" &&
            prev2 === "i32.const 1";

        if (isDecrement) {
            decrement++;
            continue;
        }

        real++;

        console.log("");
        console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
        console.log(`REAL FIELD +4 WRITER #${real}`);
        console.log(`FUNC ${b.func} BODY ${hx(b.start)} .. ${hx(b.end)}`);
        console.log("STORE :", hx(ins[i].addr));
        console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

        const a = Math.max(0, i - 8);
        const z = Math.min(ins.length, i + 4);

        for (let j = a; j < z; j++) {
            const mark = j === i ? ">>" : "  ";
            console.log(
                `${mark} ${hx(ins[j].addr)} ${ins[j].text}`
            );
        }
    }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FINAL");
console.log("ALL +4 STORES :", total);
console.log("DECREMENT      :", decrement);
console.log("REAL WRITERS   :", real);
console.log("READ ONLY      : YES");
console.log("NETWORK        : NO");
console.log("TRANSACTIONS   : NO");
console.log("============================================================");

import fs from 'fs';

const WASM_PATH =
    'G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm';

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM_PATH);

const IMPORTS = 83;

const TARGETS = [
    { fn: 107, offset: 54073, size: 357 },
    { fn: 116, offset: 55544, size: 1181 },
    { fn: 122, offset: 61125, size: 127 },
];

function readU32(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[pos++];

        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: pos };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function readI32(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let b = 0;

    while (true) {
        b = wasm[pos++];

        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
                value |= (~0 << shift);
            }

            return { value, next: pos };
        }
    }
}

function skipBlockType(pos: number) {
    const b = wasm[pos];

    if (
        b === 0x40 ||
        b === 0x7f ||
        b === 0x7e ||
        b === 0x7d ||
        b === 0x7c ||
        b === 0x7b ||
        b === 0x70 ||
        b === 0x6f
    ) {
        return pos + 1;
    }

    return readI32(pos).next;
}

        case 0x02:
        case 0x03:
        case 0x04:
            return skipBlockType(pos);

        case 0x0c:
        case 0x0d:
        case 0x10:
        case 0x20:
        case 0x21:
        case 0x22:
        case 0x23:
        case 0x24:
        case 0x41:
        case 0x42:
            return readU32(pos).next;

        case 0x0e: {
            const n = readU32(pos);
            pos = n.next;

            for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
                pos = readU32(pos).next;
            }

            return readU32(pos).next;
        }

        case 0x11: {
            const type = readU32(pos);
            pos = type.next;

            const table = readU32(pos);
            pos = table.next;

            return pos;
        }

        case 0x28:
        case 0x29:
        case 0x2a:
        case 0x2b:
        case 0x2c:
        case 0x2d:
        case 0x2e:
        case 0x2f:
        case 0x30:
        case 0x31:
        case 0x32:
        case 0x33:
        case 0x34:
        case 0x35:
        case 0x36:
        case 0x37:
        case 0x38:
        case 0x39:
        case 0x3a:
        case 0x3b:
        case 0x3c:
        case 0x3d:
        case 0x3e:
        case 0x3f:
        case 0x40:
            pos = readU32(pos).next;
            pos = readU32(pos).next;
            return pos;

        default:
            return pos;
    }
}

function skipLocals(pos: number) {
    const groups = readU32(pos);
    pos = groups.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < groups.value; i++) {
        pos = readU32(pos).next;
        pos++;
    }

    return pos;
}

for (const target of TARGETS) {

    const start = target.offset;
    const end = start + target.size;

    let p = skipLocals(start);

    console.log(`------------------------------------------------------------`);
    console.log(`F#${target.fn}`);
    console.log(`body       : ${start}`);
    console.log(`instructions: ${p}`);
    console.log('');

    let count = 0;

    while (p < end) {

        const op = wasm[p];

        if (op === 0x11) {

            const type = readU32(p + 1);
            const table = readU32(type.next);

            count++;

            console.log(
                `[${count}] REAL CALL_INDIRECT`
            );

            console.log(
                `  absolute : ${p}`
            );

            console.log(
                `  relative : ${p - start}`
            );

            console.log(
                `  type     : ${type.value}`
            );

            console.log(
                `  table    : ${table.value}`
            );

            const from = Math.max(start, p - 32);
            const to = Math.min(end, p + 20);

            console.log('');
            console.log('  bytes/context:');

            for (let q = from; q < to; q++) {

                const mark = q === p ? ' <<< CALL_INDIRECT' : '';

                console.log(
                    `    ${q.toString().padStart(7)}  ${wasm[q]
                        .toString(16)
                        .padStart(2, '0')}${mark}`
                );
            }

            console.log('');
        }

        const next = skipInstruction(p);

        if (next <= p) {
            console.log(`Parser stopped at ${p}`);
            break;
        }

        p = next;
    }

    console.log(`REAL CALL_INDIRECT count: ${count}`);
    console.log('');
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\findRefcountCallSites.mjs
====================================================================================================

import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";

const WASM_PATH = path.resolve(
    process.cwd(),
    "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm"
);

const IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS = 83;
const TARGETS = [871, 1393, 2527];

function readULEB(buf, state) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (state.pos < buf.length) {
        const b = buf[state.pos++];
        value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) return value;

        shift += 7;
        if (shift > 49) throw new Error("Invalid ULEB128");
    }

    throw new Error("Unexpected EOF while reading ULEB128");
}

function encodeULEB(value) {
    const bytes = [];

    do {
        let b = value & 0x7f;
        value = Math.floor(value / 128);

        if (value !== 0) b |= 0x80;
        bytes.push(b);
    } while (value !== 0);

    return bytes;
}

function getSections(buf) {
    const state = { pos: 8 };
    const sections = new Map();

    if (
        buf[0] !== 0 ||
        buf[1] !== 0x61 ||
        buf[2] !== 0x73 ||
        buf[3] !== 0x6d
    ) {
        throw new Error("Not a WASM file");
    }

    while (state.pos < buf.length) {
        const id = buf[state.pos++];
        const size = readULEB(buf, state);
        const start = state.pos;
        const end = start + size;

        if (end > buf.length) {
            throw new Error(`Section ${id} exceeds file bounds`);
        }

        if (!sections.has(id)) {
            sections.set(id, { start, end });
        }

        state.pos = end;
    }

    return sections;
}

function getFunctionBodies(buf, section) {
    const state = { pos: section.start };
    const count = readULEB(buf, state);
    const bodies = [];

    for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < count; ordinal++) {
        const bodySize = readULEB(buf, state);
        const bodyStart = state.pos;
        const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bodySize;

        if (bodyEnd > section.end) {
            throw new Error(
                `Function body ${ordinal} exceeds code section`
            );
        }

        bodies.push({
            ordinal,
            globalFunction: IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS + ordinal,
            bodyStart,
            bodyEnd,
        });

        state.pos = bodyEnd;
    }

    return bodies;
}

function findPattern(buf, start, end, pattern) {
    const matches = [];

    for (let i = start; i <= end - pattern.length; i++) {
        let equal = true;

        for (let j = 0; j < pattern.length; j++) {
            if (buf[i + j] !== pattern[j]) {
                equal = false;
                break;
            }
        }

        if (equal) matches.push(i);
    }

    return matches;
}

function hex(value) {
    return `0x${value.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`;
}

function main() {
    console.log("REFCOUNT CALL-SITE SCAN");
    console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
    console.log("============================================================");

    const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM_PATH);
    const sections = getSections(buf);
    const codeSection = sections.get(10);

    if (!codeSection) {
        throw new Error("WASM code section (section 10) not found");
    }

    const bodies = getFunctionBodies(buf, codeSection);

    console.log(`FILE SIZE       = ${buf.length}`);
    console.log(`IMPORTED FUNCS  = ${IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS}`);
    console.log(`DEFINED FUNCS   = ${bodies.length}`);
    console.log(`CODE SECTION    = ${hex(codeSection.start)} .. ${hex(codeSection.end)}`);

    for (const target of TARGETS) {
        const ordinal = target - IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS;

        console.log("");
        console.log(`TARGET GLOBAL FUNCTION ${target}`);
        console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

        if (ordinal < 0 || ordinal >= bodies.length) {
            console.log("Target is outside the defined-function range.");
            continue;
        }

        const pattern = [0x10, ...encodeULEB(target)];
        console.log(
            `RAW CALL PATTERN = ${pattern.map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join(" ")}`
        );

        let total = 0;

        for (const body of bodies) {
            const matches = findPattern(
                buf,
                body.bodyStart,
                body.bodyEnd,
                pattern
            );

            for (const offset of matches) {
                console.log(
                    `OWNER GLOBAL ${body.globalFunction}` +
                    ` | ORDINAL ${body.ordinal}` +
                    ` | BODY ${hex(body.bodyStart)}..${hex(body.bodyEnd)}` +
                    ` | MATCH ${hex(offset)}`
                );

                total++;
            }
        }

        console.log(`RAW MATCH COUNT = ${total}`);
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log("SCAN COMPLETE");
    console.log("NOTE: Raw byte matches are candidates, not decoded instructions.");
}

try {
    main();
} catch (error) {
    console.error("SCAN FAILED");
    console.error(error);
    process.exitCode = 1;
}

import { StonApiClient } from '@ston-fi/api';

const ROOSTER_MASTER =
    'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx';

const PTON_MASTER =
    'EQBnGWMCf3-FZZq1W4IWcWiGAc3PHuZ0_H-7sad2oY00o83S';

export async function run() {
    console.log('===== FIND ROOSTER / pTON POOLS VIA STON API =====');

    console.log('ROOSTER:', ROOSTER_MASTER);
    console.log('pTON:', PTON_MASTER);

    const api = new StonApiClient({
        baseURL: 'https://api.ston.fi',
    });

    try {
        const pools = await api.getPoolsByAssetPair({
            asset0Address: ROOSTER_MASTER,
            asset1Address: PTON_MASTER,
        });

        console.log('');
        console.log('POOL COUNT:', pools.length);

        if (pools.length === 0) {
            console.log('');
            console.log('NO ROOSTER / pTON POOL FOUND.');
            return;
        }

        for (const pool of pools) {
            console.log('');
            console.log('===== POOL =====');
            console.log('Address:', pool.address);
            console.log('Router:', pool.routerAddress);
            console.log('Token0:', pool.token0Address);
            console.log('Token1:', pool.token1Address);
            console.log('Deprecated:', pool.deprecated);
            console.log('Volume 24h USD:', pool.volume24HUsd);
        }
    } catch (e) {
        console.log('');
        console.log(
            'API ERROR:',
            e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e),
        );
    }
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\findS1AddressArithmetic.mjs
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const file = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const b = fs.readFileSync(file);

const BASE = 0x195348;
const TARGET = 0x19534c;

function uleb(pos) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, p = pos;

  while (p < b.length) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;

    if (!(x & 0x80))
      return { value: v >>> 0, end: p };

    s += 7;
  }

  return null;
}

function sleb(pos) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, p = pos;

  while (p < b.length) {
    const x = b[p++];

    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;
    s += 7;

    if (!(x & 0x80)) {
      if (s < 32 && (x & 0x40))
        v -= 2 ** s;

      return { value: v | 0, end: p };
    }
  }

  return null;
}

function hex(x) {
  return "0x" + (x >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function dump(start, end) {
  for (let p = start; p < end; p++) {
    console.log(
      "  " + hex(p),
      b[p].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")
    );
  }
}

console.log("S1 ADDRESS ARITHMETIC SCAN");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("BASE   :", hex(BASE));
console.log("TARGET :", hex(TARGET));
console.log();

let baseHits = [];

//
// Find every literal 0x195348
//
for (let p = 0; p < b.length - 5; p++) {

  if (b[p] !== 0x41)
    continue;

  const r = sleb(p + 1);

  if (!r)
    continue;

  if ((r.value >>> 0) === BASE) {
    baseHits.push({
      pos: p,
      end: r.end
    });
  }
}

console.log("BASE CONST HITS :", baseHits.length);
console.log();

let candidates = [];

for (const h of baseHits) {

  let foundAdd4 = false;
  let foundTarget = false;
  let storePos = null;

  const end = Math.min(b.length, h.end + 96);

  for (let p = h.end; p < end; p++) {

    //
    // i32.const
    //
    if (b[p] === 0x41) {

      const r = sleb(p + 1);

      if (r) {
        const v = r.value | 0;

        if (v === 4)
          foundAdd4 = true;

        if ((v >>> 0) === TARGET)
          foundTarget = true;
      }
    }

    //
    // i32.add
    //
    if (b[p] === 0x6a && foundAdd4) {
      candidates.push({
        base: h.pos,
        add: p,
        store: null,
        type: "BASE + 4 + ADD"
      });
    }

    //
    // i32.store
    //
    if (b[p] === 0x36) {

      const a = uleb(p + 1);
      if (!a) continue;

      const o = uleb(a.end);
      if (!o) continue;

      if (foundAdd4) {
        storePos = p;

        candidates.push({
          base: h.pos,
          add: null,
          store: storePos,
          offset: o.value,
          type: "BASE + 4 -> STORE"
        });
      }
    }

    //
    // Direct literal TARGET
    //
    if (foundTarget && b[p] === 0x36) {

      const a = uleb(p + 1);
      if (!a) continue;

      const o = uleb(a.end);
      if (!o) continue;

      candidates.push({
        base: h.pos,
        add: null,
        store: p,
        offset: o.value,
        type: "TARGET LITERAL -> STORE"
      });
    }
  }
}

console.log("ARITHMETIC CANDIDATES :", candidates.length);
console.log();

for (const c of candidates) {

  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
  console.log("TYPE  :", c.type);
  console.log("BASE  :", hex(c.base));

  if (c.add !== null)
    console.log("ADD   :", hex(c.add));

  if (c.store !== null)
    console.log("STORE :", hex(c.store));

  if (c.offset !== undefined)
    console.log("OFFSET:", c.offset);

  const focus = c.store ?? c.add ?? c.base;

  console.log("BYTES:");

  const start = Math.max(0, focus - 40);
  const end = Math.min(b.length, focus + 40);

  dump(start, end);

  console.log();
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\findS1BulkDestination.mjs
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function u(p) {
  let v=0,s=0;
  while(true){
    const x=b[p++];
    v+=(x&127)*2**s;
    if(!(x&128)) return [v>>>0,p];
    s+=7;
  }
}

let p=8, code=0;

while(p<b.length){
  const id=b[p++];
  const [n,q]=u(p); p=q;
  if(id===10){ code=p; break; }
  p+=n;
}

p=code;
const [count,q0]=u(p); p=q0;

for(let i=0;i<count;i++){
  const [size,q]=u(p); p=q;

  const func=83+i, start=p, end=p+size;

  for(let x=start;x<end;x++){

    // memory.copy = fc 0a
    // memory.fill = fc 0b
    if(b[x]!==0xfc) continue;

    const [sub,qx]=u(x+1);

    if(sub!==0x0a && sub!==0x0b) continue;

    const from=Math.max(start,x-80);
    const to=Math.min(end,x+40);

    const hex=[...b.subarray(from,to)]
      .map(v=>v.toString(16).padStart(2,"0")).join(" ");

    if(hex.includes("cc a6 e5 00")){
      console.log(
        `FUNC ${func} BULK @ 0x${x.toString(16)} `+
        `SUB=0x${sub.toString(16)}`
      );
      console.log(hex);
      console.log("--------------------------------------------");
    }
  }

  p=end;
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\findS1StoreAlias.mjs
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function u(p) {
  let v=0,s=0;
  while(true){
    const x=b[p++];
    v+=(x&127)*2**s;
    if(!(x&128)) return [v>>>0,p];
    s+=7;
  }
}

let p=8, code=0;

while(p<b.length){
  const id=b[p++];
  const [n,q]=u(p); p=q;
  if(id===10){ code=p; break; }
  p+=n;
}

p=code;
const [count,q0]=u(p); p=q0;

for(let i=0;i<count;i++){
  const [size,q]=u(p); p=q;

  const func=83+i;
  const start=p, end=p+size;

  // Look for:
  // i32.const 0x19534c
  // local.set / local.tee
  // ...
  // local.get SAME
  // i32.store

  for(let x=start;x<end;x++){

    if(b[x]!==0x41) continue;

    const [v,qv]=u(x+1);
    if(v!==0x19534c) continue;

    let z=qv;

    // Find local.set / tee shortly after target constant
    let local=null;

    for(let k=0;k<16 && z<end;k++){
      if(b[z]===0x21 || b[z]===0x22){
        const [l,qz]=u(z+1);
        local=l;
        z=qz;
        break;
      }
      z++;
    }

    if(local===null) continue;

    // Search entire remainder of function for local.get + store
    for(let y=z;y<end;y++){

      if(b[y]!==0x20) continue;

      const [l,qy]=u(y+1);
      if(l!==local) continue;

      let w=qy;

      // allow small address arithmetic
      for(let k=0;k<8 && w<end;k++){

        if(b[w]===0x36 || b[w]===0x37 || b[w]===0x38){

          const [,qa]=u(w+1);
          const [off]=u(qa);

          console.log(
            `FUNC ${func} S1 ALIAS L${local} `+
            `CONST @ 0x${x.toString(16)} `+
            `STORE @ 0x${y.toString(16)} `+
            `OFFSET=${off}`
          );

          console.log(
            [...b.subarray(
              Math.max(start,x-16),
              Math.min(end,w+12)
            )]
            .map(v=>v.toString(16).padStart(2,"0")).join(" ")
          );

          console.log("--------------------------------------------");
          break;
        }

        // stop at control flow
        if(b[w]===0x0b || b[w]===0x0c || b[w]===0x0d)
          break;

        w++;
      }
    }
  }

  p=end;
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\findS1StoreDataflow.mjs
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const IMPORTS = 83;
const TARGET = 0x19534c;
const BASE   = 0x195348;

function uleb(pos) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (pos < b.length) {
    const x = b[pos++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) return { value: v >>> 0, end: pos };
    s += 7;
  }
  throw new Error("ULEB overflow");
}

function sleb(pos) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, x;
  while (pos < b.length) {
    x = b[pos++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;
    s += 7;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) {
      if (s < 32 && (x & 0x40))
        v -= 2 ** s;
      return { value: v | 0, end: pos };
    }
  }
  throw new Error("SLEB overflow");
}

function hex(x) {
  return "0x" + (x >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function findBodies() {
  let p = 8;
  let bodies = [];

  while (p < b.length) {
    const id = b[p++];

    const r = uleb(p);
    p = r.end;

    const end = p + r.value;

    if (id === 10) {
      const c = uleb(p);
      let q = c.end;

      for (let i = 0; i < c.value; i++) {
        const z = uleb(q);
        q = z.end;

        bodies.push({
          start: q,
          end: q + z.value
        });

        q += z.value;
      }
    }

    p = end;
  }

  return bodies;
}

const bodies = findBodies();

const names = {
  0x1a:"DROP",
  0x20:"LOCAL.GET",
  0x21:"LOCAL.SET",
  0x22:"LOCAL.TEE",
  0x23:"GLOBAL.GET",
  0x24:"GLOBAL.SET",

  0x28:"I32.LOAD",
  0x2d:"I32.LOAD8_U",
  0x36:"I32.STORE",
  0x3a:"I32.STORE8",

  0x41:"I32.CONST",
  0x45:"I32.EQZ",
  0x46:"I32.EQ",
  0x47:"I32.NE",

  0x6a:"I32.ADD",
  0x6b:"I32.SUB",
  0x6c:"I32.MUL",

  0x71:"I32.AND",
  0x72:"I32.OR",
  0x73:"I32.XOR",
  0x74:"I32.SHL",
  0x75:"I32.SHR_S",
  0x76:"I32.SHR_U"
};

const arithmetic = new Set([
  0x6a,0x6b,0x6c,0x71,0x72,0x73,0x74,0x75,0x76
]);

const results = [];

for (let fi = 0; fi < bodies.length; fi++) {

  const func = IMPORTS + fi;
  const body = bodies[fi];

  let p = body.start;

  const groups = uleb(p);
  p = groups.end;

  let localCount = 0;

  for (let i = 0; i < groups.value; i++) {
    const n = uleb(p);
    p = n.end;
    p++;
    localCount += n.value;
  }

  const stack = [];
  const locals = new Map();

  function push(v) {
    stack.push(v);
  }

  function pop() {
    return stack.length ? stack.pop() : { kind:"unknown" };
  }

  function setLocal(id, value) {
    locals.set(id, value);
  }

  function getLocal(id) {
    return locals.get(id) || { kind:"local", id };
  }

  function valueConst(v, at) {
    return {
      kind:"const",
      value:v | 0,
      at
    };
  }

  function valueAdd(a, b, at) {
    if (a.kind === "const" && b.kind === "const")
      return valueConst((a.value + b.value) | 0, at);

    if (a.kind === "const" && b.kind === "target")
      return { kind:"target", offset:a.offset + b.offset, at };

    if (a.kind === "target" && b.kind === "const")
      return { kind:"target", offset:a.offset + b.value, at };

    if (a.kind === "const" && b.kind === "base")
      return { kind:"base", offset:a.offset + b.value, at };

    if (a.kind === "base" && b.kind === "const")
      return { kind:"base", offset:a.offset + b.value, at };

    return { kind:"expr", op:"add", a, b, at };
  }

  while (p < body.end) {

    const at = p;
    const op = b[p++];

    if (op === 0x41) {
      const r = sleb(p);
      p = r.end;

      if ((r.value >>> 0) === TARGET)
        push({ kind:"target", offset:0, at });
      else if ((r.value >>> 0) === BASE)
        push({ kind:"base", offset:0, at });
      else
        push(valueConst(r.value, at));

    }

    else if (op === 0x20) {
      const r = uleb(p);
      p = r.end;
      push(getLocal(r.value));
    }

    else if (op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) {
      const r = uleb(p);
      p = r.end;

      const v = pop();

      setLocal(r.value, v);

      if (op === 0x22)
        push(v);
    }

    else if (op === 0x23 || op === 0x24) {
      const r = uleb(p);
      p = r.end;

      if (op === 0x23)
        push({ kind:"global", id:r.value });
      else
        pop();
    }

    else if (op === 0x6a) {
      const b2 = pop();
      const a = pop();
      push(valueAdd(a, b2, at));
    }

    else if (arithmetic.has(op)) {

      const b2 = pop();
      const a = pop();

      if (
        (a.kind === "target" || a.kind === "base") ||
        (b2.kind === "target" || b2.kind === "base")
      ) {
        push({
          kind:"expr",
          op:names[op] || `0x${op.toString(16)}`,
          a,
          b:b2,
          at
        });
      } else {
        push({ kind:"unknown" });
      }
    }

    else if (
      op === 0x28 ||
      op === 0x2d
    ) {
      const align = uleb(p);
      p = align.end;

      const off = uleb(p);
      p = off.end;

      const addr = pop();

      if (
        addr.kind === "target" ||
        addr.kind === "base" ||
        addr.kind === "expr"
      ) {
        push({
          kind:"load",
          addr,
          offset:off.value,
          at
        });
      } else {
        push({ kind:"unknown" });
      }
    }

    else if (
      op === 0x36 ||
      op === 0x3a
    ) {
      const align = uleb(p);
      p = align.end;

      const off = uleb(p);
      p = off.end;

      const value = pop();
      const addr = pop();

      let hit = false;
      let type = "";

      if (addr.kind === "target" && addr.offset + off.value === 0) {
        hit = true;
        type = "DIRECT S1 STORE";
      }

      if (addr.kind === "base" && addr.offset + off.value === 4) {
        hit = true;
        type = "BASE+4 = S1 STORE";
      }

      if (addr.kind === "expr") {
        type = "DERIVED ADDRESS";
      }

      if (hit || type === "DERIVED ADDRESS") {
        results.push({
          func,
          at,
          op,
          type,
          addr,
          value
        });
      }
    }

    else if (op === 0x1a) {
      pop();
    }

    else if (op === 0x10) {
      const r = uleb(p);
      p = r.end;

      // Unknown stack effect: preserve analysis conservatively.
      push({ kind:"call_result", func:r.value });
    }

    else if (op === 0x11) {
      const r = uleb(p);
      p = r.end;

      p++;

      push({ kind:"indirect_result", type:r.value });
    }

    else if (
      op === 0x45 ||
      op === 0x46 ||
      op === 0x47
    ) {
      pop();
      if (op !== 0x45)
        pop();
      push({ kind:"unknown" });
    }

    else if (
      op === 0x0b ||
      op === 0x02 ||
      op === 0x03 ||
      op === 0x04 ||
      op === 0x05 ||
      op === 0x0c ||
      op === 0x0d ||
      op === 0x0f
    ) {
      // Control flow is intentionally not followed here.
      // This scan is candidate generation only.
    }

    else {
      // Skip immediate operands for common instructions.
      if (op === 0xfc) {
        const r = uleb(p);
        p = r.end;
      }
    }
  }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("S1 STORE DATAFLOW SCAN");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TARGET :", hex(TARGET));
console.log("BASE   :", hex(BASE));
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

if (!results.length) {
  console.log("NO S1 DATAFLOW STORE CANDIDATES");
} else {

  for (const r of results) {

    console.log("");
    console.log(`FUNC ${r.func}`);
    console.log(`STORE @ ${hex(r.at)}`);
    console.log(`TYPE : ${r.type}`);

    console.log(
      "ADDR :",
      JSON.stringify(r.addr)
    );

    console.log(
      "VALUE:",
      JSON.stringify(r.value)
    );
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\findS1StorePatterns.mjs
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGET = 0x19534c;
const BASE   = 0x195348;

function uleb(pos) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (pos < b.length) {
    const x = b[pos++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;
    if (!(x & 0x80))
      return { value: v >>> 0, end: pos };
    s += 7;
  }
  return null;
}

function hex(x) {
  return "0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function readSLEB(pos) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (pos < b.length) {
    const x = b[pos++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;
    s += 7;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) {
      if (s < 32 && (x & 0x40))
        v |= (-1 << s);
      return { value: v | 0, end: pos };
    }
  }
  return null;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("S1 EXACT STORE PATTERN SEARCH");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TARGET : 0x19534C");
console.log("BASE   : 0x195348");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let exactConst = [];
let baseConst = [];
let stores = [];

for (let p = 0; p < b.length; p++) {

  // i32.const
  if (b[p] === 0x41) {
    const r = readSLEB(p + 1);
    if (!r) continue;

    const v = r.value >>> 0;

    if (v === TARGET) {
      exactConst.push({
        pos: p,
        end: r.end
      });
    }

    if (v === BASE) {
      baseConst.push({
        pos: p,
        end: r.end
      });
    }
  }

  // i32.store / i32.store8
  if (b[p] === 0x36 || b[p] === 0x3a) {
    const r = uleb(p + 1);
    if (!r) continue;

    const r2 = uleb(r.end);
    if (!r2) continue;

    stores.push({
      pos: p,
      op: b[p],
      end: r2.end,
      align: r.value,
      offset: r2.value
    });
  }
}

console.log("EXACT TARGET CONST HITS :", exactConst.length);
console.log("BASE CONST HITS         :", baseConst.length);
console.log("STORE HITS              :", stores.length);

  }

import fs from "fs";

const file = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const b = fs.readFileSync(file);

const TARGET = 0x19534c;

function u32(p) {
  return (b[p] | (b[p+1] << 8) | (b[p+2] << 16) | (b[p+3] << 24)) >>> 0;
}

function readULEB(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, q = p;
  while (true) {
    const x = b[q++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) return { value: v >>> 0, next: q };
    s += 7;
  }
}

function readSLEB(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, q = p, x;
  while (true) {
    x = b[q++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;
    s += 7;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) break;
  }
  if (s < 32 && (x & 0x40)) v |= (~0 << s);
  return { value: v | 0, next: q };
}

function getSections() {
  let p = 8;
  const out = [];

  while (p < b.length) {
    const id = b[p++];
    const z = readULEB(p);
    p = z.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + z.value;

    out.push({ id, start, end });
    p = end;
  }

  return out;
}

const sections = getSections();

function findCodeSection() {
  return sections.find(x => x.id === 10);
}

const codeSec = findCodeSection();

if (!codeSec) {
  throw new Error("CODE SECTION NOT FOUND");
}

let p = codeSec.start;
const fc = readULEB(p);
p = fc.next;

const bodies = [];

for (let i = 0; i < fc.value; i++) {
  const sz = readULEB(p);
  p = sz.next;

  const bodyStart = p;
  const bodyEnd = p + sz.value;

  bodies.push({
    func: i + 83,
    start: bodyStart,
    end: bodyEnd
  });

  p = bodyEnd;
}

function matchTargetConst(pos) {
  if (b[pos] !== 0x41) return false;
  const x = readSLEB(pos + 1);
  return (x.value >>> 0) === TARGET;
}

function decodeMemStore(pos) {
  // i32.store = 0x36
  // followed by alignment + offset ULEB
  if (b[pos] !== 0x36) return null;

  const a = readULEB(pos + 1);
  const o = readULEB(a.next);

  return {
    end: o.next,
    align: a.value,
    offset: o.value
  };
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("S1 WRITER DATAFLOW SCAN");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TARGET : 0x${TARGET.toString(16)}`);
console.log("");

let direct = 0;
let localCandidates = 0;

for (const fn of bodies) {
  const hits = [];

  for (let q = fn.start; q < fn.end; q++) {

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // DIRECT:
    // i32.const 0x19534c
    // ...
    // i32.store
    // --------------------------------------------------------
    if (matchTargetConst(q)) {
      hits.push({
        pos: q,
        kind: "TARGET_CONST"
      });
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // Look for i32.store
    // --------------------------------------------------------
    if (b[q] === 0x36) {
      const st = decodeMemStore(q);

      if (st) {
        // Immediate preceding instruction may be i32.const target.
        // Scan backward up to 20 bytes for exact target constant.
        let found = false;

        for (let back = Math.max(fn.start, q - 20); back < q; back++) {
          if (matchTargetConst(back)) {
            found = true;

            hits.push({
              pos: q,
              kind: "STORE_NEAR_TARGET_CONST",
              constPos: back,
              offset: st.offset
            });

            direct++;
            break;
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }

  if (hits.length) {
    console.log("");
    console.log("############################################################");
    console.log(`FUNC ${fn.func}`);
    console.log(
      `BODY : 0x${fn.start.toString(16)} .. 0x${fn.end.toString(16)}`
    );
    console.log("############################################################");

    for (const h of hits) {
      if (h.kind === "TARGET_CONST") {
        console.log(
          `TARGET CONST @ 0x${h.pos.toString(16)}`
        );

        const a = Math.max(fn.start, h.pos - 12);
        const z = Math.min(fn.end, h.pos + 40);

        for (let x = a; x < z; x++) {
          console.log(
            `  0x${x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ${b[x].toString(16).padStart(2,"0")}`
          );
        }
      } else {
        console.log(
          `STORE @ 0x${h.pos.toString(16)} ` +
          `TARGET CONST @ 0x${h.constPos.toString(16)} ` +
          `OFFSET=${h.offset}`
        );
      }
    }
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`DIRECT / NEAR WRITERS : ${direct}`);
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGET_ADDR = 0x195350;
const TARGET_VALUE = 4789;

console.log("S2 STORE FAST SCAN");

// Exact little-endian byte patterns
const addr = Buffer.from([
  0x50, 0x53, 0x19, 0x00
]);

const value = Buffer.from([
  0xb5, 0x25
]);

let addrHits = 0;
let valueHits = 0;

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Exact raw address occurrences
// ------------------------------------------------------------
for (let i = 0; i <= b.length - 4; i++) {

  if (
    b[i] === addr[0] &&
    b[i + 1] === addr[1] &&
    b[i + 2] === addr[2] &&
    b[i + 3] === addr[3]
  ) {
    addrHits++;

    console.log("");
    console.log(`ADDRESS RAW HIT @ 0x${i.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")}`);

    const from = Math.max(0, i - 16);
    const to = Math.min(b.length, i + 32);

    for (let x = from; x < to; x += 16) {
      console.log(
        `0x${x.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")}  ` +
        [...b.slice(x, Math.min(x + 16, to))]
          .map(v => v.toString(16).padStart(2,"0"))
          .join(" ")
      );
    }
  }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Exact raw value occurrences
// ------------------------------------------------------------
for (let i = 0; i <= b.length - 2; i++) {

  if (b[i] === value[0] && b[i + 1] === value[1]) {
    valueHits++;

    if (valueHits <= 30) {
      console.log(
        `VALUE 4789 HIT #${valueHits} @ 0x${i.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")}`
      );
    }
  }
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\findS2WritersRaw.mjs
