import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGET = 0x195350;

function uleb(pos) {
  let v = 0, shift = 0;
  while (true) {
    const x = b[pos++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) return { value: v, next: pos };
    shift += 7;
  }
}

function sleb(pos) {
  let v = 0, shift = 0, x;
  while (true) {
    x = b[pos++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;
    shift += 7;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) break;
  }
  if (shift < 32 && (x & 0x40)) v -= 2 ** shift;
  return { value: v | 0, next: pos };
}

function hex32(v) {
  return "0x" + (v >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Find CODE SECTION
// ------------------------------------------------------------
let p = 8;
let codeStart = 0;
let codeEnd = 0;

while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p];
  const s = uleb(p + 1);
  const payload = s.next;
  const end = payload + s.value;

  if (id === 10) {
    codeStart = payload;
    codeEnd = end;
    break;
  }

  p = end;
}

let q = codeStart;
const count = uleb(q);
q = count.next;

console.log("S2 WRITER SEARCH");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TARGET ADDRESS : ${hex32(TARGET)}`);
console.log(`TARGET VALUE   : ${TARGET}`);
console.log(`CODE SECTION   : ${hex32(codeStart)} .. ${hex32(codeEnd)}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let hits = 0;

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Scan every defined function
// ------------------------------------------------------------
for (let func = 0; func < count.value; func++) {

  const sz = uleb(q);
  const start = sz.next;
  const end = start + sz.value;

  for (let pos = start; pos < end;) {

    const opPos = pos;
    const op = b[pos++];

    // i32.const
    if (op === 0x41) {
      const r = sleb(pos);

      if ((r.value >>> 0) === TARGET) {

        hits++;

        console.log(`\n############################################################`);
        console.log(`FUNC ${func}`);
        console.log(`CONST @ ${hex32(opPos)}`);
        console.log(`VALUE  : ${r.value}`);
        console.log(`HEX    : ${hex32(r.value)}`);

        // Show nearby raw bytes
        const a = Math.max(start, opPos - 12);
        const z = Math.min(end, r.next + 20);

        console.log("CONTEXT:");
        for (let x = a; x < z; x += 16) {
          const e = Math.min(x + 15, z - 1);
          console.log(
            hex32(x) + "  " +
            [...b.slice(x, e + 1)]
              .map(v => v.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
              .join(" ")
          );
        }

        // Look forward for memory stores
        let scan = r.next;
        const limit = Math.min(end, scan + 40);

        while (scan < limit) {
          const sop = b[scan++];

          if (
            sop === 0x36 ||
            sop === 0x37 ||
            sop === 0x3a ||
            sop === 0x3b ||
            sop === 0x3c ||
            sop === 0x3e ||
            sop === 0x3f
          ) {
            const al = uleb(scan);
            const off = uleb(al.next);

            console.log(
              `POSSIBLE STORE @ ${hex32(scan - 1)} ` +
              `opcode=0x${sop.toString(16).padStart(2,"0")} ` +
              `offset=${off.value}`
            );

            scan = off.next;
            continue;
          }

          if (sop === 0x41) {
            scan = sleb(scan).next;
            continue;
          }

          if (sop === 0x20 || sop === 0x21 || sop === 0x22) {
            scan = uleb(scan).next;
            continue;
          }

          if (sop === 0x10) {
            scan = uleb(scan).next;
            continue;
          }

          if (sop === 0x11) {
            const a1 = uleb(scan);
            const a2 = uleb(a1.next);
            scan = a2.next;
            continue;
          }
        }
      }

      pos = r.next;
      continue;
    }

    // Skip common immediates
    if (op === 0x10) {
      pos = uleb(pos).next;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x11) {
      const a1 = uleb(pos);
      const a2 = uleb(a1.next);
      pos = a2.next;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 || op === 0x22 ||
        op === 0x23 || op === 0x24) {
      pos = uleb(pos).next;
      continue;
    }

    if (
      op === 0x28 || op === 0x29 ||
      op === 0x2a || op === 0x2b ||
      op === 0x2c || op === 0x2d ||
      op === 0x2e || op === 0x2f ||
      op === 0x30 || op === 0x31 ||
      op === 0x32 || op === 0x33 ||
      op === 0x34 || op === 0x35 ||
      op === 0x36 || op === 0x37 ||
      op === 0x38 || op === 0x39 ||
      op === 0x3a || op === 0x3b ||
      op === 0x3c || op === 0x3d ||
      op === 0x3e || op === 0x3f
    ) {
      const al = uleb(pos);
      const off = uleb(al.next);
      pos = off.next;
      continue;
    }
  }

  q = end;
}

console.log("\n============================================================");
console.log(`TOTAL DIRECT S2 ADDRESS HITS : ${hits}`);
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\findSegment413IndirectConsumers.ts
====================================================================================================
import * as fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_MIN = 4057;
const TARGET_MAX = 4188;

const CODE_START = 0x00006e7d;
const CODE_END   = 0x00324ef5;

function readULEB(
    buf: Buffer,
    p: number
): { value: number; next: number } {

    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {

        const b = buf[p++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: p };
        }

        shift += 7;

        if (shift > 35) {
            throw new Error("ULEB overflow");
        }
    }
}

function hex(n: number): string {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function readU32LE(
    buf: Buffer,
    p: number
): number {

    return (
        buf[p] |
        (buf[p + 1] << 8) |
        (buf[p + 2] << 16) |
        (buf[p + 3] << 24)
    ) >>> 0;
}

function parseSections(buf: Buffer) {

    const sections: {
        id: number;
        start: number;
        end: number;
        size: number;
    }[] = [];

    let p = 8;

    while (p < buf.length) {

        const id = buf[p++];

        const s = readULEB(buf, p);
        p = s.next;

        const start = p;
        const end = p + s.value;

        sections.push({
            id,
            start,
            end,
            size: s.value
        });

        p = end;
    }

    return sections;
}

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * FUNCTION SECTION
 *
 * Defined function N has a type index.
 * Function index = imported_count + defined_index.
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 */

function parseFunctionTypes(
    buf: Buffer,
    section: {
        start: number;
        end: number;
    },
    importedFunctions: number
) {

    let p = section.start;

    const countInfo = readULEB(buf, p);
    p = countInfo.next;

    const result = new Map<number, number>();

    for (let i = 0; i < countInfo.value; i++) {

        const typeInfo = readULEB(buf, p);
        p = typeInfo.next;

        const functionIndex =
            importedFunctions + i;

        result.set(
            functionIndex,
            typeInfo.value
        );
    }

    return result;
}

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * IMPORT SECTION
 *
 * Count imported functions.
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 */

function countImportedFunctions(
    buf: Buffer,
    section: {
        start: number;
        end: number;
    }
) {

    let p = section.start;

    const countInfo = readULEB(buf, p);
    p = countInfo.next;

    let functionCount = 0;

    for (
        let i = 0;
        i < countInfo.value;
        i++
    ) {

        /*
         * module name
         */
        const moduleLen = readULEB(buf, p);
        p = moduleLen.next + moduleLen.value;

        /*
         * field/name
         */
        const fieldLen = readULEB(buf, p);
        p = fieldLen.next + fieldLen.value;

        /*
         * import kind
         */
        const kind = buf[p++];

        if (kind === 0x00) {

            /*
             * func type index
             */
            const type = readULEB(buf, p);
            p = type.next;

            functionCount++;

        } else if (kind === 0x01) {

            /*
             * table
             */
            p++;

            const flags = readULEB(buf, p);
            p = flags.next;

            const min = readULEB(buf, p);
            p = min.next;

            if (flags.value & 1) {
                const max = readULEB(buf, p);
                p = max.next;
            }

        } else if (kind === 0x02) {

            /*
             * memory
             */
            const flags = readULEB(buf, p);
            p = flags.next;

            const min = readULEB(buf, p);
            p = min.next;

            if (flags.value & 1) {
                const max = readULEB(buf, p);
                p = max.next;
            }

        } else if (kind === 0x03) {

            /*
             * global
             */
            p += 2;

        } else {

            throw new Error(
                `Unknown import kind ${kind}`
            );
        }
    }

    return functionCount;
}

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * ELEMENT TABLE
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 */

function parseElementTable(
    buf: Buffer,
    section: {
        start: number;
        end: number;
    }
) {

    let p = section.start;

    const segmentCount = readULEB(buf, p);
    p = segmentCount.next;

    if (segmentCount.value !== 1) {
        throw new Error(
            `Expected 1 element segment, got ${segmentCount.value}`
        );
    }

    const flags = readULEB(buf, p);
    p = flags.next;

    if (flags.value !== 0) {
        throw new Error(
            `Expected active table segment flags=0, got ${flags.value}`
        );
    }

    const offsetOpcode = buf[p++];

    if (offsetOpcode !== 0x41) {
        throw new Error(
            `Expected i32.const, got ${hex(offsetOpcode)}`
        );
    }

    const offset = readULEB(buf, p);
    p = offset.next;

    const endOpcode = buf[p++];

    if (endOpcode !== 0x0b) {
        throw new Error(
            `Expected end, got ${hex(endOpcode)}`
        );
    }

    const countInfo = readULEB(buf, p);
    p = countInfo.next;

    const tableToFunction =
        new Map<number, number>();

    const functionToTable =
        new Map<number, number>();

    for (
        let i = 0;
        i < countInfo.value;
        i++
    ) {

        const fnInfo = readULEB(buf, p);
        p = fnInfo.next;

        const tableIndex =
            offset.value + i;

        tableToFunction.set(
            tableIndex,
            fnInfo.value
        );

        if (!functionToTable.has(fnInfo.value)) {
            functionToTable.set(
                fnInfo.value,
                tableIndex
            );
        }
    }

    return {
        count: countInfo.value,
        cursor: p,
        tableToFunction,
        functionToTable
    };
}

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * CODE SECTION
 *
 * Build exact function-body ranges.
 * We don't decode instructions.
 *
 * We only need to determine which function body contains
 * each byte-pattern candidate.
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 */

function parseCodeBodies(
    buf: Buffer,
    section: {
        start: number;
        end: number;
    }
) {

    let p = section.start;

    const countInfo = readULEB(buf, p);
    p = countInfo.next;

    const bodies: {
        functionIndex: number;
        bodyStart: number;
        bodyEnd: number;
        bodySize: number;
    }[] = [];

    for (
        let i = 0;
        i < countInfo.value;
        i++
    ) {

        const sizeInfo = readULEB(buf, p);
        p = sizeInfo.next;

        const bodyStart = p;
        const bodyEnd =
            bodyStart + sizeInfo.value;

        bodies.push({
            functionIndex: 83 + i,
            bodyStart,
            bodyEnd,
            bodySize: sizeInfo.value
        });

        p = bodyEnd;
    }

    return {
        count: countInfo.value,
        cursor: p,
        bodies
    };
}

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * LEB ENCODER
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 */

function encodeULEB(
    value: number
): number[] {

    const out: number[] = [];

    do {

        let byte =
            value & 0x7f;

        value =
            Math.floor(value / 128);

        if (value !== 0) {
            byte |= 0x80;
        }

        out.push(byte);

    } while (value !== 0);

    return out;
}

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * MAIN
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 */

export async function run() {

    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" SEGMENT 413 INDIRECT CONSUMER SEARCH");
    console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
    console.log("============================================================");

    const buf =
        fs.readFileSync(WASM);

    console.log("");
    console.log("WASM size :", buf.length);

    const sections =
        parseSections(buf);

    const importSection =
        sections.find(x => x.id === 2);

    const functionSection =
        sections.find(x => x.id === 3);

    const elementSection =
        sections.find(x => x.id === 9);

    const codeSection =
        sections.find(x => x.id === 10);

    if (
        !importSection ||
        !functionSection ||
        !elementSection ||
        !codeSection
    ) {
        throw new Error(
            "Required section missing"
        );
    }

    /*
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     * IMPORTS
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     */

    const importedFunctions =
        countImportedFunctions(
            buf,
            importSection
        );

    console.log("");
    console.log(
        "Imported functions :",
        importedFunctions
    );

    /*
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     * FUNCTION TYPES
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     */

    const functionTypes =
        parseFunctionTypes(
            buf,
            functionSection,
            importedFunctions
        );

    console.log(
        "Function type entries :",
        functionTypes.size
    );

    /*
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     * ELEMENT TABLE
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     */

    const element =
        parseElementTable(
            buf,
            elementSection
        );

    console.log("");
    console.log(
        "Element segments : 1"
    );

    console.log(
        "Table offset     : 1"
    );

    console.log(
        "Function count   :",
        element.count
    );

    console.log(
        "Element cursor   :",
        hex(element.cursor)
    );

    console.log(
        "Expected cursor  :",
        hex(elementSection.end)
    );

    /*
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     * TARGET MAP
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     */

    type Target = {
        fn: number;
        type: number;
        table: number;
        pattern: number[];
    };

    const targets: Target[] = [];

    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" TARGET DISPATCH MAP");
    console.log("============================================================");

    for (
        let fn = TARGET_MIN;
        fn <= TARGET_MAX;
        fn++
    ) {

        const type =
            functionTypes.get(fn);

        const table =
            element.functionToTable.get(fn);

        if (
            type === undefined ||
            table === undefined
        ) {
            console.log(
                `function ${fn}: MISSING TYPE OR TABLE`
            );
            continue;
        }

        /*
         * call_indirect encoding:
         *
         * 0x11
         * typeidx
         * tableidx
         *
         * We search exact ULEB sequences.
         */

        const typeBytes =
            encodeULEB(type);

        const tableBytes =
            encodeULEB(table);

        const pattern = [
            0x11,
            ...typeBytes,
            ...tableBytes
        ];

        targets.push({
            fn,
            type,
            table,
            pattern
        });
    }

    /*
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     * CODE BODY INDEX
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     */

    const code =
        parseCodeBodies(
            buf,
            codeSection
        );

    console.log("");
    console.log("Code bodies :", code.count);

    console.log(
        "Code cursor :",
        hex(code.cursor)
    );

    console.log(
        "Expected    :",
        hex(codeSection.end)
    );

    /*
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     * SEARCH
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     */

    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" SEARCHING CODE SECTION");
    console.log("============================================================");

    const matches: {
        targetFunction: number;
        type: number;
        table: number;
        codeOffset: number;
        caller: number;
    }[] = [];

    /*
     * Binary-search body containing an offset.
     */

    function findBody(
        offset: number
    ) {

        let lo = 0;
        let hi = code.bodies.length - 1;

        while (lo <= hi) {

            const mid =
                (lo + hi) >> 1;

            const body =
                code.bodies[mid];

            if (
                offset >= body.bodyStart &&
                offset < body.bodyEnd
            ) {
                return body;
            }

            if (offset < body.bodyStart) {
                hi = mid - 1;
            } else {
                lo = mid + 1;
            }
        }

        return undefined;
    }

    for (const target of targets) {

        const pat =
            target.pattern;

        for (
            let p = CODE_START;
            p + pat.length <= CODE_END;
            p++
        ) {

            let ok = true;

            for (
                let j = 0;
                j < pat.length;
                j++
            ) {

                if (buf[p + j] !== pat[j]) {
                    ok = false;
                    break;
                }
            }

            if (!ok) {
                continue;
            }

            const body =
                findBody(p);

            if (!body) {
                continue;
            }

            matches.push({
                targetFunction: target.fn,
                type: target.type,
                table: target.table,
                codeOffset: p,
                caller: body.functionIndex
            });
        }
    }

    /*
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     * OUTPUT
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     */

    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" INDIRECT CONSUMER MATCHES");
    console.log("============================================================");

    if (matches.length === 0) {

        console.log(
            "No exact call_indirect(type,table) byte-pattern matches."
        );

    } else {

        for (const m of matches) {

            console.log(
                `target function ${m.targetFunction}`
            );

            console.log(
                `  type  = ${m.type}`
            );

            console.log(
                `  table = ${hex(m.table)}`
            );

            console.log(
                `  call_indirect @ ${hex(m.codeOffset)}`
            );

            console.log(
                `  caller = function ${m.caller}`
            );

            console.log("");
        }
    }

    /*
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     * SUMMARY BY TARGET
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     */

    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" SUMMARY BY TARGET");
    console.log("============================================================");

    for (const target of targets) {

        const found =
            matches.filter(
                x =>
                    x.targetFunction === target.fn
            );

        console.log(
            `function ${target.fn}` +
            `  table=${hex(target.table)}` +
            `  type=${target.type}` +
            `  matches=${found.length}`
        );

        for (const x of found) {

            console.log(
                `    caller=${x.caller}` +
                ` @ ${hex(x.codeOffset)}`
            );
        }
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" END");
    console.log("============================================================");
}

import fs from "fs";

function hx(n: number, w = 8): string {
    return n.toString(16).padStart(w, "0");
}

function uleb(n: number): number[] {
    const out: number[] = [];

    while (n >= 0x80) {
        out.push((n & 0x7f) | 0x80);
        n >>>= 7;
    }

    out.push(n);
    return out;
}

function findAll(buf: Buffer, pattern: Buffer): number[] {
    const hits: number[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i <= buf.length - pattern.length; i++) {
        let ok = true;

        for (let j = 0; j < pattern.length; j++) {
            if (buf[i + j] !== pattern[j]) {
                ok = false;
                break;
            }
        }

        if (ok) hits.push(i);
    }

    return hits;
}

function dump(buf: Buffer, pos: number, radius = 48) {
    const start = Math.max(0, pos - radius);
    const end = Math.min(buf.length, pos + radius + 16);

    console.log(
        `\n--- file offset 0x${hx(pos)} (${pos}) ---`
    );

    for (let p = start; p < end; p += 16) {
        const bytes: string[] = [];

        for (let j = 0; j < 16 && p + j < end; j++) {
            bytes.push(
                buf[p + j].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")
            );
        }

        console.log(
            `0x${hx(p)}  ${bytes.join(" ")}`
        );
    }
}

function readLeb(buf: Buffer, pos: number): {
    value: number;
    length: number;
} {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < 5 && pos + i < buf.length; i++) {
        const b = buf[pos + i];

        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return {
                value: value >>> 0,
                length: i + 1,
            };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }

    return {
        value: 0,
        length: 0,
    };
}

async function main() {
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" SEGMENT 669 CODE REFERENCE SEARCH v22");
    console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
    console.log("============================================================");

    const wasmPath =
        "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

    const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

    console.log(`WASM size: ${wasm.length.toLocaleString()}`);

    console.log("\n============================================================");
    console.log(" RUNTIME ADDRESSES");
    console.log("============================================================");

    const targets = [
        0x00195348,
        0x0019534c,
        0x00195350,
        0x00195354,
        0x00195358,
    ];

    for (const addr of targets) {
        const encoded = Buffer.from(uleb(addr));

        console.log(
            `addr=0x${hx(addr)}`
        );

        console.log(
            `  ULEB128 = ${encoded.toString("hex")}`
        );
    }

    console.log("\n============================================================");
    console.log(" SEARCH ULEB128 ADDRESS ENCODINGS");
    console.log("============================================================");

    for (const addr of targets) {
        const encoded = Buffer.from(uleb(addr));
        const hits = findAll(wasm, encoded);

        console.log(
            `\n0x${hx(addr)} -> ${hits.length} hit(s)`
        );

        for (const h of hits.slice(0, 50)) {
            console.log(
                `  file=0x${hx(h)} (${h})`
            );
        }

        for (const h of hits.slice(0, 15)) {
            dump(wasm, h, 32);
        }
    }

    console.log("\n============================================================");
    console.log(" SEARCH i32.const + ADDRESS");
    console.log("============================================================");

    // WASM i32.const opcode = 0x41
    // Followed by signed LEB128 immediate.
    //
    // For these positive addresses the ULEB representation
    // is also the relevant byte sequence for this search.

    for (const addr of targets) {
        const encoded = Buffer.from(uleb(addr));

        const pattern = Buffer.concat([
            Buffer.from([0x41]),
            encoded,
        ]);

        const hits = findAll(wasm, pattern);

        console.log(
            `\ni32.const 0x${hx(addr)}`
        );

        console.log(
            `pattern=${pattern.toString("hex")}`
        );

        console.log(
            `hits=${hits.length}`
        );

        for (const h of hits.slice(0, 50)) {
            console.log(
                `  file=0x${hx(h)} (${h})`
            );
        }

        for (const h of hits.slice(0, 15)) {
            dump(wasm, h, 48);
        }
    }

    console.log("\n============================================================");
    console.log(" SEARCH TARGET VALUES AS i32.const");
    console.log("============================================================");

    for (const value of [4788, 4789, 4790, 4791, 58423]) {
        const encoded = Buffer.from(uleb(value));

        const pattern = Buffer.concat([
            Buffer.from([0x41]),
            encoded,
        ]);

        const hits = findAll(wasm, pattern);

        console.log(
            `\nvalue=${value} pattern=${pattern.toString("hex")} hits=${hits.length}`
        );

        for (const h of hits.slice(0, 30)) {
            console.log(
                `  file=0x${hx(h)} (${h})`
            );
        }
    }

    console.log("\n============================================================");
    console.log(" RAW BYTE SEQUENCE: TARGET + LOAD INSTRUCTIONS");
    console.log("============================================================");

    // Common WASM load opcodes:
    // i32.load       = 0x28
    // i64.load       = 0x29
    // i32.load8_s    = 0x2c
    // i32.load8_u    = 0x2d
    // i32.load16_s   = 0x2e
    // i32.load16_u   = 0x2f
    //
    // Search for target address encoding and inspect whether
    // a load instruction occurs nearby.

    for (const addr of targets) {
        const encoded = Buffer.from(uleb(addr));
        const hits = findAll(wasm, encoded);

        console.log(
            `\nADDRESS 0x${hx(addr)}`
        );

        for (const h of hits.slice(0, 20)) {
            const start = Math.max(0, h - 8);
            const end = Math.min(wasm.length, h + encoded.length + 12);

            const nearby = wasm.subarray(start, end);

            console.log(
                `  offset=0x${hx(h)}`
            );

            console.log(
                `  nearby=${nearby.toString("hex")}`
            );
        }
    }

    console.log("\n============================================================");
    console.log(" _emulate CONTEXT");
    console.log("============================================================");

    const emulate = 69882;

    console.log(
        `_emulate known file offset = 0x${hx(emulate)}`
    );

    dump(wasm, emulate, 96);

    console.log("\n============================================================");
    console.log(" DISTANCE CHECK");
    console.log("============================================================");

    const interesting = [
        0x004b3f11,
        0x004b4047,
        0x0019534c,
        emulate,
    ];

    for (const a of interesting) {
        for (const b of interesting) {
            if (a === b) continue;

            console.log(
                `0x${hx(a)} -> 0x${hx(b)} : ${b - a}`
            );
        }
    }

    console.log("\n============================================================");
    console.log(" DONE");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log("READ ONLY: no TON connection");
    console.log("READ ONLY: no wallet");
    console.log("READ ONLY: no transaction");
    console.log("READ ONLY: no signing");
}

main().catch(err => {
    console.error("\nSCRIPT ERROR:");
    console.error(err);
});

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGET = 0x19534c;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FIND SELECTOR 0x19534C - RAW ULEB");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TARGET : 0x${TARGET.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`);
console.log(`WASM   : ${wasm.length}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

function readULEB(buf: Buffer, off: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  for (let i = 0; i < 10 && off + i < buf.length; i++) {
    const b = buf[off + i];
    value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return {
        value,
        bytes: i + 1,
        hex: buf.subarray(off, off + i + 1).toString("hex"),
      };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }

  return null;
}

let hits = 0;

for (let off = 0; off <= wasm.length - 4; off++) {
  const v =
    wasm[off] |
    (wasm[off + 1] << 8) |
    (wasm[off + 2] << 16) |
    (wasm[off + 3] << 24);

  if ((v >>> 0) !== TARGET) continue;

  hits++;

  console.log("");
  console.log(`HIT #${hits}`);
  console.log(`RAW OFFSET : 0x${off.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`);

  const start = Math.max(0, off - 32);
  const end = Math.min(wasm.length, off + 40);

  console.log(`RANGE      : 0x${start.toString(16)} .. 0x${end.toString(16)}`);
  console.log(`RAW        : ${wasm.subarray(start, end).toString("hex")}`);

  console.log("ULEB CANDIDATES:");

  for (let p = start; p < Math.min(end, off + 16); p++) {
    const u = readULEB(wasm, p);

    if (u && u.value === TARGET) {
      console.log(
        `  OFFSET 0x${p.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} ` +
        `ULEB=${u.value} BYTES=${u.bytes} HEX=${u.hex}`
      );
    }
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log(`TOTAL RAW 32-BIT HITS : ${hits}`);
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const targets = new Set([
  0x1A7914,
  0x1A7994,
  0x1A7A14
]);

function u(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, x;
  do {
    x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 127) * 2 ** s;
    s += 7;
  } while (x & 128);
  return [v >>> 0, p];
}

function hex(x) {
  return "0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

let p = 8;
let codeStart = 0;

while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p++];
  let size;
  [size, p] = u(p);

  if (id === 10) {
    codeStart = p;
    break;
  }

  p += size;
}

let q = codeStart;
let count;
[count, q] = u(q);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SELECTOR ADDRESS CODE REFERENCES");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

let hits = 0;

for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
  let size;
  [size, q] = u(q);

  const start = q;
  const end = q + size;
  const func = i + 91;

  for (let x = start; x < end; x++) {
    if (b[x] !== 0x41) continue;

    let value, z;
    [value, z] = u(x + 1);

    if (!targets.has(value)) continue;

    console.log("");
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
    console.log("FUNC   :", func);
    console.log("OFFSET :", hex(x));
    console.log("CONST  :", hex(value));

    const from = Math.max(start, x - 32);
    const to = Math.min(end, x + 48);

    console.log(
      "RAW    :",
      [...b.slice(from, to)]
        .map(v => v.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
        .join(" ")
    );

    hits++;
  }

  q = end;
}

console.log("");
console.log("TOTAL REFERENCES :", hits);
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\findSelectorConstants.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGET = 0x19534c;

function uleb(pos: number) {
  let v = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const b = wasm[p++];
    v |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;

    shift += 7;
    if (shift > 35) break;
  }

  return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };
}

function section(idWanted: number) {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const id = wasm[p++];

    const s = uleb(p);
    p = s.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + s.value;

    if (id === idWanted) return { start, end };

    p = end;
  }

  return null;
}

const code = section(10);

if (!code) throw new Error("CODE section not found");

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SELECTOR CONSTANT SEARCH");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TARGET DEC : ${TARGET}`);
console.log(`TARGET HEX : 0x${TARGET.toString(16)}`);
console.log(`CODE       : 0x${code.start.toString(16)} .. 0x${code.end.toString(16)}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let p = code.start;

const bodyCount = uleb(p);
p = bodyCount.next;

let hits = 0;

for (let f = 0; f < bodyCount.value; f++) {

  const bs = uleb(p);
  p = bs.next;

  const bodyStart = p;
  const bodyEnd = p + bs.value;

  let q = bodyStart;

  // locals
  const groups = uleb(q);
  q = groups.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < groups.value; i++) {
    const n = uleb(q);
    q = n.next;
    q++;
  }

  // Scan instructions.
  while (q < bodyEnd) {

    const opPos = q;
    const op = wasm[q++];

    // i32.const
    if (op === 0x41) {
      const x = uleb(q);
      q = x.next;

      if (x.value === TARGET) {
        hits++;

        console.log("");
        console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
        console.log(`BODY INDEX : ${f}`);
        console.log(`DEFINED    : ${f + 83}`);
        console.log(`OFFSET     : 0x${opPos.toString(16)}`);
        console.log(`i32.const  : 0x${TARGET.toString(16)}`);
      }

      continue;
    }

    // i64.const
    if (op === 0x42) {
      const x = uleb(q);
      q = x.next;
      continue;
    }

    // local.get/set/tee
    if (op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) {
      const x = uleb(q);
      q = x.next;
      continue;
    }

    // call
    if (op === 0x10) {
      const x = uleb(q);
      q = x.next;
      continue;
    }

    // br / br_if
    if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
      const x = uleb(q);
      q = x.next;
      continue;
    }

    // memory load/store immediates
    if (
      (op >= 0x28 && op <= 0x3e)
    ) {
      const align = uleb(q);
      q = align.next;

      const offset = uleb(q);
      q = offset.next;

      continue;
    }

    // block / loop / if
    if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04) {
      q++;
      continue;
    }

    // memory.size / memory.grow
    if (op === 0x3f || op === 0x40) {
      q++;
      continue;
    }
  }

  p = bodyEnd;
}

console.log("");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log(`TOTAL EXACT i32.const HITS : ${hits}`);
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");

FILE: scripts\findSelectorConstReal.mjs

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const targets = new Map([
  [5220, "FUNC1968_A"],
  [5232, "FUNC1968_B"],
  [5235, "FUNC1968_C"],
]);

function readULEB(pos) {
  let v = 0, shift = 0, n = 0;
  while (pos + n < b.length) {
    const x = b[pos + n++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << shift;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) return { value: v >>> 0, len: n };
    shift += 7;
    if (shift > 35) return null;
  }
  return null;
}

console.log("=".repeat(68));
console.log("REAL CODE SELECTOR CONSTANTS");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("=".repeat(68));

for (let p = 0; p < b.length; p++) {
  if (b[p] !== 0x41) continue; // i32.const

  const r = readULEB(p + 1);
  if (!r) continue;

  if (!targets.has(r.value)) continue;

  console.log(
    `FILE 0x${p.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")} ` +
    `i32.const ${r.value} ` +
    `(${targets.get(r.value)})`
  );

  const s = Math.max(0, p - 12);
  const e = Math.min(b.length, p + 16);

  console.log(
    "  " +
    Array.from(b.slice(s, e))
      .map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2,"0"))
      .join(" ")
  );
}

console.log("\n" + "=".repeat(68));
console.log("DONE");
console.log("=".repeat(68));

FILE: scripts\findSelectorDataValues.mjs
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGETS = new Set([5220, 5232, 5235]);

function hex(x) {
  return "0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("STATIC DATA SELECTOR VALUES");
console.log("TARGET : 5220 / 5232 / 5235");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const target of TARGETS) {
  const bytes = Buffer.from([
    target & 0xff,
    (target >>> 8) & 0xff,
    (target >>> 16) & 0xff,
    (target >>> 24) & 0xff
  ]);

  let hits = 0;

  for (let p = 0; p <= b.length - 4; p++) {
    if (
      b[p] === bytes[0] &&
      b[p + 1] === bytes[1] &&
      b[p + 2] === bytes[2] &&
      b[p + 3] === bytes[3]
    ) {
      console.log(
        "VALUE", target,
        "FILE", hex(p),
        "BYTES", [...bytes]
          .map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
          .join(" ")
      );
      hits++;
    }
  }

  console.log("TARGET", target, "HITS", hits);
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\findSelectorMapCopies.mjs
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function u(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, x;
  do {
    x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 127) * 2 ** s;
    s += 7;
  } while (x & 128);
  return [v >>> 0, p];
}

function hx(x) {
  return "0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function readU32(p) {
  return (
    b[p] |
    (b[p + 1] << 8) |
    (b[p + 2] << 16) |
    (b[p + 3] << 24)
  ) >>> 0;
}

let p = 8;
let dataStart = 0;

while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p++];
  let size;
  [size, p] = u(p);

  if (id === 11) {
    dataStart = p;
    break;
  }

  p += size;
}

let q = dataStart;
let count;
[count, q] = u(q);

const segments = [];

for (let seg = 0; seg < count; seg++) {
  let flags;
  [flags, q] = u(q);

  if (flags !== 0) break;

  if (b[q++] !== 0x41) break;

  let memStart;
  [memStart, q] = u(q);

  if (b[q++] !== 0x0b) break;

  let size;
  [size, q] = u(q);

  segments.push({
    seg,
    memStart,
    start: q,
    end: q + size
  });

  q += size;
}

// Build signatures from 1165..1167.
// Each map consists of 14 pairs = 28 u32 values.

const sourceSegs = [1165, 1166, 1167];

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SELECTOR MAP COPIES");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const source of sourceSegs) {
  const s = segments[source];

  if (!s) {
    console.log("MISSING SEGMENT", source);
    continue;
  }

  const sig = [];

  for (let off = 0; off + 8 <= s.end - s.start; off += 8) {
    const key = readU32(s.start + off);
    const selector = readU32(s.start + off + 4);
    sig.push([key, selector]);
  }

  console.log("");
  console.log("SOURCE SEGMENT :", source);
  console.log("RUNTIME        :", hx(s.memStart));
  console.log("PAIRS          :", sig.length);

  for (const [key, selector] of sig) {
    console.log(
      "  KEY", key,
      "SELECTOR", selector
    );
  }

  console.log("");
  console.log("MATCHING SEGMENTS:");

  let matches = 0;

  for (const t of segments) {
    const len = t.end - t.start;

    if (len !== s.end - s.start) continue;

    let same = true;

    for (let off = 0; off < len; off += 4) {
      if (readU32(s.start + off) !== readU32(t.start + off)) {
        same = false;
        break;
      }
    }

    if (same) {
      console.log(
        "  SEG", t.seg,
        "RUNTIME", hx(t.memStart),
        "SIZE", len
      );
      matches++;
    }
  }

  console.log("MATCH COUNT :", matches);
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
